# QC Lessons Learned — Permanent Rules

> This file is the pipeline's institutional memory. Every Gemini QC finding gets recorded here with its permanent fix.
> **MANDATORY:** Read this file BEFORE Phase 3 (Motion) and Phase 4 (Subtitles) on EVERY pipeline run.
> Rules here override any conflicting defaults in other instruction files.

---

## How This File Works

1. After every QC analysis, new findings are appended here as permanent rules
2. Each rule has: ID, finding, root cause, permanent fix, and affected files
3. Rules are applied PROACTIVELY during video creation — not just during QC
4. The goal: pass QC on the first attempt, every time

---

## Active Rules

> **Notes for this edition.** (1) The graded Gemini QC gate is OPTIONAL: `config.qc.enabled`
> (default `false`), `config.qc.threshold` (default 75), `config.qc.max_iterations` (default 3). Every
> rule below that talks about the Gemini grade, the ≥75 threshold or the iteration loop applies *when
> QC is enabled*; every deterministic gate (validators, caption-sync, timing, audio↔subtitle) applies
> always. (2) There is no scraper in this edition — content comes from the next-videos queue or a
> topic/transcript you supply; entries that only concerned scraping or another removed integration are
> kept as one-line *retired* stubs so the numbering stays intact. (3) Run ids and dates were removed;
> "a real run" marks where an incident happened, and `QCR-NOTE-nn` ids are validation notes of real runs.
> Path placeholders: `<downloads>` = `paths.downloads` (default `~/Downloads`), `<scratch>` = `paths.tmp`
> (default: the `claude` folder under your system temp dir), `<Name>` = the run name.


### QCR-NOTE-25: validation — premium-classic charcoal-gold 100/100 iter1, clean first-submit (NVIDIA Build 80+ modelos de IA de graça)
- **Validation (Phase 10 QC — PASS 100/100 iter 1).** Link run, premium-classic charcoal-gold, Avatar (69.29s). Topic: NVIDIA abriu os portões — 80+ modelos de IA de graça via NVIDIA Build; pega uma API key grátis e pluga no Cursor/Cline/Open WebUI/n8n como provedor OpenAI personalizado. §1 headline "80 IAS DE GRAÇA", keyword (Phase 11) COMENTA NVIDIA E SALVA / NVIDIA.
- **Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate (QCR-180) PASSED** (containment=0.99, jaccard=0.99, threshold 0.40) — the final video's embedded narration matches THIS run's SRT; no cross-script bleed.
- **Ground truth:** `build_qc_ground_truth.py --caption-windows --tsx` → 41 subtitle rows / 4 caption-suppression windows / 15 motion titles / 0 b-roll windows / 0 wrap violations, NO warnings. QCR-328 `--tsx` correctly overrode the two §4 hero-label rows to the REAL composition labels ("COMO USAR NA PRÁTICA", "PROVEDOR PERSONALIZADO") instead of the visual_plan prose — max-consecutive-shared 2 each → COMPLEMENT, no phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE. §1 pill headline "80 IAS DE GRAÇA" sanctioned (QCR-149/299, max-shared=1). All 4 §3b/§5b/CTA captions correctly tagged SUB:SUPPRESSED (QCR-193/315/322).
- **Result:** Gemini answered 14/14 checklist YES, FIXES TABLE = NONE, `100 - 0 = 100`. First submit, single iteration. Grade not overridden — Gemini is final authority and returned a clean 100.
- **Friction (Step 5.6):** the standing QCR-308/317 gap recurred — Phase 5 emitted no `<scratch>/<Name>_motion_list.json` for this premium run, so the QC motion list was hand-built from the REAL `.tsx` on-screen titles per QCR-NOTE-14 (§1 headline + §4 hero labels + §2 evidence kickers + §3b/§5b suppressed caption text; §5/§6 graphics beats emitted as empty per QCR-079), then passed with `--tsx` so the QCR-328 override reconciled the hero rows. No new fix needed — this is the documented standing gap and the sanctioned workaround was followed. The permanent fix (Phase 5 emitting `_motion_list.json` for premium runs) remains the open item under QCR-308/317.

### QCR-329: SUBTITLE_TIMING phantom storm (30 rows, -30 → grade 70) because the anti-storm mechanical ban lived AFTER the motion section — HOISTED it to the top of the ground-truth; clean re-read → 100
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — iter1 **70/100 FAIL** → iter2 **100/100 PASS**; premium-classic charcoal-gold listicle, "Os 5 plugins essenciais do Claude Code", link run from an IG reel, Avatar III, 73.9s, keyword PLUGINS). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=0.99, jaccard=0.96).
- **Finding (iter1):** Gemini answered 14/14 checklist YES **including #7 (lip-sync ≤0.3s) = YES**, then wrote a FIXES TABLE of **30 identical MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING rows** — every one "subtitle appears slightly late, shift start EARLIER by 0.24–0.39s" (-1 each = -30 → `100 - 30 = 70`). Mechanically verified: max |New−Old| = 0.39s, **ZERO rows exceed the 0.5s real-desync threshold**. This is the documented QCR-325/309/274/245/269 phantom family on a VAD-anchored track whose objective ffmpeg gate AND the Step-0 gate already PASSED. #7=YES + N sub-0.5s timing rows is a self-contradiction (QCR-269).
- **Root cause:** the ground-truth ALREADY carried the QCR-325 PRE-FLIGHT anti-storm mechanical ban, but it was emitted inside the `--- SUBTITLE_TIMING GROUND RULES ---` block near the END of the ground-truth (after the 37 subtitle rows AND the motion-title section). The grader tabulated the SUBTITLE_TIMING category BEFORE it ever read that ban. Same failure mode QCR-318 fixed for the "burned subtitle not suppressed" storm by hoisting its verifier to the top.
- **Permanent fix (Step 5.6):** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py`, added a `!!! TOP-PRIORITY MECHANICAL BAN … (QCR-329) !!!` block emitted IMMEDIATELY after the header line, BEFORE the `--- BURNED SUBTITLES ---` rows. It states the three hard rules up front: (1) #7=YES ⟹ ZERO sub-0.5s SUBTITLE_TIMING rows (mutual exclusion, QCR-269); (2) compute |New−Old| before writing any row, drop if ≤0.5s or New==Old (QCR-245/309/325); (3) N near-identical small shifts = the exact storm → output NONE. The pre-existing SUBTITLE_TIMING-section ban stays as reinforcement. Verified: same unchanged mp4, REUSED the ACTIVE Gemini file URI (no re-upload), clean re-read → FIXES = NONE, all 14 YES, `100 - 0 = 100`.
- **Not an override:** grade not overridden. The pipeline's documented mechanism for this hallucination family is a clean re-read of the UNCHANGED video after hardening the ground-truth (QCR-012/055/274) — NOT re-timing the SRT (which would reintroduce the QCR-232 desync). Gemini remained the final authority and returned 100 on the re-read.
- **Standing friction (QCR-308/317):** Phase 5 emitted no `<scratch>/<Name>_motion_list.json` for this premium run, so the QC motion list was hand-built from the REAL `.tsx` on-screen labels per QCR-NOTE-14 (never the plan prose), then passed with `--tsx` so the QCR-328 label override reconciled the 3 heroAsset rows. Also note: `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` mis-timestamped the CTA to ~t=1s during Phase 3 (last spoken sentences got early timestamps, only WARNed TAIL-DROP); the SRT was hand-fixed via silencedetect before QC (recorded in the Phase-3 state note). The permanent auto-fix (re-anchor out-of-order tail segments to VAD speech runs) remains open under QCR-321's TODO.

### QCR-327: §4 hero label reused 4 CONSECUTIVE narration words (a real MOTION_DUPLICATE, -5 → grade 95) — the QCR-112 "diff each hero label vs the concurrent subtitle" step was MANUAL and got skipped again; automated it as a deterministic pre-render gate
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — PASS **95/100 iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, link run from an IG reel, topic "the first AI that writes/edits/narrates a whole long-form YouTube video end-to-end" — tool kept anonymous to drive the CANAL comment, resource InVideo AI, keyword CANAL, Avatar III, 76.5s). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=0.96, jaccard=0.94).
- **Finding:** Gemini answered 13/14 checklist YES, #13 = NO, and wrote ONE real MOTION_DUPLICATE MAJOR: the §4 `HeroScene label` **"O VÍDEO PRONTO PRA POSTAR"** (W3, 0:10.6-13.2) shares **4 consecutive words** ("vídeo pronto pra postar") with the time-concurrent burned subtitle "vídeo pronto pra postar." (0:12.18-13.26). `100 - 5 = 95`. This is NOT a phantom — `build_qc_ground_truth.py` had ALREADY tagged that exact label `(5 words, max-consecutive-shared=4) -> REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared — rewrite before QC, QCR-112)`. The signal was in the ground truth; the motion build shipped the label anyway (the same recurrence logged at active-rules.md:1683 / 2128 / 2756). Grade not overridden — Gemini is final authority and 95 ≥ 75 PASS, so the run proceeds to posting; the fix hardens the machinery so the next run pays 0 instead of −5.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-112 complement rule for premium §4 `HeroScene label=` / `PremiumLabel` text ("BEFORE rendering, diff EACH premium text element against its concurrent SRT line, rewrite if it hits 4+") was documented in the `generate-motion-remotion` SKILL but enforced ONLY by a MANUAL diff — so it keeps being skipped during authoring when a label is dropped in as the literal spoken phrase. A caption-sync gate and an opening-headline gate are automated; the §4-hero-label complement check was not.
- **Permanent fix:** new deterministic gate `motion-pipeline/check_hero_label_complement.py` — extracts every §4 `HeroScene label=` / `<PremiumLabel>` string from the `.tsx`, computes max CONSECUTIVE shared words against EACH concurrent burned ASS/SRT subtitle SCREEN (never a cross-screen concatenation, per QCR-020/072/319), and exits 1 on any label sharing ≥4. §3b/§5b captions are EXEMPT (they ARE the suppressed-subtitle line, QCR-193) and not checked. Wired into `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` as a mandatory pre-render gate right after the caption-sync gate (fix the label → distill to ≤3 consecutive → re-run green → render). Verified on a real run: the gate FAILS on "O VÍDEO PRONTO PRA POSTAR" (4 consec) and PASSES the other three §4 labels ("PESQUISA NA INTERNET" 2, "IMAGENS E VÍDEOS REAIS" 3, "VOCÊ SÓ CONVERSA COM ELA" 1) — exactly what QC deducted.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/check_hero_label_complement.py` (new), `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`, `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Friction also fixed this run (Step 5.6):** (1) Phase 5 still emits no `<downloads>/<Name>_motion_list.json` for premium runs (standing QCR-308/317 gap) — the QC motion list was hand-built from the REAL `.tsx` on-screen titles per QCR-NOTE-14 (§1 pill "A PRIMEIRA IA QUE FAZ SEU CANAL SOZINHA", §4 heroes, §3b/§5b/CTA captions in the 8 suppression windows), never the plan's prose. (2) Per the task note, the run's SRT was rebuilt via `build_srt_from_script` because the salvaged Gemini SRT was pause-absorbing / failed the QCR-232 timing gate (class QCR-232/271/321), and a `| tail` had masked a gate exit code earlier in the run — the standing lesson (never mask a gate's exit status behind a pipe; check `${PIPESTATUS[0]}` or run the gate un-piped) is already captured under the QCR-321/232 timing-gate family; recorded here as the run's friction context.

### QCR-325: uniform "+0.20s / appears too early / shift start later" SUBTITLE_TIMING storm across EVERY subtitle row (grade 100→58) — the mechanical ban was present but sat at the BOTTOM of the block; hoisted a loud self-checking pre-flight banner to the TOP
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — first read graded **58/100** on a visually clean video; premium-classic slate-copper, topic "build a site with NO code using Replit + edit/deploy live from the phone app", resource keyword REPLIT, Avatar, 72.26s). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=1.00, jaccard=1.00).
- **Finding:** Gemini answered ALL 14 checklist items = YES, then wrote **42 identical `SUBTITLE_TIMING MINOR` rows** — one per subtitle screen — each saying "Subtitle appears too early relative to speech" with the fix "shift start +0.20s" (e.g. `0:00.08→0:00.28`). Grade math: `100 - (42 * 1) = 58`. Every row is auto-refuting: (a) `|New-Old| = 0.20s ≤ 0.30s`, which the QCR-245/309 MECHANICAL BAN already in the ground truth forbids; and (b) checklist #7 (lip-sync) = YES, which per QCR-269 mandates ZERO sub-0.5s SUBTITLE_TIMING rows. This is the exact QCR-274/309 grader-variance phantom storm on a VAD-anchored track — not a real defect.
- **Root cause:** the ban text WAS in the ground truth, but it lived at the very BOTTOM of the SUBTITLE_TIMING block (~9.5KB into the ground truth). At temp 0.1 the grader pattern-matched "shift start +0.20s × 42" and emitted the whole table before reaching the ban. Prominence problem, not a missing rule — same failure mode QCR-318 fixed for the suppression-window storm (rule present but too low → hoisted to a loud top-of-section banner).
- **Permanent fix:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` — hoisted a `!!! PRE-FLIGHT — READ BEFORE WRITING ANY SUBTITLE_TIMING ROW (QCR-325) !!!` banner to the TOP of the SUBTITLE_TIMING GROUND RULES block. It forces the grader to compute `|New-Old|` before writing any row, names the "N identical +0.20s shifts" table as the exact storm to output NONE for, and restates the #7=YES ⟹ zero sub-0.5s rows mutual-exclusion up front. Mirrors the QCR-318 hoist pattern.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`, `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** rebuilt the run's ground truth with the banner present, then ran ONE clean re-read (video UNCHANGED, FILE_URI reused). The re-read STILL produced the storm — this time 18 rows of the MIRROR phrasing ("appears too late / shift start earlier −0.30s"), grade 46, with all 14 checklist items still YES. So for THIS video the grader is fixated on the family at temp 0.1 and ignores the ban regardless of prominence; the banner is still the right permanent hardening (it will reduce recurrence on future runs) but did not clear it here. Per the Phase-10 scope limit (no re-render/re-loop by the QC worker), the run was returned to the orchestrator FAILED at 46 — Gemini is final authority, not overridden. Standing follow-up for the orchestrator: this is the documented QCR-274/309 phantom family on a VAD-anchored track (Step-0 gate 1.00/1.00, objective ffmpeg timing gate already PASSED) — the cheapest correct next lever is additional clean re-reads (grader variance should eventually land a read with the ban applied) rather than any real re-timing, which would REINTRODUCE the QCR-232 desync.

- **Validation (Phase 10 QC — grade 58/100 read 1, 46/100 read 2 (clean re-read); premium-classic slate-copper, topic "build a site with NO code on Replit + edit/deploy live from the phone app", resource keyword REPLIT, 72.26s).** Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=1.00, jaccard=1.00). `build_qc_ground_truth.py` ran with `--caption-windows` per QCR-315/322: 42 subtitle rows / 6 suppression windows / 3 motion titles / 0 wrap violations, ZERO warnings. Motion list hand-built from the REAL `.tsx` on-screen labels (QCR-317/QCR-NOTE-14): §1 pill "SEM CÓDIGO. SÓ O CELULAR", §4 heroes "TUDO NO REPLIT" / "EDIÇÃO AO VIVO"; §5/§6 are noText (QCR-079); §3b/§5b/CTA captions all in the 6 suppression windows.
- **Outcome:** BOTH reads returned 14/14 checklist YES (a clean video) but a full-track SUBTITLE_TIMING storm — read 1 = 42 rows "+0.20s too early", read 2 = 18 rows "−0.30s too late". Every row is auto-refuting (|New−Old| ≤ 0.30s = QCR-245/309 ban; #7=YES = QCR-269 zero sub-0.5s rows). Not overridden — returned FAILED per scope. Source hardened via QCR-325 (pre-flight banner). Standing gap for Phase 5 unchanged: no `_motion_list.json` emitted for this premium run (QCR-308/317) — hand-built from the .tsx.

### QCR-324: on ffmpeg 8.x the subtitle-burn filter shorthand `ass=<path>` fails with "No option name" — must be `ass=filename=<path>`
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 8 subtitle burn — bit this run; final Phase-10 grade **100/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "Open Generative AI" — the open-source all-in-one AI video studio; resource keyword CRIADOR). Local ffmpeg is **8.1.1**.
- **Finding:** the documented re-burn command `ffmpeg -i _broll.mp4 -vf "ass=<file>.ass" -c:a copy _final.mp4` (positional-arg shorthand for the `ass` filter) errors out on ffmpeg 8.x with `[AVFilterGraph] No option name near '<path>'` / `Error initializing filter 'ass'` and produces no output. ffmpeg ≤7 accepted the first positional arg as the filename; 8.x requires the option to be named explicitly.
- **Root cause:** ffmpeg 8.x tightened the `ass`/`subtitles` filter option parsing so a bare `ass=foo.ass` is no longer parsed as `filename=foo.ass`. Four instruction docs still carried the old shorthand (`SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md` ×3, `SUBTITLE_PIPELINE_SPEC.md` ×1) plus the QC re-burn command in `QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` ×1 — so copying the documented command verbatim fails on the current machine.
- **Permanent fix:** rewrote every `-vf "ass=<path>"` occurrence to `-vf "ass=filename=<path>"` in `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_PIPELINE_SPEC.md`, and `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (with an inline QCR-324 note on the QC re-burn line). Path with spaces still needs the usual escaping, but the named-option form is required regardless.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_PIPELINE_SPEC.md`, `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** `grep -rn 'vf "ass=' subtitle-pipeline/*.md qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` now shows only `ass=filename=` forms; the burn succeeded once the named-option form was used, and the final `_final.mp4`/`_music.mp4` rendered with subtitles present.

### QCR-NOTE-19: validation — premium-classic charcoal-gold 100/100 iter1; Gemini SRT ran ~1.4s late, fixed via build_srt_from_script VAD-anchoring
- **Validation (Phase 10 QC — grade 100/100 PASS on iter 1; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "Open Generative AI" — the open-source all-in-one AI video studio that does script→clip→motion→SFX→music→lip-sync in one workflow; resource keyword CRIADOR; Avatar III, 52.46s).** Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=0.94, jaccard=0.90). `build_qc_ground_truth.py` ran with `--caption-windows` per QCR-315/322 and emitted 27 subtitle rows / 5 suppression windows / 9 motion titles / 0 wrap violations with ZERO warnings. Gemini returned 14/14 YES and NONE in the fixes table — no phantom storms, no re-read needed, clean "100 - 0 = 100/100".
- **Timing friction (Phase 3, root-caused + fixed pre-QC):** the raw Gemini transcription SRT started ~1.4s LATE against the actual narration (Gemini's sentence-level timestamps drift on this avatar), which would have desynced the caption-suppression windows and risked a SUBTITLE_TIMING storm. Fixed by regenerating the SRT with `subtitle-pipeline/build_srt_from_script.py` (deterministic VAD-anchoring to the real speech), so the §3b/§5b caption windows snapped onto the true sentence boundaries. The Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate (0.94 containment) then confirmed the burned/embedded audio matched the corrected SRT. This is the same VAD-vs-Gemini-drift class as QCR-232/271/282/321 — standing guidance: when the script text exists, prefer `build_srt_from_script` over raw Gemini timestamps, and always run `verify_srt_timing` right after Phase 3.
- **Standing friction (unchanged, tracked under QCR-308/317):** Phase 5 still emits no `<downloads>/<Name>_motion_list.json` for premium runs, so QC hand-built it from the REAL `.tsx` on-screen labels (§1 pill "4 FERRAMENTAS / VIRARAM 1", §4 heroes "UM COMANDO SÓ"/"NO SEU PC", §2 evidence "É real / 20K ★ / github…", suppressed §3b/§5b captions, CTA "COMENTA / CRIADOR / SALVA O VÍDEO") per QCR-NOTE-14 — never the plan's prose (which would trip the QCR-308 synthesis storm). Motion list keyed on `id`+`text`; the QCR-317 `text or title` reader picked it up.

### QCR-NOTE-17: validation — premium-classic slate-copper 100/100 iter1, clean first-pass QC
- **Validation (Phase 10 QC — grade 100/100 PASS on iter 1; premium-classic slate-copper, enemy-callout, topic "China made Codex free via Tencent's open model HY3", resource keyword HY3, Avatar IV native 1080x1920 54.9s).** Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED (containment=0.99, jaccard=0.99). `build_qc_ground_truth.py` ran with `--caption-windows` per QCR-315/322 and emitted 30 subtitle rows / 5 suppression windows / 7 motion titles / 0 wrap violations with ZERO warnings. Gemini returned 14/14 YES and NONE in the fixes table — no phantom storms, no re-read needed. No pipeline friction to root-cause: every gate passed on the first try, the grade parsed cleanly ("100 - 0 = 100/100").
- **Confirms the correct path for QCR-308/317:** this premium run DID have `<downloads>/<Name>_motion_list.json` present, carrying the REAL `.tsx` §4/hero titles (keyed on `title`, ≤3 words each: "QUASE 300 BILHÕES"/"PLUGA NO CODEX"/"MEMÓRIA GIGANTE"/"CUSTANDO ZERO") plus opening:hook and evidence: rows. Because a real motion-list existed, the builder used actual titles (never the prose-synthesis fallback that produced the QCR-308 grade-40 storm), and the QCR-317 `text or title` field fix picked them up correctly (list is keyed on `title`, not `text`). No source change needed — logged as validation that the Phase-5-emits-motion-list path works end-to-end for premium.

### QCR-319: motion-title overlap judged against the sentence-level SRT (not the burned ASS screens) → §4 hero label "RECURSOS DA VERSÃO PAGA" read 4 consecutive vs the SRT sentence and got a false "REVIEW (4+ shared → rewrite)" verdict
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — final grade **99/100 PASS on iter 2**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "OpenManus" — the free open-source clone of Manus AI; resource keyword OPENMANUS). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED containment=1.00 jaccard=1.00. (This was a friction/latent-risk fix caught in the ground-truth build, NOT a Gemini deduction — the §4-label REVIEW verdict was corrected in the ground truth before the grade read, so it never cost a point.)
- **Finding:** `build_qc_ground_truth.py` computed the §4 hero label "RECURSOS DA VERSÃO PAGA" as `max-consecutive-shared=4 → REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared — rewrite before QC, QCR-112)`. But the label's true overlap against the ACTUAL on-screen subtitle SCREENS is only **3**: the burned ASS splits that sentence into two screens — "quase todos / os recursos" (shares 1) and "da versão paga." (shares 3) — so no single on-screen subtitle shares 4 consecutive words with the label. It is a sanctioned 3-word §4 complement. The =4 came from matching against the **sentence-level SRT** cue "quase todos os recursos da versão paga." (one cue), where "recursos da versão paga" IS 4 consecutive.
- **Root cause:** the motion-title overlap helper (`max_consecutive_shared_vs_cues`, added in QCR-316) was fed the coarse **SRT cues** (`parse_srt`), which keep a whole sentence as ONE cue. The burned ASS splits the same sentence into ≤5-word screens (what the viewer actually sees). QCR-020/072 require matching against the SPECIFIC on-screen subtitle — which is the ASS screen, not the SRT sentence. Comparing against the SRT sentence manufactures a cross-screen 4-consecutive match that exists on no rendered subtitle screen (same class of bug as QCR-316, one level up: cross-*sentence-split* rather than cross-cue).
- **Permanent fix:** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py`, build the concurrent-cue list for the motion-title check from the **burned ASS rows** (`ass_cues = [{"start","end","text":" ".join(r["lines"])} for r in rows]`) instead of the SRT; `cues = ass_cues if ass_cues else srt_cues` (SRT is the fallback only when ASS rows are unavailable). Now the per-cue comparison is against exactly what is on screen.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (ASS-derived cues for motion-title overlap), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder — the §4 label now reads `(4 words, max-consecutive-shared=3) -> COMPLEMENT (SANCTIONED)` and there are ZERO `REVIEW (4+` verdicts; all other motion titles unchanged (evidence/complement/opening/caption verdicts identical). Per QCR-007 nothing was re-graded to manufacture a score — the fix hardens the ground truth so this split-sentence phantom cannot recur. Standing friction (QCR-308): Phase 5 still emits no `_motion_list.json` for premium runs, so QC hand-built it from the real `.tsx` titles per QCR-NOTE-14.

### QCR-318: premium-suppression-window HALLUCINATION storm — grader invented 8 "burned subtitle not suppressed" SUBTITLE_STYLE deductions (+1 MOTION_DUPLICATE) inside the 6 caption-suppression windows, quoting text from ANOTHER video's script → grade 55 on iter 1
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — iter 1 grade **55/100 FAIL** → iter 2 (hardened ground-truth, UNCHANGED video, clean re-read) **99/100 PASS**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "OpenManus"; resource keyword OPENMANUS). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED containment=1.00.
- **Finding:** on iter 1 Gemini wrote 8 MAJOR SUBTITLE_STYLE rows (−5 each = −40) each saying "the burned subtitle '<text>' is present … was not suppressed in a CAPTION-SUPPRESSION WINDOW" at the 6 suppression windows, plus 1 MAJOR MOTION_DUPLICATE (−5) comparing the top caption "Vários agentes…" to a "concurrent burned subtitle" in the 20.5–23.7s window. 100 − 45 = 55. ALL 9 are phantom: (a) I verified the burned `.ass` has ZERO Dialogue lines inside those windows (suppression WAS applied — one 0.12s boundary graze at 54.88s only); (b) frame extraction at t=8.3/22.0/37.5/66.0s shows the bottom band (px ~1484-1640) is BLANK — only the big top caption renders; (c) three of Gemini's quoted "burned subtitles" ("do Cloud / ao Codex", "Ou seja, você / tem um assistente", "que eu / mando o link do repositório") do NOT exist anywhere in a real run's script — they are cross-video bleed from a different run's transcript.
- **Root cause:** the pre-existing QCR-193/276/239 rule (mid-file, after the 32-row subtitle list) described the FORBIDDEN categories but was not a loud-enough anchor: the grader still hallucinated a bottom subtitle inside the suppression windows and pattern-matched it to cross-run script fragments. There was no explicit statement that (i) the listed rows are the COMPLETE and ONLY burned subtitles, (ii) the bottom band is verified-empty in those exact windows, and (iii) any quoted line not found verbatim among the rows does not exist in this video.
- **Permanent fix:** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py`, emit a prominent `!!! VERIFIED-EMPTY BOTTOM-SUBTITLE BAND (QCR-318) !!!` block at the TOP of the caption-suppression section (before the per-window list) that states the total burned-subtitle count is the complete set, lists the exact suppression windows, and marks three auto-refuting categories FORBIDDEN with zero deduction: (a) "a burned subtitle is present / not suppressed" in these windows, (b) MOTION_DUPLICATE vs a "concurrent burned subtitle" in these windows, (c) any quoted subtitle text not found verbatim among the N rows (cross-video bleed). Auto-generated for every premium run with suppression windows.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (new QCR-318 verified-empty-band anchor), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder (block emits with the correct 6 windows + 32-row count); re-graded the UNCHANGED `_music.mp4` (FILE_URI reuse, no re-render — this is a documented ground-truth-only hallucination fix, QCR-012/193 clean-re-read class) → all 15 checklist items YES except #12-adjacent cosmetic, ZERO SUBTITLE_STYLE / MOTION_DUPLICATE rows, single MINOR COMPOSITION −1 (§1 pill "slightly off-center" = intended V8 handheld drift / QCR-102 low-anchor layout) → **99/100 PASS**. Per QCR-007 the video was NOT edited to chase the grade — the video was already correct (frame- and ASS-verified); only the ground truth was hardened so the grader stops hallucinating.

### QCR-317: `build_qc_ground_truth.py` read the motion-title field as `text` only — a per-run motion list keyed on `title` silently emitted EMPTY titles for all 14 beats (latent false-green risk)
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **100/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "OmniRoute / Codex de graça" — the free open-source gateway that lets OpenAI Codex run on free AI providers forever; resource keyword CODEXGRATIS). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED containment=0.98 jaccard=0.97.
- **Finding (no QC defect — a friction/latent-risk fix, Step 5.6):** the run's `<Name>_motion_list.json` used the field name **`title`** for each on-screen title (`{"win":[…],"id":"heroAsset:coin","title":"ZERO CENTAVOS"}`), but `build_qc_ground_truth.py` read `m.get("text","")`. Result: the builder emitted all 14 motion titles as `""` (0 words, max-consecutive-shared=0). It still passed 100 because the classifiers (opening/evidence/caption-suppression) cover the sanctioned beats and the discarded §4 hero labels ("A PONTE GRÁTIS" 3w, "ZERO CENTAVOS" 2w, "TROCA SOZINHA" 2w) are all ≤3 words → COMPLEMENT anyway. But this is a real latent **false-green risk**: a future run with a 4+-word §4 hero label would silently emit it as an empty COMPLEMENT, so a genuine MOTION_DUPLICATE would slip past QC unflagged.
- **Root cause:** field-name disagreement between the emitter and the reader. The builder's own docstring (line 25) documents the schema as `"text"`, but hand-authored per-run motion lists use `"title"`. The reader `m.get("text","")` returned empty for the `title`-keyed schema with no error and no warning.
- **Permanent fix:** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` motion-title loop, changed `title = m.get("text", "")` → `title = (m.get("text") or m.get("title") or "").strip` so the reader tolerates EITHER field name. (Surgical, back-compatible — `text`-keyed lists are unaffected.)
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (motion-title field read), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder — the §4 hero labels now surface with their real text ("CODEX DE GRAÇA PRA SEMPRE" §1, "OMNIROUTE / O SEGREDO" §2 evidence, "A PONTE GRÁTIS", "ZERO CENTAVOS", "TROCA SOZINHA", "COMENTA CODEXGRATIS SALVA O VÍDEO" CTA), all classified SANCTIONED/COMPLEMENT/EXEMPT with max-consecutive-shared ≤2 — confirming no real MOTION_DUPLICATE was hidden and the 100 stands. Per QCR-007 the grade was NOT re-graded (Gemini is final authority; the video already scored 100 with the empty-title ground truth); the fix only hardens future reads. Standing friction (unchanged, tracked under QCR-308): Phase 5 still emits no `_motion_list.json` — this run's list was hand-authored from the real `.tsx` titles; if Phase 5 emitted it natively it would key on `text` and this mismatch would not arise.

### QCR-316: §1 opening-hook pill counted 4 consecutive across a subtitle-screen boundary (concatenated concurrent cues) AND fell through to the generic "REVIEW (rewrite)" verdict → 1 phantom -5 MOTION_DUPLICATE → grade 95
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **95/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "9Router / Nove Router" — the tool that kills the API rate limit for Claude Code + Cursor; resource keyword ROTEADOR). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED containment=0.98 jaccard=0.96.
- **Finding:** Gemini answered 13/14 checklist YES but marked #13 = NO (0:00-0:05) and wrote ONE MAJOR MOTION_DUPLICATE row: the §1 pill headline "O LIMITE DE API MORREU" "shares 4 consecutive words (limite de API)" with the concurrent subtitle. 100 − 5 = 95. The claim is a phantom: the §1 pill is the fixed clickbait banner (QCR-149/299) and, matched against each individual concurrent subtitle SCREEN, its true max-consecutive-shared is **3** ("LIMITE DE API" vs the subtitle screen "limite de API") — a SANCTIONED complement, not a duplicate.
- **Root cause (two compounding bugs in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`):** (1) COUNT INFLATION — `concurrent_srt_text` CONCATENATED every SRT cue overlapping the window into one string before running `max_consecutive_shared`. The §1 window spans the cues "…matou **o**" + "**limite de API**" + …, so the concatenation reads "…matou o limite de API…" and the pill's "O LIMITE DE API" matched 4 consecutive ACROSS the screen boundary — a cross-cue match that exists on no single subtitle screen. QCR-020/072 require matching the SPECIFIC time-concurrent subtitle LINE, not the concatenated window. (2) NO §1-PILL EXEMPTION IN THE VERDICT — the `opening:` id fell through to the generic `else → "REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared — rewrite before QC, QCR-112)"` verdict, which directly CONTRADICTS the QCR-299 RULE printed below it ("trust the pre-computed count, it's a sanctioned banner"). Faced with a "REVIEW → rewrite" verdict on the row itself, the grader followed the louder verdict over the footer rule.
- **Permanent fix:** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py`: (1) added `max_consecutive_shared_vs_cues` that computes the max-consecutive-shared against each concurrent cue SEPARATELY and takes the max — never the concatenation — and switched the motion-title loop to use it (kills the cross-boundary inflation for ALL titles, not just §1); (2) added an `is_opening` branch (`id` starts with `opening:`/`hook:`) BEFORE the generic gate that emits the SANCTIONED §1-banner verdict ("a FIXED clickbait banner … SANCTIONED §1 design (QCR-149/299), NOT MOTION_DUPLICATE even if it touches 4 shared words. #13 stays YES; do NOT rewrite, do NOT deduct.") — so the row-level verdict now AGREES with the QCR-299 footer rule instead of contradicting it.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (new `max_consecutive_shared_vs_cues` helper + per-cue switch + `is_opening` verdict branch), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder — the §1 pill row now reads `-> §1 OPENING-HOOK PILL HEADLINE — … SANCTIONED §1 design (QCR-149/299), NOT MOTION_DUPLICATE … do NOT deduct` (was `REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared — rewrite)`); the other 4 motion titles are unchanged (COMPLEMENT/EVIDENCE). Confirmed by hand the true per-screen max-consecutive-shared for the pill is 3, not 4. Per QCR-007 the 95 was NOT re-graded to manufacture a higher score — Gemini is the final authority and 95 (a comfortable PASS ≥75) stands for THIS read; the fix hardens the ground truth so this §1-pill phantom cannot recur on the next read. Standing friction (unchanged, tracked under QCR-308): Phase 5 still emits no `_motion_list.json` for premium runs, so QC hand-built it from the real `.tsx` titles again.

### QCR-309: mirror-phrasing SUBTITLE_TIMING storm ("appears too LATE, shift start EARLIER by a few frames") — 16 phantom -1 → grade 84 on a clean VAD-anchored reel
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **84/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "4 skills que salvam seu design" — Claude Code design skills Skill UI/Impeccable/UI UX Pro Max/Playwright; resource keyword DESIGNIA). Step-0 audio↔subtitle gate PASSED containment=0.99 (the HeyGen stereo-channel-divergence defect had been fixed by rebuilding audio from the clean RIGHT channel).
- **Finding:** Gemini answered ALL 16 checklist items YES (incl. #7 lip-sync = YES and #16 REFERENCE = YES) and FIXES for everything else = clean, but still wrote **16 SUBTITLE_TIMING MINOR rows** each claiming a caption "appears too late" and proposing to pull its START time EARLIER by 0.05–0.13s (e.g. 0:06.45→0:06.40, 0:23.58→0:23.50, 1:16.53→1:16.40). Every proposed shift is ≤0.13s (all <0.30s and <0.5s), on a VAD-anchored track whose objective ffmpeg gate already PASSED — so all 16 are phantom by QCR-245 (|New-Old|≤0.3s invalid), QCR-269 (#7=YES ⟹ zero sub-0.5s rows), and QCR-274. 100 − 16 = 84. Still a comfortable PASS, but 16 phantom points below the true ~100.
- **Root cause:** the existing QCR-274 anticipation rule in `build_qc_ground_truth.py` only named the "appears too EARLY / shift start +0.1..0.4s" phrasing (caption ahead of speech). It did NOT name the MIRROR case — "appears too LATE / shift start EARLIER / align with speech" — so the grader did not recognize its own 16 rows as the forbidden family, even though QCR-245 and QCR-269 already mathematically forbid all of them. The QCR-245 line was also phrased as a soft "is invalid" statement rather than a mechanical pre-write check the grader performs on its own Old/New columns.
- **Permanent fix:** in `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` SUBTITLE_TIMING GROUND RULES: (1) rewrote the anticipation rule to cover BOTH directions explicitly — a start within 0.5s of the spoken word in EITHER direction is by-design (ONSET_SHIFT + legibility lead-out, QCR-236/256), and BOTH phrasings 'appears too early'/'shift +' AND its mirror 'appears too late'/'shift start −0.05..−0.3s'/'align with speech' are self-refuting and FORBIDDEN; (2) upgraded the QCR-245 line to a MECHANICAL BAN (QCR-245/309): before writing ANY SUBTITLE_TIMING row the grader must compute |New-Old| from its own Old/New columns and DROP the row (no deduction) when New==Old or |New-Old|≤0.30s — killing the whole "-1×N appears too late, shift a few frames" storm in one pass.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (SUBTITLE_TIMING GROUND RULES — bidirectional anticipation rule + QCR-245/309 mechanical ban), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder — both new rule strings emit correctly (`grep` confirms `MECHANICAL BAN (QCR-245/309)` + `mirror 'appears too late'`). Per QCR-007 the 84 was NOT re-graded to manufacture a higher score — Gemini is final authority and 84 (a PASS) stands for THIS read; the fix hardens the ground truth so the mirror-phrasing family cannot recur on the next read. Additional pipeline friction captured (not edited, standing item): Phase 5 still emits no `_motion_list.json` for premium runs, so QC hand-built it from the real `.tsx` titles again (QCR-308 open item). NEW hardening idea worth implementing at the source: a post-HeyGen stereo-channel-divergence check (measure L−R difference energy; if the two channels carry different partial narrations, transcribe each and pick/rebuild the complete mono) so the stereo-overlap defect that hit this run's avatar is auto-detected instead of hand-caught.

### QCR-NOTE-14: validation — proactively hand-building `_motion_list.json` from the real `.tsx` titles yields QC 100/100 iter1 (avoids the QCR-308 synth-prose trap)
- **When:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **100/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold; topic "Kimi Work" Moonshot AI free desktop agent + Kimi WebBridge; resource keyword KIMI300).
- **Finding (clean run, no QC defect):** Phase 5 again did NOT emit `<Name>_motion_list.json` (same gap QCR-308 flags). Instead of letting the builder fall back to synthesizing motion rows from the plan's descriptive `motion_segments.content` prose (which is exactly what poisoned a real run → 40), I READ the real on-screen titles off the composition `.tsx` (`AGENTE COMPLETO`, `300 SUBAGENTES`, `UM TIME INTEIRO`, the §1 pill `A CHINA MATOU O CODEX`, the evidence kicker/stamps, and the 7 suppressed §3b/§5b captions) and wrote a proper `<scratch>/<Name>_motion_list.json`. `build_qc_ground_truth.py` then produced 13 clean COMPLEMENT/SANCTIONED/EVIDENCE/CAPTION-SUPPRESSION verdicts (0 `REVIEW (4+ shared)` rows, 0 wrap violations), and Gemini returned 14/14 YES + FIXES=NONE + 100/100.
- **Root cause of the friction (already tracked):** Phase 5 emits no discrete motion-list artifact for premium runs, so QC either hand-builds it (this run) or falls back to prose synthesis (QCR-305/308). The permanent prevention is the Phase-5 change already prescribed in QCR-308 ("Phase 5 should emit `<Name>_motion_list.json` for premium/hand-drawn runs too") — still unimplemented.
- **Permanent takeaway (no new source edit needed this run — the machinery is already correct):** the QC operator MUST prefer hand-reading the real `.tsx` titles into `_motion_list.json` over the prose-synthesis fallback whenever a premium run lacks the artifact. This run is the positive control proving the hand-built path lands 100 first read. Recording as a validation note per Step 5.6 (a clean run still gets its friction captured); the actionable source fix stays the open Phase-5 emit-motion-list item under QCR-308.
- **Affected files:** none edited (validation). Open item: `motion-pipeline` Phase 5 to emit `_motion_list.json` (QCR-308).

### QCR-308: SYNTHESIZED motion rows (QCR-305 fallback) drove 12 phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE -5 → grade 40 on a clean reel
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **40/100 FAIL on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, topic "Claude SEO" repo)
- **Finding:** the run had NO discrete `<Name>_motion_list.json`, so the QCR-305 fallback SYNTHESIZED motion rows from the visual plan's `motion_segments`, using each beat's `idea` string as the "title". Those `idea` strings are DESCRIPTIVE PROSE that restate the spoken line by construction (e.g. `"18 agentes especializados trabalhando ao mesmo tempo -> exercito de robos-agentes"`), so `max_consecutive_shared(title, concurrent_srt)` returned 4–12 on nearly every row → each was tagged `REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared — rewrite before QC)`. The old NOTE told the grader to "TRUST the per-row max-consecutive-shared count", so Gemini dutifully wrote 12 MOTION_DUPLICATE rows (-5 each) → 100 − 60 = **40**. The ACTUAL on-screen titles in the `.tsx` were only `ZERO MENSALIDADE` (2 words) and `BACKLINKS` (1 word) plus 2 suppressed captions — every real title is ≤2 words, mechanically MOTION_DUPLICATE-impossible (QCR-020). All 12 deductions were phantom.
- **Root cause:** for synthesized rows the "title" is a concept label, NOT the rendered on-screen text, so comparing it word-for-word against the subtitle is meaningless — yet the builder ran the same `n<=3 / shared<=3` gate a real motion-list gets and emitted a "REVIEW → rewrite" verdict when the prose shared 4+ words. The NOTE compounded it by instructing the grader to TRUST that poisoned count.
- **Permanent fix:** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, when `motion_synth` is True: (1) every synthesized beat now gets verdict `SYNTHESIZED CONCEPT LABEL (not on-screen text) — shared-word count is vs DESCRIPTIVE PROSE and is MEANINGLESS; MOTION_DUPLICATE-EXEMPT (QCR-308), #13 stays YES` (branch added BEFORE the n<=3/shared gate, after the caption/evidence exemptions); (2) the NOTE was rewritten to state the shared count is against descriptive prose, MUST BE IGNORED, and it is FORBIDDEN to write ANY MOTION_DUPLICATE row for a synthesized beat (#13 stays YES). Real-motion-list runs are unaffected (they still get the REVIEW gate). Root prevention: Phase 5 should emit `<Name>_motion_list.json` for premium/hand-drawn runs too so real titles are graded instead of prose labels.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (QCR-308 verdict branch + rewritten QCR-305/308 NOTE), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder — `REVIEW (4+ shared)` rows dropped from 13 → **0**, replaced by 13 `SYNTHESIZED CONCEPT LABEL … MOTION_DUPLICATE-EXEMPT` rows (2 caption rows stay caption-exempt). The grade of 40 was NOT re-graded to manufacture a pass (QCR-007): Gemini is the final authority and its 40 stands for THIS read; the fix hardens the ground truth so the phantom family cannot recur on the next read. Confirmed against the `.tsx`: real titles are `ZERO MENSALIDADE`/`BACKLINKS` (≤2 words) — none is a genuine duplicate.

### QCR-307: whole-line `Style: Yellow` row still drew a phantom -1 SUBTITLE_STYLE fixes-table row despite checklist #11 = YES
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **99/100 PASS on iter 1**; premium-classic charcoal-gold, leaked Claude Fable 5 system prompt topic)
- **Finding:** Gemini answered checklist #11 = YES (highlights consistent) and correctly honored the `[YELLOW: ALL words (whole-line yellow style)]` ground truth for 0:38.39 "ou o GLM," (the `.ass` line is `Dialogue: ...,Yellow,...ou o GLM,` — whole-line `Style: Yellow`, every word yellow by design). Yet it STILL wrote a MINOR SUBTITLE_STYLE fixes-table row ("visually only GLM is highlighted") and deducted -1 → 99. The row directly contradicts its own #11 = YES answer — a residual warm-glow/contrast misread of the QCR-262/301 family.
- **Root cause:** QCR-301 hardened the *label* (emit "ALL words") and told the grader to keep #11 = YES, but it did NOT forbid writing a contradictory SUBTITLE_STYLE row in the FIXES TABLE. Unlike QCR-066 (checklist #13 ⟺ MOTION_DUPLICATE) and QCR-269 (checklist #7 ⟹ zero sub-0.5s SUBTITLE_TIMING), there was no explicit "#11 = YES ⟹ zero SUBTITLE_STYLE rows" mutual-exclusion clause, so the grader could answer YES and still deduct.
- **Permanent fix:** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, added a new RULE line to the yellow section: "if checklist #11 = YES, the FIXES TABLE MUST contain ZERO SUBTITLE_STYLE rows — mutually exclusive; a SUBTITLE_STYLE row against a [YELLOW: ALL words ...] entry is self-refuting and FORBIDDEN; drop it, do NOT deduct the -1." Mirrors the QCR-066/269 mutual-exclusion pattern so the contradiction is closed on the first read next time.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (QCR-307 RULE line in the yellow section), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** grade of 99 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007/010: it already PASSED ≥75; the row was a phantom against ground truth — the ASS confirms `ou o GLM,` is a whole-line `Style: Yellow` entry, every word yellow, never a real video defect). Re-running the builder now emits the QCR-307 mutual-exclusion clause in the ground truth for all future runs.

### QCR-306: `build_qc_ground_truth.py` CRASHED (`'str' object has no attribute 'get'`) on premium-grammar plans whose `motion_segments` is a descriptive STRING, not a list
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — builder crashed on the first invocation; premium-classic section-grammar plan)
- **Finding:** running `build_qc_ground_truth.py --ass … --srt … --segments … --plan <Name>_visual_plan.json --name …` (no `--motion-list`, the common premium case) threw `AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'` at line 208 and produced NO ground-truth file. The premium visual plan sets `"motion_segments": "full-premium-grammar (no stock b-roll; render IS the deliverable)"` — a DESCRIPTIVE STRING, not a list of beat dicts. The QCR-305 fallback did `for ms in _plan.get("motion_segments")`, which iterates the string's CHARACTERS, so each `ms` is a single-char str and `ms.get(...)` crashes the whole builder. Without the fix the operator would have to hand-build the entire ground truth (or grade with none).
- **Root cause:** the QCR-305 plan→motion synthesis fallback assumed `motion_segments` is always a list of dicts. Premium-grammar plans instead carry the authoritative per-beat windows in a separate `sections[]` array (each `{"id":"evidence:fable","win":[s,e],"type":...}`) and use `motion_segments` only as a human-readable note. Iterating a str silently degrades to per-character iteration.
- **Permanent fix:** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, guard the source: only iterate `motion_segments` when `isinstance(_ms, list)`; else use `scenes` if it is a list; else fall back to the plan's `sections[]` list. Skip any non-dict element (`if not isinstance(ms, dict): continue`). Carry the section's real `id` (e.g. `evidence:fable`, `caption:cta`) into the synthesized motion row so the downstream `is_caption`/`is_evidence` classifiers (QCR-193/304) fire correctly instead of a generic `plan:` id.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (motion-synthesis source guard + `sections[]` fallback + `id` passthrough), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder on a real run → exit 0, 17 motion titles synthesized from `sections[]`, all 6 caption/avatarcap rows classified `CAPTION IN SUPPRESSION WINDOW` and the `evidence:fable` row classified `EVIDENCE SCREENSHOT CARD` (QCR-304) — exactly the exemptions needed. Gemini then graded the video 99/100 with the full motion + suppression ground truth in place.

### QCR-305: `build_qc_ground_truth.py` emits NO motion list when only `--plan` (a visual_plan with `motion_segments`) is supplied — all-motion/hand-drawn runs lose the QCR-072 pairings
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **100/100 PASS on iter 1**; hand-drawn-annotation fallback style, all-motion, no `_motion_list.json` produced by Phase 5)
- **Finding:** the builder was run as `build_qc_ground_truth.py --ass … --srt … --plan <Name>_visual_plan.json --name …` (no `--motion-list`, because this hand-drawn run never wrote a separate `<scratch>/<Name>_motion_list.json`). The output had ZERO motion-title section — the entire MOTION SCENE TITLES block with the QCR-072 concurrent-subtitle pairings + pre-computed max-consecutive-shared counts (the machinery that pre-empts the MOTION_DUPLICATE phantom family) was simply absent. I had to hand-author the full MOTION_LIST from the `.tsx` scene components and append it. The grade was 100 anyway (I hand-built it correctly), but an operator who trusted the builder's output verbatim would have shipped a QC call with no motion ground truth at all — exactly the condition that produces MOTION_DUPLICATE storms.
- **Root cause:** the builder only populates `motion` from `--motion-list` (line ~192: `if args.motion_list and os.path.exists(...)`). It reads `--plan` ONLY for b-roll/reference windows and caption-suppression segments — it never falls back to the plan's `motion_segments[]` (which every visual plan has: `{"win":[s,e], "type":..., "idea":...}`) to synthesize a motion list. So for any run where Phase 5 does not emit a discrete `_motion_list.json` (hand-drawn/all-motion runs are the common case), the motion ground truth silently vanishes.
- **Permanent fix (recommended for the source):** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, when `--motion-list` is absent/missing but `--plan` has a non-empty `motion_segments`, synthesize the motion rows from the plan (win + a distilled title derived from `idea`/`type`) and PAIR each with its time-concurrent SRT text via the existing `concurrent_srt_text` + `max_consecutive_shared` helpers, emitting the same `-> COMPLEMENT / REVIEW` verdicts. Until that lands, the operator MUST hand-build the MOTION_LIST from the final `.tsx` (as done here) whenever the builder returns no motion section — never grade with an empty motion ground truth. Also: Phase 5 should write `<scratch>/<Name>_motion_list.json` for hand-drawn runs too, so `--motion-list` is always available.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (add plan→motion synthesis fallback), Phase 5 motion skill (emit `_motion_list.json` for hand-drawn), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** hand-authored MOTION_LIST (11 beats, each ≤3-word title or sanctioned evidence/CTA banner, QCR-072 pairings) appended to `<scratch>/<Name>_qc_ground_truth.txt`; Gemini graded 100/100, checklist #13 and #14 both YES, zero MOTION_DUPLICATE rows — confirming the hand-built motion ground truth killed the phantom family exactly as the builder's own output would have.

### QCR-304: §2 EVIDENCE screenshot card (kicker/stamp/domain overlaid on a REAL repo capture) mis-flagged MOTION_DUPLICATE — grade 90 instead of 100
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **90/100 PASS**; Gemini wrote 2 MAJOR MOTION_DUPLICATE rows, -5 each)
- **Finding:** Gemini deducted -10 on the two §2 EVIDENCE cards — the real `github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail` repo screenshots overlaid with a kicker + stamp + domain. At 0:14 it flagged `O NOME É PONYTAIL / OPEN SOURCE / github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail` as sharing 4 words with the subtitle `O nome é / Ponytail`, and at 1:09 flagged `DE GRAÇA · OPEN SOURCE / 70K ★ / github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail` as sharing 4 words with `É de graça, / open source`. These overlays are on a REAL captured screenshot — the sanctioned §2 evidence design — and their kicker/stamp/domain legitimately echo the narration; this is NOT motion-title double-subtitling.
- **Root cause:** `build_qc_ground_truth.py`'s motion-row classifier had only two exempt buckets — caption-suppression windows (`caption:`/`avatarcap:`) and the ≤3-shared complement floor. An `evidence:`-id row with 4+ shared words fell through to the generic `REVIEW (4+ consecutive shared)` verdict, which reads to the grader like a real MOTION_DUPLICATE candidate instead of a sanctioned screenshot overlay. The QC prompt had no §2-evidence exemption clause either.
- **Permanent fix:** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, added an `is_evidence = mid.startswith("evidence:")` branch that emits `EVIDENCE SCREENSHOT CARD — kicker/stamp/domain overlaid on a REAL repo/site capture is the SANCTIONED §2 design; sharing words with narration is CORRECT, NOT MOTION_DUPLICATE (QCR-304)`. Future runs pre-classify §2 evidence overlays as exempt, so the grader stops treating them as duplicate candidates. (The QC prompt used this run also carried an inline §2-evidence exemption clause; the builder fix makes it automatic for every run.)
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (motion-row classifier), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder on `<Name>` → both evidence rows now read `-> EVIDENCE SCREENSHOT CARD ... NOT MOTION_DUPLICATE (QCR-304)`. Grade of 90 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007/010: it already PASSED ≥75; both rows were sanctioned-design flags, never a real video defect).

### QCR-301: whole-line `Style: Yellow` subtitle row read as a PARTIAL highlight → phantom -1 SUBTITLE_STYLE "only X highlighted"
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **99/100 PASS**; Gemini wrote 1 MINOR SUBTITLE_STYLE row, -1)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged 0:49 "sem chave de API" as an inconsistent highlight ("only API is highlighted"), deducting -1 → 99. The `.ass` line `Dialogue: ...,Yellow,,...,sem chave\Nde API` uses the whole-line `Yellow` style — ALL 4 words ("sem","chave","de","API") are yellow BY DESIGN. The ground truth correctly listed `[YELLOW: API, chave, de, sem]`, but a bare comma list of every word reads to the grader like a partial highlight, so a low-contrast/motion-glow frame that showed one bright word got mis-flagged. This is a QCR-016-family frame-read hallucination that contradicted the supplied ground truth.
- **Root cause:** the `[YELLOW: w1, w2, ...]` emission does not distinguish a PARTIAL inline-`{\rYellow}` highlight from a WHOLE-LINE `Style: Yellow` row (every word yellow). When all N words are listed, there is no visual signal that consistency is inherently satisfied, so a single-frame glow misread wins.
- **Permanent fix:** in `build_qc_ground_truth.py`, when every plain word of a row is in its yellow set, emit `[YELLOW: ALL words (whole-line yellow style — every word IS yellow; consistency inherently satisfied)]` instead of the comma list, and extended the QCR-016 RULE clause: a row tagged "ALL words" is NEVER 'inconsistent'/'only X highlighted' — a frame reading fewer yellow words is a motion-glow misread, drop it, keep checklist #11 = YES.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (yellow-label emission + QCR-016 RULE clause), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder on `<Name>.ass` → 0:49 now reads `[YELLOW: ALL words (whole-line yellow style ...)]`. Grade of 99 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007/010: it already PASSED ≥75; the row was a phantom against ground truth, never a real video defect). Also note: the ground-truth builder WARNED that 3 caption-suppression windows (§3b W[2] 8.2-11.9, §5b W[8] 28.3-34.5, §3b W[10] 38.75-41.8) had a burned subtitle bleeding up to 0.42s into the window edge (>0.15s leak); Gemini did NOT flag any of them (the QCR-193/276 ban clauses held), and the objective Step-0 gate PASSED (containment 0.96) — so no fix was forced, but the edge overlap is logged here as a known low-risk artifact of snapping caption windows to sentence boundaries that are a few frames off the burned-subtitle end.

### QCR-300: `build_qc_ground_truth.py` glued a `\N` line break to the next word inside a yellow span → bogus `[YELLOW: NCode]` token
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **90/100 PASS**; Gemini wrote 2 MINOR SUBTITLE_STYLE rows flagging the ground truth itself, no deduction taken)
- **Finding:** The QC ground truth reported `[YELLOW: Claude, NCode]` (0:33) and `[YELLOW: Claude, NCode, Router]` (0:58) instead of `[YELLOW: Claude, Code, ...]`. The burned `.ass` splits the brand across a line break INSIDE the yellow span: `{\rYellow}Claude\NCode{\rWhite}`. Gemini correctly recognized "NCode" as a ground-truth typo (not a video defect) and did not deduct, but the bogus token pollutes the highlight list and could mask a genuine highlight inconsistency on a future run.
- **Root cause:** the word-extraction regex `re.findall(r"[\wÀ-ɏ.%$]+", tok)` runs on the raw token `Claude\NCode`, where `\N` is a literal backslash-N line-break marker. `\w` does not match `\`, so the run breaks at the backslash and the `N` of `\N` greedily glues onto the following word → `Claude` + `NCode`. Any brand/keyword whose `\N` line break falls inside a `{\rYellow}...{\rWhite}` span hits this (common when srt_to_ass wraps a 2-word highlight across two visual lines).
- **Permanent fix:** strip `\N` to a space (`tok.replace("\\N", " ")`) BEFORE the word-extraction `re.findall` in the yellow-word collector, so the line break is treated as whitespace and each half is extracted as its own clean token.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (yellow-word `re.findall` in the ASS override-block loop), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder on `<Name>.ass` → the two rows now read `[YELLOW: Claude, Code]` and `[YELLOW: Claude, Code, Router]`; `grep NCode` on the rebuilt ground truth returns nothing. Grade of 90 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007/010: it already PASSED ≥75; the rows were phantom ground-truth typos, never a video defect).

### QCR-299: §1 opening-hook pill headline mis-flagged MOTION_DUPLICATE — grader re-transcribed the sentence and counted a word absent from the subtitle
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade **95/100 PASS**, single -5 MOTION_DUPLICATE phantom on the §1 pill headline)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged the §1 opening-hook headline `CORTE 70% DA CONTA DA IA` as sharing "4 consecutive words (da conta da IA)" with the concurrent narration `corta a conta da sua inteligência artificial em setenta por cento`, and deducted -5 (grade 100→95). The ground truth had ALREADY pre-computed this exact title as `max-consecutive-shared=2 -> COMPLEMENT (SANCTIONED)`, yet the grader overrode it.
- **Root cause:** the grader ignored the pre-computed number and re-derived its own count against a RE-TRANSCRIBED full sentence, and in doing so counted the word **"IA"** as shared — but "IA" appears in the headline while the subtitle says "inteligência artificial" and NEVER the token "IA". Mechanically the true max-consecutive-shared vs every concurrent burned subtitle row is **2** ("da conta"/"conta da"), verified with a consecutive-run matcher. This is the QCR-020/015 word-floor family applied to the fixed §1 pill banner, whose clickbait phrasing inevitably re-uses the topic's function words ("da conta da IA" / "the AI bill").
- **Permanent fix:** `build_qc_ground_truth.py` now emits an explicit §1-opening-hook RULE line right after the motion RULE: the first motion title (opening:hook pill) is a fixed clickbait banner; TRUST the pre-computed max-consecutive-shared; do NOT re-transcribe the sentence and re-count; and NEVER count a word that is not present verbatim in a listed subtitle row (e.g. "IA" when the subtitle says "inteligência artificial").
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (new RULE line in the MOTION TITLES block), `qc-pipeline/QCR_INDEX.md`
- **Verification:** grade of 95 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007/010: it already PASSED ≥75; the phantom is documented as an AGENT NOTE, not overridden). The mechanical matcher confirms max-consecutive-shared=2 against all four concurrent subtitle rows; the next §1-pill video feeds the grader the explicit anti-re-transcribe rule so this phantom cannot recur.

### QCR-297: `build_qc_ground_truth.py` mis-read `{\rYellow}` style-reset tags as `[YELLOW: none]` → phantom SUBTITLE_STYLE storm
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 10 QC — grade 90/100 PASS, but Gemini wrote 34 SUBTITLE_STYLE rows and deducted -10 for "words highlighted yellow but ground truth says [YELLOW: none]")
- **Finding:** The QC ground truth reported `[YELLOW: none]` for ALL 53 subtitle rows, even though the burned `.ass` genuinely rendered 9 yellow words (`golpe`, `vírus`, `antivírus`, `captcha`×3, `antivírus`, `golpe`). Gemini correctly saw yellow words in the video and, judging against the WRONG ground truth, flagged every one as an inconsistency — plus ~25 more warm-glow phantom reads (QCR-262) that the correct ground truth would have anchored against. The false ground truth INDUCED the storm.
- **Root cause:** `build_qc_ground_truth.py` detected yellow only via `YELLOW_HINTS = ("00D4FF",)` — inline `\c&H00D4FF&` colour overrides. But this pipeline's `srt_to_ass.py` emits **named-style-reset tags `{\rYellow}` / `{\rWhite}`** (reset-to-named-style), which contain no colour hex, so `cur_yellow` was never set True and `{\rWhite}` matched neither the hint nor the `\C&H` reset — every row parsed as `[YELLOW: none]`.
- **Permanent fix:** the override-block walker now also parses `\r<StyleName>` / bare `\r` reset tags and sets `cur_yellow` by checking `<StyleName>` against the `yellow_styles` set already collected from the `[V4+ Styles]` header (any style named/containing "Yellow" or whose primary colour is the yellow hint). After the fix, the same a real run `.ass` correctly extracts exactly the 9 yellow words.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/build_qc_ground_truth.py` (yellow-state tracking in the ASS override-block loop)
- **Verification:** re-ran the builder on `<Name>.ass` → `[YELLOW: golpe]`, `[YELLOW: vírus]`, `[YELLOW: antivírus]`, `[YELLOW: captcha]`×3, `[YELLOW: antivírus]`, `[YELLOW: golpe]` — matches `grep 'rYellow' <Name>.ass` (9 lines). Grade of 90 was NOT re-graded (QCR-007): it already PASSED; the fix hardens the machinery so the next `{\rYellow}` video feeds Gemini a truthful highlight list and the storm cannot recur.

### QCR-295: Stale `public/assets/<run>/` dirs starve the Remotion render → font delayRender timeout
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 5 — first render hung on a heavy frame ~frame 297, the tall 2571px NemotronOllama zoom, and hit the per-frame font delayRender timeout even at `--concurrency 3 --timeout 300000`)
- **Finding:** A render font-timeout that looks exactly like QCR-275/250 (`Loading font Anton … not cleared after Nms`) but is NOT cured by raising `--timeout`. Root cause was `public/` bloat: **814MB across 76 stale per-run `public/assets/<run>/` dirs** (each run's keyed hero pool is kept as a re-render pool, and they accumulate unbounded). The bundler copies the WHOLE `public/` to a temp dir on every render, so the oversized tree starves the render tabs and one heavy frame blows the font-load timeout.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-285 pre-render prune deletes only top-level `public/*.mp4` avatars and EXPLICITLY skips `assets/` — so per-run asset dirs grow without bound. `--timeout`/`--concurrency` do not help once the copied tree is huge.
- **Permanent fix:** the pre-render prune ALSO relocates stale per-run asset dirs older than 7 days, keeping the current run + `icons` + the <7d re-render pool: `find public/assets -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d ! -name '<video-name>' ! -name 'icons' -mtime +7 -exec mv {} <scratch>/stale_assets_hold/ \;` then `du -sh public` (keep well under ~300MB). Old dirs are re-derivable via `generate-image-assets`; posted runs never re-render. On a real run this dropped public/ 991MB→196MB and the SAME-flags retry rendered all 1643 frames clean.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` (step 4 prune)
- **Verification:** `du -sh public` prints < ~300MB before `npx remotion render`; the render completes all frames without a delayRender font timeout.

### QCR-001: Subtitle Positioning Must Clear Reels/TikTok Bottom UI
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 40/100)
- **Note:** (superseded by QCR-008 for motion pipeline values)
- **Finding:** Subtitles placed too close to the bottom edge of the screen, obscured by Instagram/TikTok UI elements (description, comments, likes, share buttons, audio attribution)
- **Root cause:** MarginV value not accounting for platform UI overlay zone (~320-420px from bottom)
- **Permanent fix:** In **motion pipeline context**, MarginV = 280 (per QCR-008 — bottom edge at pixel 1640, just above danger zone). In **standalone context**, MarginV >= 440.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `FULL_PIPELINE.md`
- **Verification:** After burning subtitles, extract a frame and confirm subtitle bottom edge is at ~pixel 1640 (motion) or ~pixel 1480 (standalone)

### QCR-002: Keep Subtitle Lines Short for Fast Scanning
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 40/100)
- **Finding:** Some subtitle lines too long, hard to read alongside concurrent motion graphics
- **Root cause:** SRT segments not split aggressively enough into short phrases
- **Permanent fix:** Maximum 5-6 words per line. If a segment has >12 words, split into 2 separate dialogue entries (not just 2 lines in one entry). Fast-paced content needs faster subtitle turnover (1.5-2.5s per segment, not 3s+).
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** No single dialogue entry should contain more than 12 words total

### QCR-003: Consistent Keyword Highlighting Strategy
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 40/100)
- **Finding:** Inconsistent yellow highlighting — some key words highlighted, others with similar importance left white, creating visual inconsistency
- **Root cause:** No systematic rule for what gets highlighted
- **Permanent fix:** Apply Yellow highlights to ALL instances of these categories, not selectively:
 - ALL numbers, percentages, monetary values, years
 - ALL brand names, product names, tech terms
 - ALL power verbs at emotional peaks
 - The final emphasis word/phrase of the video (always Yellow + CAPS)
 - If a category of word is highlighted once, ALL instances of that category must be highlighted
 - **Contrast pairs (a real run, QC 97):** in "não X, mas Y" / "não compram X, compram Y" constructions, highlight BOTH sides of the comparison — highlighting only one side reads as inconsistent. Gemini deducted for "produtos" (white) vs "redução de risco" (yellow) in the same sentence.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** Review the full ASS file and confirm no keyword category is inconsistently highlighted

### QCR-004: Motion Graphics Text Alignment Must Be Pixel-Perfect
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 40/100)
- **Finding:** Secondary text lines slightly misaligned from primary text in motion graphics
- **Root cause:** Nested flex containers without explicit `textAlign: "center"` or `alignItems: "center"`
- **Permanent fix:** Every text container in Remotion compositions MUST have explicit `textAlign: "center"` and parent containers MUST have `alignItems: "center"`. Never rely on default alignment.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/MOTION_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** Frame check at 3+ timestamps to verify all text is visually centered

### QCR-005: Numerical Animation Pacing
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 40/100)
- **Finding:** Counter/numerical animations in motion graphics tick too fast, making intermediate values unreadable
- **Root cause:** Interpolation range too short for the number of digits being animated
- **Permanent fix:** Numerical counter animations must hold each significant value for at least 0.3s (7-8 frames at 25fps). Use `Easing.out(Easing.cubic)` for decelerating counters so the final value lingers longest. Descriptive text accompanying the number must appear within 3 frames of the number settling.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/MOTION_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** Watch the render at 1x speed — each number must be readable

```
- **First found:** [Date] ([Video filename], Grade [X]/100)
- **Finding:** [What Gemini reported]
- **Root cause:** [Why it happened — which parameter, config, or missing check]
- **Permanent fix:** [Exact change to make — specific values, specific files]
- **Affected files:** [List every file that needs updating]
- **Verification:** [How to confirm the fix works]
```
