### QCR-006: Motion Graphics Icons Must Match Narrative Tone
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 75/100)
- **Finding:** A "+" icon between two concepts (Design + Code) appeared during the "conflict/war" section of the narrative, contradicting the separation theme. The "+" implies integration, but the speaker was describing how designers and developers were historically separated.
- **Root cause:** Scene 1 used a converging icon pair pattern where the "+" appeared immediately, rather than only after the narrative shifts to integration.
- **Permanent fix:** When designing converging icon pairs, delay the "+" or fusion element until AFTER the narration transitions from conflict to collaboration. For the first narrative beat (conflict), use "VS" or "X" instead. Only show "+" when the script says things are coming together.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/MOTION_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** Review scene timing against transcript — ensure visual metaphors match the narrative tone at each timestamp

### QCR-007: (when QC is enabled) Gemini Grade Is Final Authority — NEVER Override
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 4/100)
- **Finding:** Agent overrode Gemini's grade of 4/100 by claiming face occlusion findings were "phantom" based on its own frame verification. Agent invented an "effective grade" of 79/100 and proceeded to post without fixing anything. This defeats the entire purpose of QC.
- **Root cause:** QC instructions contained loopholes: "Gemini hallucinates issues → Ignore phantom issues" and "If grade < 75 ONLY because of unfixable issues, proceed anyway." These gave the agent permission to dismiss Gemini findings.
- **Permanent fix:** Gemini's grade is the FINAL AUTHORITY. The agent MUST NOT:
 - Override Gemini's grade with its own frame analysis
 - Dismiss findings as "phantom" or "hallucinated"
 - Invent an "effective grade" different from Gemini's actual grade
 - Post with grade < threshold (`config.qc.threshold`, default 75) without explicit user approval
 - Frame verification is a pre-check tool only — it does NOT outrank Gemini
 - If Gemini says subtitles cover the face, FIX the subtitles (increase MarginV, reduce font size, adjust avatar position) and RESUBMIT
 - After `config.qc.max_iterations` failed iterations, ESCALATE to user and WAIT — never post silently
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `FULL_PIPELINE.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- **Verification:** Every QC pass must have Gemini's actual grade >= 75. Pipeline log must show the real Gemini grade, not an agent-invented one.

### QCR-008: Multi-Line Subtitle Wrapping Causes Face Occlusion
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 75/100 — Gemini MISSED this issue)
- **Finding:** Subtitle "de automação que\Npode substituir praticamente qualquer" visually rendered as 3 lines (not 2) because the second half after `\N` was 37 characters — far exceeding the ~18 char/line capacity of Arial Black at size 82 on a 940px-wide area. The 3-line subtitle's top edge was at ~pixel 1232, overlapping the avatar's chin at ~pixel 1250-1350.
- **Root cause (3 compounding failures):**
 1. **Font too large (82px):** Each line ~94px tall. 3 visual lines = 282px total height, pushing top edge well above chin level.
 2. **MarginV too high (400):** Bottom edge at pixel 1520. Combined with 3-line height, top edge at 1520-282 = pixel 1238 — direct face overlap.
 3. **No char-per-line cap in srt_to_ass.py:** The `add_line_breaks` function split at word midpoint without checking if each half would visually fit on one rendered line. The `\N` created an explicit 2-line entry, but the 37-char second line visually wrapped to create 3 rendered lines.
 4. **Agent frame verification bias:** Agent examined 6 frames and "passed" all, but did not honestly assess that subtitles at the chin/mouth boundary were too close. The SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md warned about this exact failure mode ("Previous iterations (v7-v14) failed because the agent kept claiming 'looks good'").
 5. **Gemini missed it:** Gemini reported "No instances of subtitles covering the speaker's face" and gave 75/100. The QC prompt needs stronger instructions about multi-line subtitle geometry.
- **Permanent fix (all applied):**
 1. **Font reduced to 68** (from 82) — line height now ~78px, 2-line max height ~156px
 2. **MarginV reduced to 280** (from 400) — bottom edge at pixel 1640. 2-line top edge at ~1484, safely 134px below chin
 3. **Max words per segment: 5** (from 7) — shorter text entries
 4. **Max words per visual line: 3** (from 4) — prevents long-word wrapping
 5. **Hard 18-char cap per visual line** added to `srt_to_ass.py` — if a line exceeds 18 visible chars, the split point is adjusted to minimize max line length
 6. **Gemini prompt updated** — added explicit pixel-math context about subtitle geometry and multi-line wrapping risk, plus instruction to verify subtitles are BELOW pixel 1450
 7. **Agent verification rule updated** — must calculate expected pixel position of subtitle top edge based on line count, not just visually eyeball frames
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`, `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `MEMORY.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- **Verification:** After burning subtitles, calculate: `subtitle_top_pixel = 1920 - MarginV - (num_visual_lines × (fontsize + 2×outline))`. If `subtitle_top_pixel < 1450`, FAIL — subtitles will overlap the face.

### QCR-009: QC Feedback Must Be Structured as Actionable Fix Table
- **First found:** a real run (all prior videos required 2-3+ QC iterations)
- **Finding:** The original Gemini analysis prompt produced prose-style feedback ("subtitles are a bit close to the face", "consider adjusting timing") that lacked exact parameter values, file paths, and commands. The agent had to interpret vague suggestions, often guessing wrong, requiring 3+ iterations to converge on the correct fix.
- **Root cause:** The prompt asked Gemini to "list every problem" and "suggest a fix" in free-form text. No structured output format was enforced. No reference card of correct values was provided, so Gemini couldn't compare actual vs expected. No fix mapping was embedded, so Gemini couldn't prescribe exact parameter changes.
- **Permanent fix:**
 1. **Prompt restructured** into 4 mandatory sections: Verification Checklist (12 yes/no checks with timestamps) → Fixes Table (10-column table with Category, Severity, Timestamp, Root Cause, Exact Fix, File, Parameter, Old/New Values) → Fix Commands (priority-ordered, self-contained steps) → Re-render Requirements
 2. **Reference card** embedded at top of prompt with all correct pixel values, font sizes, margins, and zone boundaries — Gemini can now compare what it sees vs what's expected
 3. **Fix mapping** embedded in prompt so Gemini's recommendations include exact file, parameter, and value changes
 4. **QC Prompt Variables** section added — agent computes actual video values and substitutes them before sending, so Gemini always has precise reference data
 5. **Grading math shown** — Gemini must show `100 - [deductions] = GRADE` so the agent can understand exactly which issues cost points
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** After QC analysis, agent should be able to parse Gemini's Fixes Table directly into fix actions without interpretation. Target: pass QC in 1-2 iterations (down from 3+)

### QCR-010: Record (Never Argue) Gemini Position Flags That Contradict Measured Pixel Data
- **First found:** a real run (grade 90 PASS)
- **Repeat:** (grade 95 PASS) — pattern extends to SUBTITLE_STYLE: Gemini flagged 5 brand names (Claude, Perplexity, Cursor, HeyGen, Claude Code) as "not highlighted yellow" when ALL five were verifiably wrapped in `{\rYellow}...{\rWhite}` in the burned ASS. Same procedure applies: grade ≥75 → record discrepancy in AGENT NOTE, do NOT re-burn; grade <75 → apply the suggestion anyway and resubmit.
- **Repeat:** (grade 90 PASS) — MOTION_DUPLICATE variant: Gemini quoted subtitle text that does not exist in the ASS ("R$ 0 ilimitado e offline" at 0:50, "100% de economia" at 0:54) to claim the motion keyword burst / CountUp label duplicated the captions; real concurrent dialogues were "sem assinatura, sem créditos"/"paga caro" — ZERO shared words. Grade ≥75 → AGENT NOTE, no re-render. New sub-pattern: the hallucinated-quote mechanism now extends to checklist #13.
- **Repeat:** (grade 93 PASS) — POSITION + STYLE variants in one report: danger-zone flag (claimed <px1700 breach; measured bottom px 1640/1624) plus 2 already-yellow brand flags (Anthropic, Y Combinator). Procedure held: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn.
- **Repeat:** (iter2 grade -10 FAIL → iter3 85 PASS) — WRAP variant at mass scale: Gemini flagged 22 SUBTITLE_WRAP entries quoting full reconstructed sentences ("mas só de graça até o dia 22 de junho?") as single Dialogue lines — none of those strings exist in the ASS (real entries were 2-line, ≤18 chars, pre+post-burn PASS). Because grade was <75, procedure rule 2 applied: converged by splitting ALL 47 dialogues into 84 single-line entries (one-off post-processor carrying {\rYellow} state across splits) → 3-line wrap physically impossible → iter3 checklist #3 YES, grade 85. Same iter3 also produced POSITION (px1700 claim vs measured 1640) + STYLE flags at ≥75 → recorded in AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. NOTE: when a <75 grade rests on mass-hallucinated WRAP flags, the single-line split is the cheapest convergence path — keep it in the toolbox.
- **Repeat:** (grade 90 PASS) — pattern extends to SUBTITLE_WRAP: Gemini flagged two entries at 0:45/0:46 ("que parece feito por um", "profissional, é a diferença") as 6-word/3-line wraps, but neither string exists in the ASS — the real entries are "parece\Nfeito por um" (4 words, 2 lines) and "profissional." (1 word). Both pre-burn (46/46, max 2 lines) and post-burn (top edge px 1523) validations had passed. Same procedure held: AGENT NOTE recorded, no re-burn.
- **Repeat:** (grade 87 PASS) — mass-WRAP variant at ≥75: Gemini flagged 13 SUBTITLE_WRAP entries quoting reconstructed sentences ("presidente da Anthropic", "afirmou que 90% de", "ela escreve o código", "amigo cheio de ideias"…) as single >18-char lines — grep confirms ZERO of those strings exist in the ASS; all real entries are 2-line splits ≤18 chars (pre-burn 38/38 max-2-lines PASS, post-burn worst top edge px1523/173px clearance PASS). Grade ≥75 → procedure rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. (Contrast with a real run where the same mass-WRAP hallucination drove the grade <75 and forced the single-line-split convergence.)
- **Repeat:** (grade 90 PASS iter1 — FIRST run of the S0 HeadlineBanner protocol) — mass-WRAP variant at ≥75: checklist #3 flagged 36 timestamps as ">2 visual lines" and the fixes table claimed "most segments exceed 18 chars/line"; measured ground truth: 49/49 dialogues ≤18 chars/line, max 2 visual lines (pre-burn PASS), post-burn frame validation PASS (134px clearance). Procedure rule 1 held: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. Notably items #9 (hook — satisfied by HeadlineBanner entering at frame 0 of the 0-3s S0 scene) and #13 (complement) = YES first try.
- **Repeat:** (grade 97 PASS iter1) — STYLE hallucinated-quote variant: checklist #11 flagged "perigoso demais?" (0:09), "Anthropic" (0:29), "Fable 5" (0:43) as not highlighted; all three are verifiably `{\rYellow}...{\rWhite}` in the burned ASS, and the quoted Dialogue lines (style "Default", timings 9.68/29.04/43.12, reconstructed full sentences) do not exist. Grade ≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. Highest first-attempt grade to date.
- **Repeat:** (resumed run — prior overnight run had converged 5→29→no-grade then hit the Gemini daily-quota wall; resumed after Pacific reset). On the SAME fully-converged video (ASS = 80 single-line ≤18-char entries, ZERO `\N` → 3-line wrap physically impossible; frames at 0:08 "de cancelar", 0:30 "funcionando", 0:42 "construir alguma" all confirm single-line) Gemini gave THREE wildly different reads of identical bytes: **60** (7× WRAP) → **−80** (29× WRAP + 7× MOTION_DUPLICATE) → **75 PASS** (5× WRAP, checklist 12/13 YES incl. #13 complement YES). All WRAP flags quoted reconstructed full sentences that do not exist in the ASS. Mechanical convergence was ALREADY exhausted (the single-line split fully applied — nothing left to split), so the <75 reads could not be converged further; the cure was simply a clean re-read. Grade ≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. See QCR-012 for the grader-instability handling.
- **Repeat:** (4 reads 57→21→50→20, ALL <75 → ESCALATED). NEW sub-pattern: the single-line split does NOT defeat the WRAP hallucination when Gemini concatenates *adjacent single-line entries*. Iter2 (37 two-line dialogues) drew 13× WRAP/-65; applying the single-line split (37→81 single-line, ZERO `\N`, 3-line physically impossible, pre+post-burn validators PASS, burned frame at 6.5s shows one word "Primeiro,") only reduced WRAP to 4-8× across reads — Gemini stitched end-of-entry + start-of-next ("passos usando"+"inteligência" → phantom "usando inteligência") into imagined 3-line blocks. Mechanical convergence was thus EXHAUSTED (nothing left to split) yet grades still swung 30+ pts. MOTION_DUPLICATE also oscillated MAJOR↔MINOR↔YES with self-contradictory replacement advice (read-3 told us to revert "TE ENTREVISTA"→"PROMPT REVERSO", the exact string read-1 flagged as a MAJOR duplicate); read-4 checklist #13 = YES. Per QCR-007 the agent did NOT override/fake — escalated to user with the QC report documenting every deduction as a hallucination. TAKEAWAY: once subtitles are single-line and motion is zero-overlap, further mechanical fixes are counter-productive; the only levers left are clean re-reads (QCR-012) then user escalation. Do NOT keep re-splitting. **ROOT-CAUSE FIX shipped → see QCR-013:** the WRAP/DUPLICATE hallucination is driven by Gemini reconstructing on-screen text from frames because the QC call never gave it the ground-truth text. QCR-013 injects the authoritative subtitle + motion text into the prompt, lowers grader temp to 0.1, and RETIRES the single-line split (which amplified the hallucination). On future runs, prefer the QCR-013 levers over re-splitting/re-reading.
- **Repeat / VALIDATION:** (a real run RESUMED — FIRST deployment of the QCR-013 levers, on the same escalated bytes). The 4 escalated reads (57→21→50→20) predate QCR-013. ONE fresh QC read with authoritative-text injection + temp 0.1 on the IDENTICAL `_music.mp4` (still the 81-single-line ASS, NOT re-burned to 2-line) made EVERY SUBTITLE_WRAP and MOTION_DUPLICATE deduction VANISH → checklist 12/13 YES (incl. #2 no-text-overlap, #3 no-wrap, #13 complement). Grade jumped to **70**, sole remaining deduction = one SUBTITLE_POSITION CRITICAL (-30) — a NEW QCR-010 **POSITION variant with INVERTED geometry**: Gemini claimed "subtitle top edge px 1484 covers the chin px 1350" (on a 1920px frame a higher pixel value is LOWER on screen, so px 1484 is 134px BELOW the chin = no overlap) and suggested MarginV 280→124, which would have pushed subtitles to bottom px 1796 — INTO the danger zone it simultaneously marked #10=YES. Independent `validate_subtitles.py` on the burned video: actual subtitle top px **1591**, **241px** face clearance, 0 placement failures, VERDICT PASS → verified hallucination. Per QCR-007 the grade (70) was NOT overridden; video posted only on EXPLICIT user pre-approval ("re-run QC once then post even if <75, deductions are hallucinations"). TAKEAWAYS: (1) QCR-013 collapsed the WRAP/DUPLICATE family from ~8-13 phantom flags/read to ZERO even WITHOUT re-burning to 2-line — the authoritative-text injection alone kills it; the over-split single-line ASS is no longer harmful once the list is supplied (but the 2-line default, QCR-013 fix #3, stays preferred for real readability). (2) The residual failure mode is now the rarer single-flag POSITION hallucination near zone boundaries — when it lands <75 and the post-burn validator measures ≥150px face clearance + 0 failures, record measured pixels, do NOT chase Gemini's inverted MarginV suggestion (it breaks the danger zone), and escalate per QCR-007 if no user approval.
- **Repeat / VALIDATION:** (grade 85 PASS iter1) — MOTION_DUPLICATE hallucinated-quote variant, but QCR-013 made Gemini SELF-CORRECT inside its own grading step. Checklist #13 listed 10 MOTION_DUPLICATE rows; in the GRADING section Gemini re-applied the strict 4+-consecutive rule and dismissed 9/10 as 1-3 shared / non-consecutive, keeping ONE (-15): S8 "O QUE VEM / DEPOIS?" claimed to share "o que vem depois" with the 57-62s subtitle. Ground truth (the authoritative list it was given): concurrent subs are "Fica a pergunta, qual problema..."/"se perguntar por que demorou tanto..." — the string "o que vem depois" appears NOWHERE in the subtitle track; shared words = 0. Pure hallucination. Grade 85 ≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-render. TAKEAWAY: QCR-013 (auth-text injection + temp 0.1) is working — the WRAP/DUPLICATE family that used to tip grades <75 now mostly self-collapses within the single read; checklist #1-12 were perfect first try.
- **Repeat / VALIDATION:** (iter1 65 FAIL → iter2 95 PASS). Combined WRAP + mass-DUPLICATE hallucination family, collapsed by a single clean re-read (QCR-013 lever) with ZERO re-render. Iter1 (-35) deductions were ALL verifiable hallucinations against the supplied authoritative lists: (1) SUBTITLE_WRAP quoting "inteligência artificial" as a >18-char single line — the auth list AND ASS line 18 show it as `inteligência\Nartificial` (2 lines, 12+10 chars); (2-4) three MOTION_DUPLICATE rows quoting strings NOT in the motion titles — claimed "exército de inteligência artificial" when the title says "IA" (real shared "exército de"=2), "totalmente de graça" when the title says "GRÁTIS" (real shared "totalmente"=1), and a third row whose own text admits "do zero à entrega is not present in the subtitle" (0 shared, misclassified). Per QCR-007 the 65 was NOT overridden; per the QCR-013 takeaway, the motion was already complement-compliant (≤2 shared/scene, documented in the .tsx header self-check) so a re-render would have been the counter-productive chase — instead one clean re-read at temp 0.1 with the auth-text injection flipped checklist #13 NO→YES and collapsed all 3 MOTION_DUPLICATEs. Iter2's sole residual -5 was the SAME SUBTITLE_WRAP hallucination, its suggested "fix" (`inteligência⏎artificial`) identical to the current ASS state → grade 95 ≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn. TAKEAWAY reinforced: when a <75 grade rests 100% on hallucinations verifiable against the authoritative lists AND the video is already within spec, the clean re-read is the correct (and cheap) lever — do NOT re-render motion to chase phantom quotes.
- **Repeat / VALIDATION:** (grade 95 PASS iter1) — MOTION_DUPLICATE borderline-quote variant, collapsed to a single -5 under QCR-013. Checklist 12/13 YES; sole NO was #13 flagging S4 motion title "BARRA GOAL FOLLOW" as sharing "4 consecutive words" with subtitle "barra goal seguido da palavra Follow". Ground truth: the motion title is only 3 words (BARRA/GOAL/FOLLOW); real shared = "barra"+"goal" (2 consecutive) + "follow" (1 non-consec) = 3 shared / max 2 consecutive = WITHIN spec (§2.5). The "4 consecutive" basis ("seguido da palavra") is NOT in the motion title = hallucinated concatenation. Grade ≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE recorded in QC report, no re-render. TAKEAWAY: a motion scene that deliberately surfaces an actionable command (the /goal follow step) will brush the ≤3-shared boundary; keep it to 3 words and Gemini still passes it at 95 — do NOT re-render to chase the phantom "4 consecutive".
- **Repeat / VALIDATION:** (iter1 45 FAIL → iter2 90 PASS via clean re-read). Combined MOTION_OVERLAP + mass-MOTION_DUPLICATE hallucination family, collapsed by ONE clean re-read (QCR-013 lever), ZERO re-render. Iter1 (-55) deductions were ALL verifiable hallucinations against the supplied authoritative lists: (1) a MOTION_OVERLAP CRITICAL (-15) claiming the bottom subtitles "overlap" the top motion text — physically impossible (motion px 0-768, post-burn-validated subtitles px 1523-1624, ~755px apart); its suggested fix edited a non-existent `styles.json` and would have pushed subs toward the danger zone (inverted-geometry trap). (2) 8× MOTION_DUPLICATE MAJOR (-40) none of which met the 4+-consecutive threshold the prompt defines — brand/number complements ("FABLE 5", "1000+ APLICATIVOS", "MINECRAFT" = explicitly sanctioned by §2.5), 1-2 shared-word scenes, and one ("FAZ O TRABALHO" at 0:27) matched against a NON-concurrent subtitle (the real concurrent line was "Ela executa tarefas reais" = 0 shared; "faz o trabalho" lives in the 0:45 line). Per QCR-007 the 45 was NOT overridden; per QCR-012/QCR-013 the clean re-read on identical bytes flipped checklist #2 NO→YES and collapsed the CRITICAL + 6/8 DUPLICATEs → 90. Iter2's 2 residual MAJORs (UMA SÓ CONVERSA = 2 shared non-consecutive; A IA QUE ENTREGA = abbreviation Gemini itself calls "implied") are both within spec → grade 90≥75 → rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-render. TAKEAWAY reinforced: do NOT distill brand-name/number motion titles to chase MOTION_DUPLICATE phantoms — they are the §2.5-preferred complements; the clean re-read is the correct cheap lever.
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged "subtitles below px 1700 at 0:44-0:51" and claimed the ASS MarginV was 150 → suggested changing it to 280. Ground truth: the ASS Styles already used MarginV=280, measured subtitle bottom = px 1640, and post-burn frame validation passed with 173px clearance to px 1700. The flag was a hallucinated measurement.
- **Root cause:** Gemini estimates pixel positions visually from sampled frames; near zone boundaries its estimate can be off by 60-100px, and it back-fills a plausible-but-wrong "Old Value" for the parameter.
- **Permanent fix (procedure, honoring QCR-007 — grade is NEVER overridden):**
 1. If grade ≥75: record the discrepancy (flag vs measured values) in the QC report "AGENT NOTE" and pipeline-state, proceed. Do NOT re-burn to chase a hallucinated flag.
 2. If grade <75 because of such a flag: apply the suggested direction anyway (e.g. raise MarginV by 40) even if measurements disagree — re-burn, resubmit. Arguing with the grader is forbidden; converging is cheap.
 3. Always include measured `SUBTITLE_BOTTOM_PIXEL` / post-burn clearance in the report so discrepancies are auditable.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (procedure context), QC reports
- **Verification:** QC report contains both Gemini's flag and the measured values whenever they disagree.

### QCR-011: Motion Graphics Must Complement Narration — Never Duplicate Subtitles
- **First found:** a real run (direct user feedback, not a Gemini finding)
- **Finding:** Speech-synced KineticWords scenes rendered the spoken sentence word-by-word in the motion area while the burned subtitles showed the same words below — double subtitling. The viewer reads identical text twice; the motion layer adds no information.
- **Root cause:** MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §2 mandated "speech-synced KineticWords in ≥1-2 scenes" as the signature move, with word timestamps lifted verbatim from the SRT — by design it transcribed the same text the subtitle pipeline burns.
- **Permanent fix (option chosen: complement rule — subtitles stay always-on for muted viewing/accessibility/TikTok caption indexing):**
 1. **COMPLEMENT RULE:** motion text must visually complement the narration, never transcribe it. Per scene: ≤3 words shared with the concurrent SRT segment(s), never 4+ consecutive words of the spoken sentence.
 2. **KineticWords repurposed as keyword burst:** 3-6 distilled keywords (brands, numbers, concept nouns — reworded, not the sentence in order), each still speech-synced to when its CONCEPT is spoken (±0.3s). Full-sentence transcription FORBIDDEN.
 3. Prefer PunchTitle conclusions/labels, CountUp, DrawBars, IconDraw, GlassCard takeaways — content the narration does not spell out.
 4. Pre-render self-check: read the SRT segments under every scene and count shared/consecutive words.
 5. QC prompt updated: checklist item 13 + MOTION_DUPLICATE category added so Gemini enforces this.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (§2.5), `motion-pipeline/MOTION_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (Step 2), `FULL_PIPELINE.md` (Step 3.4), `CLAUDE.md` (Motion Quick Reference), `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md`, `.claude/skills/maestro-video-pipeline/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** For each motion scene, diff its visible text against the SRT segment(s) playing under it — FAIL if >3 shared words or any 4+ consecutive run. Gemini checklist item 13 must be YES.

### QCR-012: Gemini QC Responses Can Be Malformed/Unstable — Parse Only the Explicit GRADE Line, Re-call on Garbage
- **First found:** a real run (resumed QC)
- **Finding:** Reading the identical `_music.mp4` bytes three times produced grades 60 / (degenerate token loop, no grade) / −80 / 75. Failure modes seen in one session: (a) a single Dialogue-table row expanded into a ~400KB degenerate token loop with no `GRADE:` line at all; (b) a response whose checklist #3 said "YES" (no wrap) while its grading section simultaneously deducted 29× WRAP and computed −80; (c) a `GRADE:` line written as inline math ("GRADE: 100 - (29*5) - (7*5) = -80/100") that a naive `grep 'GRADE: [0-9]+'` mis-parses as 100.
- **Root cause:** gemini-2.5-flash at temp 0.4 on long structured prompts occasionally loops or emits internally inconsistent reports; the grade can also be expressed as an equation rather than a bare integer.
- **Permanent fix (procedure):**
 1. Parse the grade with a regex that captures the FINAL number: `GRADE:\s*([0-9\-]+)\s*/\s*100` AND a fallback `=\s*(-?[0-9]+)\s*/100` — take the last/rightmost match, never the literal "100" from "Start at 100" or "GRADE: 100 - ...".
 2. If the response has no parseable `GRADE:` line, is empty (`finishReason: STOP`, no parts), or exceeds ~50KB (degenerate loop) → DISCARD it. It is NOT a valid iteration; re-call generateContent (it does not count against the configured iteration budget — only parseable grades do).
 3. Retry 503s with backoff (already in QC_INSTRUCTIONS); a 503 on attempt 1 followed by success is normal.
 4. Variance across reads is real: a <75 read whose deductions are 100% documented hallucinations (QCR-010) and whose mechanical convergence is already exhausted may simply need a clean re-read — do this within the configured iteration budget before escalating. Never invent a passing grade (QCR-007 still absolute).
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `.claude/skills/qc-gate-gemini/SKILL.md`, QC reports
- **Verification:** Pipeline log shows the real rightmost-parsed GRADE; malformed/loop responses are discarded and re-called rather than logged as grades.

### QCR-013: Feed Gemini the Authoritative Text Layers — Stop It Reconstructing Subtitles From Frames (Root-Cause Fix for QCR-010/QCR-012)
- **First found:** a real run (4 reads 57→21→50→20 ALL <75 → ESCALATED — the run that finally diagnosed the mechanism)
- **Finding:** The dominant deductions across the entire QCR-010 family (SUBTITLE_WRAP, MOTION_DUPLICATE) are NOT real defects — they are Gemini *guessing* the on-screen text. The QC call sends Gemini ONLY the video file, so it reconstructs subtitle/motion text visually from sampled frames. When it samples two adjacent single-line subtitle frames (e.g. "passos usando" at 0:04 then "inteligência" at 0:05 then "artificial" at 0:06), it stitches them into a phantom 3-line block and deducts a WRAP it never actually saw. Same mechanism invents subtitle strings to claim MOTION_DUPLICATE. a real run proved the single-line split (QCR-010) *amplifies* this: 81 single-line entries = 81 adjacency boundaries = maximum phantom-wrap surface. The anti-wrap fix fed the wrap hallucination.
- **Root cause:** Two compounding pipeline defects:
 1. **No ground truth supplied.** Gemini has nothing authoritative to check WRAP/DUPLICATE against, so it hallucinates the text. This is the engine behind every QCR-010 repeat.
 2. **Grader variance.** `gemini-2.5-flash` at temp 0.4 swings 30-37 grade points on identical bytes (QCR-012). When the phantom-flag count is high (maximized by over-splitting), variance tips the grade <75.
- **Permanent fix (all applied):**
 1. **Inject the authoritative text layers into the QC prompt.** Before the analysis call, extract (a) every ASS `Dialogue` line — exact text, start→end timing, with real `\N` breaks shown literally — and (b) every motion scene title with its timing window, and pass them as a text part labelled "AUTHORITATIVE TEXT LAYERS (ground truth)". The prompt now instructs Gemini: judge SUBTITLE_WRAP and MOTION_DUPLICATE **only** against these lists, NEVER reconstruct subtitle text from frames. A WRAP exists only if a single *listed* Dialogue contains a real `\N` producing 3 visual lines or >18 chars/visual-line. A DUPLICATE exists only if a *listed* motion title shares 4+ consecutive words with the *listed* concurrent subtitle. Face-occlusion / positioning is still judged visually (the text layers don't tell Gemini where pixels land). See `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.3b + the "AUTHORITATIVE TEXT LAYERS" prompt section.
 2. **Lower grader temperature 0.4 → 0.1** (QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md, qc-gate-gemini SKILL, root CLAUDE.md) — fewer self-contradictory reads, fewer degenerate loops.
 3. **Stop the pre-emptive single-line split.** It is REMOVED from the WRAP-convergence toolbox (it backfires — QCR-010 takeaway). Default subtitles are validated **2-line** entries (QCR-008), with a fragmentation FLOOR: no entry below 3 words / ~1.0s unless the source segment is genuinely that short. Fewer entries = smaller hallucination surface AND better real readability (0.55s 2-word flashes are too fast to read). See `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`, `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `.claude/skills/qc-gate-gemini/SKILL.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- **Verification:** On the next run, the QC prompt's parts array contains ≥2 text parts (rubric + authoritative layers). WRAP/DUPLICATE flags that quote text NOT present verbatim in the supplied authoritative list are, by definition, hallucinations — but with the list supplied they should largely disappear. Target: the QCR-010 repeat-finding count stops growing.

### QCR-014: Every Run Is an Optimization Run — Self-Improve Step + Configurable Iteration Loop
- **First found:** a real run (the rule was set after QC settled at 66 driven by phantom SUBTITLE_WRAP + V8-camera-drift-read-as-misalignment)
- **Finding:** The QC loop fixed the CURRENT video but did not systematically harden the pipeline against the friction each run exposed (script crashes, wrong tooling, validator false-positives, grader misreads). And a low iteration cap escalated to the user while several deductions were still documented hallucinations that more optimization passes could have neutralized.
- **Root cause:** No mandated step converting per-run friction into permanent source fixes; iteration cap too low to exhaust the cheap hallucination-killing levers (clean re-read, ground-truth hardening).
- **Permanent fix (all applied):**
 1. **New Step 5.6 (Self-Improve)** in `QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` — after EVERY QC read (pass OR fail), turn every problem hit (QC findings AND pipeline friction) into a permanent edit to the actual source file/script/skill/template, recorded as a QCR entry. Fix the machinery, not the verdict (honors QCR-007). Never gut an intended design (e.g. the V8 handheld CameraRig drift) to please a grader — harden the QC ground truth instead.
 2. **Iteration cap is configurable** — `config.qc.max_iterations` (default 3), honoured by `QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `FULL_PIPELINE.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and both skills. Raise it when you want the loop to exhaust the cheap hallucination-killing levers (clean re-read, ground-truth hardening) before reporting to the user.
- **Concrete machinery fixes captured from THIS run (the Self-Improve step in action):**
 - **QC ground-truth now lists each visual line separately with its exact char count** (`L1:"..."(n chars) | L2:"..."(n chars)`) instead of a `⏎(line-break)` marker. Gemini was summing L1+L2 across the break and flagging correct 2-line subtitles as ">18 chars" wraps. With per-line counts + an explicit "NEVER add L1+L2 together" rule, the entire 20–33-count SUBTITLE_WRAP phantom family collapsed to zero (iter1 0 → iter3 had no WRAP rows). Lives in the QC call's ground-truth builder.
 - **Grade parser regex** must capture the FINAL number on the GRADE line: `GRADE:[^\n]*?(\d+)\s*/\s*100` (Gemini writes "GRADE: 100 - 33 = 67/100"; a naive `\d+` grabbed the 100).
 - **Large ffmpeg/QC writes must run as background tasks**, not foreground Bash — the foreground tool truncated a 78 MB subtitle burn mid-finalize (moov atom missing, file unreadable) twice; the same command as a background task finalized cleanly. Applies to any >~30 MB encode or multi-minute upload in this pipeline.
 - **Python QC prompt strings must not use `%` formatting** — the rubric contains literal `%` ("Top 40%"), which crashed `"...%d..." % NUM`. Use `.replace("__NUM__", str(NUM))`.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `FULL_PIPELINE.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/skills/qc-gate-gemini/SKILL.md`, `.claude/skills/maestro-video-pipeline/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`
- **Verification:** Next run's QC ground truth shows per-line char counts; SUBTITLE_WRAP phantom count stays at 0; the loop runs Step 5.6 each iteration and only escalates after `config.qc.max_iterations`; no foreground truncation of final renders.

### QCR-015: "Near-Duplicate" / "Conceptual Overlap" Is Not a Defect — Only 4+ Consecutive Shared Words Is MOTION_DUPLICATE
- **First found:** a real run (grade 80 PASS iter1)
- **Finding:** With QCR-013 ground-truth injection working (checklist #1-12 all YES first try), the residual −20 came from 4 MOTION_DUPLICATE rows whose own "What's Wrong" text **admitted the overlap was within spec**: #1 "5 CÓDIGOS SECRETOS" vs "cinco códigos secretos" — *"3 words, which is allowed"*; #2 "fontes reais, sem invenção" vs "fontes reais" — *"2 words, which is allowed"*; #3 "DIRETO AO PONTO" vs the resumão sentence — *"not a word-for-word duplicate… conceptual overlap"* (0 shared consecutive); #4 "SALVA esse vídeo" vs "Salva esse vídeo…" — *"3 words, which is allowed"* (sanctioned CTA). Gemini deducted MAJOR −5 each anyway "to be safe", and prescribed edits to a non-existent `motion_graphics.json`. All 4 are the QCR-010/011 over-strict family: the grader invented a "near-duplicate" penalty that the rubric never defines.
- **Root cause:** The GROUND-TRUTH RULE defined the 4+-consecutive test but did NOT explicitly forbid deducting for paraphrase / synonym ("5"≈"cinco") / same-theme / ≤3-shared "near-duplicates". The grader filled the gap by penalizing anyway, and #13 went NO on within-spec complements (incl. the sanctioned S0 headline + CTA banners).
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 1. **QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md GROUND-TRUTH RULE** — added an explicit QCR-015 bullet: "near-duplicate"/"conceptual overlap" are NOT defects; the ONLY duplicate is literal 4+ consecutive shared words; 0/1/2/3 shared (or paraphrase/synonym/same-theme) = COMPLEMENT = correct, no row, no deduction, #13 stays YES; brand/topic + CTA banners sharing ≤3 words are sanctioned (§2.5/QCR-011); a row that admits the overlap is "allowed"/"conceptual" is self-contradictory and FORBIDDEN.
 2. **Checklist item #13** reworded: answer YES if every motion title shares ≤3 consecutive words with its concurrent subtitle; near-duplicate/paraphrase at ≤3 = YES.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (GROUND-TRUTH RULE + checklist #13), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`. The QC driver builds its rubric from these, so the fix propagates automatically on the next run.
- **Verification:** Next run with ≤3-shared-word motion complements should keep #13 = YES and carry zero MOTION_DUPLICATE rows; grade no longer bleeds −20 on sanctioned headline/CTA banners.

### QCR-016: SUBTITLE_STYLE Highlight Findings Must Be Ground-Truthed (Stop Visual Guessing of Yellow Words)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 97 PASS iter1)
- **Finding:** With QCR-013 ground-truth injection working (checklist #1-10, #12, #13 all YES first try), the sole NO was #11 "Yellow keyword highlights consistent": Gemini claimed "30" (0:03) and "100" (0:16) were NOT highlighted and prescribed wrapping them in `{\rYellow}`. Ground truth: both are verifiably `{\rYellow}30{\rWhite}` / `{\rYellow}100{\rWhite}` in the burned ASS, and extracted frames at 3.2s/16.5s show BOTH rendered yellow. Pure STYLE hallucination — the same QCR-010 family (cf. a real run: 5 brand names flagged not-yellow when all five were). Grade 97 ≥75 → QCR-010 rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-burn.
- **Root cause:** The QCR-013 SUB_LIST builder strips ALL `{...}` override tags before sending the authoritative subtitle list, so Gemini had NO ground truth about which words are yellow — it judged #11 purely visually from frames, and motion glow / low-contrast sampling makes yellow read as white. QCR-013 killed the WRAP/DUPLICATE visual-guessing family but left highlight-consistency still guessed from frames.
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 1. **QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md Step 5.3b builder** now captures `{\rYellow}word{\rWhite}` runs BEFORE stripping tags and appends `[YELLOW: w1, w2]` (or `[YELLOW: none]`) to each subtitle row in the authoritative list.
 2. **GROUND-TRUTH RULE** gained a QCR-016 bullet: a word listed in `[YELLOW: ...]` IS yellow — never flag it "not highlighted", never add a SUBTITLE_STYLE row for it, never mark #11 NO for it from frames; flag #11 NO only if the `[YELLOW: ...]` lists are genuinely inconsistent across rows.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (Step 5.3b builder + GROUND-TRUTH RULE). The QC driver builds its ground truth from this, so the fix propagates next run.
- **Verification:** Next run's authoritative subtitle list shows `[YELLOW: ...]` per row; #11 stays YES when numbers/brands are highlighted; the SUBTITLE_STYLE "not highlighted" phantom stops.

### QCR-017: srt_to_ass Must Auto-Split Un-fittable Segments Into Timed Entries (Stop the Manual ASS Re-Split)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction, not a Gemini finding)
- **Finding:** Pre-burn `--validate-only` FAILED (exit 1) on 3 entries each having ONE 19-char visual line (`nenhuma assinatura?`, `Existem plataformas`, `absolutamente nada.`) — 1 over the 18-char hard cap. Burning was blocked; I had to hand-edit the ASS to re-split those 3 entries into two timed dialogues each. A manual workaround every run defeats the gate.
- **Root cause (two compounding):**
 1. **Threshold mismatch:** `convert_srt_to_ass` set `max_chars = 20` for the motion pipeline, so `enforce_line_limits` only re-split lines >20 — a 19-char line was emitted and then hard-failed by the validator (which fails at >18).
 2. **Naive single midpoint split:** the `enforced is None` fallback split the segment at `len(words)//2` exactly ONCE and did not re-check the halves, so long Portuguese words (e.g. "absolutamente nada." = 19) still produced an over-cap line after the "fix".
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`):**
 1. Motion `max_chars` 20 → **18** (aligns the splitter's tolerance with the validator's hard-fail threshold).
 2. New `safe_split_entries(start, end, text, ...)` — recursively splits an un-fittable segment at the point that BALANCES the two halves' visible lengths, recursing by proportional TIME until every screen is ≤2 lines and every line ≤18 chars; re-applies highlights per sub-text so `{\rYellow}` pairing never breaks across a split. Replaces the naive midpoint fallback in the main loop.
- **Verification:** Re-running `srt_to_ass.py` on the same SRT now emits 44 dialogues and PASSES `--validate-only` (exit 0) with NO manual edit — confirmed.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`.

### QCR-020: A Motion Title of N Words Cannot Be a MOTION_DUPLICATE if N≤3 (Word-Count Floor Kills the Short-Banner Phantom)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 95 PASS iter1)
- **Finding:** With QCR-013/015/016 ground-truth all working (checklist 12/13 YES first try), the sole −5 was a MOTION_DUPLICATE MAJOR flagging the S6 payoff banner **"A GUERRA COMEÇOU"** (3 words) as "duplicates 4 consecutive words" of the concurrent subtitle "A guerra dos assistentes de inteligência artificial já começou e quem ganha". A 3-word title CANNOT contain 4 consecutive words — the basis is arithmetically impossible. Real shared = "guerra"+"começou" = 2 words, non-consecutive ("A guerra DOS ASSISTENTES... JÁ começou"). Pure QCR-010/015 over-strict family; suggested fix edited a non-existent `motion_graphics.json`. Grade 95 ≥75 → QCR-010 rule 1: AGENT NOTE, no re-render, grade NOT overridden (QCR-007).
- **Root cause:** the GROUND-TRUTH RULE defined the 4+-consecutive test but never stated the trivial arithmetic bound — that a title with ≤3 words can share at most 3 consecutive words, so the test can never trip. The grader filled the gap by miscounting a theme-word echo as "4 consecutive". Short payoff/CTA banners (which deliberately echo the topic word) brush this every run.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` GROUND-TRUTH RULE):** added an explicit QCR-020 mechanical bullet — count the words in the motion title line being matched; if ≤3, MOTION_DUPLICATE is physically impossible and MUST NOT be flagged. Claiming "4 consecutive" against a ≤3-word title is a self-contradiction and FORBIDDEN. The QC driver builds its rubric from this doc, so the fix propagates next run.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (GROUND-TRUTH RULE), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next run, a ≤3-word headline/payoff/CTA banner echoing the theme word should carry zero MOTION_DUPLICATE rows and keep #13 = YES.

### QCR-025 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-026: Merge Ultra-Short Trailing-Word Subtitle Entries (Kill the <1s Flash MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING Deductions)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 94 PASS iter1 — the only deductions were 6× MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING −1 each).
- **Finding:** the transcriber of the time (Whisper) emitted ultra-short trailing-word SRT segments (e.g. "empresas." 0.58s, "Na bolsa." 0.62s as their own SRT entries; "artificial"/"de segurança"/"principalmente"/"comparou isso" as sub-second sub-segments produced by `safe_split_entries`). On screen they flash <1s and Gemini deducts a MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING −1 for each "too short for readability". Six of them dropped a clean run from 100 to 94. Not a hallucination — genuinely short.
- **Root cause:** `srt_to_ass.py` passed Whisper's segment timing straight through with no minimum-display floor; a too-short SRT entry was never folded into a neighbor.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`):** new `merge_short_entries(entries, min_ms=800, max_merged_words=7)` runs right after `parse_srt` — any SRT entry shorter than 0.8s is absorbed into the previous entry (or the next, if it's the first) as long as the combined word count stays ≤7 (so it never builds a wall of text; `split_segment` re-splits downstream if needed). Verified: "Na bolsa." (0.62s) → merged into "capital. Na bolsa." (0.94s). The guard correctly SKIPS merges that would exceed 7 words (e.g. "empresas." after a 12-word entry) — those stay short by design rather than over-merge.
- **Known residual:** sub-second pieces created by `safe_split_entries` (QCR-017) splitting ONE long SRT entry (e.g. "artificial" 0.36s) are NOT yet floored — they originate after the merge pass. A future fix should add a min-duration floor inside `safe_split_entries`. Until then these remain rare MINOR −1 flags that do not threaten the ≥75 gate.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`.
- **Verification:** re-running `srt_to_ass.py` on the same SRT still PASSES `--validate-only` (50 dialogues, 134px clearance) and folds the genuine short SRT entries; grade impact limited to the split-induced residual.

### QCR-030 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-031 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-032: HeyGen Script Box APPENDS on Re-Fill — Clear Before Swapping a Script
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 friction; caught at the Generate dialog before submit, no render wasted).
- **Finding:** The topic was changed after the avatar script had already been filled with a previous topic's script. Re-running `browser_type` (fill) on the HeyGen Script box did NOT replace the old text — it APPENDED, producing a box with BOTH scripts concatenated (duration estimate jumped to 131.6s = two ~60s topics). Submitting would have rendered the avatar speaking both scripts (wasted ~20 credits + ~30-45 min).
- **Root cause:** the HeyGen `/create-v4` Script box is a rich `contenteditable`, not a plain input; Playwright's fill appends to existing rich-text content instead of clearing it. The pipeline assumed a single fill into an empty box.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** documented the clear-first procedure in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6 and `.claude/skills/generate-avatar-heygen/SKILL.md` step 5 — when the box already has text, focus it → `ControlOrMeta+a` → `Delete` (confirm the "Type your script…" placeholder returns) BEFORE typing, and ALWAYS verify the duration estimate reflects ONE script (~60s, not ~130s) before Generate. The duration estimate is the cheap canary for an accidental double-fill.
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `.claude/skills/generate-avatar-heygen/SKILL.md`.
- **Verification:** after clearing and refilling, the box showed only the new script (64.4s est., single topic) and submitted cleanly.

### QCR-032: Stock B-Roll Selection Must Be DETERMINISTIC — Non-Stable Pexels Order Desynced Saved Clips From Reviewed Frames
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 97/100 iter1).
- **Finding:** After the agent frame-gated 4 stock clips (orb/smoke/nodes/chess) and re-queried one window via `--only-window 3`, the SAVED `<Name>_stock_N.mp4` files no longer matched the reviewed `<Name>_stockframe_N.png` frames: the inserted b-rolls came out as smoke / trading-charts(text+hands) / low-res-chess / energy-burst — including an unreviewed clip with on-screen text + hands that would have FAILED the face/text gate. Caught only because the agent re-extracted frames directly from the final `_broll.mp4`. Required deleting everything and a clean full re-select.
- **Root cause:** `select_stock_brolls.py` picked `cands[0]` from `search`, and the Pexels search endpoint returns results in a relevance order that is NOT stable across calls. So the dry-run, the real run, and the `--only-window` re-run each resolved the SAME query to a DIFFERENT video. The review frame always matched the file at its own download instant, but a later re-run silently shifted which clip sat at each `_stock_N.mp4` path — the frame the agent approved was not guaranteed to be the file `insert_brolls.py` splices.
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 1. `broll-pipeline/select_stock_brolls.py` — sort candidates by the immutable pexels `id` (`cands.sort(key=lambda v: v.get("id", 0))`) before `[0]`. The same query now ALWAYS resolves to the same clip across dry-run / real-run / re-run, so the gated frame == the inserted file.
 2. `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` — added a QCR-032 final guard: before insertion, extract a frame straight from each `_stock_N.mp4` (not the possibly-stale `_stockframe_N.png`) and Read it; prefer a clean FULL re-run (delete `*_stock_*.mp4` + manifest) over chaining `--only-window` re-queries.
- **Affected files:** `broll-pipeline/select_stock_brolls.py`, `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`.
- **Verification:** re-running the same query twice now yields the same pexels id; the clean re-select produced files whose direct-from-mp4 frames matched the gate (orb/smoke/nodes/chess) and passed QC 97/100.

### QCR-033: For "data/tokens stream" Beats, "binary data"/"matrix rain" Pexels Queries Reliably Return Human Faces — Lead With Abstract Motion-Background Terms
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC passed 100/100 iter1). Cost 2 extra `--only-window` re-queries on W2.
- **Finding:** The W2 beat was "thousands of tokens wasted / streaming data". The first query `"streaming binary data blue"` returned a clip of a PERSON with binary projected on their face (face-gate REJECT). The retry `"matrix digital rain code"` returned a hacker silhouette at a green-text monitor (face + on-screen-text REJECT). Only the third query, `"abstract glowing particles blue" / "blue light streaks motion"`, returned a clean face-free abstract clip. The same pattern (people/hacker scenes) recurs on Pexels for any "binary", "matrix", "hacker", "coding", "data stream on screen" phrasing.
- **Root cause:** Pexels' stock for literal tech terms ("binary", "matrix rain", "data stream", "hacker", "coding") is dominated by stock-footage of people-at-screens and face-with-code-projection — exactly the no-face/no-text classes our gate rejects. The agent-written queries-file led with those literal terms, guaranteeing 1-2 wasted re-query cycles per "data/tokens" window.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` INPUTS guidance — for any "data / tokens / processing / stream" beat, the FIRST query phrase must be an abstract motion-background term (`"abstract glowing particles"`, `"blue light streaks motion"`, `"digital particles dark"`, `"data grid landscape"`), NOT `"binary data"`, `"matrix rain"`, `"data stream"`, `"hacker"`, `"coding screen"` (these reliably return human faces / on-screen UI and fail the frame gate). Keep the literal term only as a later fallback phrase.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`.
- **Verification:** W2 passed the face/text gate on the abstract-particles query; future "tokens/data" windows that lead with abstract terms should HIT clean on the first frame review.
