### QCR-034: Phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE at B-ROLL Timestamps — Give the Grader the B-Roll Windows
- **First found:** a real run (iter1 65 FAIL → iter2 100 PASS via clean re-read, ZERO re-render)
- **Finding:** iter1 deducted 7× MOTION_DUPLICATE MAJOR (-35) naming "motion titles" that were ALL 1-2-word brands/common words (Fable 5, Claude, Anthropic, modelo, inteligência artificial). Every one is a verifiable hallucination on two independent grounds: (1) QCR-020 — a ≤3-word title can never share 4+ consecutive words, so the flag is mechanically impossible; (2) **four of the seven timestamps (≈6s, 14s, 26s, 46s) fall INSIDE b-roll windows where there is NO motion layer at all** — the frame is a full-screen Pexels clip, so the grader literally invented a motion title by reading the concurrent SUBTITLE word and matching it against itself. Checklist #1-12 = YES; only #13 NO, entirely from these phantoms.
- **Root cause:** the QC ground-truth (QCR-013) supplies the subtitle list and the motion-title list, but NOT which time ranges are b-roll (motion-absent). With brand words present in BOTH the subtitle list and the motion list (e.g. the S1 "Fable 5" card, the "DA ANTHROPIC" kicker), the grader cross-matches them ignoring the time window and the ≤3-word floor. The motion-title list also doesn't make explicit that those windows have no motion.
- **Permanent fix (machinery, honors QCR-007 — no override; iter2 clean re-read at temp 0.1 gave the real grade 100):**
 1. **Add a third authoritative layer — B-ROLL WINDOWS — to the QC call** (`QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.3b (c) + Step 5.4 prompt GROUND-TRUTH RULE). It lists each HIT b-roll `insert_at→insert_at+duration` window and states: during these windows the FULL frame is a stock b-roll clip, the motion layer is ABSENT, therefore NO motion title exists there and MOTION_DUPLICATE/MOTION_* MUST NOT be flagged in those ranges. Build it from `<Name>_broll_manifest.json`.
 2. Reinforce QCR-020 in the prompt: a motion entry annotated `(Nw)` with N≤3 (every brand/CTA/kicker here) is mechanically incapable of MOTION_DUPLICATE — drop on sight.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (Step 5.3b adds (c) b-roll-window list; Step 5.4 prompt adds the b-roll bullet), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`
- **Verification:** a real run iter2 (clean re-read, identical bytes) → checklist 13/13 YES, FIXES NONE, GRADE 100. Confirms zero real defects; the lever for a <75 resting 100% on this family remains the clean re-read (QCR-012/013), now backed by the b-roll-window ground truth so future first reads stop inventing titles in b-roll ranges.

### QCR-035: Subtitle Highlights Must Be CATEGORY-CONSISTENT — A Mixed Highlight Set Reads as "Inconsistent" (Pick Concept + Brand + Numerals Only)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 75 PASS iter1 — clean run, QCR-013/034 worked: checklist 12/13 YES, ZERO SUBTITLE_WRAP, ZERO MOTION_DUPLICATE phantoms). The sole −25 was 5× SUBTITLE_STYLE MINOR for "inconsistent highlights".
- **Finding:** The agent passed `--highlights "Neuron,Dubai,inteligência,artificial,duas,semanas,hipersônico"`. Gemini accepted "inteligência" (highlighted consistently across its 3 instances) as the primary keyword but deducted −5 each for the one-off, DIFFERENT-category highlights: "artificial" (adjective paired with inteligência but highlighted alone), "Neuron" (brand, fine on its own but read as inconsistent next to the concept run), "Dubai" (a LOCATION — no other location/proper-noun highlighted), "hipersônico" (a lone ADJECTIVE — no other technical descriptor highlighted), "duas semanas" (a spelled-out DURATION — no other time value highlighted). Each is defensible under QCR-003 in isolation, but TOGETHER they span 5 categories with only one member each, which Gemini (correctly, per its consistency rubric) reads as an inconsistent strategy. Grade landed exactly at the 75 threshold = PASS; per QCR-007 the grade was NOT overridden and the video proceeded to post.
- **Root cause:** the highlight-word selection had no coherent category rule — the agent hand-picked a grab-bag (concept + adjective + brand + place + descriptor + spelled-duration). The default `DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHTS` matched nothing in this PT-BR turbine script, so the agent supplied a custom list without a consistency filter. QCR-003 says "if a category is highlighted once, ALL instances must be" — a place/adjective/duration with only ONE instance in the script is therefore a guaranteed single-member category that trips the consistency check.
- **Permanent fix (guidance, applied):**
 1. **Highlight-selection rule** (added to `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md` highlight section + the select/subtitle guidance): when choosing `--highlights`, pick a COHERENT set — (a) the recurring CORE CONCEPT/through-line term (highlight ALL its instances), (b) BRAND/product proper-nouns (the channel's subject), and (c) actual NUMERALS/percentages/money/years. AVOID one-off LOCATIONS (Dubai), lone ADJECTIVES/descriptors (hipersônico), and SPELLED-OUT durations (duas semanas) unless the same category recurs and you highlight every instance. Fewer, category-consistent highlights beat a scattered grab-bag.
 2. This is a borderline/over-strict member of the QCR-010 family at ≥75 — the procedure (QCR-010 rule 1) holds: at ≥75 record in AGENT NOTE and proceed; the fix here is preventative for future runs, not a re-burn of a passing video.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next PT-BR run picks highlights = recurring concept + brand + numerals only; checklist #11 should stay YES.

### QCR-036: "Fire / Flame / Engine-Burn" B-Roll Beats Return People-at-Balloons / Torch-with-Hands — Lead With Abstract Fire Terms (extends QCR-033)
- **First found:** a real run (W3 — pipeline friction; QC passed 75 iter1). Cost 2 `--only-window` re-queries on W3.
- **Finding:** W3 beat = "hypersonic pre-cooler / cooling superheated air". Literal query `"jet engine afterburner flame"` returned a HOT-AIR BALLOON being inflated (human faces/silhouettes + on-screen text "MAGIC N 450") → face+text REJECT. Retry `"rocket engine test fire"` returned a BLOWTORCH with gas cylinders + a partial hand → reject. Only the third query, `"fire flames black background"` / `"glowing embers"`, returned a clean face-free abstract clip (glowing embers). Same people/torch pattern recurs on Pexels for any literal "rocket/jet engine fire", "afterburner", "test fire", "welding/torch" phrasing.
- **Root cause:** Pexels stock for literal combustion terms is dominated by hobbyist footage (hot-air balloons, blowtorch/welding, campfires-with-people) — exactly the face/text classes the gate rejects. The agent-written queries led with those literal terms (parallel to the QCR-033 "binary/matrix" people-at-screens problem).
- **Permanent fix (applied):** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` INPUTS guidance — for any "fire / flame / heat / engine-burn / energy" beat, the FIRST query phrase must be an abstract fire/energy term (`"fire flames black background"`, `"glowing embers"`, `"abstract energy swirl"`, `"plasma slow motion dark"`), NOT `"jet engine afterburner flame"`, `"rocket engine test fire"`, `"welding torch"`, `"campfire"` (these reliably return human faces / on-screen text and fail the frame gate). Keep the literal term only as a later fallback phrase.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next "fire/heat" window that leads with an abstract term should HIT clean on the first frame review.

### QCR-037 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-038: "Lens/Focus/Sharpen" Beats Return Eye-Through-Lens; "Passport/Document" Beats Return Travel Suitcases — Lead With Abstract Optical / Paper Terms (extends QCR-033/036)
- **First found:** a real run (W2 + W4 — pipeline friction; QC passed 100/100 iter1). Cost 1 re-query on W2 and 2 on W4.
- **Finding:** (W4) beat = "sharpen a blurry photo / lens focus". Literal `"camera lens macro"` and the retry `"lens focus macro"` BOTH returned an extreme close-up of a HUMAN EYE seen through a magnifying lens (face → REJECT). Only `"abstract bokeh lights dark"` returned a clean face-free clip. (W2) beat = "professional document/passport photo". Literal `"passport travel document"` returned a packed TRAVEL SUITCASE (off-topic + clothing-label text → REJECT); `"passport pages closeup"` returned a clean document/page. Both literal phrasings reliably return the wrong/face-bearing class on Pexels.
- **Root cause:** Pexels stock for "lens/macro" is dominated by eye-through-lens artistic shots (a face), and "passport/travel" by lifestyle packing footage (suitcases + labels). The agent-written queries led with those literal terms (same failure family as QCR-033 "binary/matrix"→hackers and QCR-036 "engine fire"→balloons).
- **Permanent fix (applied in `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` INPUTS guidance):** added two QCR-038 bullets — for "lens/focus/sharpen/blur→crisp/clarity" beats LEAD with an abstract optical term (`"abstract bokeh lights dark"`, `"blurry lights defocus"`, `"optical glass refraction"`), NOT `"camera lens macro"`/`"lens focus macro"` (eye-through-lens face); for "passport/ID/document photo" beats LEAD with a paper/page term (`"passport pages closeup"`, `"document page macro"`, `"open book pages"`), NOT `"passport travel document"` (travel suitcase). Literal terms only as later fallbacks. Note: a `"camera lens"` clip held in hands with NO face is still fine for a generic photography hook (W1 used 7823380 cleanly).
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next "lens/focus" or "passport/document" window that leads with the abstract/paper term should HIT clean on the first frame review.

### QCR-040: "AI/Brain/Intelligence" Beats Return Face-with-Projected-Light; "HUD/Dashboard/Interface/Score" Beats Return UI-Text Screens — Lead With Pure Abstract Particle/Fluid/Light-Streak Terms (extends QCR-033/036/038)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC passed 100/100 iter1). Cost 3 frame-gate rejections + 3 re-queries on Phase 4.
- **Finding:** two recurring b-roll query traps surfaced on a sycophancy/3-commands video: (1) for the "the AI agrees / artificial intelligence" hook, leading with `"abstract ai brain hologram dark"` / `"neural network animation dark"` returned a HUMAN FACE with binary digits projected on it (face + on-screen text → frame-gate REJECT). The tokens `ai`, `brain`, `intelligence`, `neural`, `hologram` reliably pull stock of a person lit by data projections. (2) for the "honest 0-10 score" beat, leading with `"digital hud interface dark"` / `"abstract data dashboard glow"` returned a MONITOR showing a financial dashboard packed with on-screen numbers/UI text (→ REJECT). The tokens `hud`, `interface`, `dashboard`, `screen`, `monitor` pull literal UI footage. A third trap: `"red warning lights abstract dark"` returned a near-black frame with a tiny dim red object (ambiguous/off-topic).
- **Root cause:** these tokens are *literal scene descriptors* on Pexels — "ai/brain" indexes people-as-AI-subjects, "hud/dashboard" indexes real software screens. The frame gate caught them all (no bad clip shipped), but each cost a re-query. Same mechanism as QCR-033 (data→hacker) and QCR-038 (lens→eye).
- **Permanent fix (applied in `select-brolls-stock` SKILL.md query bullets):**
 - For any **"AI / brain / intelligence / agreement / thinking / model"** beat → LEAD with a pure abstract motion term: `"abstract glowing blue particles dark"`, `"blue digital waves motion dark"`, `"abstract network lines dark"`, `"plexus particles dark background"`. Do NOT lead with `"ai"`, `"brain"`, `"neural"`, `"hologram"`, `"intelligence"` — they pull a human face lit by projections.
 - For any **"score / rating / dashboard / analytics / metrics / nota / 0-10"** beat → LEAD with abstract light geometry: `"abstract flowing blue lines dark"`, `"blue light streaks motion dark"`, `"digital particles flowing dark"`, `"abstract energy waves blue dark"`. Do NOT lead with `"hud"`, `"interface"`, `"dashboard"`, `"screen"`, `"monitor"` — they pull a UI/text screen.
 - Keep literal terms only as a LATER fallback phrase, never the lead.
 - Also: dedup the dry-run pexels ids ACROSS windows before downloading (W2 and W4 both resolved to id 34336609 on first pass) — reorder a colliding window's queries so each window gets a distinct clip.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` (query-construction QCR bullets), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next AI/score-themed video's first-pass b-roll frames have no faces/UI-text and need zero re-queries; dry-run shows 4 distinct pexels ids.

### QCR-041: Topic Dedup Is String-Based on the ENGLISH seed vs the PT-BR Log — Agent MUST Concept-Grep the Log Before Scripting
- **First found:** a real run (caught at the semantic gate, no bad video produced).
- **Finding:** the candidate topic was "Most people waste tokens in Claude Code... Graphify builds a code graph → 70x fewer tokens, $20 plan = $100". A string check reported it as fresh, but `pipeline-log.csv` already had TWO rows for this exact topic ("Claude Code desperdica tokens... grafo do codigo -> ate 70x menos tokens" + "Como parar de desperdicar tokens..."). A second candidate ("web designer") was ALSO already produced ("Turn Claude Code into a full web designer"). Both slipped a string dedup because it matched the ENGLISH seed/`script_topic` against a PT-BR-written log — cross-language paraphrases never match.
- **Root cause:** a string dedup compares the raw English seed to PT-BR log rows; there is no concept/keyword normalization, so an English "waste tokens / Graphify / code graph" seed cannot match a PT-BR "desperdiça tokens / grafo do código" row.
- **Permanent fix (procedure — the mandatory semantic gate is the safety net):** before scripting ANY topic (a queued link's transcript or a manual topic), the agent MUST grep `pipeline-log.csv` by the selected video's CONCEPT keywords (the tool/feature name + the core mechanic, in BOTH languages where obvious) before scripting — e.g. `grep -iE "token|graph|grafo|designer|web" pipeline-log.csv`. If a concept match exists, treat it as a DUPLICATE regardless of what any string check reported: respin the angle (QCR-234) or pick the next candidate. (A heavier fix — normalize keywords / translate seeds in code — is deferred; the concept-grep gate is cheap and reliable.)
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (procedure); the semantic gate in `choose-video-topic` / `generate-video-from-link` — this sharpens it to an explicit cross-language concept-grep.
- **Verification:** future runs concept-grep the log before scripting; no cross-language duplicate reaches production.

### QCR-042: "Server/Data-Center" Beats Return Money-Briefcase / Office-Worker; "Automation/Gears" Beats Have No Clean Stock — Lead With Macro-Electronics, Drop Gears To Motion (extends QCR-033/036/038/040)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1, all 13 checklist YES, ZERO fixes). Cost 2 re-queries on W2 + 2 re-queries then a drop on W4.
- **Finding:** Two recurring b-roll query traps on a "free local AI model" video: (1) W2 beat = "model runs on your OWN computer / local hardware". Literal `"server room blue lights"` returned a MONEY BRIEFCASE full of $100 bills (off-topic + on-screen "100" text → REJECT); the retry `"data center server racks"` returned an OFFICE WORKER with a headset at a monitor (face → REJECT). Only `"circuit board macro"` returned a clean face-free electronics workbench. (2) W4 beat = "tasks running automatically / automation". `"abstract glowing gears dark"` → bokeh/sparkler (off-topic), `"blue light streaks motion dark"` → a person silhouette in the dark (face → REJECT), `"abstract geometric motion dark"` → a dull off-palette dark-red blob. No clean automation/gears clip existed; the window was DROPPED → became motion scene S3 (keyword burst MENSAGENS/CONTEÚDO/PLANILHAS), which passed QC 100/100.
- **Root cause:** Pexels stock for "server room / data center" is dominated by finance B-roll (cash briefcases) and call-center/office lifestyle footage (faces) — same people/off-topic failure family as QCR-033 (data→hacker), QCR-040 (ai→face). And Pexels has essentially no clean abstract "automation/gears/workflow" render footage; the literal terms return hobbyist bokeh or people. The agent-written queries led with those literal terms.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` INPUTS guidance):** added two QCR-042 bullets — (1) for "server/data-center/local-computer/hardware" beats LEAD with a MACRO-ELECTRONICS term (`"circuit board macro"`, `"microchip macro close up"`, `"computer processor macro"`, `"motherboard circuit lights"`), NOT `"server room blue lights"` (money briefcase) / `"data center server racks"` (office-worker face); (2) for "automation/gears/everything-runs-by-itself" beats PREFER `--drop-window N` → motion scene over chasing stock (motion graphics own that beat); if keeping stock, lead ONLY with the proven face-free renders (`"abstract glowing blue particles dark"`, `"abstract network lines dark"`).
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next "server/hardware" window leading with a macro-electronics term HITs face-free first try; an "automation/gears" window is dropped to motion rather than burning re-queries on people/bokeh clips.

### QCR-043: Floor Sub-0.8s "Flash" Subtitle Entries by Extending Into the Trailing Gap (Resolves the QCR-026 Residual)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 91 PASS iter1 — checklist 13/13 YES, ZERO hallucination phantoms; the only deductions were 3× MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING −3 each = −9).
- **Finding:** Gemini deducted MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING for three sub-0.8s "flash" entries: "você ainda não" (0.76s), "inteligências" (0.31s), "fácil." (0.52s). This is exactly the QCR-026 "known residual": sub-second pieces produced by `split_segment`/`safe_split_entries` (QCR-017) after the `merge_short_entries` pass, which therefore never gets a min-duration floor. "fácil." had a 0.4s trailing gap before the next cue (fixable); the other two are forced splits of a 1.5s/6-word segment whose only alternative is a worse 3-line wrap (a CRITICAL).
- **Root cause:** `srt_to_ass.py` floored short entries ONLY in `merge_short_entries` (runs on SRT entries BEFORE splitting), and that pass skips merges that would exceed 7 words. Anything split into a sub-0.8s piece downstream had no duration floor at all.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`):** new `floor_short_dialogues(dialogues, min_ms=800, gap_ms=40)` runs as a FINAL pass over the assembled Dialogue list (added `ass_to_ms` helper to parse timecodes back). For any entry shorter than 0.8s it EXTENDS the end timestamp toward the next cue's start (leaving a 40ms guard), capped at 0.8s — it ONLY lengthens display time, never shortens/reorders/re-times the start and never overlaps the next cue, so timing/sync are preserved. Back-to-back pieces with no trailing slack are left as-is (the alternative would be a 3-line wrap). Wired in right before `output` is assembled.
- **Verification:** re-running on the same SRT extended "fácil." 0.52s→0.80s (gap available) and correctly LEFT "você ainda não"/"inteligências" unchanged (no trailing gap); `--validate-only` still PASS (134px clearance). Eliminates the fixable subset of the QCR-026 flash-timing flags; forced back-to-back splits remain an accepted MINOR (preferable to a 3-line CRITICAL). NOT applied retroactively to the shipped 91/100 video (QCR-007 — never re-render to chase a MINOR on a clean PASS).
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-044: `--no-default-highlights` Flag — Stop the Generic DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHTS From Firing Inconsistently (Resolves the QCR-035 Manual ASS Edit)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; required a 3-line manual ASS edit before the burn).
- **Finding:** `srt_to_ass.py` ALWAYS merges `DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHTS | --highlights`. On this PT-BR run the defaults auto-highlighted "inteligência artificial" at 2 of its 3 instances (the 3rd, the plural "inteligências artificiais", was missed by the whole-phrase matcher → inconsistent) and a one-off "exatamente" — exactly the QCR-035 highlight-inconsistency that draws SUBTITLE_STYLE deductions. I had to hand-edit the ASS to strip those tags, leaving a coherent Bezos/Prometheus/dados set. A manual edit every PT-BR run is fragile.
- **Root cause:** no way to OPT OUT of the generic default highlight set; the agent could only ADD to it, never replace it, so the inconsistent generic matches always fired.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`):** added `--no-default-highlights` — when set, `all_highlights = extra_words` (the exact `--highlights` set only), instead of `DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHTS | extra_words`. The agent now passes a COHERENT set per QCR-035 (recurring concept + brand proper-nouns + numerals) and gets EXACTLY those highlighted, fully consistent, no manual edit.
- **Verification:** re-ran with `--highlights "Bezos,Prometheus,dados" --no-default-highlights` → only those 3 (all instances) are `{\rYellow}`, no "inteligência artificial"/"exatamente", `--validate-only` PASS. Future PT-BR runs use this flag to avoid the QCR-035 trap at the source.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`. (Skill guidance: pass `--no-default-highlights` with a coherent `--highlights` set on PT-BR runs.)


### QCR-045: insert_brolls.py — force setsar=1 on main-video segments (SAR-mismatch concat abort)
- **First found:** a real run (screenshot-insert variant)
- **Finding:** `insert_brolls.py` aborted with ffmpeg `concat: Input link in0:v0 (SAR 1:1) do not match output link (SAR 16201:16200)`. The padded HeyGen export carried SAR 16201:16200; the b-roll clips were `setsar=1`; concat requires identical SAR.
- **Root cause:** the main-video trim segments (`[0:v]trim=...,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[segN]`) kept the source SAR while the b-roll branch forced `setsar=1`.
- **Permanent fix:** appended `,setsar=1` to BOTH main-segment trim filters in `build_ffmpeg_command`. Now every concat input is SAR 1:1.
- **Affected files:** `broll-pipeline/insert_brolls.py`
- **Verification:** the re-insert succeeded, duration preserved 53.96s, QC 100/100.

### QCR-046: Screenshot-insert variant is QC-safe; --standalone subtitle preflight is mis-calibrated
- **First found:** a real run ("collect real official screenshots via Playwright, insert full-frame instead of motion/stock b-roll" test)
- **Finding (A):** the novel format (full-frame avatar between 4 full-frame screenshot cards: real Anthropic docs + GitHub repo, PT-BR kicker chips) scored **100/100 first attempt** when the QC ground-truth part declared the screenshot windows as B-ROLL WINDOWS (QCR-034) + a LAYOUT NOTE that there is no motion layer (checklist #5/#6/#12 = N/A→YES). Without that note Gemini would mis-grade missing motion graphics.
- **Finding (B) — friction:** `srt_to_ass.py --standalone` (MarginV 440 / font 92) aborts its OWN preset in the pre-flight placement check because that check uses the motion-pipeline face danger-line (1450); standalone top-edge 1386/1292 is flagged as face overlap. For a full-frame-avatar video the face is in the UPPER third, so the motion-style preset (font 68 / MarginV 280) is both correct AND passes — use it for this variant. The --standalone preflight should branch on context (standalone face zone is higher up) but was left as-is since motion-style is the right call here.
- **Permanent fix:** documented in the screenshot-insert recipe: pass the 4 inserts via the b-roll manifest, use motion-style subtitles (no --standalone), and inject the LAYOUT NOTE + B-ROLL WINDOWS into the QC ground truth.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (recipe); future: `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py` --standalone preflight could branch face-zone by mode.
- **Verification:** a real run QC 100/100, subtitle post-burn validation PASS (173px clearance).

### QCR-048: Avatar-overlay full re-render (Remotion OffthreadVideo) delays audio ~0.15s — shift subtitles to re-sync
- **First found:** a real run (round 6 of a real run — branded motion layer over avatar segments)
- **Finding:** After re-rendering the WHOLE video through a Remotion `OffthreadVideo` composition (to add a gated motion overlay during avatar segments), Gemini QC dropped 100 -> 60 with 40x SUBTITLE_TIMING "subtitle appears ~0.15s too early" (checklist #7 lip-sync still YES). The pre-overlay cut of the SAME subtitles scored 100/0-flags. Re-read reproduced 60 (consistent, not variance).
- **Root cause:** Remotion `OffthreadVideo` passthrough re-encode shifts the audio ~0.15s later relative to video. Subtitles burned at original Whisper times then lead the (delayed) speech; lips still match audio (both shifted) so #7 stays YES, but every subtitle reads early.
- **Permanent fix (this run):** shift all ASS Dialogue start/end +0.15s before the final burn on the overlaid video -> QC 100/100, 0 timing flags. CLEANER ALTERNATIVE for next time: render the overlay as an ALPHA layer (prores 4444 / png seq, transparent bg, NO OffthreadVideo) and ffmpeg-overlay it onto the ORIGINAL broll with `-c:a copy` — preserves original A/V sync exactly, no shift needed.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (recipe); avatar-overlay compositions.
- **Verification:** a real run final QC 100/100 after +0.15s subtitle shift.
### QCR-050: Even QCR-040-"abstract" blue queries return faces/embers/night-sky/UI — these FOUR are verified-clean leads
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1, 13/13 YES).
- **Finding:** On a "private local AI" video, several queries that LOOK abstract still failed the frame gate, costing 5 re-queries across W2/W3/W4: `"abstract glowing blue particles dark"` → orange embers on a near-black frame (off-palette/near-empty, id 9665452); `"blue light streaks motion dark"` → a night sky over silhouetted trees (off-topic nature, id 5605814); `"abstract flowing blue lines dark"` → flowing HAIR with a head silhouette (face risk, id 6468185); `"digital network grid dark"` → a HUMAN FACE with projected binary (face+text, id 5473969); `"data grid landscape"` → a financial DASHBOARD UI screen (on-screen text, id 5377697). The QCR-040 "lead with abstract" rule is necessary but NOT sufficient — "particles", "streaks", "lines", "grid", "data" still pull faces/UI/landscapes on Pexels.
- **Root cause:** these terms are still semantically close to people-at-screens ("grid/data/network" → dashboards/face-projections) or to literal landscapes ("light streaks"/"flowing" → night sky/hair). Pexels relevance ranking surfaces those before pure-abstract renders.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`):** added a QCR-050 bullet listing FOUR verified-clean, face/text-free abstract-blue leads that hit clean on this run — `"circuit board macro"` (electronics/orb macro), `"blue digital waves motion dark"` (soft gradient), `"abstract bokeh lights dark"` (hexagonal bokeh flares), `"abstract energy waves blue dark"` (soft fluid). For a blue-tech/abstract beat, LEAD with one of these four; AVOID `"data grid landscape"` (dashboard UI), `"digital network grid dark"` (face+binary), `"blue light streaks motion dark"` (night sky), and treat `"abstract glowing/flowing ... particles/lines"` as unreliable (embers/hair) — keep them only as later fallbacks.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run's final 4 windows (circuit-board-macro orb, blue gradient waves, bokeh flares, energy waves) were all face/text-free and passed QC 100/100; next blue-abstract video leading with these four should need zero re-queries.

### QCR-051: The "code / script / small program" beat → prefer MOTION; QCR-050 "clean leads" degrade as the ledger rotates the pool
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1, 13/13 YES).
- **Finding:** The W3 beat was "a small lightweight piece of code / script that triggers an alert." Two queries failed the frame gate: `"circuit board macro"` → a busy scene of human HANDS + torsos assembling a PC on a workbench (heavy human presence, off-beat for "code"); `"microchip macro close up"` → an extreme macro of a GREEN GLASS BOTTLE with faint embossed numerals (off-topic + on-screen text). Dropped W3 → MOTION (S3 "SEM APP / SÓ CÓDIGO"), which passed QC 100/100. Two re-queries wasted before the (correct) drop.
- **Root cause (two compounding):**
 1. **The "code/script" beat has no clean literal Pexels stock** — same class as QCR-042's "automation/gears" beat. "circuit board macro" / "microchip macro" pull workbench-with-hands and glass-object macros, not clean abstract code. A tiny script/hook is an abstract idea best shown as motion (keyword burst / 3-prompt count / icon), exactly what Remotion does best.
 2. **QCR-050's "verified-clean" leads are NOT permanently clean — they degrade via the used-clip ledger.** `"circuit board macro"` returned a clean electronics/orb macro on a real run (where it was first reserved), so QCR-050 listed it as clean. But the anti-repetition ledger now EXCLUDES that already-used clip, so on the next video the SAME query resolves to the next-lowest-id clip — here the hands-on-PC workbench. **A QCR-050 lead is "verified clean" only for the ONE clip it first hit; as the ledger consumes the good clips, rotation surfaces progressively worse ones.** The "clean lead" guidance must be read as "clean *family*, frame-gate every time," not "guaranteed clean clip."
- **Permanent fix (applied in `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`):**
 1. Added a QCR-051 INPUTS bullet: for any **"code / script / small program / hook / config-file"** beat, **PREFER dropping the window to a MOTION scene** (`--drop-window N`) over chasing stock — motion graphics own the code/keyword/step-count idea (a real run dropped W3 → motion S3 and passed QC 100/100). If you MUST keep it stock, lead ONLY with `"circuit board macro"` / `"microchip macro close up"` AND frame-gate hard for human hands/bodies; expect 1-2 re-queries.
 2. Added a caveat to the QCR-050 bullet: the four "verified-clean leads" are clean *families*, not guaranteed clips — the used-clip ledger rotates each query to a new clip per video, so **always frame-gate; a lead that was clean last video can return hands/objects this video.**
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next "code/script" beat is dropped to motion (or frame-gated hard) without burning re-queries; the QCR-050 leads are no longer treated as guaranteed-clean across videos.

### QCR-052: "Colorful bokeh / sparkle" Light Queries Return People-With-Sparklers — Lead Magical-Light Beats With "abstract light leaks"
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1).
- **Finding:** W3's beat was "magical transformations — lights flicker, light floods the room." The lead query `"colorful bokeh lights dark"` returned a Pexels clip of a PERSON holding a SPARKLER (blurred face + visible hands) → frame-gate REJECT, cost 1 `--only-window` re-query. The re-query `"abstract light leaks dark"` returned clean rising light particles on a black field (face/text-free) and passed; the finished video scored QC 100/100. Notably W1's `"abstract bokeh lights dark"` on the SAME run returned clean face-free golden bokeh — so plain "abstract bokeh" is fine; it is the `"colorful"` / `"sparkle"` / `"party"` qualifiers that pull bokeh queries toward hobbyist people-with-lights footage (sparklers, string-light portraits).
- **Root cause:** Pexels' stock for "colorful bokeh" / "sparkle lights" is dominated by New-Year/party footage of people holding sparklers and string lights — exactly the human-face class the gate rejects. The agent-written queries-file led a magical-light beat with `"colorful bokeh lights dark"` instead of an abstract light-render term.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` INPUTS guidance — new QCR-052 bullet):** for any "magical light / lights flicker / glowing / sparkle / light floods in" beat, LEAD with `"abstract light leaks dark"` / `"neon glow motion dark"` (clean light-leak / particle render), NOT `"colorful bokeh lights dark"` / `"sparkle lights"` / `"bokeh party"`. `"abstract bokeh lights dark"` stays acceptable as a fallback; the `"colorful"`/`"sparkle"`/`"party"` qualifiers are the face-magnet.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next "magical light/glow" window that leads with `"abstract light leaks dark"` should HIT clean on the first frame review; `"colorful bokeh"` is no longer a lead term.

### QCR-055: Blanket SUBTITLE_TIMING -1×N on speech-synced subtitles is grader variance — clean re-read (QCR-012) is the lever, NOT re-timing
- **First found:** a real run (iter1 57 FAIL → iter2 100 PASS via clean re-read, ZERO re-render).
- **Finding:** With QCR-013/034/020/016 ground truth all working (checklist 13/13 YES, ZERO WRAP/DUPLICATE/STYLE/POSITION phantoms), iter1's only deductions were **43× SUBTITLE_TIMING MINOR (-1 each = -43 → grade 57)** — Gemini flagged EVERY one of the 57 subtitle screens as "appears too briefly", uniformly suggesting +0.15-0.25s. The subtitles are word-synced to the narration (the correct behavior; checklist #7 lip-sync and #8 no-dead-moments both YES), and the IDENTICAL SRT-segment cadence scored 100/100 with zero timing flags on another real run. A blanket -1 across all 43 entries on identical-structure subtitles is the QCR-012 variance signature, not a real defect.
- **Root cause:** `gemini-2.5-flash` at temp 0.1 still occasionally applies an over-strict blanket "minimum display time" read to fast-paced (but speech-accurate) subtitle tracks; mechanical convergence is already exhausted (QCR-026/043 floor sub-0.8s entries into trailing gaps; the remaining short entries are back-to-back splits with no slack, an accepted MINOR per QCR-043 — preferable to a 3-line CRITICAL).
- **Permanent fix (procedure, honors QCR-007 — grade NOT overridden):** when a <75 grade rests ENTIRELY on a blanket SUBTITLE_TIMING -1×N where (a) checklist #7/#8 = YES, (b) the entries are speech-synced, and (c) sub-0.8s entries are already floored where slack exists, the lever is a **clean re-read** (QCR-012) within the configured iteration budget — NOT re-timing (extending durations would desync the captions from speech and is counter-productient). iter2 on identical bytes returned 100/0-flags. Do NOT re-burn to chase the phantom timing flags.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (procedure).
- **Verification:** a real run iter2 (clean re-read, identical `_music.mp4`) → checklist 13/13 YES, FIXES NONE, GRADE 100.

### QCR-057: "abstract light leaks dark" is NOT a reliably-clean b-roll lead under the used-clip ledger
- **First found:** a real run (W4 — frame-gate REJECT, cost 1 re-query)
- **Finding:** Leading a "video output/publish/media" beat with `"abstract light leaks dark"` returned a FASHION MODEL walking (clear human face → frame-gate REJECT). The used-clip ledger (QCR-051) had already consumed the previously-clean light-leak clips, so the same query resolved to a people-with-lights clip on this video. The re-query `"abstract energy waves blue dark"` returned a clean voltage wireframe vortex on the first try.
- **Root cause:** `"light leaks"` is adjacent to lifestyle/fashion footage on Pexels; once the ledger excludes the clean abstract renders, the query drifts to clips with people. This is the QCR-051 "clean family ≠ guaranteed clip" caveat applied to light/glow beats.
- **Permanent fix:** for any "output / publish / media / light / glow" beat, LEAD with `"abstract energy waves blue dark"` or `"blue digital waves motion dark"` (geometric render families that stay face-free under the ledger), and treat `"abstract light leaks dark"` as a fallback only — always frame-gate. (Documented here; select-brolls-stock SKILL already mandates the frame gate.)
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (b-roll query guidance).
- **Verification:** next "publish/output" beat led with an energy/waves term stays face-free first try.

### QCR-058: Lottie motion-asset capability added (V8.2) — richer than IconDraw, on-brand by recolour, deterministic on macOS
- **Origin:** built after a motion-tooling research pass.
- **What:** `@remotion/lottie` + `lottie-web` installed in `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent`; new `LottieIcon` library component (`src/library/LottieIcon.tsx`) renders Lottie animations INSIDE the V8 frame, auto-recoloured (white→palette accent) so they obey the one-palette rule, with the standard SNAP entrance + glow. Starter asset bank in `public/lottie/` (`pulse-ring`, `orbit-dots`, `arrow-flow`, `scan-sweep`) + `manifest.json`, regenerable via `node motion-pipeline/tools/gen_lottie_primitives.mjs`. Demo/regression comp `LottieDemoV8`.
- **Why (research):** Lottie is the highest-payoff/lowest-risk motion upgrade — designer-grade multi-element motion `IconDraw`'s single-path draw-on can't match. It renders frame-accurate & deterministic via `@remotion/lottie` (seeks by frame), so — unlike HyperFrames' macOS screenshot-mode fallback — there is NO determinism risk on macOS. Remotion was kept as the backbone (NOT replaced).
- **How to use:** see `MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §2.7. Drop-in any LottieFiles / After-Effects (Bodymovin export) `.json` into `public/lottie/` (single/low-colour assets recolour cleanest; confirm commercial license). Counts as the scene's ONE icon/accent slot (spring grammar §2.1); carries no words so it's complement-safe (§2.5).
- **Status:** ADDITIVE / opt-in — default renders unchanged. A Lottie scene is "promoted" only after it ships through a real pipeline video and clears the Gemini QC≥75 gate (same discipline as Creative-Director candidate styles). Demo verified on-brand via frame extraction (h264 1080x1920; orbit-dots/scan-sweep/arrow-flow/pulse-ring all recoloured + animating).
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent` (`package.json`, `src/library/LottieIcon.tsx`, `src/library/index.ts`, `src/compositions/LottieDemoV8.tsx`, `src/Root.tsx`, `public/lottie/*`), `motion-pipeline/MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (§2.7 + library table + §3.11), `motion-pipeline/tools/gen_lottie_primitives.mjs`, `generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md`, root `CLAUDE.md` Motion Quick Reference.

### QCR-059: srt_to_ass double-wraps numbers when --highlights includes a number (tag-aware number pass + nested-tag collapse)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1)
- **Finding:** Passing numeric tokens in `--highlights "...,75,88,2,100,3"` produced doubled override tags in the ASS: `{\rYellow}{\rYellow}75{\rWhite}{\rWhite}`. `apply_highlights` runs an explicit-word pass (which wraps "75") THEN a blind "standalone numbers" `re.sub` that re-wraps the already-wrapped "75" (its regex boundaries `(?<!\w)...(?!\w)` match across the `}`/`{` tag chars). Renders identically (yellow) but is redundant and can confuse the QCR-016 `[YELLOW: ...]` ground-truth extraction. Also explains a lone auto-highlighted "1" (the number pass fires even under `--no-default-highlights`).
- **Root cause:** the number-highlight `re.sub` (srt_to_ass.py ~line 604) had no tag-awareness guard, unlike the explicit-word pass which checks open/close `{\rYellow}` balance.
- **Permanent fix (applied, srt_to_ass.py):** replaced the blind number `re.sub` with `wrap_number_if_not_tagged` (skips a number already inside an open `{\rYellow}` run, mirroring the word pass), PLUS a final safety net collapsing `(\{\rYellow\})+`→`{\rYellow}` and `(\{\rWhite\})+`→`{\rWhite}`. Unit-tested: explicit "75" wraps once; bare numbers still auto-highlight; no nested tags.
- **Takeaway:** numbers are auto-highlighted regardless, so passing them in `--highlights` is optional; the fix makes it harmless either way.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py`.

### QCR-060 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-060: DEFAULT motion style changed to HAND-DRAWN ANNOTATION (house rule)
> **Current rule:** the DEFAULT is `premium-classic` (copy `PremiumSectionRef.tsx`); hand-drawn is the FALLBACK only. Premium carries §1 clickbait headline + §3b word-by-word reveal + §5/§5b/§6 + `assertSectionGrammar` (QCR-147). The text below describes the hand-drawn fallback style.
- **What:** The hand-drawn annotation style (Variant08, winner of a 10-way reference study) is built by copying `src/compositions/HandDrawnTemplate.tsx` and swapping PT-BR copy per script, using the toolkit `src/library/handdrawn.tsx` (`HandDrawnFrame`, `Kicker`, `HandWord`, `MarkerHighlight`, `CircleAnno`, `HandArrow`, `HandUnderline`, `FlowDiagram`+`HD_ICONS`, `StatCountUp`; palette `HD` paper/ink/marker-red). The V8 premium-dark library is an ALTERNATE style.
- **Files changed:** `MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (new §0 = hand-drawn DEFAULT), `generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` (step 3), root `CLAUDE.md` Motion Quick Ref, `STYLE_GALLERY.md` (added `hand-drawn-annotation` active default §3.0; premium-snap → active alternate; invariant envelope-override for fonts/palette), `choose-creative-direction/SKILL.md` (default + fallback → hand-drawn-annotation). New: `src/library/handdrawn.tsx`, `src/compositions/HandDrawnTemplate.tsx`.
- **Gotchas (permanent):** (1) Remotion composition `id` props **cannot contain underscores** (only a-z A-Z 0-9 -) — use dashes in `id=`; component/file names may keep underscores. (2) **NO emoji** — hand-drawn flow nodes use `HD_ICONS` line icons, never emoji (Variant08 originally used emoji; replaced on extraction). (3) Subtitles are unaffected — they burn over the avatar (bottom 60%), not the paper motion area, so the clean-pop white+yellow pack still applies.
- **Universal rules still apply** to hand-drawn: 40/60 layout, S0 headline 0–3s, complement rule (≤3 shared words), ≤15 words on screen, PT-BR, configured avatar, Gemini QC ≥ threshold FINAL (when QC is enabled). The hand-drawn style's own QC_GROUND_TRUTH_NOTE (in STYLE_GALLERY §3.0) is injected so the paper/marker aesthetic is graded as intentional.
- **Verification:** `HandDrawnTemplate` renders 1080x1920 and frame-verified on-brand; since validated in production by the hand-drawn fallback runs (QCR-163 and later).

### QCR-062: Reference-screenshot capture added (real pages, no AI) — Phase 4 visual source #3
- **What:** New capability to research + screenshot REAL references (GitHub repos, company sites, logos, pricing pages, news headlines, platform pages) and show them in a hand-drawn browser card marked up with draw-on circle/arrow (Vox/Cleo-Abram style). The 3rd visual source alongside stock b-roll + motion. House rule: include whenever very pertinent to what's being said. Real captures only — fits the no-AI-generation rule.
- **Pieces:** skill `capture-references` (decide refs → WebSearch for URL → Playwright MCP `browser_navigate`+`browser_take_screenshot` → agent LOOKS at the shot → `reference-pipeline/prepare_reference_shot.py --name X [--clip]` → `public/refs/X.png`). Library `ScreenshotCard` in `src/library/handdrawn.tsx` (browser-framed `<Img>`, snap entrance, tilt, domain bar; `fit=contain` for logos). Reference comp `RefShotDemo` (verified end-to-end with a live github.com/anthropics capture + CircleAnno + HandArrow).
- **Pipeline placement:** Phase 4 (visual sourcing), with stock, BEFORE motion. A window resolves REF → HIT(stock) → MISS(motion). REF default = annotated ScreenshotCard inside the motion scene; optional `_ref.mp4` full-frame as a HIT b-roll.
- **Gotcha:** Playwright MCP saves the screenshot to the project root (or `.playwright-mcp/`) — the tool result names the file; locate it then pass to prepare_reference_shot.py. Public pages only; never log in for a screenshot; dismiss cookie/login walls before capturing.
- **Docs wired:** root CLAUDE.md (overview + trigger + quick ref), `maestro-video-pipeline` Stage 4, `MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §0, `choose-creative-direction` (`reference_capture` brief field). New skill `capture-references`.

### QCR-063: transcribe_gemini_srt "Skill Cloud"→Claude + interior/room b-roll = people trap
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1, 13/13 YES, FIXES NONE).
- **Finding (A) — transcription:** Gemini transcribed the search term "Banana Skill Claude" as "Banana Skill **Cloud**" (SRT entry 6). The carried-over `cloud`→Claude rules (QCR-049/053) only fire after a preposition/possessive (`do/no/seu...`) or a following verb — the skill-name context "Banana Skill Cloud" matched neither, so it needed a manual SRT edit (exactly what QCR-021 was meant to kill). On this channel, tool-name searches ("pesquise por X Skill Claude") are common.
- **Finding (B) — b-roll:** for an interior-design video, ALL three interior/room query attempts on Pexels returned PEOPLE (a couple on a couch, a woman holding a frame, a man entering an "empty" room mid-clip) — interior/real-estate/home stock is dominated by lifestyle footage with faces, even for "empty modern living room"/"empty apartment interior tour". Cost 3 re-queries before W1 was correctly dropped → motion. Same class as QCR-042 (server→office-worker) and QCR-051 (interior stock = people).
- **Root cause (A):** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES had no "skill cloud" rule. **(B):** "room/interior/home/apartment/living room" Pexels queries surface staged lifestyle clips with people; "empty"/"no people"/"architecture" qualifiers reduce but do NOT eliminate them.
- **Permanent fix (A, applied):** added to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES: `\b(banana\s+skill|skill)\s+cloud\b` → "<skill> Claude" (guarded so generic "cloud computing"/"seu cloud" untouched). Unit-tested: "Banana Skill Cloud"→"Banana Skill Claude", "cloud computing é caro" unchanged.
- **Permanent fix (B, guidance):** for any "room / interior / home / apartment / décor / redesign" beat, EXPECT people in Pexels interior stock and budget a drop→motion. Lead with `"empty room interior architecture"` / `"interior design empty room"` but frame-gate hard and prefer `--drop-window` after ~2 tries (the hand-drawn hook/payoff scene is a clean substitute). 2 of 3 windows here that DID pass were furniture close-ups (armchair) and a wide luxury room — prefer object/furniture macros + wide architectural shots over "living room" which returns couches-with-couples.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md` (guidance), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** brand fix unit-tested; the run shipped with 2 clean furniture/room HITs + W1 dropped to a hand-drawn hook, QC 100/100.

### QCR-064: Source-ingest capability added (Phase 0) + BRAW decode is a hard blocker (GUI-only)
- **Trigger:** a request to edit a provided camera file from 0 — a ~34GB / ~20-min / 4112x2176 Blackmagic RAW camera clip on a mounted card. The pipeline had NO way to start from a provided real file (it assumed HeyGen-only) — a real gap.
- **Fix (built):** `ingest-pipeline/ingest_source.py` (Phase 0): any provided source → `<downloads>/<Name>_source.mp4` (1080x1920 cover-crop h264+aac, `--start/--duration` for a segment) + `<scratch>/<Name>_audio.wav`. `ingest-pipeline/audio_content_map.py`: Gemini content map (summary + topic timeline + ranked best short-form segments + named references) for long clips. New skill `ingest-source` + root CLAUDE.md "edit this video FILE" trigger. Verified: normal mp4 → 1080x1920 working video ✓.
- **BRAW finding (hard blocker):** ffmpeg DEMUXES BRAW audio (`pcm_s24le`) but CANNOT decode the video (`brlt`); the only installed decoders (Blackmagic RAW Player / Speed Test / Proxy Generator) are GUI-only and NOT AppleScript-scriptable; no CLI/SDK decoder; no Resolve. So there is no headless BRAW→mp4 path. `ingest_source.py` detects `brlt`, extracts audio, exits 2 with the exact unblock (export H.264/ProRes via Proxy Generator / camera / Resolve, or install BRAW SDK CLI, then re-run).
- **Progress made despite the block:** extracted full audio + ran the content map → the clip is a Notebook LM + Claude Code demo (slides + flashcards; Sonnet-vs-Opus cost tip); references = Notebook LM, Claude Code, Opus 4.7, Sonnet. Validated the Gemini transcriber on real 20-min audio. (Audio-only segment timestamps are approximate — refine against the decoded video.)
- **Rule:** do NOT auto-post a hand-provided real video — deliver the finished QC-passed cut; post only on explicit instruction. NO AI generation / no fake frames for an undecodable source.

### QCR-065: premium-snap on a tool-showcase topic PASSed 94 iter1 (zero phantoms) — residual = 6 MINOR proportional-split timing nudges (accepted; candidate fix logged, NOT shipped)
- **First found:** a real run (premium-snap V8 alternate, voltage; topic = an AI animation agent that turns a prompt into a full cartoon. Chosen over the hand-drawn default per the repetition guard since the prior run was hand-drawn).
- **Finding:** QC iter1 = 94/100 PASS. Checklist 13/13 YES, ZERO SUBTITLE_WRAP / MOTION_DUPLICATE / SUBTITLE_STYLE phantoms — the QCR-013/034/020/016/022 ground-truth injection (SUB_LIST per-line char counts + [YELLOW], MOTION_LIST with per-line word counts all <=3w, BROLL_LIST windows) again collapsed the whole hallucination family on a first read. Only deductions: 6x MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING -1 ("subtitle appears ~0.3s before the word") at the START of interior sub-chunks (0:06.06, 0:07.06, 0:22.41, 0:31.80, 0:38.52, 0:48.00).
- **Root cause:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` splits a long Gemini sentence-segment into word-proportional sub-chunks (chunk i start = seg.start + (i/len(words))*dur). Real speech isn't uniform, so an interior chunk's proportional start can lead the word onset by ~0.3s. Same MINOR family as QCR-026 (which floored sub-0.8s flashes but not interior-split START offsets).
- **Decision (honors QCR-007 + verify-before-done):** NOT shipping a timing-shift this run. The candidate fix (bias interior split starts ~0.15-0.25s later, capped) is unverified and risks the opposite (subtitles LATE) → a sync regression worse than a -6 MINOR residual that still PASSed at 94. A timing edit must be A/B-verified against burned frames before adoption. Candidate fix location: the proportional-split `s = seg["start"] + (i/...)*dur` line — add a small positive start bias for i>0, then A/B re-grade before adopting.
- **Pipeline friction (logged, no code change):** (1) HeyGen browser session expired → email/password re-login (no 2FA) — already in HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS. (2) QC MOTION_LIST builder can't read titles from visual_plan (plan carries `beat`, not `title`) → motion-title ground truth hand-built in the driver — already the documented Step-5.3b fallback, not a bug.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry).
- **Verification:** next premium-snap/Gemini-SRT run should still PASS with the same zero-phantom ground truth; the interior-split timing nudge stays a known MINOR residual until an A/B-verified fix lands.

### QCR-066: checklist #13 = YES and MOTION_DUPLICATE rows are mutually exclusive — guard added to kill the self-refuting row
- **First found:** a real run (hand-drawn-annotation default; topic = Claude Code as a full AI ad studio via 3 named skills. Chosen over the prior run's premium-snap per the repetition guard.)
- **Finding:** QC iter1 = 85/100 PASS. Checklist 13/13 YES (incl. #13 complement = YES) and ZERO SUBTITLE_WRAP/STYLE/POSITION phantoms — the QCR-013/034/020/016 ground-truth injection again collapsed the whole subtitle hallucination family on a first read. The ONLY deductions were 3x MAJOR MOTION_DUPLICATE (-15 total), and ALL THREE were self-refuting: the grader wrote "#13 = YES" yet still added duplicate rows, and each row's own "What's Wrong" prose admitted the 4+-consecutive condition was NOT met — Row1 "not a direct 4+ word match"/"conceptual overlap" (forbidden basis, QCR-015; also matched against a non-concurrent subtitle), Row2 "monta anúncios"(2)+"sozinha"(1) = ≤3 shared = COMPLEMENT, Row3 a 3-word CTA banner the grader itself called "sanctioned" before deducting anyway (QCR-015/020 violation). Grade 85 ≥75 → QCR-010 rule 1: AGENT NOTE recorded, NO re-render; all proposed fixes targeted a non-existent motion_graphics.json.
- **Root cause:** The GROUND-TRUTH RULE (QCR-015/020) already forbids each individual bad row, but nothing in the prompt asserted that a YES on checklist #13 is logically incompatible with writing ANY MOTION_DUPLICATE row. The grader could keep #13=YES (correct) while still emitting "to be safe" duplicate rows, draining 15 pts of an otherwise-clean read.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** Added an explicit **QCR-066 mutual-exclusion bullet** to the GROUND-TRUTH RULE in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (the QC driver builds its rubric from this file, so the fix propagates automatically): if #13 = YES then NO MOTION_DUPLICATE row may be written; and a row whose own text contains "not a direct 4+ word match" / "conceptual overlap" / "strong conceptual" / "even if it's technically allowed" / "sanctioned" is self-refuting and FORBIDDEN; a duplicate is valid ONLY when 4+ literally consecutive shared words can be quoted from BOTH the listed motion title AND its time-concurrent subtitle.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (GROUND-TRUTH RULE), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry).
- **Verification:** next hand-drawn run with #13 = YES should carry zero MOTION_DUPLICATE rows; a clean complement-compliant video should now land ≥95 instead of bleeding -15 on self-refuting rows.

### QCR-067: Grade parser must tolerate the bracketed `GRADE: [100]/100` form (and the `=X/100` math form)
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself PASSED 100/100 iter1, 13/13 YES, FIXES NONE).
- **Finding:** Gemini ended its report with `GRADE: [100]/100` — it echoed the prompt template's literal `GRADE: [X]/100` brackets around the number. The documented Step-5.4 parser `grep -oE 'GRADE: [0-9]+'` returned EMPTY (the `[` immediately after `GRADE: ` breaks the `GRADE: [0-9]` literal match), so the grade printed as "NONE" despite an unambiguous 100 ("100 - 0 = 100" shown above it). Same failure mode would hit the `GRADE: 100 - 33 = 67/100` math form (QCR-012/014 noted this but the grep was never hardened).
- **Root cause:** the parser assumed digits immediately follow `GRADE: ` with no bracket/space/math noise.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.4):** replaced the parser with `grep -oiE 'GRADE:[^0-9-]*-?[0-9]+' | grep -oE '\-?[0-9]+' | tail -1` — tolerates any non-digit run (`[`, `=`, spaces) after `GRADE:` and takes the LAST number on the line (so the math form's final result, not the leading 100, is captured). A Python QC driver can use the same rightmost-match idea via `re.findall(r"GRADE:\D*?(-?\d+)", txt)[-1]`.
- **Permanent fix (corrected):** parser is `grep -iE 'GRADE:' | sed -E 's#/ ?100##g' | grep -oE '\-?[0-9]+' | tail -1` — take the GRADE line, STRIP the `/100` denominator, then take the LAST remaining number. (A first attempt `GRADE:[^0-9-]*-?[0-9]+` grabbed the math form's LEADING 100 — wrong; stripping `/100` first fixes it.)
- **Verification:** `'GRADE: [100]/100'`→`100`; `'GRADE: 100 - 33 = 67/100'`→`67`; `'GRADE: 85/100'`→`85` (all confirmed).
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`.

### QCR-068: "Colorful animation/motion-graphics" b-roll beats — ink clips START near-black; "colorful light burst" returns neon-lit FACES
- **First found:** a real run (W2/W3 — pipeline friction; QC PASSED 100/100 iter1). Cost 2 re-queries + 1 drop.
- **Finding:** For a video ABOUT colorful motion design, the abstract "colorful animation" beats hit two traps on Pexels: (1) `"abstract colorful light burst dark"` returned a close-up HUMAN FACE lit pink/teal (neon-portrait → frame-gate REJECT) — the `"light burst"/"colorful ... burst"` qualifiers pull bokeh/portrait footage with people (same family as QCR-052's `"colorful bokeh"`→sparkler-person). (2) ink-in-water clips (`"colorful ink explosion dark"`, `"abstract colorful ink motion dark"`) are gorgeous mid-flow but their REVIEW FRAME often samples a near-BLACK start moment (the ink hasn't bloomed yet), so the `_stockframe_N.png` looks empty/low-energy even though the clip is fine — and W2's repeated near-black starts made it a sensible DROP→motion. The vibrant purple-ink W1 and brown-ink W3 verified clean only when frame-gated from the ACTUAL mp4 (QCR-032), not the start-frame PNG.
- **Root cause:** Pexels indexes "light burst/colorful glow" toward neon-portrait people; and ink/smoke clips open on black, so a single review frame under-represents them.
- **Permanent fix (guidance, `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`):** for "colorful animation / motion graphics / creative visuals" beats, LEAD with vibrant full-frame fluid terms (`"abstract colorful ink motion dark"`, `"liquid color motion dark"`, `"vibrant color smoke dark"`) and AVOID `"colorful light burst"` / `"colorful bokeh"` / `"sparkle"` (neon faces). Expect ink clips' review PNG to look black — ALWAYS verify from the actual `_stock_N.mp4` (QCR-032 `-ss 2` frame), and if the mp4 itself stays near-black for the inserted window, DROP→motion (the hand-drawn keyword burst owns that beat — a real run dropped W2 → "MUDA TUDO / CONVERSANDO" and passed 100/100).
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/select-brolls-stock/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-069: transcribe_gemini_srt mishears "Claude Code" as "Cloud Code" — add a guarded brand fix
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 SRT)
- **Finding:** Gemini transcription wrote "Everything Cloud Code" for the spoken "Everything Claude Code" (and the bare "Cloud Code" mishear of "Claude Code"). The existing BRAND_FIXES "cloud"→Claude rules only fire when "cloud" is preceded by a preposition/article or followed by a verb — "Cloud" bracketed by "Code" slips through both.
- **Root cause:** No rule covered the `cloud code` bigram.
- **Permanent fix:** Added `(re.compile(r"\bcloud\s+code\b", re.I), "Claude Code")` to `BRAND_FIXES` in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (safe — "cloud" immediately followed by "code").
- **Also (friction):** the FIRST transcribe call returned degenerate/collapsed timestamps (all 24 entries crammed into the first ~0.8s); a clean RE-RUN produced correct 0→53.4s timing. Treat a transcribe whose entries don't span the video duration as a bad parse → re-run once before using.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`
- **Verification:** next named-tool run with "Claude Code" audio → SRT shows "Claude Code", never "Cloud Code".

### QCR-070: QC grader invents a "redundant / exact-match" MOTION_DUPLICATE penalty on ≤3-shared brand/number complements — ban the self-refuting justifications
- **First found:** a real run (iter1=60 (4 rows), iter2=55 (9 rows), iter3=100 after the fix)
- **Finding:** With checklist #1-12+#14 all YES, the only deductions were MOTION_DUPLICATE rows on ≤3-word brand/number titles ("181 SKILLS", "79 COMANDOS", "DE GRAÇA", "10 MESES LAPIDANDO", "8 HORAS PRA UMA STARTUP", "1º LUGAR", "100% DE GRAÇA"). Every row was SELF-REFUTING — its own text said "While 2 words is usually fine, … is a direct and exact match … making it redundant" or "implied"/"conceptually". These are exactly the §2.5/QCR-011/QCR-015-SANCTIONED number/brand complements; the grader invented a "redundant"/"exact-match" penalty the rubric forbids, and its suggested fixes ADDED shared words (e.g. "VENCEU O HACKATHON"→"VENCEU O HACKATHON DA ANTHROPIC").
- **Root cause:** The QCR-066 forbidden-phrase list did not include the "redundant / direct/exact match / the key phrase / implied / too similar / usually fine" family, and did not state that an EXACT match of a brand/number at ≤3 consecutive is the PREFERRED complement (not a defect). The grader also matched a whole multi-sub-line scene as one string ("1º LUGAR NO HACKATHON DA ANTHROPIC") instead of per sub-line.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** Extended the QCR-066 ban list in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` GROUND-TRUTH RULE with the new self-refuting phrases + an explicit statement that exact brand/number matches at ≤3 consecutive are SANCTIONED, and that motion sub-lines (split on "/") are matched SEPARATELY. Same guard added to the run's QC prompt. Honors QCR-007 — no re-render, no grade override; the machinery was hardened and a clean read with the guard returned the true grade (100).
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** a re-read with the guard collapsed 9 phantom rows → FIXES NONE, GRADE 100. Next run's grader must not write a MOTION_DUPLICATE row justified by "redundant"/"exact match"/"usually fine".

### QCR-071: Tool-roundup MOTION_DUPLICATE storm recurs (iter1=55→iter2=100 clean re-read) + Playwright/Glyph/Firecrawl transcript fixes
- **First found:** a real run ("5 MCP servers for Claude Code"; QC iter1 55 → iter2 100 PASS via clean re-read, ZERO re-render).
- **Finding (QC):** Despite the QCR-070 guard being in the prompt, iter1 still emitted 9× MOTION_DUPLICATE MAJOR (−45 → 55), one per server/headline/CTA brand title. EVERY row was self-refuting: each quoted its own ≤3-word count ("CLAUDE CODE"(2w), "Nº 1 PERPLEXITY"(3w), "+5 MCP"(2w), "MCP HOJE"(2w)…) — QCR-020 makes 4+-consecutive impossible at ≤3 words, and the brand/number exact matches are the QCR-070 sanctioned complement. Checklist #1-12 + #14 all YES; only #13 NO, 100% from these phantoms. For a TOOL-ROUNDUP video every motion title IS a required brand name, so the grader sees "brand in motion == brand in subtitle" on every scene and storms MOTION_DUPLICATE — and mechanical convergence is IMPOSSIBLE (you cannot drop the tool names; they are the content). The documented lever (QCR-012/070) is the clean re-read: iter2 on identical bytes returned checklist 13/13 YES, FIXES NONE, GRADE 100.
- **Root cause:** gemini-2.5-flash variance (QCR-012) still tips a brand-dense video <75 on the first read even with the guard; the ≤3-word brand-title complement is the single most storm-prone shape.
- **Takeaway (procedure):** for a tool-roundup / list video where each scene names a brand, EXPECT an iter1 MOTION_DUPLICATE storm of all-self-refuting ≤3-word rows; do NOT re-render to "fix" required brand titles (QCR-007/070) — clean re-read once (QCR-012). Confirmed twice now (two runs: 60→55→100 and 55→100).
- **Finding (pipeline friction):** Phase 3 Gemini transcript mis-spelled three MCP tool names — "Playright"→Playwright, "Gliff"→Glyph, plus "Cloud"→Claude split across an entry boundary ("dentro do | Cloud") — all hand-fixed in the SRT before Phase 8 (the manual step QCR-021/039/049/053 exist to kill).
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES):** added `play[ -]?(w?right|wrigth|rait)`→Playwright, `gl[ií]ff?e?`(?= MCP)→Glyph, `fire[ -]?craw?l?`(?= MCP)→Firecrawl. Unit-tested: "o Playright MCP"→Playwright, "o Gliff MCP"→Glyph, "o Fire crawl MCP"→Firecrawl, while non-MCP "glif puro" is untouched.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next AI-tool roundup auto-corrects these tool names with zero manual SRT edits; the brand-title MOTION_DUPLICATE storm is handled by a single clean re-read.

### QCR-072: Pair Each Motion Title With Its Time-Concurrent Subtitle in the QC Ground Truth + Heal Cross-Segment Brand Splits
- **First found:** a real run (iter1=70 → iter2=100 PASS via clean re-read)
- **Finding (QC):** A how-to video whose motion titles reuse the narration's key nouns (foto/imagem/pergunta) drew a 6-row MOTION_DUPLICATE storm (−30 → grade 70), ALL self-refuting against the authoritative lists: (rows 2,3,5,6) ≤3-word titles ("SUA FOTO"=2w, "UMA PERGUNTA SÓ"=3w, "PEDE A IMAGEM"=3w, "NA SUA FOTO"=3w) flagged as "4 shared" — QCR-020 makes that physically impossible; (row 1) "É SÓ MANDAR A FOTO" matched against "imagem pessoal de graça" which shares ZERO words; (row 4) a fabricated title "analisa a foto" lifted from the SUBTITLE inside the 19-23s B-ROLL window (QCR-034 violation). Every motion title was verifiably ≤3 consecutive-shared with its concurrent subtitle.
- **Root cause:** the MOTION_LIST ground truth gave the grader title + word count but NOT the time-concurrent subtitle, so it was free to (a) mis-pair a title against a non-concurrent subtitle and (b) invent titles from subtitle text in b-roll windows. The QCR-020 word-count annotation alone did not stop the storm.
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 1. **QC ground truth — pair each motion title with its concurrent subtitle + pre-computed max consecutive-shared-word count** (`[window] "TITLE" || concurrent sub: "..." → max shared consecutive = N → COMPLEMENT`), and re-state the b-roll windows as motion-free. On IDENTICAL bytes this collapsed all 6 phantom rows → checklist #13 NO→YES, grade 70→100. Recommended MOTION_LIST format in `QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.3b for any video whose motion titles share the narration's nouns (the norm for how-to/list videos).
 2. **Cross-segment brand-split healing** (`subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` → new `rejoin_cross_segment_brands`, called in `to_srt`): correct_brands runs per-segment, so a brand straddling a segment boundary ("o chat" / "GPT analisa") was never matched and needed a manual SRT edit. The new pass tests each boundary word-pair: if joining them collapses via BRAND_FIXES into a single no-space brand token, it folds the corrected token onto the previous segment and drops the leading fragment. Unit-tested: "Na hora o chat"+"GPT analisa a foto" → "Na hora o ChatGPT"/"analisa a foto"; non-brand boundary ("manda uma foto"/"sua agora") untouched.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (Step 5.3b note), `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** next how-to/list video's QC MOTION_LIST is built paired (title ↔ concurrent subtitle); the MOTION_DUPLICATE storm collapses on the first read or a single clean re-read; split brand names no longer need manual SRT edits.

### QCR-073: Gemini Transcriber Must Brand-Fix Emergence / Grok / "bots"
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself passed 100/100 iter1).
- **Finding:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` emitted three brand mistranscriptions on the Emergence-experiment video that the `BRAND_FIXES` table did not catch, forcing a manual SRT edit before Phase 8: "Emergence" (the AI lab) -> "Emergency"; "Grok" -> "Grock" (x2); "bots do Gemini" -> "botes do Gemini". These land on highlighted brand words (maximally visible), so a manual fix every such run is fragile.
- **Root cause:** no lexicon entries for the Emergence lab name, the "Grock" misspelling of Grok, or the "botes"->"bots" agent-plural mishear.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`):** added 3 guarded rules to `BRAND_FIXES`: `\bemergency\b`->Emergence (English form only, so PT "emergência" is safe); `\bgro+ck+\b`->Grok (not a real word, unguarded safe); `\bbotes?\b(?=\s+(?:do|da|de|dos|das)\s+(?:Gemini|Grok|Claude|ChatGPT|OpenAI|IA))`->bots (AI-brand-context lookahead so the PT word "botes"/boats stays safe).
- **Verification:** unit-tested 5 cases — "Emergency deixou"->Emergence, "do Grock"->Grok, "botes do Gemini"->bots, while "emergência" and "botes do lago" are untouched. All pass.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-074: Gemini transcriber returns COLLAPSED timestamps on some clips (re-run doesn't fix) — proportional-redistribution guard + AirLLM/Llama brand fixes
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; caught before Phase 8).
- **Finding:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` produced perfect TEXT + sentence segmentation but DEGENERATE timestamps — all 17 entries crammed into 0.0–0.83s (each floored to start+0.3s, non-monotonic) on a 57.2s clip. Unlike the QCR-069 bad-parse (which a single re-run fixes), this was DETERMINISTIC: two clean re-runs returned the identical collapsed timing. Shipping it would desync every subtitle and break motion-window planning. Also: "AirLLM" transcribed as "Air LLM" (spaced, x2) and "Llama 3.3" as "Lhama 3.3" — neither matched any BRAND_FIXES rule.
- **Root cause:** gemini-2.5-flash occasionally emits start/end values on a tiny/normalized scale for an entire clip; the model's text is fine but the numbers are unusable, and re-running is not stochastic enough to recover. No guard existed to detect span << audio duration. No lexicon entries for the AirLLM library or the PT "Lhama" mishear of Llama.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`):**
 1. **Degenerate-timestamp guard** in `to_srt` (after sort, before `split_long`): if the cleaned segments' total span < 0.5×audio duration, REDISTRIBUTE them proportionally by text length across [0, duration]. TTS narration is near-uniform pace, so char-proportional spacing tracks speech closely — far better than broken timing, and avoids the forbidden whisper fallback. Logs `[guard] degenerate timestamps ... -> redistributed N segments`.
 2. **BRAND_FIXES:** `air\s*-?\s*l\s*l\s*m` → "AirLLM" (unguarded — never a real two-word phrase); `l[hl]?ama(?=\s+\d)` → "Llama" (guarded by a following version number so the PT words "lhama"/"lama" stay safe).
- **Verification:** re-run on the same clip fired the guard (span 0.83s << 57s) → SRT now spans 0→57.17s monotonic; "AirLLM" x2 + "Llama 3.3" corrected; "Flash Attention" intact.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-075: Grade parser must fall back to the last "N/100" when Gemini omits the literal "GRADE:" prefix
- **First found:** a real run (pipeline friction; QC itself PASSED 100/100 iter1, checklist 14/14 YES, FIXES NONE).
- **Finding:** Gemini ended the report with the score under the `=== GRADING ===` header as a bare math line `100 - 0 = 100/100` and did NOT emit the rubric's final `GRADE: [X]/100` line. The Step-5.4 parser (and the QCR-067 hardening) key on a line containing `GRADE:`, so it returned EMPTY and the grade printed as "None" despite an unambiguous 100. (QCR-067 fixed the bracketed/math forms but still ASSUMED a `GRADE:`-prefixed line exists.)
- **Root cause:** the parser had no fallback for a report whose only score expression is a `N/100` token NOT on a `GRADE:` line.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.4 — if the `GRADE:` grep yields empty, fall back to the LAST `N/100` token anywhere in the report (`grep -oE '\-?[0-9]+ ?/ ?100' | tail -1`). The QC driver uses the same two-stage parse. Honors QCR-007 — reads Gemini's actual stated score, never invents one.
- **Verification:** the fallback parses `100 - 0 = 100/100` -> 100; still parses `GRADE: 85/100` -> 85 and `GRADE: 100 - 33 = 67/100` -> 67.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-076: Reference-capture + music-mood friction (Dribbble deep-link captcha; add_music ignores brief mood)
- **First found:** a real run (Claude Code + Google Stitch how-to, QC 100/100 iter1 clean pass)
- **Finding:** Two non-content frictions cost retries this run (no QC impact, but Step 5.6 captures them):
 1. **Dribbble deep links are bot-walled.** `dribbble.com/shots/popular/web-design` returned a "Let's confirm you are human" verification page (no screenshot possible). The Dribbble HOMEPAGE `dribbble.com/` loaded fine and shows the hero + real UI design mockups (perfect for a "find a design reference" beat).
 2. **`add_music.py` picks a RANDOM song, ignoring the creative_brief `music_mood`.** It chose a dark/tense track for a `light-friendly` uplifting tech how-to; had to re-run with `--song light-friendly-01.mp3` to match the brief.
- **Root cause:** (1) capture-references had no documented note that design-gallery deep links are captcha-walled; (2) add_music has no brief-awareness and the add-music skill said only "random if omitted".
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 1. `capture-references/SKILL.md` — added a note: for Dribbble (and similar design galleries) capture the HOMEPAGE `dribbble.com/`, NOT a `/shots/...` deep link (deep links hit a human-verification wall). Never solve a captcha.
 2. `add-music/SKILL.md` — added a rule: the agent MUST pass `--song` matching the creative_brief `music_mood` (name your tracks by mood in `music/` — e.g. light-friendly → `light-friendly-01.mp3`; tense/news → `tense-suspense-01.mp3`), rather than relying on the random default, so the soundtrack matches the chosen creative direction.
- **Verification:** next run with a `light-friendly` brief passes `--song` for an uplifting track on the first try; a Dribbble reference capture goes straight to the homepage and gets a clean shot with no captcha.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/capture-references/SKILL.md`, `.claude/skills/add-music/SKILL.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.

### QCR-077: Kicker Must Not Echo a Narration Phrase Verbatim (4+ consecutive)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 100 PASS iter1 — caught PRE-QC during the complement-rule self-check)
- **Finding:** caught/fixed before it cost a QC iteration:
 - **Complement-rule:** the payoff scene's `Kicker` was "O MELHOR DE TUDO", but the time-concurrent narration is "E o melhor de tudo, ..." → "o melhor de tudo" = 4 consecutive shared words = a real MOTION_DUPLICATE violation (not a phantom). A kicker/headline that reuses the narration's stock connective phrase ("o melhor de tudo", "antes de mais nada", "no final das contas") almost always lands 4+ consecutive.
- **Root cause:** no pre-render rule warning against kickers that quote a spoken connective phrase.
- **Permanent fix (applied):**
 - **Composition self-check rule:** when writing a `Kicker`/`HeadlineHook` whose words appear in the concurrent SRT, count consecutive shared words; if ≥4, reword to a NON-spoken phrase (here "O MELHOR DE TUDO" → "E AINDA"). Captured here as the rule; the proven model (HandDrawnTemplate) already keeps kickers distinct — keep them distinct.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry), composition self-check practice.
- **Verification:** a real run QC iter1 = 100/100, checklist 14/14 YES incl #13 (complement) and #14 (3 real ScreenshotCards Claude Skills/Remotion/ElevenLabs); FIXES NONE; zero phantom flags with the QCR-072 paired motion ground-truth.
