### QCR-078 — `FullMotionFrame` step icons must be HD_ICONS KEYS, not raw paths
- **Symptom:** Section 4 (`FullMotionFrame`) flow diagram rendered EMPTY icon boxes (labels showed, glyphs didn't) when `steps` were given as `{icon: "doc", label: "ROTEIRO"}`.
- **Root cause:** `FlowDiagram`→`FlowNode` renders `<path d={icon}>` expecting an SVG path string, but the ergonomic/consistent API (matching `SplitGraphicMotion`/`FullGraphicFrame`) passes HD_ICONS KEYS like `"doc"`. `d="doc"` is invalid → nothing drawn.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** `FullMotionFrame` now resolves each step icon via `HD_ICONS[s.icon] ?? s.icon` before handing to `FlowDiagram` — so KEYS resolve to paths AND raw paths still pass through. All three new section components now take icon KEYS uniformly.
- **Affected files:** `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent/src/library/sections.tsx` (`FullMotionFrame`).
- **Verification:** HandDrawnGrammarRef re-render — ROTEIRO(doc)/REMOTION(gear)/VÍDEO(play) glyphs all render; SectionCycleDemo re-rendered clean too.

### QCR-079 — 6-section repeating grammar + graphics-only sections (house rule)
- **Change:** canonical motion order is now `1 Headline → 2 Evidence → 3 AvatarOnly → 4 Motion+Text → 5 SplitGraphics(noText) → 6 FullGraphics(noText)`, then repeat `3→6` until the script ends. Sections 1–2 fire once (opening); 3–6 are the repeating body.
- **New components (`src/library/sections.tsx`):** `SplitGraphicMotion` (split: avatar below, top-panel graphics only, ZERO text — CHILD of HandDrawnFrame) and `FullGraphicFrame` (fullscreen hand-drawn system diagram, ZERO text, no avatar — OVERLAY). Helpers: `IconBadge` (FlowNode box w/o label + gentle continuous float), `PulseRing`, `FlowDots` (data-moving-through-system dots along connectors).
- **Text rule:** sections 5 & 6 bake NO words — meaning via HD_ICONS line icons + motion only; the only on-screen text during them is the Phase-8 burned subtitle.
- **Reference builds:** `SectionCycleDemo` (generic, full cycle + repeat) and `HandDrawnGrammarRef` (real coherent PT-BR script). Spec table: `motion-pipeline/SECTION_PIPELINE.md`.

### QCR-081 — StatBurst count-up samples a wrong mid-value on scrub; CircleAround/MarkerHighlight bounding clips long accents (candidates, NOT shipped)
- **First found:** a real run (grade 94 PASS iter1 — checklist 14/14 YES, only 2 MINOR COMPOSITION deductions; clean first-attempt pass, zero phantom flags via QCR-072 paired ground truth).
- **Finding:** The two −3 MINORs on an otherwise-perfect pass were both cosmetic: (1) `IdeaStatBurst` counts 0→`to` over ~18 frames, so a frame sampled early in the window shows an intermediate value ("9 MIL" instead of the final "85 MIL") that doesn't match the concurrent spoken number — a scrubbed/sampled frame reads as a factually wrong number even though the animation settles correctly. (2) The S0 `HeadlineHook` accent circle (`CircleAround`) and the motion `MarkerHighlight`/`CircleAnno` bounding box slightly clip the top/bottom of letters on a long accent line ("PRA APRENDER IA"), because the box is sized from a character-width estimate (`estW`) that under-pads tall/wide glyph runs.
- **Root cause:** (1) StatBurst's count-up start at 0 maximizes the window where a sampled frame is wrong; for a "headline number" stat (a salary, a price) the wrong intermediate is more misleading than for a generic metric. (2) `estW(text, size) = text.length * size * 0.55` + fixed circle `pad` under-estimates the visual bbox for long accent strings, so the self-drawing circle clips.
- **Decision (honors QCR-007 + QCR-065 precedent):** NOT shipping a change on this passing (94) run. A count-up timing change (e.g. start the count at ~70% of `to`, or hold the final value longer / shorten the ramp) and a bbox-padding bump both risk regressions (a number that snaps without animating; an over-large circle) and MUST be A/B-verified against burned frames before adoption — exactly the QCR-065 rule (never ship an unverified motion-timing/measurement change reactively on a pass). Logged as candidates.
- **Candidate fixes (for a future verified change):** (a) `IdeaStatBurst` — add an optional `from` prop (default 0) and for headline numbers start nearer the target, OR reduce the ramp to ~10 frames and hold ≥1.0s on the final value, so a mid-window sample is far more likely to show the correct number. (b) `handdrawn.tsx` `estW` / `CircleAround` `pad` — add ~12–16px vertical pad and widen the char-width factor to ~0.6 for ≥3-word accent lines so the circle never clips. Verify both by extracting the affected frames before/after.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry); candidate edits in `src/library/motionideas.tsx` (`IdeaStatBurst`) + `src/library/handdrawn.tsx` (`estW`/`CircleAround`).
- **Verification:** a real run shipped at 94 PASS with these as the only deductions; next StatBurst/long-accent run should re-confirm they stay MINOR until an A/B-verified fix lands.

### QCR-080 — Motion must MATCH the line + VARY the mechanism (house rule)
- **Symptom:** the first 6-section cut reused ONE visual idea (icon badges wired by lines + pulse rings) for every motion beat — sections 4, 5 & 6 all looked the same. User feedback: "the motions are very similar… should be a perfect match with what is being said and avoid similar motion ideas."
- **Root cause:** sections hard-coded a single motion mechanism; no mapping from the spoken line's MEANING to a distinct visual, and no guard against reusing a mechanism.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** new `src/library/motionideas.tsx` — a registry of 6 DISTINCT mechanisms (`IdeaProcessFlow` linear / `IdeaSpotlight` one-element / `IdeaRadialSystem` hub / `IdeaStatBurst` number / `IdeaGridRepeat` tiled / `IdeaCycleLoop` orbit). Two HARD rules: (1) MATCH — pick the idea whose mechanism mirrors the beat's sentence; (2) VARIETY — `assertVariety([...ids])` THROWS at render if any two adjacent motion beats reuse a mechanism. SECTION = canvas, IDEA = what plays on it. Documented in `motion-pipeline/SECTION_PIPELINE.md` (Motion Ideas section).
- **Affected files:** `src/library/motionideas.tsx` (new), `src/library/sections.tsx` (export IconBadge/PulseRing/FlowDots), `src/library/index.ts`, `src/compositions/HandDrawnGrammarRef.tsx` (6 distinct line-matched ideas), `motion-pipeline/SECTION_PIPELINE.md`.
- **Verification:** HandDrawnGrammarRef re-rendered; all 6 motion beats frame-verified visually distinct AND matching their PT-BR lines (flow/spotlight/radial/burst/grid/loop); assertVariety passed.
- **Phase-4 rule going forward:** `generate-motion-remotion` must classify each beat's meaning → pick the matching idea from `MOTION_IDEAS` → never repeat a mechanism within a video.

### Run note — first full pipeline on the 6-SECTION GRAMMAR. QC iter1 = 100/100, FIXES NONE. Self-Improve fixes below.

### QCR-081 — Avatar zoom-punch on every Section-3 beat (house rule, WORKS → craved)
- **Change:** `FullFrameAvatar` (src/library/sections.tsx) now does a fast zoom-in (1.0→1.16 over frames 2–9, easeOutCubic, origin 50%/38%) then HOLDS — "full frame → fast zoom → zoomed frame". Props `zoom` (default true) + `zoomTo`. Verified across all 3 avatar beats; Gemini QC #6 = YES (zoom-punch intentional, not a clip).
- **Crave:** documented as the Section-3 default in generate-motion SKILL + SECTION_PIPELINE.md.

### QCR-082 — Draw-on circle must ring the ACTUAL main element of a screenshot (house rule)
- **Symptom:** the EvidenceReveal circle sometimes ringed empty space, not the real main element.
- **Fix:** `reference-pipeline/prepare_reference_shot.py` gained `--focus "sx,sy"` (+ `--focus-size "w,h"`): given the main element's pixel in the RAW screenshot, it computes the exact card-local `focus={fx,fy}` + `focusR={rx,ry}` (using the ScreenshotCard cover-geometry: ×scale + 46px bar; warns if the element is cropped out of the card). Agent MUST look at the screenshot, pick the real main element (hero/logo/price/headline), and pass its pixel. example: hero "Get paid for waiting." → focus={308,131}; Gemini QC #15 = YES (rings the actual hero).
- **Crave:** documented in the capture-references skill.

### QCR-083 — Semantic icons added to HD_ICONS for concept match
- **Change:** added `coin, eye, cursor, ad, loader, lock` line-icons to `HD_ICONS` (src/library/handdrawn.tsx) so motion ideas can MATCH concrete concepts (money/view/click/ad/spinner/privacy), not just generic doc/gear/play. Used e.g. as (loader=spinner, ad=megaphone, lock=privacy, coin=money). NO emoji.

### QCR-084 — validate_subtitles.py false-fails the full-frame grammar (FIXED)
- **Symptom:** post-burn validator FAIL "worst top-edge 1382 < 1450" on a real run, but every frame was actually fine — full-frame MOTION sections have NO face, and full-frame ZOOMED-avatar sections put the face high (~px 700), so a 2-line subtitle at ~1382 has huge face clearance.
- **Root cause:** the 1450 threshold is hard-calibrated for the OLD fixed 40/60 layout (avatar low in frame).
- **Fix:** `subtitle-pipeline/validate_subtitles.py` gained `--section-grammar` → lowers face-clearance threshold to 1340 (still well above the px-1700 platform danger zone). Pass it for any 6-section-grammar video. Verified: PASS on the same run.

### QCR-085 — QC reference card must be GRAMMAR-AWARE for the 6-section grammar
- **Symptom risk:** the QC_INSTRUCTIONS reference card hard-codes "Top 40% motion / Bottom 60% avatar" + a fixed face zone. The new grammar has full-frame motion AND full-frame zoomed-avatar AND text-free sections — the stock card would induce false flags ("motion outside top 40%", "empty section", "avatar clipped").
- **Fix:** for 6-section-grammar videos, the QC call MUST use a grammar-aware reference card: layout VARIES by section (full-frame motion / full-frame zoomed avatar / split); sections 5 & 6 bake ZERO text by design; checklist #5/#6 are N/A where full-frame; add #14 (motion-idea variety+match) and #15 (circle rings the real main element). Baked into QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md as the SECTION-GRAMMAR reference-card variant. A real run graded 100/100 with it, zero phantom flags.

### QCR-086 (pipeline-friction) — Playwright screenshots don't persist; use browser_run_code with an absolute path
- **Friction:** `browser_take_screenshot(filename=...)` returns the image inline but does NOT leave a findable PNG on disk in this MCP setup (only browser *downloads* persist, e.g. the HeyGen mp4 → .playwright-mcp/). prepare_reference_shot.py then can't find the screenshot.
- **Fix:** to capture a reference screenshot to disk, use `browser_run_code` → `await page.screenshot({ path: '/absolute/path/to/<downloads>/<name>.png', type:'png' })` (absolute path), then feed THAT to prepare_reference_shot.py. Documented in the capture-references skill.

### Run note — 2nd full run on the 6-SECTION GRAMMAR. QC iter1 = 100/100, all 15 checks YES, FIXES NONE. Friction-only fix below.

### QCR-087 (pipeline-friction) — HeyGen script-fill: first-contenteditable miss + STRAY empty Scene 2
- **First found:** a real run (QC 100/100 iter1 — caught/fixed live in Phase 3, cost no QC iteration).
- **Friction (two HeyGen v4 nuances when driving the AI Studio script box via `browser_run_code`):**
 1. A `page.locator('[contenteditable="true"]').first` + `keyboard.insertText` can land in the wrong/unfocused node — the first attempt inserted only ~93 chars (placeholder still showing). Clicking the editor BODY middle `(box.x+box.width/2, box.y+30)` first, then `keyboard.insertText`, landed the full 1040-char script.
 2. After the long script filled, HeyGen auto-created an EMPTY **Scene 2** (white thumbnail + red ⚠). The total estimate matched scene 1 alone (74.8s) so scene 2 was a 0s blank, but it carries a warning and would add a blank scene to the render.
- **Root cause:** (1) fill timing vs editor focus; (2) HeyGen v4 auto-splits/append behavior spawns a trailing empty scene on long fills.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** documented both in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6 (QCR-087 bullet): verify `editor.innerText.length` ≈ script length (re-click the editor body + re-insert if it's ~90), and DELETE any stray empty Scene 2 before Generate via right-click the timeline thumbnail → `getByText('Delete Scene')`, confirming one scene remains and the total estimate matches scene 1.
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run — after deleting Scene 2, single 74.8s scene, Generate 21 credits, render clean; QC 100/100, checklist #6 (avatar not clipped) + #8 (no dead moments) YES (no blank tail).

### Run note — 3rd full run on the 6-SECTION GRAMMAR. QC iter1 = 99/100, only finding = #14 (one non-adjacent motion-idea reuse, MINOR -1). Self-Improve below.

### QCR-088 — `assertVariety` only blocks ADJACENT repeats; a NON-adjacent mechanism reuse still trips checklist #14 (MINOR)
- **First found:** a real run (QC 99/100 iter1 — sole −1: IdeaProcessFlow used at 19.1–29.3s AND 57.6–63.5s; Gemini #14 = NO, "same visual mechanism reused").
- **Finding:** With 8 motion beats and only 6 distinct ideas in `MOTION_IDEAS`, two beats necessarily reuse a mechanism (here processFlow×2 and spotlight×2). `assertVariety([...])` passed because the reuses were NON-adjacent (it only throws on back-to-back duplicates), but a human/Gemini reviewer still notices "two sections look like the same idea" → checklist #14 = NO, −1 MINOR. The grade (99) is a clean PASS; no re-render was done (QCR-007/QCR-065/QCR-081: never re-render to chase a MINOR on a passing video).
- **Root cause:** (1) `assertVariety` is an ADJACENCY guard, not a global-uniqueness guard, so it cannot catch non-adjacent reuse. (2) The idea registry has exactly 6 mechanisms; videos with >6 motion beats are forced to repeat one.
- **Permanent fix / candidates (NOT shipped reactively on a 99 pass — verify before adopting, per QCR-065):**
 1. **Authoring rule (cheap, ship now):** keep text-bearing+graphics motion beats to **≤6 per video** so each uses a unique mechanism; when a 7th/8th beat is unavoidable, make the reused mechanism VISUALLY distinct (different icon set AND a different layout/orientation — e.g. processFlow horizontal vs a vertical step-stack) OR fold two adjacent same-theme beats into one. Documented here for the next run.
 2. **Candidate code fix (verify first):** add a 7th/8th distinct idea to `src/library/motionideas.tsx` (e.g. `IdeaBeforeAfter` split-compare, `IdeaStepStack` vertical checklist) so 7–8-beat scripts never reuse; and/or extend `assertVariety` to also `console.warn` (not throw) on ANY global duplicate so the author is nudged at render time. Both need a real QC≥75 render before adoption.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry); candidate edits in `src/library/motionideas.tsx` + `generate-motion-remotion` SKILL authoring note.
- **Verification:** a real run shipped at 99 PASS with this as the only deduction; next >6-beat run applies the ≤6 authoring rule (or distinct-reuse) and #14 should return to YES.

### QCR-089 (pipeline-friction) — HeyGen left nav is a HOVER flyout that intercepts the script-editor click (extends QCR-087); + QC runner `http` name collision
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 + Phase 10 — both caught/fixed live, cost no QC iteration).
- **Friction:**
 1. **HeyGen `/create-v4` left navigation OverlayDrawer (`role="dialog" aria-label="Navigation"`) expands on HOVER and intercepts pointer events on the Script editor**, which sits at the far-left edge (x≈20). A `page.mouse.click` near the editor's left re-triggered the drawer → click intercepted → only a 69-char partial insert (the QCR-087 first-contenteditable miss compounded). **Fix:** before filling, move the mouse to a neutral canvas spot (e.g. `page.mouse.move(650,450)` + wait ~1.2s) to collapse the drawer, then click the editor body at **x ≥ 175** (past the ~64px rail) — `page.mouse.click(Math.max(box.x+box.width/2,175), box.y+40)` — then `keyboard.insertText`. Landed the full 1117-char script. Documented as a QCR-087 addendum in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6.
 2. **QC runner Python:** a helper named `http` was shadowed by `import http.client` (the module rebinds the name) → `TypeError: 'module' object is not callable`. **Fix:** name the helper `httpget` (never reuse stdlib module names for locals). Noted for future QC-runner scripts.
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (QCR-087 hover-rail addendum), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run — after collapsing the drawer + clicking x≥175, full script inserted, single scene, no stray Scene 2; QC 99/100 (no Phase-3 defect).

### Run note — 4th full run on the 6-SECTION GRAMMAR. QC iter1 = 100/100, all 15 checks YES, FIXES NONE. Self-Improve below.

### QCR-090 — Gemini transcription TRUNCATES the JSON array when it over-segments (parse error at a fixed char offset) — FIXED with a salvage + compact prompt
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 — `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` failed TWICE with `json.JSONDecodeError` at ~char 27100 / line ~2185 on a 56.8s clip; SRT never written, pipeline blocked).
- **Root cause:** `gemini-2.5-flash` returned an over-granular (near word-level, 2000+ line) segment array and the JSON got cut off mid-object when it exceeded `maxOutputTokens: 16384` → invalid JSON → `json.loads(text)` raised and the whole transcription aborted (no fallback by design — local whisper forbidden).
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`):**
 1. Added `_loads_segments(text)` — tries a clean `json.loads`; on failure SALVAGES the longest valid prefix (trims to the last complete `}`, strips a trailing comma, closes the array with `]`, retries; walks back object-by-object until it parses) and logs `WARN: salvaged N segments from truncated JSON`. Also strips accidental ```fences.
 2. Bumped `maxOutputTokens` 16384 → 32768.
 3. Added a prompt line: "Keep the array COMPACT: at most ~60 segments for a one-minute clip." to discourage word-level over-segmentation.
- **Verification:** after the fix the same clip transcribed cleanly → 30 segments, SRT written, accents intact, Gemini x4. (QCR-074 degenerate-timestamp guard then redistributed proportionally as usual.) No re-block.

### QCR-091 (pipeline-friction) — writing multi-line PT-BR Python state-updaters via `bash cat <<'EOF'` heredoc is fragile; use the Write tool
- **Friction:** updating `pipeline-state.json` with a `python3 << 'PYEOF' ... PYEOF` heredoc twice produced a `SyntaxError: '{' was never closed` (the heredoc body was corrupted/truncated, once accompanied by unrelated leaked terminal output). Cost two retries per state update.
- **Root cause:** long heredocs carrying `{...}` dict literals + accented PT-BR strings + `->`/parentheses are brittle under this shell; a transient output-buffer leak compounded it.
- **Fix (process):** write the throwaway updater with the **Write tool** to an absolute `/tmp/...py` path, then `python3 /tmp/...py`. Never inline a multi-line Python dict via a bash heredoc for state updates. (Same spirit as the QCR rule against bash heredoc/echo for PT-BR script files — UTF-8 + braces don't survive the shell reliably.)
- **Verification:** all subsequent state updates this run used the Write-tool→python3 pattern and succeeded first try.

---

### Run note — 5th full run on the 6-SECTION GRAMMAR. QC iter1 = 100/100, all 15 checks YES, FIXES NONE. Self-Improve below.

### QCR-092 (pipeline-friction) — a low HeyGen duration estimate right after fill is the 429 `text_draft.save` rate-limit, NOT a partial fill; do NOT chase it by clearing/re-filling
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 — fill landed the FULL 946-char script, `editor.innerText.length`≈946 with the right head+tail, yet the scene/player estimate read **"6.8s est." / "00:06 est."** ≈ only the first sentence. Misread as a partial fill; I then ran select-all + Delete / Ctrl+A+Backspace / re-insert cycles — which fired a burst of **HTTP 429 on `api2.heygen.com/v1/text_draft.save`**, corrupted the editor state, and DROPPED the first sentence. Cost a full reload + several minutes.)
- **Root cause:** the duration estimate is computed SERVER-SIDE after `text_draft.save` succeeds. Rapid editor mutations (and rapid polling) rate-limit that endpoint (429), so the estimate is stuck at whatever last saved (often just the first auto-saved sentence) even though the contenteditable DOM already holds the full script. The low number is a STALE-ESTIMATE symptom, not a fill defect — and clearing/re-filling only makes it worse (more 429s + Lexical/Ctrl+A selecting the whole PAGE not the editor → partial deletes).
- **Permanent fix (process, documented in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6, QCR-092 bullet):** after a fill, TRUST `editor.innerText` head+tail+length as the source of truth for "did the script land", NOT the duration estimate. If the estimate looks implausibly low: do NOT clear/re-fill. Instead **reload the draft URL once** (`/create-v4/<draftId>?vt=l&panel=scene` — avatar/look/voice persist server-side; the editor comes back clean), do ONE clean `keyboard.insertText(fullScript)` into the empty editor, then **wait ~12s UNTOUCHED** (no polling) so `text_draft.save` succeeds and the server estimate settles. Only then read it — it will show the true ~Ns. Verified: reload → single insert → 12s wait → estimate settled at "01:09 est." (69s) for the full 936-char script; render came out 58s with the whole script spoken.
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (step 6 QCR-092 addendum), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run shipped QC 100/100; the avatar spoke the full script start ("Você sabia…") to end ("…Gemini Omni.").

- **Directive:** the young/glossy hero look (bright marker, sparkles, ONE big cartoon object) reads low-effort. The house style is premium/classic, multi-element (1–5) concepts, with generated IMAGES (via the image provider) instead of ready-made line icons.
- **Built + craved:** new style `premium-classic` (STYLE_GALLERY §3.05, status active) + library `src/library/premium.tsx` (exported from `../library`): `PremiumBg` (marble + gold double-rule frame), `PremiumLabel`/`PremiumDisplay` (Cormorant Garamond serif), `PremiumAsset` (refined fade-rise + `depth`), `PremiumGraph`, `PremiumImageIcon`, `PremiumFlow`. Per-element graphics = image-provider greenscreen-keyed IMAGES (premium objects + an optional icon set you generate once into `public/assets/icons/`), NOT `HD_ICONS`. Compose 2–5 elements/scene with hierarchy+depth; restrained motion.
- **Reference compositions (shipped):** `PremiumSectionRef.tsx` (the canonical premium grammar), `OpeningHookRef.tsx` + `PremiumOpeningDemo.tsx` (§1 opening), `SplitFlowDemo.tsx` / `FullFlowDemo.tsx` (§5/§6 graphics).
- **Skill:** `generate-image-assets` updated with the PREMIUM prompt style + the re-prompt gotcha (model sometimes puts the object on a studio/gray backdrop not green → re-prompt "ENTIRE background is one solid flat pure chroma green, nothing else"; hit on gear+cursor, fixed by regen).
- **QC note:** inject the §3.05 QC_GROUND_TRUTH_NOTE so the grader treats restrained slow motion + keyed-image elements as intentional premium taste, not low-effort/stock.


## QCR-093 (QC 100/100 first read — clean pass + resume friction)
**Finding:** None from Gemini — section-grammar QC card (QCR-085) + per-beat motion-title→concurrent-subtitle pairing (QCR-072) produced 100/100 on the first read for a named-tool how-to (AppDeploy in ChatGPT): 2 real ScreenshotCard evidence beats (appdeploy.ai hero circled + ChatGPT Apps Connect circled) + 6 distinct line-matched motion ideas + avatar zoom in/out/in.
**Pipeline friction (root cause → fix):**
1. Prior session left `pipeline-state.json` at `resume_from:5` while `<Name>_motion.mp4` already existed AND the comp `.tsx` (23:59) was NEWER than the render (23:57) — Phase 5 render finished but the state update + a follow-up tsx edit straddled a session end. RESOLUTION (correct): on resume, when the `.tsx` mtime > the `_motion.mp4` mtime, RE-RENDER (don't trust the stale mp4); when mp4 is newer, reuse it. This run re-rendered → identical-or-better, QC 100. Rule reaffirmed: motion phase must write the Phase-5 state entry immediately after the ffprobe gate, before any further .tsx touch.
2. macOS/zsh has no `timeout` binary — wrapping `npx remotion render` in `timeout 580` fails with "command not found". Use the Bash tool's own `timeout` param instead of the shell `timeout` command on this host.
**Verification:** Re-render gate PASS (h264 1080x1920 49.84s aac); subtitles `--section-grammar` 6/6; QC 100/100, FIXES=NONE.

### QCR-094: First PREMIUM-CLASSIC build passes QC 100/100 + Microsoft Scout stack SRT brand fixes
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 iter1, PASS)
- **Finding:** First end-to-end run on the ENFORCED-DEFAULT `premium-classic` style (house rule PIPELINE_DIRECTIVES §1). All 15 checklist items YES, FIXES TABLE NONE, on the FIRST read. Editorial marble+gold premium look (PremiumFrame/PremiumHeadline/PremiumEvidence/PremiumAsset/PremiumImageIcon/PremiumFlow) with real image-provider greenscreen-keyed brass objects (clock/calendar/envelope/padlock/bust) + 1 real PremiumEvidence screenshot (microsoft.com Scout announcement, gold circle on the hero). Confirms premium-classic is a QC-passing default, not just hand-drawn.
- **Root cause (QC ground truth):** the premium-classic QC card must (a) declare the editorial restrained-motion + real-image-provider-image style as BY DESIGN (so the grader doesn't penalize "too static"/"stock"), (b) describe the 6-section grammar's varying layout (split / full-frame-motion / full-frame-avatar), and (c) pair each premium motion title with its time-concurrent subtitle + shared-word count (QCR-072). (The deterministic `build_qc_ground_truth.py` now assembles this — QCR-286.)
- **Pipeline friction (root cause → permanent fix):**
 1. **SRT brand-name misses:** Gemini transcription rendered "OpenClaw" → "Open Claw" and "Work IQ" → "Work e Key"; not in BRAND_FIXES, hand-fixed in the SRT. FIX: added two BRAND_FIXES regexes to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` — `open[ -]?claw → OpenClaw` and `work (e key|i q|iq|e q) → Work IQ` (Work-prefixed, so generic text is safe). Future Scout/OpenClaw/Work IQ runs auto-correct.
 2. **Premium asset cohesion note (not a defect this run):** the reusable `robot_cut.png` is a glossy white/blue cartoon robot, off the antique-brass/marble premium family — it read clearly as "AI agent team" and QC passed 100, but a future premium run depicting an AI agent should generate a brass/marble automaton for full material cohesion. Logged, not blocking.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (BRAND_FIXES), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1, FIXES=NONE, all 15 YES; post-burn subtitles `--section-grammar` 6/6 0 failures 173px clearance; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 58.2s.


### QCR-095: Gemini transcriber TAIL-DROP — final spoken clause missing from SRT (a real run)
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3 — Gemini SRT had 29 entries ending at 59.9s while the audio was 65.08s; the final clause "e fazer o seu computador finalmente trabalhar por você" (~60–65s) was DROPPED → the closing CTA line would have shipped UNCAPTIONED). Caught manually by inspecting the last SRT entry vs duration; appended entry 30 by hand.
- **Finding:** `gemini-2.5-flash` sometimes omits the FINAL spoken clause from its segment array (and the QCR-090 salvage/compaction in `_loads_segments` can independently trim the tail when the JSON is truncated). The result: the last subtitle ends seconds before the audio, so the ending plays with no caption. QC didn't catch it (the burned video just lacked a caption at the very end — not a flagged defect), so it could ship silently.
- **Root cause:** No guard compared the last segment's end time against the audio duration; a multi-second tail gap went unnoticed.
- **Permanent fix (applied in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` `to_srt`):** after the QCR-074 degenerate-timestamp guard, added a TAIL-DROP guard — if `duration - last_segment_end > 2.5s`, emit a loud `*** WARN TAIL-DROP ... LISTEN to the tail and APPEND the missing SRT entry before burning. ***` to stderr. It cannot re-fabricate the missing words (would need a re-transcribe), but it stops the silent ship: the agent sees the warning and appends the final clause (as was done manually this run).
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run final SRT = 30 entries covering 0–64.8s; QC 100/100. Next run with a tail gap >2.5s prints the WARN so the closing line is never left uncaptioned.

### QCR-096: HeyGen v4 submission can SILENTLY FAIL (no processing card) — verify within ~5min, else re-submit + Avatar V exports native 1920 (QC 100/100 iter1)
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3). First Generate→Submit (draft d5f33322) showed the "Video submitted✌🏻" toast and the draft was consumed (`text_draft.get` 404), yet NO project EVER appeared on `/projects` — not as a finished card and not as a `% Ready` processing card — after 33 minutes of polling. It silently failed. A clean RE-SUBMIT (fresh draft b1ca8884) immediately showed a `38% Ready → 100%` processing card and finished in ~10min. So the absence of a processing card is the tell.
- **Finding:** A HeyGen v4 "Video submitted" toast is NOT proof the render started. When the submission silently fails, the projects grid shows NOTHING (the consumed draft just vanishes). A SUCCESSFUL submission shows a top-of-grid card with a live `NN% Ready` badge within ~1-3 minutes.
- **Root cause:** transient HeyGen backend drop between draft-submit and render-enqueue; no client-side error surfaced.
- **Permanent fix (process, documented in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 8, QCR-096 bullet):** after Submit, poll `/projects` and CONFIRM a `NN% Ready` processing card (or a fresh duration badge) appears at the top within ~3-5 minutes. If after ~5 min there is NO processing card AND no new finished card, treat the submission as FAILED → open a fresh "New video", re-setup (Portrait + **swap the default stock avatar to <AVATAR_LOOK>** — a blank v4 draft defaults to a stock avatar + Landscape), re-fill, re-Generate, re-Submit. Do NOT wait 30+ min on a submission that never showed a processing card.
- **Avatar V exports native 1080x1920** (no 1906→1920 pad needed, unlike Avatar IV). The blank `/create-v4/draft` defaults Motion Engine to Avatar V; ffprobe confirmed 1080x1920 directly. Pad step is a no-op then (harmless to run).
- **Pre-QC complement self-check WORKED:** while building the QC MOTION_LIST I counted the PARAMS display "1 milhão de tokens" against its concurrent subtitle "1 milhão de tokens de contexto" = **4 consecutive shared words** = a real MOTION_DUPLICATE; fixed BEFORE grading (display → "428 bilhões", 2-word match) + COST label "O custo despenca" → "O custo cai" (3→2). Reaffirms: always compute the shared-word count per beat BEFORE the QC call, not after a fail.
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (step 8), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run re-submit rendered in ~10min; QC 100/100 iter1, all 15 YES, FIXES NONE; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 58.56s.

### QCR-097 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-098: PREMIUM asset-SEMANTICS gate — `prem_stack.png` is a stack of COINS, never a "stack of documents" (concept-match the reused brass object to the spoken noun)
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 5 — caught at the frame-verification gate BEFORE QC, fixed, re-rendered, then QC 100/100 iter1)
- **Finding:** When reusing the brass greenscreen library for a premium-classic build, the CONTEXT step ("joga todos os DOCUMENTOS / quanto mais contexto") was composed with `assets/prem_stack.png` because its name reads "stack". But `prem_stack.png` is a stack of GOLD COINS (money), not papers — so the scene depicted money under a "documentos/contexto" line: a QCR-006 visual-metaphor mismatch (and coins read as off-topic for a prompting tutorial). The same coin stack was also wired as the "Contexto" node in the recap flow.
- **Root cause:** asset chosen by NAME ("stack") without LOOKing at what the image actually depicts. The brass library names (`prem_stack`, `prem_coin`, `coin_cut`, `prem_bust`, `mega_cut`…) describe the OBJECT, and several are money/coins — they do NOT map to arbitrary concepts.
- **Permanent fix (process, applied this run + recorded here):** in a premium-classic build, after composing each scene EXTRACT A FRAME and confirm every reused brass object actually DEPICTS the spoken noun — money objects (`prem_stack`/`prem_coin`/`coin_cut`) ONLY for cost/price/revenue/value beats; for "documents/context" use a CLUSTER of document icons (e.g. `assets/icons/doc.png`, generated once via `generate-image-assets`), for "the model/AI" use `prem_bust`, for "task/action" `cursor.png`, for "structure/format" `gear.png`, for "goal/vision" `eye.png`. The fix this run: CONTEXT scene → 3 offset `doc.png` icons (a documents pile); recap "Contexto" node → `doc.png` (and "Tarefa" → `cursor.png`). Asset→concept cheat-sheet now lives in this entry; consult it before reusing the brass library, OR generate a fresh topic-correct object via `generate-image-assets`.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this cheat-sheet); per-video comps must apply it. Asset bank `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent/public/assets/` is unchanged (the assets are fine; the MAPPING was wrong).
- **Verification:** a real run re-render showed 3 brass document icons under "ENTREGUE TUDO QUE IMPORTA" (correct) and an eye→cursor→doc→gear recap flow; QC 100/100 iter1, all 15 YES, FIXES NONE, checklist #14 (variety+match) = YES.

### QCR-099: BIG full-frame screenshots + THEME-DRIVEN premium palette (house rule)
- **First found:** a real run (user feedback: "screenshots should be way bigger — on mobile it's hard to read what's written; and define a different color concept for fonts+background per video theme")
- **Two changes, both now DEFAULT on every premium-classic run:**
 1. **Screenshots are FULL-FRAME & BIG.** `PremiumEvidence` (premium) and `EvidenceReveal` (hand-drawn fallback) now render full-frame by default — cardW = width−132 ≈ 948, image ≈ 60% of frame height (was a 624px in-panel card, illegible on a phone). **Section 2 is now a standalone full-frame `<Sequence>` SIBLING placed AFTER the frame** (like `FullFrameAvatar`), NOT a `HandDrawnFrame`/`PremiumFrame` child (a child is clipped to the top 40% panel). The frame behind renders continuously and carries the **avatar narration audio** under the overlay (avatar visually hidden for the proof beat — intended). Legacy premium comps that nested evidence in the frame were pinned to the old look with the new `split` prop (back-compat). Geometry mirrored in `reference-pipeline/prepare_reference_shot.py` (CARD_W 948 / CARD_H 1208 / BAR_H 56) + a rewritten `compute_focus` (general cover mapping: center-X crop + top-Y anchor, reports `visible`) so `--focus` still lands the circle on the real element.
 2. **Premium palette varies by VIDEO THEME.** `src/library/premium.tsx` now exposes `PREMIUM_PALETTES` (six concepts: `marble-gold` money/luxury [DEFAULT], `charcoal-gold` AI/tech, `midnight-azure` data/SaaS, `ivory-emerald` growth/health, `bordeaux-rose` luxury/lifestyle, `slate-copper` engineering/crypto) + a `PremiumTheme` context provider + `usePremPalette` hook. Every premium component reads the palette from context (background + serif + accents recolor coherently; dark themes flip ink light). The creative brief now carries `premium_palette` (chosen by theme in `choose-creative-direction`); the motion phase wraps the comp root in `<PremiumTheme palette={brief.premium_palette}>`. Missing/unknown → `marble-gold`.
- **Permanent fix (deployed):** `premium.tsx` (palette context + big full-frame `PremiumEvidence`), `sections.tsx` (`EvidenceReveal` full-frame + `PaperBg`), `prepare_reference_shot.py` (geometry + cover math), `index.ts` (exports), skills `choose-creative-direction` (theme→palette table + `premium_palette` field) + `generate-motion-remotion` (PremiumTheme wrap + full-frame sibling evidence), `SECTION_PIPELINE.md` (Section 2 = full-frame sibling). Reference wiring: `PremiumOpeningDemo.tsx` (theme + full-frame evidence), `SectionDemo.tsx` (hand-drawn full-frame evidence). Verified renders: premium charcoal-gold headline + full-frame ClaudeCodePage evidence; hand-drawn full-frame Remotion evidence (headline "Make videos programmatically" large + legible, circle on the main element).
- **Apply every run:** premium Section 2 is a full-frame sibling overlay (omit `split`); set `premium_palette` from the topic theme; run `prepare_reference_shot.py --focus` to get the circle coords for the big card.

### QCR-100: Premium font → Proxima-style caps · no kicker tag · LITERAL images · MOVING elements (house rule)
- **First found:** a real run (user feedback: "default font → Proxima Nova caps lock instead of the premium serif; remove the little tag above the headline; make every image actually match the specific narrated text, not an adjacent/figurative relationship; prioritize MOVING graphical elements, not static or pulsing")
- **Four changes, now DEFAULT on every premium-classic run:**
 1. **Font = Proxima-Nova-style geometric sans, ALL CAPS** (was Cormorant Garamond serif). Proxima Nova is a paid Adobe font with no installable file here, so the pipeline uses **Montserrat** (its standard free twin, near-identical letterforms) via `@remotion/google-fonts/Montserrat`, weight 800, uppercase across headline/display/caption/stamp/labels. `premium.tsx` exports `PROXIMA` (and a back-compat `SERIF = PROXIMA` alias). To use a LICENSED Proxima Nova: drop the .otf/.ttf in `public/fonts/` and load via `@remotion/fonts loadFont({family:"Proxima Nova"})`.
 2. **Kicker tag REMOVED above the Section-1 headline** — both `PremiumHeadline` (premium) and `HeadlineHook` (hand-drawn) no longer render the little label chip; headline phrase only. `kicker` prop kept optional for back-compat (ignored).
 3. **LITERAL IMAGE-SEMANTICS (HARD rule 3 in generate-motion + generate-image-assets).** Every image/icon/object must depict the LITERAL noun/action spoken AT THAT MOMENT — never an adjacent/figurative stand-in (coin for "documents", robot for "coding", rocket for "fast"). New **LITERAL-IMAGE GATE** at the motion frame-verification step: read the window's exact line, confirm the image shows that noun, else swap/generate and re-render. Reinforces QCR-006/QCR-098.
 4. **MOVING ELEMENTS, not static/pulse (HARD rule 4).** Each motion beat must contain ≥1 element in continuous purposeful TRANSLATION (travel along a path / flow / orbit / cascade / count-up / sweep) — a fade-in-then-hold or breathe/pulse-only beat is a DEFECT. New **MOVING-ELEMENTS GATE**: extract ≥2 frames/beat at different times and confirm an element actually moved (not just opacity/scale). Concrete primitive added: `PremiumFlow` now has a gold pulse that TRAVELS along each connector on a loop; `PremiumAsset` directional drift stands. Still NO bouncy AI-slop springs — movement is smooth + purposeful, but it MUST move.
- **Deployed in:** `src/library/premium.tsx` (Montserrat/PROXIMA, caps, kicker removed from `PremiumHeadline`, traveling pulse in `PremiumFlow`), `src/library/sections.tsx` (kicker removed from `HeadlineHook`), skills `generate-motion-remotion` (rules 3+4 + two new gates) + `generate-image-assets` (literal-depiction rule), `MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`. Verified render: charcoal-gold headline in bold all-caps Montserrat with NO kicker tag.
- **Apply every run:** premium type is ALL CAPS Montserrat; no kicker above the headline; pick/generate images by the LITERAL spoken noun; every motion beat has a genuinely moving element.

### QCR-101: Charcoal-gold premium + two-evidence WARNING video passes QC 100/100 first try; Gemini-SRT degenerate-timestamp guard recurring
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 PASS iter1)
- **Finding (validated approach — no defect):** A WARNING-shape topic (China grey-market selling Claude/GPT API keys at 97% off via stolen accounts) built in `premium-classic` with the **`charcoal-gold`** theme palette (DARK), TWO real `PremiumEvidence` screenshots (claude.com = the product sold cheap + anthropic.com/news real article "...832 accounts banned for malicious cyber activity" = the enforcement proof), and literal-object brass scenes (coin→key market, padlock stolen, doc→eye logged, doc+key+lock harvest, coin+graph price) scored 100/100 with all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE. Confirms: (a) per-theme palette variety WITHIN premium (charcoal-gold vs the prior run's marble-gold) is a clean feed-freshness lever — Gemini did NOT flag the dark bg as "too dark/low contrast" once the REFERENCE CARD declared it intentional; (b) literal-object LABELS that echo the spoken noun at ≤3 consecutive ("chave de API", "seu código", "suas chaves", "servidor deles", "fica registrado") are SANCTIONED complements (QCR-015) and did NOT draw MOTION_DUPLICATE under the ground-truth injection; (c) a SECOND evidence beat (Anthropic article) placed on the exact "Anthropic derrubou as contas" line is a high-credibility move — finding the real on-message article (not the generic homepage) is worth the extra Playwright navigation.
- **Genuine friction this run (recurring):** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` again returned DEGENERATE timestamps (all 26 segments clustered in a 1.35s span << 67.5s audio); the script's existing guard redistributed them proportionally by text length. The redistributed SRT covered the full duration (ends 1:07.5 vs 67.56s, no tail-drop) and PASSED QC, but timing is approximate (not word-accurate). This guard has now fired on multiple runs — it is RELIABLE as a fallback but the root Gemini timestamps remain unusable. Acceptable as-is (premium scenes are time-boxed by the agent from the SRT text order, not by exact word timing; subtitles re-split downstream). No source change required, but logged so a future run knows the proportional-redistribution path is the norm for this transcriber, not an anomaly.
- **Machinery learning (reference capture):** `prepare_reference_shot.py --focus` maps the raw-capture pixel into the full-frame card geometry; a LEFT-EDGE element (e.g. a top-left logo at x≈119) maps to a NEGATIVE fx ("NOT VISIBLE in card") — target a CENTERED main element instead (the right-card Claude wordmark mapped cleanly to fx 852). The "NOT VISIBLE" warning worked as designed and caught it pre-render.
- **Affected files:** none required (validated approach + recurring-friction log). `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` only.
- **Verification:** next premium WARNING/AI-security topic can reuse `charcoal-gold` + two-evidence pattern; expect the Gemini-SRT guard to fire and redistribute — that is normal, not a failure.

### QCR-101: Evidence screenshot → SPLIT, in the UPPER panel (corrects QCR-099's full-frame) — house rule
- **First found:** a real run (user feedback: "the screenshots are not fitting in the screen. the important parts of screenshots should be visible in the upper part of the splitter screen")
- **What was wrong:** QCR-099 made `PremiumEvidence`/`EvidenceReveal` FULL-FRAME with `objectFit:cover` — which CROPPED content horizontally (center-crop cut the sides of a landscape page) and removed the avatar split. The important part was getting cut off ("not fitting").
- **Fix (now DEFAULT):** evidence is a **SPLIT card in the UPPER (top-40%) panel**, rendered as a **CHILD of `HandDrawnFrame`/`PremiumFrame`** (avatar shows in the bottom 60% and carries the audio). The card fills the panel WIDTH (CARD_W 1008) and the screenshot is shown **WIDTH-BOUND + top-anchored** (CSS `width:100%`, `height:auto`, top-clipped) — so NOTHING is cropped horizontally; the important top-of-page content is always fully visible and ~1.6× larger than the original small card. Only the bottom of the page is clipped (least important).
- **Geometry contract:** `prepare_reference_shot.py` updated to CARD_W 1008 / CARD_H 594 / BAR_H 46 with a WIDTH-BOUND `compute_focus` (scale = card_w/prepW, no horizontal offset; `visible:false` flags an element below the ~696px top window → recapture scrolled/tighter). Verified: hero/headline elements map exact and `visible:true`; below-fold elements correctly flagged.
- **Deployed in:** `src/library/premium.tsx` (`PremiumEvidence` split top-panel, stamp right-anchored nowrap), `src/library/sections.tsx` (`EvidenceReveal` split top-panel, PaperBg removed — it's a frame child), `prepare_reference_shot.py`, demos `PremiumOpeningDemo.tsx` + `SectionDemo.tsx` (evidence reverted to frame CHILD), docs `generate-motion-remotion` + `SECTION_PIPELINE.md` + `STYLE_GALLERY.md` + `CLAUDE.md`. Verified renders (PremiumOpeningDemo frame 145 + SectionDemo frame 125): screenshot in the upper panel, hero fully visible + circled, avatar below, stamp/caption clean.
- **Supersedes QCR-099's "full-frame sibling overlay"** — evidence is SPLIT (upper panel), not full-frame.

### QCR-102: Premium-classic MIDNIGHT-AZURE + two-evidence DEBUNK/myth-bust video passes QC 100/100 first try
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 PASS iter1, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE)
- **Finding (validated approach — no defect):** A MYTH-BUST / listicle-debunk topic ("5 códigos secretos do Claude" virais no TikTok — /ghost, /artifacts, UDA, L99, /godmode — honest take: most are marketing, Artifacts is the one that's REAL) built in `premium-classic` with the **`midnight-azure`** theme palette (DARK navy, AI/tools/trust), TWO real `PremiumEvidence` screenshots BOTH on claude.com — (1) the Claude product hero ("Think fast, build faster", circle on the Claude wordmark) and (2) claude.com/blog/artifacts, the REAL official "Artifacts are now generally available" page (circle on the headline, proving Artifacts is real/shipped) — plus literal-object brass scenes (cursor→key codes flow + enumerated code list, brass PADLOCK "Não existe" for the fake-command beat, keyed dark-glass APP/DASHBOARD window for the Artifacts-builds-app beat, brass BUST + document "Dê contexto" for the expert beat, cursor→gear secret flow) scored 100/100 first try. Confirms (extends QCR-101): (a) `midnight-azure` is a clean third premium palette for AI/tools/trust topics (after marble-gold, charcoal-gold) — Gemini did NOT flag the dark navy bg as "too dark" once the REFERENCE CARD declared it intentional; (b) a debunk/myth-bust angle pairs naturally with the two-evidence move — the FIRST evidence shows the thing being questioned (the codes' claimed home), the SECOND proves the one real feature (Artifacts GA page), which is a strong credibility arc; (c) enumerated tool/code NAMES shown as a distilled "/ghost · /artifacts · UDA / L99 · /godmode" list are keyword complements, never MOTION_DUPLICATE, under the ground-truth injection; (d) two motion titles that exactly echo a 3-word narration phrase ("secretos do Claude" S0 headline; "comando mágico escondido" rhetorical label) at EXACTLY 3 consecutive shared words are SANCTIONED (≤3, QCR-015/020) and pre-annotated as such in the MOTION_LIST — they passed clean.
- **Pre-QC complement self-check WORKED again:** computed the max consecutive-shared-word count for every motion beat against its time-concurrent subtitle BEFORE the QC call; flagged the two boundary 3-word labels as sanctioned-≤3 in the ground truth rather than leaving them for Gemini to mis-flag. Zero MOTION_DUPLICATE phantoms resulted. Reaffirms QCR-096: always compute the pairing pre-call.
- **Minor friction (already-documented, no new fix):** Phase 3 HeyGen fill hit the `text_draft.save` 429 stale-estimate again (state note: char-by-char `keyboard.type` workaround). This is the QCR-092 family — the canonical fix (reload draft URL → ONE clean `insertText` → wait ~12s untouched, trust `editor.innerText` not the estimate) remains the recommended path; the char-by-char type is an acceptable alternate but slower. No source change needed.
- **Affected files:** none required (validated approach + recurring-friction log). `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` only.
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 66.77s; next premium AI-tools/myth-bust topic can reuse `midnight-azure` + two-evidence (product page + feature-announcement page) pattern.

## QCR-103 — Phase 3 HeyGen: for LONG scripts the `insertText` save genuinely fails (only first sentence persists) — char-by-char `keyboard.type` + blur + reload-VERIFY is the reliable path
- **Finding:** On a ~1110-char PT-BR script, `page.keyboard.insertText(fullScript)` showed the full text in `editor.innerText` (1115 chars) but the duration estimate stayed stuck at "11.2s" (≈ first sentence). Reloading the draft URL proved only **167 chars (the first sentence) actually persisted server-side** — and this repeated even after the QCR-092 canonical path (reload → one clean `insertText` → wait ~12s untouched). `text_draft.save` 429-rate-limits the bulk insert and never commits the remainder.
- **Root cause:** A single bulk `insertText`/paste fires one debounced `text_draft.save` that gets 429'd; Lexical does not retry, so the server keeps only the first auto-saved sentence. `editor.innerText` (client DOM) is NOT proof of persistence — the earlier QCR-092 "trust innerText, don't re-fill" guidance is INSUFFICIENT for long scripts because the render reads the server draft, not the DOM.
- **Fix that worked (use for any script > ~1 paragraph):** (1) focus editor, clear via DOM range (`selectNodeContents`+`Delete`, NOT Ctrl+A which selects the page), (2) `page.keyboard.type(script, {delay: 7})` — char-by-char fires incremental input events that each commit, (3) click a neutral spot to BLUR (forces a final save), (4) **reload the draft URL and VERIFY `editor.innerText.length ≈ script length AND the duration estimate reflects the full script (~Ns)** before Generate. The Generate dialog credit cost (e.g. "23 credits" for ~78s) is a second confirmation the full script committed.
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry). Recommend updating `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6 to make char-by-char `keyboard.type` + reload-verify the PRIMARY path for long scripts (insertText only for short one-liners).
- **Verification:** a real run — full 1115-char script persisted after char-by-char type + reload (estimate settled to 01:18), render 66.48s, QC 100/100 iter1.

## QCR-104 — Phase 11 must RE-CHECK for a concurrent same-topic post right before publishing (parallel-run race)
- **Finding:** A PARALLEL session ran the same pipeline on the same topic and posted+logged a real run at 15:34 (pipeline-log.csv row 86) ~8 min before this run posted its own copy at 15:42 → 8 near-identical posts (2 full sets) queued to every account. The Phase-1 dedup passed for BOTH because neither had been logged yet when each run selected its topic; the duplicate only became visible at Phase 11.
- **Root cause:** Dedup happens ONLY at topic selection. A run that takes 30-60 min between Phase 1 and Phase 11 can be lapped by a parallel run that logs the same topic in between. Nothing re-checked the log/scheduler at post time.
- **Fix (resolved this run):** Detected via the mandatory read-back (saw two 15:34 + 15:42 sets), confirmed row 86 owns the topic (matching ids), DELETED my 4 duplicate posts (HTTP 200, verified gone), and did NOT append a duplicate log row.
- **Permanent fix to apply:** `post-and-log` (Phase 11) should, BEFORE the first POST, (a) re-grep pipeline-log.csv for the same script_topic/source-URL, and (b) GET today's scheduler and check for a post whose text covers the same topic. If found → SKIP posting (topic already live), do not double-post, do not double-log. Add this guard to `how-to-post-videos.md` / the post-and-log skill.
- **Verification:** 15:42 duplicates deleted (scheduler shows NONE); canonical 15:34 set intact (4/4 draft:false autoPublish:true). No duplicate log row added.

### QCR-102: Higher safe margins (text was going out of frame when posted) + headline sits LOWER (house rule)
- **First found:** a real run (user feedback: "several written elements are getting out of frame when posted — need a higher margin distancing text from borders; and the 1st headline frame should have the headline a bit lower, closer to the split-screen line")
- **Two changes, now DEFAULT:**
 1. **Wider safe margins on every written element** (platforms crop the edges + overlay UI when posted): premium headline padding 80→**116px** sides; `PremiumLabel`/`PremiumDisplay`/`PremiumEvidence` caption → **90–96px** sides (`boxSizing:border-box`); hand-drawn `FullMotionFrame` padding was asymmetric 120/70 → **symmetric 110/110 + 96 bottom**; `AvatarMotionFrame` 110/60 → **100/100**; **subtitles** MarginL/MarginR 70→**96** (motion) and 80→**96** (standalone), `USABLE_TEXT_WIDTH` 940→**888** (so wrapping happens earlier). Long text now WRAPS to stay in-frame instead of overflowing.
 2. **Section-1 headline sits LOW, near the split line.** `PremiumHeadline` + `HeadlineHook` (in-panel) changed from vertically-centered to `justifyContent:flex-end` with a ~64–96px bottom pad — the headline now sits in the lower part of the top-40% panel, just above the split line (the full-frame title-card variant of `HeadlineHook` stays centered).
- **Deployed in:** `src/library/premium.tsx` (`PremiumHeadline` flex-end + 116px; `PremiumLabel`/`PremiumDisplay`/caption padding), `src/library/sections.tsx` (`HeadlineHook` flex-end in-panel; `FullMotionFrame`/`AvatarMotionFrame` symmetric margins), `subtitle-pipeline/srt_to_ass.py` + `templates/v6_final.ass` (96px margins, USABLE_TEXT_WIDTH 888). Verified renders: premium + hand-drawn headlines sit low near the split line with text comfortably inset from the borders.
- **Apply every run:** keep all written elements inside a ≥96px horizontal safe margin (≈9% each side); Section-1 headline sits low near the split line, not vertically centered.

### QCR-105: Premium-classic CHARCOAL-GOLD honest-take/myth-bust passes QC 100/100 first try — and "reuse the brass library" beats "fall back to hand-drawn" when premium asset-gen can't run
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 PASS iter1, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE)
- **Finding (validated approach — no defect):** An HONEST-TAKE / myth-bust topic (Gemini 3.5 Flash @ Google I/O 2026 — REAL = 4x faster + beats last year's Pro on code/agents + now the default in the Gemini app & Google Search; MYTH = "cheaper", the per-use cost TRIPLED vs the old Flash) built in `premium-classic` with the **`charcoal-gold`** palette, TWO real `PremiumEvidence` screenshots — (1) deepmind.google official Gemini 3.5 Flash model page ("Best for frontier performance across agents and coding" = proves the REAL speed/code claim) and (2) blog.google "Gemini 3.5: frontier intelligence with action" announcement (= proves it launched/is the default) — plus literal brass scenes (cursor→gear speed flow, TWO marble busts small-beats-big graphics-only, brass app+eye "já é o padrão", brass coin + rising gold graph "TRIPLICOU", hourglass→gear verdict) scored 100/100 first read. Extends QCR-101/102: `charcoal-gold` is a clean palette for an AI honest-take; a two-evidence arc where evidence #1 proves the REAL half and evidence #2 proves the launch/default is a strong credibility structure for a myth-bust; a GRAPHICS-ONLY beat (two differently-sized busts = small model beats big model) reads clearly with NO baked text once the REFERENCE CARD declares it by-design.
- **Process insight (the real Self-Improve lever this run):** the prior session's Stage-4 `pipeline-state.json` note recorded "IMAGE ASSET-GEN ABORTED (provider error) → AUTO-FALLBACK to hand-drawn-annotation". On resume, the correct move was NOT to drop to hand-drawn: the antique-brass premium library (`public/assets/` + `public/assets/icons/`: coin/bust/app/lock/mega + cursor/gear/eye/hourglass/key/doc) ALREADY existed from prior premium runs (your own library accumulates the same way — the this edition ships only the `public/assets/sample/` placeholders) and semantically COVERED every literal noun this topic needs (código=cursor, agente=gear, modelo=bust, app=prem_app_cut, busca=eye, custo=prem_coin, rápido=hourglass). Per QCR-098 you REUSE the existing keyed brass objects (LOOK at each, match it to the spoken noun) — so the video stayed on the ENFORCED-DEFAULT premium-classic with ZERO image-provider calls and passed 100. **Rule:** the hand-drawn fallback (PIPELINE_DIRECTIVES §1) is for when premium asset-gen can't run AND the existing brass library can't depict the topic — NOT a reflex whenever image-provider is unavailable. Check `public/assets/` for a semantic match FIRST; only fall back to hand-drawn if the topic genuinely needs an object the library lacks and can't be generated.
- **Pre-QC complement self-check WORKED again:** computed max consecutive shared-word count per beat vs the time-concurrent subtitle BEFORE the QC call; pre-annotated the one boundary case (evidence-1 caption "GEMINI 3.5 FLASH" = 3 consecutive vs the concurrent "O Gemini 3.5 Flash que o Google lançou" → sanctioned ≤3 evidence-card product label, QCR-015/101). Zero MOTION_DUPLICATE phantoms resulted.
- **Affected files:** none required (validated approach + process insight). `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` only.
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 63.9s; subtitles `--section-grammar` PASS 0 failures 173px clearance; next premium AI honest-take can reuse `charcoal-gold` + two-evidence (model page + announcement page) + reuse-brass-library-before-fallback.

### QCR-103: Animated screenshots protocol (scroll default · zoom on 2nd · clean full-page capture) — house rule
- **First found:** a real run (user feedback: "implement [scroll] as protocol instead of pure screenshot; exception only if there's no relevant info below the hero or you want to show something very specific; on the 2nd screenshot apply aggressive zoom from the just-opened page to a focused zoom, no scroll; keep the whole structure the same")
- **Now DEFAULT — the evidence screenshot is ANIMATED, not a static image:**
 - **`motion="scroll"` (DEFAULT)** — the page scrolls down inside the card window over the beat (eased). The fixed circle/arrow auto-hide (the scroll IS the highlight). Requires a full-page capture so there's content below the hero. Pass `scroll={px}` (from `prepare_reference_shot.py`'s `[motion]` line) + `durFrames={F(beat)}`.
 - **`motion="zoom"` — the 2ND evidence beat** (and any "show this exact detail"): aggressive push from the just-opened page INTO the `focus` element (no scroll), `zoomTo≈2.3–2.6`. The circle tracks the element as it enlarges.
 - **`motion="static"` — EXCEPTION** only: nothing relevant below the hero, or a fixed single proof (logo/one number). Keeps the classic draw-on circle + arrow.
 - Cadence: multiple beats → 1st scroll, 2nd zoom, more = vary.
- **Capture protocol (cookie-cleanup + full-page):** `capture-references` step 2 now, BEFORE every screenshot, (a) clicks a cookie-accept button (Accept/Aceitar/Agree/Got it/OK/Allow all) and (b) removes consent widgets + every `position:fixed`/`sticky` overlay (cookie bars, sticky nav, chat bubbles), then captures `fullPage:true`. This killed the "Cookie settings" box that was polluting captures. Viewport capture is the exception (zoom/static beats, logos).
- **Structure unchanged:** split card (screenshot upper / avatar below), kicker, stamp, caption, gold chrome, the 6-section grammar — all identical; only the image inside the card now moves.
- **Implemented in:** `src/library/premium.tsx` (`PremiumEvidence` — inner transformed layer; circle moved into the layer so it tracks the element on zoom), `src/library/handdrawn.tsx` (`ScreenshotCard` — optional motion, back-compat static default), `src/library/sections.tsx` (`EvidenceReveal` — motion props, circle/arrow gated to static), `reference-pipeline/prepare_reference_shot.py` (scroll recommendation), skills `capture-references` + `generate-motion-remotion`, `SECTION_PIPELINE.md`, `STYLE_GALLERY.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. Verified (PremiumOpeningDemo): scroll travels hero→features (frames 108→188); zoom pushes full-page→aggressive focus with the circle on the element (frames 206→288).

### QCR-103b: Zoom must target the MAIN ELEMENT center + clamp to image (was zooming into blank space) — house rule
- **Found:** (user, on the test video: "the zoom was pointed at the wrong place, a blank space; identify the center of the main element and zoom it; it can be much more subtle").
- **Two bugs fixed:**
 1. **Wrong focus (eyeballed).** The demo used `focus={fx:330,fy:250}` guessed by eye → it landed in the blank gap between the hero headline and the graphic. FIX: the zoom `focus`/`focusR` MUST come from `prepare_reference_shot.py --focus "sx,sy"` on the main element's CENTER pixel (read off a coordinate-grid crop of the capture). For this page the hero "Meet your thinking partner" center = raw (200,365) size 290×200 → `focus={fx:159,fy:337} focusR={rx:138,ry:96}`.
 2. **No clamp → blank edges.** Centering a near-edge element pushed image content off-frame, exposing white. FIX: the zoom now centers the element but CLAMPS the visible region into the image (`rx0∈[0,cardW-cardW/z]`, `ry0≥0`), so a near-edge element ends as-centered-as-possible and never shows blank.
- **Subtler by default:** `zoomTo` default 2.3 → **1.6** (demo uses 1.5). Range 1.4–1.7.
- **Deployed:** `premium.tsx` (`PremiumEvidence` zoom clamp + default 1.6), `handdrawn.tsx` (`ScreenshotCard` zoom clamp + 1.6), `PremiumOpeningDemo.tsx` (real focus + zoomTo 1.5), skills `generate-motion-remotion` + `capture-references` (focus-from-`--focus` MANDATORY, subtle zoom). Verified: zoom starts on the full opened page and subtly lands on the headline with the circle on it, no blank space. Test video re-rendered → `<downloads>/screenshot_motion_test.mp4`.

### QCR-106: Hand-drawn fallback for a VIDEO-tool topic passes QC 100/100 first try + a source fix (validator motion-box false-positive)
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 PASS iter1, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE)
- **Finding (validated approach — no defect):** A how-to/honest-take topic (connect an AI-video service via an MCP connector to Claude → Claude generates AI video in chat; REAL = 30+ models, 4K, no install; MYTH = not magic ecommerce money, paid credits + 15s clips) built in **hand-drawn-annotation (the FALLBACK, §1)** with the 6-section grammar, TWO real annotated ScreenshotCards (EvidenceReveal #1 SCROLL through the tool's product page; #2 ZOOM into claude.com "Think fast, build faster"), and DISTINCT line-matched motion ideas (processFlow cursor→gear→check connector, cursor spotlight, 30+ statBurst with Sora·Veo·Kling, eye spotlight honest-pivot, coin gridRepeat debunk, 15s statBurst, coin/bolt/loader cycleLoop, play radial) scored 100 first read. **Fallback-decision rule reaffirmed (extends QCR-105):** premium-classic was intended (bordeaux-rose) but the brass asset library has NO video/film/play hero object and this topic's CENTRAL noun is *video*, AND image asset-gen was unavailable — BOTH §1 fallback conditions met, so hand-drawn was correct (HD_ICONS has `play` = literal video; the two real ScreenshotCards carry credibility). Hand-drawn remains a clean QC-100 path for a named-tool how-to.
- **Source fix — `validate_subtitles.py --section-grammar` false-positived on motion-graphic ICON BOXES:** at a full-frame motion beat (the cursor→gear→check flow-diagram white boxes at px ~1039-1183), the frame detector picked the icon boxes as the "lowest cluster" and flagged a danger-zone subtitle breach — but there was NO burned subtitle in that frame (a 0.4s SRT gap). Real subtitles ALWAYS burn at MarginV 280 (bottom ~1640). FIX: added `SECTION_GRAMMAR`/`SUBTITLE_BAND_FLOOR=1430` — in `--section-grammar` mode `analyze_frame_pillow` now drops clusters whose BOTTOM edge < 1430 (those are motion graphics, not the subtitle). Verified: the same `_final.mp4` re-validated → VERDICT PASS, 0 placement failures (was 1 FAIL at t=14.7s). Extends QCR-084 (the section-grammar validator must ignore the motion region, not just lower the face threshold).
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/validate_subtitles.py` (SECTION_GRAMMAR band floor), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 69.76s; the source fix unit-verified.

### QCR-104: NEW section "3b — Kinetic Caption" + ~2s section pacing (house rule)
- **Added to the grammar:** a new full-frame section **3b**, RIGHT AFTER the full-frame avatar (section 3): a solid **theme-PALETTE-color** background with the narration popping up **2–3 words at a time, BIG + centered**, in the Proxima-style sans (`PROXIMA`/Montserrat, ALL CAPS), each group entering with a **subtle fade-up** (opacity + 28px rise + slight scale, ease-out ~7 frames). No avatar, no screenshot — a kinetic breather. Component `PremiumKineticCaption` (src/library/premium.tsx, exported): `text` (auto-split by `wordsPerGroup`, default 2) or explicit `groups[]`, `durFrames`, `size` (132), `bg`/`color`/`accent` overrides. Background = the video's `premium_palette`; it's an OVERLAY sibling (frame behind keeps the audio).
- **New canonical order:** 1 → 2 → 3 → **3b** → 4 → 5 → 6, then repeat 3 → 3b → 4 → 5 → 6.
- **PACING:** sections are now **~2 seconds each** (was 3–4s) — snappier; real boundaries still snap to the SRT sentence timings.
- **Deployed:** `premium.tsx` (`PremiumKineticCaption`), `index.ts` (export), `PremiumOpeningDemo.tsx` (rebuilt: 2s sections + full-frame avatar + caption), `Root.tsx` (10s), docs `SECTION_PIPELINE.md` + `generate-motion-remotion` + `CLAUDE.md`. Verified: caption renders the line "É de graça e roda sozinho" as "É DE → GRAÇA E → RODA SOZINHO", big centered Montserrat caps on the charcoal-gold bg with the fade-up. Test video re-rendered → `<downloads>/screenshot_motion_test.mp4` (now 10s, includes the new section).

- **Run:** FIRST end-to-end pipeline run on the new template (animated screenshots scroll+zoom, kinetic-caption section 3b, ~2s pacing, theme palette charcoal-gold, Proxima/Montserrat). HeyGen <AVATAR_LOOK> (no 2FA), full pipeline → QC 95/100 (15/15 checklist YES except a #10 false-positive: subtitles on full-frame sections read as "below 1700" though MarginV 280 → bottom ~1640px, above the danger zone; validate_subtitles top-edge 1484 PASS; -5 minor). 95 ≥ 75 PASS, Gemini final authority.

 2. **Headline-vs-hook duplicate caught pre-QC:** the S1 headline initially echoed the hook subtitle ("o Claude agora trabalha sozinho" = 5 consecutive) → changed to a distilled complement ("Ele faz tudo / no automático", 0 shared). ### QCR-108: An honest-take/myth-bust (Fable 5 banned by US gov) passes QC 100/100 first try — + two transcription brand-fix gaps closed
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 100/100 PASS iter1, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE)
- **Finding (validated approach — no defect):** A myth-bust / honest-take topic — the viral TikTok claim "alguém criou o GTA 6 em 10 horas com o Claude e o governo dos EUA mandou tirar do ar" — is in large part TRUE (verified via WebSearch: Anthropic released Claude Fable 5, its most powerful public Mythos-class model, and the US gov forced it OFFLINE within days via an export-control directive; Composio really connects Claude to 1000+ apps). The HYPE half ("GTA 6 in 10h", "hacks every browser") is the embellishment. Built in `premium-classic` / **charcoal-gold** with the two-evidence move — (1) the REAL anthropic.com/news/claude-fable-5-mythos-5 announcement (SCROLL) proving Fable 5 is real, (2) the REAL infoq.com "Anthropic Releases and Temporarily Suspends Claude Fable 5" headline (ZOOM) proving the shutdown — plus brass-library reuse (lock=offline, bust=model, mega=hype, app+envelope=Composio/Gmail, gear=power) and two kinetic captions, scored 100 first read. **Confirms (extends QCR-105): the announce→suspension two-evidence arc is a strong myth-bust credibility structure; charcoal-gold is the right palette for an AI/power/dramatic-news story; brass-library reuse (NO image-provider call) kept it on premium-classic and passed 100.**
- **Source fixes (Self-Improve, permanent):** Gemini transcription mis-heard two brands the existing BRAND_FIXES missed → `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` gained (1) `classe mitos -> classe Mythos` + `mitos(?= liberad/públic/class) -> Mythos` (the existing `Mito(?=\d|cinco)` rule only caught singular "Mito 5", not plural "classe mitos"); (2) `comp[óôo]sio -> Composio`. Unit-verified. Also: the Gemini transcribe degenerate-timestamp guard word-fragmented the FIRST sentence (split "GTA6"→"GT"/"A6", 19 one-word entries) — rebuilt the SRT cleanly from the known script (proportional timing; srt_to_ass re-splits downstream). Future: consider having the guard merge sub-0.5s adjacent word fragments back into phrase-level entries before redistribution.
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 58.56s; subtitles placement PASS (top-edge 1484) + post-burn PASS 6/6 (173px clearance); brand-fix regexes unit-tested.

### QCR-109: Gemini transcription degenerate timestamps can ALSO mis-ORDER segments — rebuild SRT from the verbatim script when one exists
- **Finding:** Phase-3 `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` hit its degenerate-timestamp guard ("span 1.37s << 69s -> redistributed proportionally by text length") on BOTH attempts. Worse than the timing being approximate, Gemini returned the SEGMENTS OUT OF ORDER — the clause "de quatro programadores de inteligência artificial" landed AFTER "enquanto você dorme?" instead of before "dentro do Claude Code". The proportional redistribution preserves Gemini's (wrong) order, so the burned captions would have been out of sequence vs the spoken narration.
- **Root cause:** the guard redistributes by text length but trusts Gemini's segment ORDER; there is no real timing/ordering info when timestamps are degenerate. The avatar, however, speaks the EXACT Phase-2 script in order — that script is ground truth.
- **Permanent fix (source):** added `subtitle-pipeline/build_srt_from_script.py` — splits the known script into ≤8-word phrase chunks at sentence/comma boundaries and distributes proportionally by char length over the ffprobe duration, guaranteeing correct ORDER + exact TEXT. When the avatar speaks a verbatim script and the transcribe guard reports degenerate timestamps, build the SRT from the script with this tool instead of trusting Gemini's order. Verified: a real run SRT rebuilt (32 chunks/68.84s), correct order, QC 100/100.
- **How to apply:** `python3 subtitle-pipeline/build_srt_from_script.py <downloads>/<Name>_script.txt <downloads>/Quick-Avatar-Video-1080p.mp4 --output <scratch>/<Name>.srt`. Only when (a) the narration is a known verbatim script AND (b) the Gemini transcribe guard reports degenerate timestamps. Real word-level timing (when Gemini returns valid timestamps) still wins — this is the degenerate-case fallback.
- **Also validated this run:** premium-classic / slate-copper via the QCR-108 (QC100) model with brass-library reuse (QCR-105, no image-provider call) passes QC 100/100 first try on a how-to/tutorial topic with two real evidences (Claude Code page scroll + sub-agents docs zoom). The number-promise headline + PremiumFlow 4-node role pipeline (traveling pulse) read clean.

### QCR-110: HeyGen look-card pick — only `browser_click` on the inner button ref works (overlay eats raw/dispatched clicks)
- **Finding (pipeline friction, Phase 3):** selecting the avatar look "<AVATAR_LOOK>" cost ~6 failed attempts. `mouse.click(x,y)` on the card center, and `dispatchEvent(pointerdown/mousedown/mouseup/click)` on `document.elementFromPoint` (which resolves to the `tw-absolute tw-inset-0 tw-z-[8]` overlay), BOTH returned silently with NO selection (look never moved to front, Avatar & Voice stayed on the default stock avatar). Also: the look-grid geometry from `boundingBox` was polluted by an overlapping marketing-landing layer (`heading "Create talking avatar video online"`) giving wrong coords; trustworthy coords came only from `img[alt^="<AVATAR_NAME> --"]` rects.
- **Root cause:** HeyGen v4 look cards put a transparent `z-[8]` pointer-catcher over the thumbnail; React's onClick is bound to an inner element, not the overlay, so synthetic events on the overlay never reach the handler. Raw coordinate clicks land on the overlay too.
- **Fix (permanent):** hardened HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md step 5 — the ONLY reliable pick is `browser_snapshot` → find the card's accessibility `button [ref]` sibling of `img "<AVATAR_LOOK>"` → `browser_click` that button ref (Playwright drives the real React target through the overlay). Verify the look jumps to FRONT, then reload `?panel=scene` and confirm Avatar=<AVATAR_LOOK> / Voice=config.avatar.voice before scripting.
- **Verification:** a real run — after switching to the button-ref click, the look moved to front first try, avatar swapped to <AVATAR_NAME>, voice auto-set config.avatar.voice. Render completed, QC 100/100 iter1.
- **Also confirmed working:** Gemini transcription TAIL-DROP guard (QCR-109) fired again (dropped last 4.3s) → `build_srt_from_script.py` rebuilt full 64.16s SRT from the verbatim script. The guard + rebuild path is solid; no change needed.

### QCR-111: Gemini transcription NORMALIZES PT-BR colloquial contractions (tá→está) → subtitle/audio mismatch; prefer build_srt_from_script when the script is colloquial
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 99/100 PASS iter1 — the sole −1). premium-classic / midnight-azure, two-evidence (Stanford canaries page SCROLL + a real "170M jobs by 2030, 92M at risk, WEF warns" news headline ZOOM), brass-library reuse (no image-provider call), all 15 checklist YES.
- **Finding:** Phase-2 script wrote the colloquial CTA "...alguém que **tá** com medo de perder o emprego." Gemini transcription (`transcribe_gemini_srt.py`) returned an otherwise-clean, full-coverage, correctly-ordered SRT (no degenerate-timestamp guard fired), BUT it NORMALIZED "tá" → "está" in the final caption. The burned subtitle then read "está com medo" while the avatar audibly says "tá com medo" → Gemini QC caught the 1-word subtitle-vs-audio mismatch (−1 minor). Same class would hit other contractions Gemini formalizes: pra→para (it already did "para"), tô→estou, cê→você, né, vô.
- **Root cause:** Gemini ASR transcribes what it thinks is "correct" PT-BR, not the verbatim colloquial wording the avatar speaks (which IS the Phase-2 script). When the avatar reads a known verbatim script, the SCRIPT is ground truth for wording; Gemini's transcription is only needed for word-level TIMING.
- **Permanent fix (process rule):** when the Phase-2 verbatim script contains PT-BR colloquial contractions (`\b(tá|tô|pra|pro|cê|né|vô|cadê)\b`), PREFER `subtitle-pipeline/build_srt_from_script.py` (which splits the EXACT script text → preserves "tá/pra/…") over Gemini transcription — OR, if Gemini timing is wanted, reconcile the Gemini SRT against the script and restore the script's colloquial tokens before `srt_to_ass.py`. Gemini transcription stays the default ONLY for non-colloquial scripts. (Gemini word-level timing still wins for sync; this is purely a WORDING fix.) The mismatch is cosmetic (−1) so it never blocks a PASS, but the rule gets the clean 100.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/SUBTITLE_INSTRUCTIONS.md` + `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (note the colloquial-script → build_srt_from_script preference), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md` (this entry).
- **Verification:** a real run shipped at 99/100 (held, build-but-don't-post). Next colloquial-script run: build the SRT from the script (or reconcile contractions) and the subtitle matches the spoken "tá" → expect the −1 to vanish.
- **Also validated this run:** premium-classic / **midnight-azure** (a clean 3rd+ data/economy palette after marble/charcoal/azure used before) + the a real run base-layer structure (one continuous `PremiumFrame` for avatar+audio 0→end, full-frame sections as muted/opaque overlay siblings) + brass-library reuse (robot=IA, prem_bust=jovem/trabalhador, cursor=código, doc=vaga, gear=aprender) passes QC 99/100 first try on an abstract jobs/economy topic — confirms QCR-105 "reuse the brass library before falling back to hand-drawn" holds even when the topic has few concrete objects, as long as 2 REAL evidence screenshots carry the credibility. Heavy-SPA capture note: weforum.org rendered blank/timed-out headless (overlay/paint); a clean WEF-attributing news headline page is an acceptable, honest substitute (label the real domain, let the headline carry the WEF attribution).
