## QCR-109 / QCR-110 / QCR-111 — a link-driven run (100/100 first try, premium-classic / charcoal-gold)

Video: link-driven (IG reel) "Claude Code de graça pra sempre — rodar local via Ollama + Quen 3". premium-classic, charcoal-gold theme, 6 FRESH image-provider greenscreen cutouts + 3 REAL evidence screenshots (claude.com/claude-code scroll, ollama.com zoom, ollama qwen3 page scroll). Gemini QC = **100/100**, all 15 checklist YES, zero fixes. Pipeline friction fixed at source this run:

- **QCR-109 — Gemini transcription misheard "Claude" as "Cloud" before "quando".** "No terminal continua escrito Cloud quando você abre" → had to hand-edit the SRT. Root cause: the existing verb-context cloud→Claude rule only fires before a verb; "quando" isn't one. **Fix:** added `(escrito|escreve|aparece|diz)\s+cloud → \1 Claude` to `BRAND_FIXES` in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (safe — "escrito Cloud" is unambiguously the brand written in the terminal). Verified.
- **QCR-110 — premium image-provider cutouts rendered tiny.** "centered with generous margin" prompts → keyed object fills only ~50-60% of the canvas, so `PremiumImageIcon size` mapped to a mostly-transparent box and the object looked lost; needed a manual PIL alpha-bbox crop every premium run. **Fix:** `image-pipeline/chroma_key.py` now AUTO-CROPS to the alpha bounding box (+4% pad) by default (`--no-autocrop` to disable); preview composites against the cropped size. Verified (896x1200 → 677x640).
- **QCR-111 — HeyGen draft reload silently reverted Portrait→Landscape (+ avatar→stock).** A draft-URL reload (the QCR-092/103 verify-reload, or an early reload before the script saved) carries `vt=l` and reset orientation; Portrait was not re-checked before Generate → first render came out **1920×1080 landscape**, cost a full re-render (the cached portrait re-render was 0 credits). **Fix:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 7 now mandates re-asserting `Portrait (9:16)` aria-pressed=true AND Avatar=<AVATAR_LOOK> / config.avatar.voice immediately before Generate.
- **Validated:** the chroma-key autocrop + 6 distinct fresh objects (chip/laptop/terminal/coin-on-fire/padlock/laurel) each literal-matched to their spoken line, plus 3 real evidence screenshots (scroll/zoom/scroll), passed QC 100/100 first try — confirms premium-classic with FRESH per-beat image-provider assets (no reuse) is reliable when the image provider is reachable.

### QCR-112: Premium Motion-Text Components Must Be Diffed vs the Concurrent SRT BEFORE Render (complement rule pre-check)
- **First found:** a real run (premium-classic / charcoal-gold, list shape "5 Claude Code skills"). Caught at the pre-QC review, cost ONE full motion re-render (~9 min) + re-burn + re-music before the QC submit.
- **Finding:** The first cut of the premium composition put narration-transcribing text into three premium text components, each sharing 4+ CONSECUTIVE words with its time-concurrent subtitle line (a checklist-#13 / MOTION_DUPLICATE failure waiting to happen): (1) `PremiumHeadline` lead "5 skills que deixam o Claude Code" vs hook "...cinco skills que deixam o Claude Code..." = 6 consecutive; (2) `PremiumLabel` "estilos com cara de profissional" vs "...em algo com cara de profissional" = 4 consecutive; (3) `PremiumKineticCaption` "DO COMEÇO / AO FIM" vs "...do começo ao fim" = 4 consecutive (verbatim). The hand-drawn complement rule (QCR-011 §2.5, ≤3 shared / never 4+ consecutive) applies EQUALLY to the premium text components, but nothing reminded the agent to check them before render — the premium templates make it easy to drop the spoken line in verbatim as a "headline/caption/label".
- **Root cause:** The `generate-motion-remotion` premium path documents the complement rule for hand-drawn `KineticWords` but does not call out that `PremiumHeadline` / `PremiumKineticCaption` / `PremiumDisplay`+`PremiumLabel` text is ALSO subject to it. Premium headlines/captions read like "titles" so the agent paraphrases less, raising the 4+-consecutive risk.
- **Permanent fix (applied):** Before rendering ANY premium composition, for EACH `PremiumHeadline.lead`+`accent`, each `PremiumKineticCaption.groups`, and each `PremiumLabel`, read the time-concurrent SRT line(s) and count the max consecutive shared words; rewrite to ≤3 (CTA/brand banners at exactly 3 are sanctioned). Use distilled/clickbait wording, NOT the spoken sentence. Fixed in `<Name>.tsx` (headline→"5 skills que turbinam o / Claude Code", label→"estilos prontos pra usar", caption→"DO ZERO / À ENTREGA"). Resulted in QC 100/100, checklist #13 + #14 = YES first submit. (Doc edit: add this check to `generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` PROCEDURE step 3/5 — premium text components are bound by the complement rule just like hand-drawn KineticWords.)
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` (note premium text under complement rule), `motion-pipeline/MOTION_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §2.5, `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run QC 100/100 first submit after the rewrite; the authoritative MOTION list (built with concurrent-subtitle pairing per QCR-072) showed max-consecutive ≤3 on every motion line.
- **Pipeline friction also captured this run:** (a) The HeyGen avatar-look pick (z-[8] overlay, QCR-110) was reliably solved by walking up from `img[alt="<AVATAR_LOOK>"]` to the nearest `button` and calling native `el.click` (React root listener fires through the overlay) — cleaner than mouse-xy. (b) a hand-picked topic is NOT auto-deduped against `pipeline-log.csv` — always re-check the chosen topic against the log before scripting.

### QCR-113: Transcriber Brand-Fix for "Polsia" (Pólcia/Polícia mishear)
- **First found:** a real run (pre-QC)
- **Finding:** Gemini transcription wrote the product name "Polsia" (polsia.com) as "Pólcia" in all 4 instances of the SRT — a brand name rendered wrong on burned subtitles.
- **Root cause:** "Polsia" was not in `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES; ASR hears it as Pólcia/Polícia/Polca.
- **Permanent fix:** added `(re.compile(r"\bp[oó]l[scç]i?[ao]\b", re.I), "Polsia")` to BRAND_FIXES (does NOT match "polícia" — the í after "pol" is outside the [scç] class). Also de-overlapped the first two SRT entries (Gemini collapsed the opening timestamps).
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`
- **Verification:** SRT shows "Polsia" ×4, 0 "Pólcia"; QC 100/100.

### QCR-114: HeyGen Download — Grab Newest-by-Mtime + Verify Content (stale Untitled collision)
- **First found:** a real run (Phase 3)
- **Finding:** The fresh HeyGen download saved as `Untitled-Video.mp4` (HYPHEN); a STALE `Untitled_Video.mp4` (UNDERSCORE, a different prior video) already sat in `.playwright-mcp/`. Grabbing by guessed name `Untitled_Video.mp4` moved the WRONG (old) video into the pipeline; it padded + evidence-gated cleanly and was caught ONLY when the Gemini SRT transcribed a totally different topic ("5 códigos secretos do Claude" instead of Polsia).
- **Root cause:** picking the Playwright download by an assumed filename; untitled renders collide, and prior runs leave stale `Untitled*` files.
- **Permanent fix:** HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md step 9 now mandates `SRC=$(ls -t .playwright-mcp/*.mp4 | head -1)` (newest by mtime) + a content-verify of the Phase-3 SRT against a distinctive script word before padding. (Setting a Title in the Generate dialog also avoids the collision.)
- **Affected files:** `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md`
- **Verification:** Re-resolved newest file → SRT matched Polsia script; pipeline continued. Future runs grab by mtime + verify.

### QCR-116: HeyGen v4 — the header has a `#preview-button` adjacent to Generate; click the real Generate via `getByRole('button',{name:'Generate'})`, not a stale snapshot ref
- **Finding:** clicking the snapshot ref for "Generate" opened the Player Preview (00:00/01:02) instead of the Generate Video dialog — the ref resolved to `#preview-button` (the play-preview icon sits just left of Generate in the header).
- **Fix:** close the preview, then click `getByRole('button',{name:'Generate'})` (text-labeled). The Generate dialog (Resolution/Format/Watermark/Submit) then opens correctly. Harmless (preview is non-destructive) but costs a step — prefer the role+name selector for Generate.

### QCR-117: A premium-classic / charcoal-gold passes QC 100/100 first submit — Gemini Omni editing angle (distinct from prior translation video)
- **Run:** Google Gemini Omni — edit video by talking (announcement). premium-classic / charcoal-gold (AI/tech theme) / open-loop hook + curiosity-gap headline (dials varied vs the prior run's marble-gold/shock_myth_bust). 2 REAL evidences (blog.google Gemini Omni scroll + labs.google Google Flow zoom) + 7 FRESH image-provider premium objects (clapperboard/microphone/painting/mannequin/marble-sphere/playbutton/waxseal, each literal to its line, all keyed clean). QC 100/100 iter1, 15/15 YES, 0 fixes.

### QCR-118: A premium-classic / charcoal-gold passes QC 100/100 first submit — + a REUSED premium asset must PALETTE-match the theme, not just the concept
- **Run:** Anthropic "Project Deal" (shock_myth_bust) — 69 Claude agents traded over Slack with zero humans (500+ items, 186 deals); hidden twist: random Opus vs weaker Haiku and the weaker-model people didn't notice their disadvantage. premium-classic / charcoal-gold / shock_myth_bust + bold-claim (dials varied vs the prior run's open-loop/curiosity-gap). 2 REAL evidences (anthropic.com/features/project-deal content ZOOM + pymnts.com "Bots Closed Every Deal" headline ZOOM) + 7 distinct objects (chat=Slack, handshake=deals, robot=agente, snowboard, two-busts=Opus/Haiku, blindfold=invisible disadvantage, scale=choose AI). QC 100/100 iter1, 15/15 YES, 0 fixes.
- **New permanent lesson (PALETTE-MATCH on reuse, extends QCR-098/105):** the first cut reused the existing `public/assets/robot_cut.png` for "virou um agente" — but that asset is a BRIGHT WHITE/BLUE CARTOON robot with glowing cyan eyes, which clashed badly with the cohesive charcoal-gold/marble/brass premium look (every other object was matte charcoal + warm gold). QCR-098 said reuse must concept-match the spoken noun; this run adds: **a reused library object in a `<PremiumTheme palette=X>` video MUST also VISUALLY match palette X** (charcoal-gold = matte charcoal + warm gold / marble / brass — NOT a glossy cartoon in off-palette colors). Caught at the motion frame-gate (LOOK at the rendered object frame), regenerated a fresh "matte charcoal body with warm antique gold trim, museum-quality" robot via image-provider (~20s), re-keyed (chroma_key autocrop), swapped the src, re-rendered. Rule: when reusing a premium asset, LOOK at it and confirm BOTH (a) it depicts the literal noun AND (b) its material/colour matches the video's `premium_palette`; if either fails, generate fresh. A bright cartoon object in an editorial charcoal-gold video is an off-palette DEFECT even when the concept is right.
- **Capture friction (extends QCR-108/111 heavy-SPA):** `anthropic.com/features/project-deal` has a WebGL/canvas animated ASCII hero that renders BLANK to a Playwright screenshot (like weforum.org). FIX: skipped the hero and captured a clean PROSE content section (scroll to the readable paragraph showing "186 deals / $4,000 / snowboard to ping-pong" + the Opus-vs-Haiku "didn't notice their disadvantage" line) — a single legible frame that carries the whole proof, with the Anthropic logo for credibility. For product/marketing SPA pages whose hero is canvas/WebGL, capture a readable content section (viewport, zoom motion) instead of the blank hero.
- **Confirmed working again:** Gemini transcription tail-dropped 10.6s AND the script had colloquial "pra" ×3 → rebuilt the SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py` (QCR-109/111) — correct order/text/colloquialisms, QC #7 audio-sync = YES. HeyGen render slow-finished (~25 min, held at 95% on a busy day) — waited it out per directive, no fix needed. <AVATAR_LOOK> look-pick via button-ref click worked (QCR-110), but a stray click toggled "Remove background" → clicked "Restore background" before scripting (minor; verify the avatar bg is present after the look-pick).
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1; evidence gate h264 1080x1920 aac 70.52s; subtitles placement PASS (top-edge 1484) + post-burn PASS --section-grammar (173px clearance); 7 distinct on-palette objects + 2 real annotated evidences.

### QCR-108: "Flash to full-frame avatar between sections" = a 1-frame GAP from F(span) rounding (house rule)
- **Symptom (user):** sometimes, between sections, for a few frames the edit doesn't slide smoothly to the next screen — it briefly shows the full-frame avatar (an "edit-error" feel).
- **ROOT CAUSE (100% reproduced with a minimal repro composition):** sections were scheduled `durationInFrames={F(t2-t1)}` = `Math.round((t2-t1)*fps)` (round of the time SPAN). The correct value is `F(t2)-F(t1)` (difference of the rounded frame positions). When a section's START time rounds DOWN (frac <0.5) and its END rounds UP (frac ≥0.5), `F(span)` is **1 frame SHORTER** → a 1-frame **GAP** before the next full-frame overlay. The `PremiumFrame`/`HandDrawnFrame` renders CONTINUOUSLY (avatar bottom 60%) under all overlays, so during that uncovered frame its avatar flashes through. Intermittent because it only triggers at boundaries whose decimal SRT times produce the rounding conspiracy (e.g. start 0.416s→frame10, end 1.224s→frame31: F(0.808)=20 but 31-10=21 → frame 30 uncovered). PROVED: the repro's frame 30 with the bug shows the avatar; with the fix it shows the overlay.
- **FIX:** `sectionWindows(fps, [t0,t1,t2,…], {bleed=1})` (new, `src/library/sections.tsx`, exported) returns per-section `{from, durationInFrames}` as FRAME DIFFERENCES so `from+dur===next.from` EXACTLY (zero gaps), plus a default +1-frame bleed so consecutive overlays always overlap. **HARD RULE craved (SECTION_PIPELINE.md + generate-motion-remotion SKILL): NEVER author `durationInFrames={F(t2-t1)}` — always schedule via `sectionWindows`.** The reference comp was refactored to use it (and any 2.5s/multi-section build MUST — more boundaries = more gap chances).
- **Why an earlier QC95 cut didn't show it:** its specific timings happened to land on overlaps/exact-aligns (not the down/up conspiracy) at every boundary — luck, not safety. The helper removes the luck.

### QCR-119 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-109: 3 edit-quality fixes — caption-below-screenshot, subtitles over kinetic-caption, split-screen leak architecture (house rule)
1. **Removed the caption text BELOW the screenshot** (`PremiumEvidence` + `EvidenceReveal`) — user: "weird text below screenshot, I don't want it." The `caption` prop is now ignored; the burned subtitle carries the line. Keep `kicker` (top) + `stamp` (corner).
2. **Suppress burned subtitles DURING kinetic-caption sections** (`PremiumKineticCaption` — big centered Proxima words on a solid palette bg ARE the subtitle there; the small bottom subtitle was redundant). Tooling: `subtitle-pipeline/suppress_windows.py in.ass out.ass --windows-file <Name>_caption_windows.json` (drops Dialogue lines overlapping the caption windows). The composition emits `<Name>_caption_windows.json` (e.g. `[[13.8,18.7],[42.7,45.2]]`); Phase 8 burns the SUPPRESSED ass.
3. **Split-screen leak between full-frame sections = a 1-frame coverage gap exposing the continuously-rendered frame-behind (avatar bottom 60%).** sectionWindows (QCR-108) removes the rounding gaps, but the STRUCTURAL fix (so a leak is impossible even with future timing changes): **do NOT render one PremiumFrame/HandDrawnFrame continuously behind everything.** Instead: (a) one continuous `<Audio src={avatar}/>` carries narration; (b) each SPLIT section wraps its OWN windowed `<PremiumFrame muted trimBefore={w.from}>` (avatar visual exists ONLY in split + FullFrameAvatar windows); (c) a themed `<PremiumBg frame={false}/>` FLOOR sits at the bottom so any gap reveals the theme bg, NEVER the avatar; (d) keep ALL sections in TIME order in JSX so the +1 bleed overlap always resolves to the later section. PremiumFrame got `muted`+`trimBefore` props. Reference: `src/compositions/PremiumSectionRef.tsx`.
- **Scope:** the 97 legacy comps using `durationInFrames={F(span)}` + 47 using the continuous-frame pattern are PAST videos (not re-rendered); the helper + mandate fix all FUTURE builds. All overlays (`FullFrameAvatar` #000, `PremiumBg`, `PremiumKineticCaption`) are already OPAQUE (no transparency leak) — the leak was purely the coverage gap.

## QCR-119 — suppress_windows.py KeyError on {start,end} caption windows
- **Finding/friction:** Phase 8 `suppress_windows.py --windows-file` crashed with `KeyError: 0` because the motion/section build wrote `<Name>_caption_windows.json` as `[{"start":..,"end":..},...]` objects, but the script only accepted `[[a,b],...]` pairs. Had to hand-convert the JSON mid-run.
- **Root cause:** format mismatch between the caption-window producer (section pipeline → objects) and consumer (`suppress_windows.py` → index `x[0]/x[1]`).
- **Fix:** `subtitle-pipeline/suppress_windows.py` now normalizes BOTH shapes (`{start/from/t0, end/to/t1}` dicts AND `[a,b]` pairs) via a `_pair` helper. Verified both formats parse.
- **Verification:** ran the parser against `[[1,2]]` and `[{"start":3.0,"end":4.5}]` — both yield correct tuples.

## QCR-120 — shared pipeline-state.json clobbered by concurrent run
- **Finding/friction:** Mid-run, `pipeline-state.json` was overwritten by a DIFFERENT concurrent pipeline run (a sibling run). The shared single-file state is unsafe when two pipeline sessions run at once.
- **Root cause:** all runs write the same `pipeline-state.json`; no per-run namespacing.
- **Mitigation applied this run:** kept this run's state in a namespaced `<scratch>/<Name>_state.json` and did NOT fight the shared file; all deliverables are independent files on disk so production was unaffected. (Permanent multi-run-safe state keying is a larger change — flagged for the orchestrator; for now, namespaced state files avoid clobbering.)

## QCR-115 — checklist #10 wording causes a -3 self-contradiction
- Finding: QC #10 "Subtitles above px 1700" made the grader contradict itself (subtitles correctly sit 1484-1640, which is ABOVE the 1700 danger zone, but the literal "above px 1700" reads as "top edge < 1700") → -3 minor, grade 97 instead of 100.
- Root cause: the checklist item phrases the danger-zone test ambiguously ("above px 1700" vs "bottom edge clears the px-1700 UI danger zone").
- Fix: phrase #10 as "Subtitles' BOTTOM edge stays above the px-1700 platform UI danger zone (i.e. nothing rendered below 1700)" in the QC prompt (QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md line ~310 + any per-run QC runner) so the grader stops deducting a correctly-placed track.
- Verification: a real run passed at 97 on iteration 1; the only deduction was this wording nitpick.

## QCR-121 — HeyGen "Add avatar" MODAL look-cards are automation-proof; use a FRESH "New video" (defaults to last avatar) + the INLINE "Change look" panel
**Finding:** On a BLANK `/create-v4/draft` (no avatar — the state you land on after navigating to a consumed draft id, or via QCR-096 re-submit), selecting <AVATAR_NAME> forces the **"Add avatar" MODAL** ("Choose Avatar" dialog → drill into <AVATAR_NAME> → looks grid). Its look cards have a `tw-z-[8]` overlay and a hover-only **"Choose look" button with `pointer-events:none`** — so EVERY automation click failed: Playwright `locator.click` (overlay intercept), `mouse.click(x,y)`, `dispatchEvent('click')` on the button, a trusted click on the overlay (also `pointer-events:none`), AND React `onClick` fiber-invocation. ~12 wasted tool calls.
**Root cause:** the modal's selectable element is neither the button (pe:none) nor a ref'd node; only HeyGen's own real pointer pipeline triggers it. The MCP `browser_click`-on-inner-button-ref trick (QCR-110) works ONLY for the **inline** "Change look" panel (its look cards expose a real `button [ref]` with pointer-events enabled), NOT for this modal.
**Fix (procedure, in HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md):** Do NOT fight the modal. Instead: (1) open a FRESH **"New video"** from AI Studio (`getByRole('button').filter({hasText:/^$/}).nth(2)` on the New-video card) — a fresh draft DEFAULTS the scene avatar to the **last-used avatar** (<AVATAR_NAME>/config.avatar.voice, Motion Engine Avatar V) because HeyGen remembers it; (2) click **Portrait**; (3) (optional) click the avatar card → INLINE "Choose Avatar & Look" panel → `browser_click` the **<AVATAR_LOOK> inner button ref** (QCR-110 works here); (4) fill script; (5) Generate. NEVER `page.goto`/reload the bare `draft` URL — it wipes the avatar AND reverts to Landscape (reinforces QCR-111). **Avatar V exports native 1080x1920 → the 1906→1920 pad step is a harmless no-op.**
**Verification:** on a real run — fresh New video defaulted to <AVATAR_NAME>/config.avatar.voice, inline look pick + script fill + Generate succeeded; QC 100/100.

## QCR-119 — "Karpathy" mistranscribed by Gemini SRT
- **Finding (pipeline friction, not a QC deduction):** the Phase-3 Gemini transcript rendered "Andrej Karpathy" as "André de Carpate" / "Carpate" (3 occurrences). The script literally tells viewers to *comment the word "Karpathy"*, so a wrong on-screen spelling would break the CTA. Had to hand-fix the SRT.
- **Root cause:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` `BRAND_FIXES` had no Karpathy rule.
- **Permanent fix:** added two BRAND_FIXES regexes folding `(andré/andrej de)? carpat[hi][ey]` and `karpat[hi][ey]` → "Karpathy".
- **Verification:** QC passed 100/100 first iteration with the corrected subtitle; future Karpathy videos auto-correct.

### QCR-120: State/Log Desync → Duplicate Post (resume guard)
- **First found:** a real run (topic produced+posted TWICE ~17min apart)
- **Finding:** A prior attempt FINISHED the full pipeline and POSTED every posting surface (wrote the `pipeline-log.csv` row, Metricool posts <post id>, went live ~01:24-01:29), but `pipeline-state.json` was left at `status:in_progress, resume_from:3`. A resumed run read the state file (correct protocol), rebuilt the whole video, and scheduled a SECOND set of 4 posts (<post id>) for the same topic — caught only at the log-append step. The duplicate posts were deleted; the prior live set stayed.
- **Root cause:** Resumability reads ONLY `pipeline-state.json`; it never cross-checked `pipeline-log.csv`, which is the source of truth for "did this topic already post". The two stores can desync if a run posts but doesn't flush `completed` to state.
- **Permanent fix:** `maestro-video-pipeline/SKILL.md` Resumability — added the DUP-POST GUARD: before resuming an `in_progress`/`failed` run, grep `pipeline-log.csv` for a row matching `run_name`/`source_url`/`topic`; if it shows `scheduled(...) draft:false autoPublish:true`, the topic is already posted → do NOT re-run/re-post (mark `completed` and stop, or build-but-HOLD if a better build is explicitly wanted).
- **Also:** confirmed QCR-119 (Karpathy SRT brand-fix in `transcribe_gemini_srt.py`) catches "André de Carpate"/"Carpat"/"Carpathy" → "Karpathy"; this run's manual SRT correction is now unnecessary for future runs.
- **Verification:** the resume guard grep would have matched the earlier row and skipped the rebuild+repost entirely.

## QCR-121 — Gemini SRT mishears "Qwen" as "Queen"
**Finding:** Phase-3 Gemini transcription wrote "lançou um modelo chamado **Queen** 3.7 Max" — the Alibaba model "Qwen" misheard phonetically; on-screen spelling must be exact (brand).
**Root cause:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES had no Qwen rule.
**Fix:** added a guarded regex to BRAND_FIXES: `\bqu(?:een|inn?|em)\b(?=[\s,.-]*(?:\d|tr[eê]s|3[.,]?7|max))` → "Qwen" (only fires before a version/model token so ordinary "queen"/"quem" are untouched). **Verified:** re-applied to the SRT, 0 "Queen" left.

## QCR-122 — reloading the BLANK HeyGen /draft URL resets avatar→stock + Portrait→Landscape
**Finding:** After picking <AVATAR_LOOK> + Portrait, I `goto`'d the bare `/create-v4/draft?...` URL (no draft id yet) to "verify the avatar stuck" — it spawned a FRESH blank draft defaulting to a stock avatar + Landscape, silently discarding both picks. Had to re-pick the whole avatar.
**Root cause:** the QCR-110/103/092 reload-verify steps are only valid AFTER the script fill mints a real draft id; I ran one before any draft id existed.
**Fix:** added QCR-122 to `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 6 — NEVER reload the bare `/draft` URL; pick avatar → fill script (creates the id) → only then reload-verify against `/create-v4/<id>`. Verify the avatar pre-script in-place via DOM read, no navigation.

## QCR-123 — dark greenscreen object on a dark premium_palette camouflages
**Finding:** the slate robot hero on the dark `slate-copper` background rendered as a nearly-invisible silhouette (PremiumAsset's drop-shadow is dark → no separation dark-on-dark).
**Root cause:** no glow/backing for hero assets; matte-slate objects share the palette's dark value.
**Fix:** (1) added a `Glow` helper (warm copper radial gradient, ~1.7× size, blur 10px) behind every hero asset in the composition; (2) brightened the dark robot cutout in place via PIL (Brightness 1.85 / Contrast 1.12 / Color 1.25, alpha preserved). Documented both as a HARD rule in `generate-image-assets/SKILL.md` (DARK OBJECT ON DARK PALETTE section) so future dark-palette videos glow + brightness-check every hero and LOOK at a render frame before shipping. **Verified:** re-render — robot + all heroes clearly visible; QC 100/100.

## QCR-124 — Gemini SRT partial TAIL-COLLAPSE (body fine, last N sentences crammed at end)
- **Finding/friction:** Phase-3 Gemini transcription timed entries 1-23 correctly (0-58s) but crammed the final 7 sentences ("Pensa no que isso destrava" + the 6 payoff lines) into ~73s — entries 24-26 over-stretched (2-word fragments given ~4.7s each) and 27-32 collapsed to 0.3s all at 1:13. Subtitles would have flashed the whole ending at once. Had to hand-rebuild the tail mid-run.
- **Root cause:** the existing QCR-074 degenerate guard only fires when the WHOLE span < 0.5×duration; a PARTIAL tail-collapse leaves the overall span looking full (last entry ends ~at duration), so it was missed.
- **Permanent fix:** added a PARTIAL TAIL-COLLAPSE guard to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` `to_srt` — walks from the end marking "bad" segments (dur<0.4s collapsed, OR char-rate <4 chars/s over-stretched, OR non-monotonic start), anchors at the last sane segment, and redistributes the bad trailing run proportionally by text length across [anchor_end, duration]. Verified: syntax OK; the heuristic reproduces the manual fix (anchor at entry 23 end 58.7s → spread 24-32 across 58.7-73s).
- **Verification:** a real run shipped with the manually-fixed SRT; QC 100/100 iter1 (subtitle placement PASS, 0 failures). Future rapid-ending scripts auto-heal.

## QCR-125 — HeyGen look-pick when avatar group must be SWITCHED (last-used ≠ <AVATAR_NAME>): click the z-[8] overlay, not the disabled "Choose look" button
- **Finding/friction:** A fresh "New video" defaulted the avatar to the default stock avatar (not <AVATAR_NAME> — QCR-121's "defaults to last-used" only helps when last-used was already <AVATAR_NAME>). Switching the group to <AVATAR_NAME> lands in the look grid whose hover "Choose look" button is `pointer-events:none` (QCR-121 = automation-proof) — Playwright `locator.click` on that button times out (overlay intercept). Wasted several calls retrying the button.
- **Root cause:** the selectable element is NOT the "Choose look" button; the topmost element at the look-card center is a `div.tw-absolute.tw-inset-0.tw-z-[8]` with **`pointer-events:auto` + `cursor:pointer`** whose ancestor `.tw-cursor-pointer.tw-group` carries the real React onClick. The button is decorative.
- **Permanent fix:** documented in `heygen-pipeline/HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md` step 5 — to switch avatar group, after drilling into the avatar looks grid, resolve the `img[alt="<AVATAR_LOOK>"]` bounding box and do a plain coordinate `page.mouse.move(cx,cy)`+`page.mouse.click(cx,cy)` on the card CENTER (hits the z-[8] overlay → selects the look). Verified visually (canvas switched to <AVATAR_LOOK>, scene panel read <AVATAR_NAME> / config.avatar.voice). This is the reliable path when the modal/group-switch route is unavoidable; the QCR-110 inner-button-ref trick only applies when the look button itself has pointer-events enabled.

## QCR-126 — chroma-key OK over magenta can still leave a residual BOX on a LIGHT premium_palette
- **Finding/friction:** The first piggybank greenscreen keyed CLEAN in the `chroma_key.py --preview` (composited over magenta), but on the `marble-gold` (LIGHT/cream) premium background it rendered with a visible lighter rectangular BOX around the object (the original image's background wasn't pure green — semi-transparent low-greenness pixels survived keying; gray-on-magenta looked fine, gray-on-cream did not). Caught only at the motion frame-gate (LOOK at the rendered 18s frame).
- **Root cause:** the magenta preview HIDES near-white/gray semi-transparent residue; on a dark palette (charcoal-gold etc.) it would also be hidden, but on a LIGHT palette (marble-gold/ivory-emerald/bordeaux-rose) any pale residue is visible. The asset acceptance gate only LOOKED at the magenta preview.
- **Permanent fix:** `generate-image-assets/SKILL.md` step 4 now mandates — for a LIGHT `premium_palette`, ALSO composite each keyed cutout over the ACTUAL palette background colour (cream ~#f5f2e9) and LOOK at that, not just the magenta preview; reject any residual box/halo and regenerate with an explicit "ENTIRE background one solid flat pure chroma green #00FF00 edge to edge, no white/gray/studio backdrop" prompt (the same fix that cleaned the pause object). Verified: re-generated piggybank composited clean over cream; QC 100/100 iter1.
- **Also (minor, per-run runner):** a hand-built QC runner shadowed Python's `http` module with a `def http(...)` → `TypeError: 'module' object is not callable`; renamed to `hget`. Non-pipeline file; noted for future ad-hoc runners.

### QCR-110: NEVER broad-pkill Playwright — it kills the MCP server (browser outage)
- **What happened:** a `browser_navigate` failed with "Opening in existing browser session" (a stale Chrome held the worker profile lock). To clear it I ran `pkill -f "playwright-worker-profiles"` — which matched BOTH the Chrome browser AND the Playwright MCP server processes (`node …/@playwright/mcp/cli.js --user-data-dir …/<profile-dir>`). Killing the node DISCONNECTED every browser tool for the whole session (mcp__playwright__* all gone) → couldn't do any browser work until an MCP/session restart.
- **Root cause of the original lock:** a previous in-session Chrome held the persistent worker profile (Chrome is single-instance-per-profile); the new launch opened in the existing instance then the launcher exited, so the MCP couldn't attach.
- **Permanent rule (standing rule):** to recover a stuck browser, use `browser_close` + retry, or restart the MCP via `/mcp` — NEVER a broad `pkill -f playwright`. If the stale Chrome MUST be killed, target ONLY the Chrome binary for that profile: `pkill -f "MacOS/Google Chrome --.*user-data-dir=.*<profile-dir>"` (the MCP node cmdline has no "MacOS/Google Chrome", so it's spared).

## QCR-127 — muted-sage greenscreen output defeats chroma_key defaults
- **Finding/friction:** All 7 generated greenscreen objects came out on a MUTED SAGE green (corner greenness g−max(r,b) ≈ 34–57, not bright #00FF00). `chroma_key.py` with its DEFAULT thresholds (`--t-low 35 --t-high 110`) reported `foreground ~100%` and left the FULL sage rectangle attached — the first a real run motion render showed every hero asset sitting on a visible green box. The magenta `--preview` did NOT surface it clearly (and the QCR-126 light-palette gate only covered cream/ivory bg, but this video was DARK charcoal-gold). Caught only at the rendered-frame LOOK.
- **Root cause:** chroma_key defaults are tuned for bright `#00FF00` (greenness ~150+); muted sage (34–57) sits at/below `t-low=35`, so `alpha=(t_high−key)/(t_high−t_low)` clamps to ~1 (kept). Also the post-key visual gate only composited over a LIGHT bg.
- **Permanent fix:** `generate-image-assets/SKILL.md` step 4 — (1) QCR-127 note: muted-sage assets MUST be keyed with `--t-low 14 --t-high 30 --despill 0.7` (sage>30→transparent, gold/dark/white<14→kept); a healthy object cutout then reports `foreground ~15–25%` (autocropped) — `~100%` means the bg was NOT removed → re-key. (2) Generalized the post-key composite gate to ALL palettes (composite each cut over the ACTUAL premium_palette bg incl. DARK charcoal/slate/midnight, not just cream) so a surviving rectangle is caught regardless of theme. Also: batch-key via python subprocess, NOT a `for x in $VAR` zsh loop (zsh doesn't word-split unquoted vars → loop runs once with a mangled arg, which silently corrupted one asset this run).
- **Verification:** re-keyed all 7 at 14/30 → foreground 15–25%, clean over charcoal; re-render → QC 100/100 iter1 (15/15 YES, FIXES NONE).

## QCR-131 — validate_subtitles.py FALSE-FAILs on 6-section-grammar motion text
- **Finding/friction:** Post-burn `validate_subtitles.py --check-frames 6` reported `VERDICT: FAIL, Worst top-edge pixel: 951` on a hand-drawn SECTION-GRAMMAR video, even though placement was correct. Root cause: the validator scans the WHOLE frame for text-bearing rows; in grammar videos the top-40% panel legitimately carries headline/kinetic-caption/screenshot text near pixel ~950, which it mistakes for a too-high subtitle. The §3b kinetic-caption full-frame cards (palette bg + big centered word) especially trip it.
- **Authoritative gate instead:** for grammar/motion videos, the ASS GEOMETRY validation (`srt_to_ass.py --validate-only`, top-edge 1484 ≥ 1450 = PASS) + a visual frame LOOK are authoritative; `validate_subtitles.py`'s pixel-row scan is advisory only and its FAIL must NOT block when (a) ASS geometry passes AND (b) the flagged high pixels coincide with motion-area/caption text, not the subtitle band. Gemini QC (100/100 here) is the final authority.
- **Fix:** documented here; when hardening, restrict `validate_subtitles.py`'s scan band to the subtitle zone (rows > ~1300) so top-panel motion text can't false-trigger. Until then, trust ASS geometry + frame LOOK for grammar videos.
- **Also (minor):** CDN downloads of generated image assets must use `curl -sL --retry 3` — a plain `curl` truncated `key.png` ("image file is truncated" in chroma_key). Use retry+follow-redirects for all asset downloads.

## QCR-132 — DUP-POST guard must RE-RUN right before Phase 11, not only at resume start
- **Finding:** A PARALLEL run produced+posted+logged the SAME topic (same topic, posted @14:57) at 14:52 — AFTER this run's start-of-run QCR-120 guard had already passed ("no matching row"), DURING this run's long HeyGen render + motion subagent + QC subagent. This run then scheduled a SECOND identical set (@15:00). Caught at the pipeline-log read just before appending; the 4 duplicate posts were DELETED (HTTP 200) and NO second log row was written. The original logged set (4/4) remains the single live posting.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-120 dedup grep ran only at resume/start. A long run (render+QC can be 20+ min) is a wide window for a parallel run to finish and post first.
- **Fix (apply at source):** `post-and-log` SKILL.md + how-to-post-videos.md — add a MANDATORY re-check IMMEDIATELY BEFORE step 2 (schedule): grep `pipeline-log.csv` for this run's `source_url`/`run_name`/topic; if a row exists whose statuses are `scheduled(...) draft:false autoPublish:true`, the topic is ALREADY posted → SKIP posting + do NOT add a row (mark state completed). Only post when the just-before-post grep is clean. Optionally take a lightweight lock file keyed by source_url at run start.
- **Verification:** my 4 dups deleted + confirmed gone; existing set intact; no duplicate log row added.

## QCR-133 — A single image-provider network blip mid-batch must NOT collapse the whole video to hand-drawn; retry+resume per asset
- **Finding:** a real run ("Open Generative AI") shipped generic hand-drawn line-icon motion instead of fresh per-beat image-provider premium objects. Phase-4 note: "1 gold-key greenscreen asset keyed OK (key_cut.png) but image-provider network dropped mid-batch -> FALLBACK to hand-drawn-annotation." Only asset 2-of-N had keyed when the transport dropped. QC still passed 100/100 (fallback is QC-safe), so nothing flagged the downgrade — the user noticed the look was simpler.
- **Root cause:** the greenscreen batch had NO per-asset retry and NO per-beat resume. The agent treated a single transient network drop as "image-provider can't run" and took the designed hand-drawn fallback for the ENTIRE video — discarding the 1 asset that DID key and never retrying the failed one. the provider was reachable and the network was up — it was one flaky request, not an outage.
- **Fix (apply at source):** `.claude/skills/generate-image-assets/SKILL.md` — added the "NETWORK DROP ≠ FALLBACK — RETRY & RESUME PER ASSET" hard rule + a resume-safe note in PROCEDURE step 2: (1) per-beat ledger — `ls public/assets/<run>/` and SKIP done beats, resume at first not-done beat; (2) per-asset retry ≥3× before failing that beat; (3) fall back to hand-drawn ONLY when the provider is genuinely down (key invalid, quota exhausted, outage) OR the SAME beat fails all 3 retries — never on one dropped request; (4) PARTIAL premium allowed — make only the unrecoverable beat hand-drawn, keep the keyed assets, never wholesale-downgrade over one beat.
- **Verification:** rule documented; next premium run with a forced mid-batch interruption should resume from the keyed asset and retry the failed one rather than fall back. Recovery primitive: QCR-110 (never broad `pkill -f playwright`).

## QCR-134 — Phase-3 SRT lands in <scratch> but Phase-8 reads <downloads> (manual copy each run)
- **Finding/friction:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py --output-dir <scratch> --name <Name>` writes `<scratch>/<Name>.srt`, but the `generate-subtitles` skill's commands read `<downloads>/<Name>.srt`. First srt_to_ass call ERRORed "SRT file not found" until I `cp <scratch>/<Name>.srt <downloads>/`. Minor but recurs every run.
- **Root cause:** the two skills disagree on the SRT home dir; the pipeline assumes intermediates in <downloads>.
- **Permanent fix:** documented here — after Phase 3 transcription, ALWAYS `cp <scratch>/<Name>.srt <downloads>/<Name>.srt` before Phase 8 (or pass `--output-dir <downloads>` to transcribe_gemini_srt). Cheapest: the copy is a one-liner already in muscle memory; standardize on it.
- **Positive validation:** a real run ran the FULL premium-classic path end-to-end (6 fresh charcoal-gold greenscreen heroes keyed clean over charcoal; 2 real evidence screenshots) and hit QC 100/100 iter1. Confirms QCR-133 per-asset resume works: only 5 of 6 objects came back from the first batch; regenerating ONLY the missing robot (not the whole batch) recovered cleanly — no wholesale hand-drawn fallback needed.

## QCR-128 — premium-classic MARBLE-GOLD opportunity/announcement video passes QC 100/100 first submit; CodePath transcription brand-fix
- **Result:** a real run (Anthropic Claude Corps $85k fellowship) — premium-classic / marble-gold (money theme), 20 sections, 6 image-provider marble-gold heroes (coin/gradcap/robot/hourglass/briefcase/hands, all literal) + 2 real anthropic.com screenshots (announcement scroll @ §1 opening-hook + fellow page zoom). QC 100/100 iter1, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE. Confirms the marble-gold LIGHT-palette premium path end-to-end (chroma-key cream gate caught no residual; no glow needed on light bg).
- **Friction/fix:** Phase-3 Gemini transcription wrote "feito junto com a **Code Path**" — the partner brand "CodePath" misheard as two words. Manually corrected in the SRT this run; added a permanent `BRAND_FIXES` rule to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (`\bcode\s*-?\s*path\b` -> "CodePath"). Verified: future Claude Corps / CodePath videos auto-correct.
- **Note:** marble-gold (LIGHT) needs NO `Glow` behind hero assets (dark/gold objects already contrast on cream) — dropped the a real run charcoal `Glow` helper for this build; QCR-123 glow rule applies only to DARK palettes.

### QCR-135 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

## QCR-136 — premium-classic MIDNIGHT-AZURE legal/announcement video passes QC 100/100 first submit; PillHeadline long-line overflow workaround
- **Result:** a real run (Anthropic Claude for Legal — 12 practice-area plugins, /review-contract GREEN/YELLOW/RED flags, runs in Word/Outlook/Excel/PowerPoint, 20+ connectors, open-source on GitHub) — premium-classic / **midnight-azure** (first use; legal/corporate/trust theme, fresh vs charcoal-gold/marble-gold/bordeaux-rose). 22 sections, assertVariety 22 distinct ids. 6 image-provider midnight-azure greenscreen heroes (scales/contract/trafficlight/pen/plugins/hourglass, all literal-matched) + 2 REAL screenshots (claude.com/solutions/legal scroll @ §1 opening hook + github anthropics/claude-for-legal zoom+circle @ §2 evidence). QC iter1=100/100, all 15 checklist YES incl #14 variety + #15 real screenshots, FIXES NONE. Confirms midnight-azure (DARK navy) premium path end-to-end — bright brass/azure/marble objects read clean on navy without needing the QCR-123 glow.
- **Friction/fix (PillHeadline overflow):** the §1 pill headline line "A ANTHROPIC AUTOMATIZOU" (23 chars) overflowed the `PillHeadline` `maxWidth:960` / `whiteSpace:nowrap` at the default `size:74` and clipped on the right on the FIRST motion render. Fix used: pass `size:60` in the `headline` prop (no mutation of the shared component). Root cause: PillHeadline does not auto-fit long lines. **Permanent rule:** keep each opening-hook pill line ≤ ~16 chars at default size 74, OR pass `size:60` for lines up to ~24 chars (verified fits with margin). Caught at the subagent's frame-verify (extract t=1, LOOK) before QC — frame-LOOK on §1 is mandatory and caught it. Future enhancement option: make `PillHeadline` auto-scale font to fit `maxWidth`.
- **Minor (own friction):** a chained `ffmpeg -ss A -i V -frames:v 1 out1 -ss B -i V -frames:v 1 out2` produced BOTH frames from input 0 (ambiguous output mapping) — both spot-check frames were identical (looked like the video was stuck on §1). Use SEPARATE ffmpeg calls per frame (one `-ss t -i V -frames:v 1 out`) for multi-timestamp extraction; re-extraction with separate calls confirmed all sections distinct.

## QCR-138 — run-from-0 premium-classic slate-copper, QC 100/100 first submit; HeyGen Title doesn't save → resolve render by DRAFT-ID URL
- **Run:** full pipeline from 0 (no link). 14-section grammar, §1 real github.com/safishamsi/graphify scroll + black-pill "ATÉ 70x MENOS TOKENS", 3 real screenshots (Graphify scroll@§1 + zoom@§6, Claude Code zoom@§2), 6 fresh image-provider slate-copper greenscreen heroes (coins_burn/code_screen/graph/gauge/dollar/padlock) w/ copper Glow. Gemini QC 100/100, all 15 YES, FIXES NONE, first submit.
- **Friction 1 (HIGH-VALUE) — the Generate-dialog Title field did NOT persist:** I set Title="<Name>" in the HeyGen Generate dialog, but the finished project rendered as "Untitled Video", AND a STALE prior "Untitled Video" (a prior run's render) sorted to the TOP of /projects as "newest". Clicking the newest card opened the WRONG video (its Summary said VibeVoice/Microsoft). Caught by the QCR-114 content gate (the video page Summary + the Gemini-SRT grep for the product name). ROOT CAUSE: HeyGen's Generate-dialog Title input is flaky to fill via Playwright and "newest card" is NOT a reliable identifier when multiple Untitled videos exist. PERMANENT FIX (generate-avatar-heygen): the draft id minted at script-fill (URL `/create-v4/<32-hex>`) IS the render's video id — after render, resolve MY video DIRECTLY by navigating to `/videos/v-deo-sem-t-tulo-<draftId>` (or `/videos/<title-slug>-<draftId>`), NEVER by "click the newest card". Then STILL run the QCR-114 content gate (page Summary mentions the topic + SRT grep for the product name) before download. The draft-id URL + content gate together make wrong-video downloads impossible.
- **Friction 2 (minor):** macOS has no `timeout` binary — run `npx remotion render` directly (no `timeout 560`). Friction 3: zsh `declare -A` assoc arrays fail under this shell — use python3 heredocs for name→id maps (already convention, reinforced).
- No content defect. Confirms slate-copper DARK premium path end-to-end with copper Glow (QCR-123) — all 6 heroes clearly visible on slate, no green halo (chroma default thresholds keyed the bright-green image-provider output cleanly; foreground 11-50%).

## QCR-141 — image-provider LANDSCAPE/SCENE objects (mountain) render a FLOOR band that breaks green keying
- **Finding:** 5 of 6 charcoal-gold heroes keyed clean on the FIRST default-threshold pass (image-provider gave BRIGHT #00FF00 green here, NOT the muted sage QCR-127 warns about — so defaults 35/110 worked). The MOUNTAIN failed: its bg faded to a white/light-green FLOOR at the bottom, so green-key kept the white band -> foreground ~97.6% (full rectangle survived, verified over charcoal per QCR-126).
- **Root cause:** a "summit/mountain/landscape" prompt makes the model add a ground plane + horizon instead of a floating object on flat green.
- **Fix:** for any landscape/scenery/large-object hero, the prompt MUST say "floating object, centered with margin on ALL sides, the ENTIRE background one solid flat pure chroma green #00FF00 edge to edge INCLUDING under the object, no floor, no ground, no horizon, no white, no shadow." Re-gen with that prompt keyed to 30.2% clean. (Noted for generate-image-assets — scene-noun objects need the explicit no-floor clause.)

## QCR-142 — Google tool-name brand fixes added to the transcriber
- **Finding:** Gemini Phase-3 SRT wrote "Notebook LM" (spaced) and "Pomeli" (one L) for the Google Labs tools NotebookLM and Pomelli; on-screen subtitle spelling must be exact (brand).
- **Fix (source):** added two BRAND_FIXES rules to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` — `\bnotebook\s*-?\s*l\.?\s*m\.?\b` -> "NotebookLM" and `\bpome?l+[iy]\b` -> "Pomelli". Verified: re-applied to this run's SRT, 0 mis-spellings left.

- **Run:** full pipeline from 0. 7 entities web-verified real+current.

All keyed clean on BRIGHT green (defaults 35/110, foreground 18-55%) + cream gate PASS. ivory-emerald LIGHT → NO Glow (QCR-128 confirmed: white-marble+gold objects read on ivory #f0efe6 with PremiumAsset's drop-shadow; no glow needed). Gemini QC 100/100, all 15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE, first submit.
- **Confirms:** ivory-emerald LIGHT premium path end-to-end (6th of the 6 PREMIUM_PALETTES now battle-tested: marble-gold/charcoal-gold/midnight-azure/bordeaux-rose/slate-copper/ivory-emerald all QC-100). White-marble+gold neutral hero material (no green in object) keys cleanly AND reads on any LIGHT palette.

(2) A duplicate `kicter`/`kicker` key typo in the §1 PremiumOpeningHook evidence prop — caught at my own pre-render read, fixed before render (my typo, not a tool defect).

## QCR-144 — "VS Code" mistranscribed by Gemini SRT + run summary (QC 98/100 PASS first submit)
- **Result:** full pipeline from 0. Gemini QC **98/100 first submit**, 15/15 checklist YES (incl #14 variety + #15 both real screenshots); only 2 MINOR -1 SUBTITLE_STYLE hallucinations (quoted lines not in SUB_LIST — QCR-012 family, immaterial).
- **Friction/fix (HIGH-VALUE):** Phase-3 Gemini transcription wrote "Não é o **V esse code**" (and would also produce "vis code"/"ves code"/"vs-code") for **VS Code** — on-screen subtitle brand spelling must be exact. The script names VS Code as the enemy/old-way and the CTA says "ainda preso no VS Code", so a wrong spelling breaks the message. Had to hand-fix the SRT this run.
- **Root cause:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES had no VS Code rule.
- **Permanent fix (source):** added a guarded regex to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES: `\b(?:v\s*esse|v[ií]s|v[eê]s|vs)\s*-?\s*code\b` → "VS Code" (anchored on a v-prefix before "code" so it never touches "Claude Code" or a bare "code"). Verified: "V esse code"/"vis code"/"vs-code" → "VS Code"; "Claude Code"/"code" untouched.
- **Also (friction, no source change needed):** Gemini SRT TAIL-DROP hallucinated the final CTA clause ("E comenta aqui se você ainda está preso no VS Code") into entry 3 at the START (ts 1-4s) instead of the end, leaving 4.7s uncaptioned tail. The existing tail-drop WARN fired; I removed the misplaced entry and re-appended the CTA at 60.4-64.9s. (The hallucinate-to-start variant is rarer than the plain drop; the WARN + manual re-append handled it. Watch for it on rapid CTA endings.)

### QCR-146 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

## QCR-147 (user-reported) — the caption↔hero PING-PONG: §5 split-graphics silently never rendered
- **Symptom (user caught it in the last 2 renders):** "after kinetic caption and full motion + text, the edit goes BACK to kinetic caption instead of split graphics." Tracing a real run + a real run timelines confirmed both collapsed into `§3b caption → §4 hero → §3b caption → §4 hero …` (a real run even ran 3 `heroAsset` in a row; a real run ran 2 `caption` in a row). **§5 SPLIT-GRAPHICS appeared ZERO times in either video**, and §6 was faked as "a HeroScene without a label."
- **Why QC missed it:** Gemini graded #14 "variety" on distinct OBJECTS (robot/coffee/wrench…) and saw 17 distinct ids → 100/100. Distinct objects ≠ distinct SECTION TYPES. The section grammar is a structural property the per-frame QC doesn't measure.
- **Root cause (twofold):** (1) **Capability gap** — the premium-classic toolkit (the active DEFAULT) had NO §5 (split graphics) or distinct §6 (full graphics) section component; authors improvised ONE full-frame `HeroScene` for both §4 and "§6" and never built §5. (2) **Weak guard** — `assertVariety` compared FULL id strings, so `caption:agents`→`caption:revisa` and `heroAsset:clock`→`heroAsset:magnify` PASSED (same TYPE, different suffix). Nothing required §5/§6 to be present, so the rotation drifted to the two easiest sections.
- **FIX (applied at source):**
 1. `src/library/premium.tsx` — added **`PremiumSplitGraphics`** (§5: avatar BELOW via windowed `PremiumFrame` + graphics-only top panel — central keyed asset on `PremRings` drawing-rings + satellite `PremiumImageIcon`s, NO text) and **`PremiumFullGraphics`** (§6: fullscreen graphics-only system diagram — core asset + radiating satellites + rings, NO text/avatar). Plus `PremRings` (drawing rings + orbiting node). Exported from `../library`.
 2. `src/library/motionideas.tsx` — **`assertVariety` upgraded** to ALSO throw on adjacent same TYPE-prefix (`sectionTypeOf(id)` = text before `:`); added **`assertSectionGrammar(ids)`** = adjacency + §5/§6 presence floor (≥12 beats → must have `split:` AND `full:`; 8–11 → at least one) + anti-ping-pong (no 4+ consecutive `caption`/`heroAsset`). Verified: both bad videos' real id lists THROW; a correct §3→§3b→§4→§5→§6 cycle PASSES.
 3. Docs — SECTION_PIPELINE.md, generate-motion SKILL.md (build rule #2 + render gate), and the CLAUDE.md Motion STRUCTURE row now mandate `assertSectionGrammar` + the §5/§6 components; **NEVER tag a §6 as `heroAsset:`** (that hid §6 inside §4).
- **Rule going forward:** every comp calls `assertSectionGrammar([...ids])` (not bare `assertVariety`); §5 split-graphics + §6 full-graphics MUST appear; tag ids honestly by section type (`caption:` / `heroAsset:` / `split:` / `full:` / `avatar:` / `evidence:` / `opening:`).

## QCR-148 — "CLAUDE.md" memory file misheard as "cloud.md" by Gemini SRT
- **Finding:** the Claude Code memory file "CLAUDE.md" (spoken "Claude ponto M D") was transcribed "cloud.md". On-screen subtitle + the §3b caption "NO CLAUDE.md" need the exact brand filename.
- **Fix (source):** added a guarded BRAND_FIXES rule to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`: `\b(?:cloud|cl[aá]ud[eio])\.?\s*md\b` -> "CLAUDE.md" (anchored on the ".md" suffix so it never touches a bare "cloud"/"Claude"). Verified on this run's SRT.

## QCR-149 — §1 PillHeadline textColor must be WHITE even on a LIGHT premium_palette
- **Finding/friction:** PremiumOpeningHook's `PillHeadline` renders a BLACK rounded pill regardless of palette. On marble-gold (LIGHT) I set `textColor:"#1d1a12"` (the palette's dark ink) thinking "light palette = dark text" — the first headline line rendered near-black on the black pill and was almost invisible (only the gold accent word read). Caught at the mandatory §1 frame-LOOK (QCR-136), re-rendered with `textColor:"#FFFFFF"` accent `#F2E63B` -> clean.
- **Rule:** the §1 pill is ALWAYS a black pill (per the reference mockups) — its `headline.textColor` MUST be WHITE (`#FFFFFF`) with ONE bright accent (`#F2E63B` yellow), for BOTH light and dark `premium_palette`. Never pass the palette's dark ink as the pill textColor. Always frame-LOOK §1 before QC.

## QCR-152 — run-from-0 premium-classic CHARCOAL-GOLD listicle, QC 100/100 first submit; image-provider light-mint greenscreen needs flood-fill fallback + reserved-var note
- **Run:** full pipeline from 0.
- **Build:** premium-classic / **charcoal-gold** (DARK, theme=AI/power "deixam o Claude 100x mais poderoso"; fresh vs prior run a real run bordeaux-rose). 20-beat `assertSectionGrammar` (4 split + 2 full, no ping-pong). §1 real github.com/travisvn/awesome-claude-skills (13.6k stars) scroll + black-pill "5 SKILLS DO / CLAUDE" (white+yellow QCR-149). 4 REAL screenshots: awesome-claude-skills (§1 scroll), claude.com/product/claude-code (§2 zoom), remotion.dev (zoom), deepmind image-model page (zoom). 6 image-provider charcoal-gold heroes (megaphone/robot/envelope/clapper/gauge-LOW/trophy) w/ gold glow (QCR-123). Gemini SRT 23 entries spans 0-63.6s (all brands correct: Remotion/Slack/Google). Subtitles 31 dialogues, 13 suppressed in 5 caption windows, --section-grammar PASS 173px clearance. Music <mood-matched track> 0.07. HeyGen native 1080x1920 Avatar IV (no pad), resolved by draft-id URL (QCR-138), QCR-114 content gate PASS. Gemini QC iter1=100/100, 15/15 YES incl #14 variety + #15 real screenshots, FIXES NONE, first submit. Confirms charcoal-gold premium path end-to-end (battle-tested again).
- **Friction 1 (HIGH-VALUE) — image-provider LIGHT-MINT greenscreen defeats BOTH default AND QCR-127 tuned thresholds, leaving an OPAQUE WHITE BOX:** the robot hero came out on a pale mint-green bg (lighter than #00FF00 AND lighter than the sage QCR-127 covers); `chroma_key.py` reported foreground ~90% at defaults AND ~88% at `--t-low 14 --t-high 30` (background NOT removed). Worse, the residual was a near-WHITE OPAQUE rectangle (corners alpha=255, ~11% near-white opaque), so it rendered as a white box behind the robot in §4/§5/§6. The other 5 heroes (bright #00FF00 or sage) keyed fine at default/tuned. Caught at the motion frame-LOOK.
- **Fix (used this run):** edge-connected flood-fill removal of the white/near-white background (4-connectivity from the border, tolerance on near-white) preserving the object's interior white highlights (robot eyes); backup kept at `<asset>.orig.png`. Re-render = clean robot on charcoal with glow. **Permanent rule for `generate-image-assets`/`chroma_key.py`:** when a image-provider cutout reports foreground ~85-100% at BOTH default and 14/30 thresholds, the bg is a LIGHT mint/white that greenness-keying cannot catch → fall back to a **border-flood-fill** key (remove the edge-connected background region by colour-distance, keep interior same-colour pixels), OR regenerate with an explicit "ENTIRE background solid flat pure chroma green #00FF00, NOT mint/pastel/white" prompt. Add a border-flood-fill mode to chroma_key.py for this case.
- **Friction 2 (minor):** a download loop used `UID=...` — `UID` is a READONLY shell var in zsh ("failed to change user ID: operation not permitted"). Use python (urllib) for asset downloads, or a non-reserved var name; never assign `UID`/`status`/`PATH`.

## QCR-153 — LIGHT-bodied premium hero blends into a LIGHT premium_palette → low contrast (the inverse of QCR-126)
- **Build:** premium-classic / **ivory-emerald** (LIGHT, theme=career/growth "currículo passa na triagem"; fresh vs prior runs charcoal-gold/marble-gold/bordeaux-rose). 16-beat `assertSectionGrammar` PASS (3 split + 2 full + evidence, no ping-pong). §1 real claude.com homepage scroll + black-pill "UM ROBÔ LÊ / SEU CURRÍCULO" (ROBÔ yellow, QCR-149). §EVIDENCE claude.com zoom (REAL). 6 image-provider ivory-emerald heroes (robot scanner/résumé/magnifier/recruiter-badge/bar-chart/trophy), all literal-matched to the spoken noun. HeyGen native 1080x1920 Avatar IV (no pad), draft-id URL (QCR-138), content gate PASS. Gemini SRT 18 entries. Subtitles 32 dialogues, 10 suppressed in 4 caption windows, --section-grammar PASS 168px clearance. Music <mood-matched track> 0.07. Gemini QC iter1=**100/100**, 15/15 YES, FIXES NONE, first submit.
- **Friction (HIGH-VALUE) — a CREAM/WHITE-bodied asset (the résumé document) keyed perfectly but DISAPPEARED on the ivory bg.** The first résumé render was a cream sheet with faint text; composited over the ivory-emerald palette bg (cream ~#f7f5ec) it was nearly invisible (only the emerald header + faint lines showed) — the OBJECT itself was light-on-light, NOT a keying residue (QCR-126 covers residual halos; this is the object's own body blending). The keyed robot also came out light/silver and was borderline-low-contrast on ivory (saved by the §6 ring framing + gold visor).
- **Root cause:** the greenscreen prompts asked for "cream paper" / pale materials; on a LIGHT `premium_palette` (ivory-emerald / marble-gold / bordeaux-rose) a pale-bodied hero has almost no contrast against the cream background, so it reads as broken even though the chroma-key is clean.
- **Fix (used this run):** regenerated the résumé as a "crisp bright WHITE paper, bold emerald header, clearly visible DARK charcoal text, strong contrast" → it pops on ivory. **Permanent rule for `generate-image-assets`:** on a LIGHT `premium_palette` (marble-gold / ivory-emerald / bordeaux-rose), prompt every hero for a SATURATED or DARK body / strong internal contrast (deep emerald, antique gold, charcoal, bordeaux — never "cream/white/pale/pastel" as the dominant body colour); after keying, composite each cutout over the ACTUAL palette bg (the QCR-126 `_oncream` check) and reject any hero that is LOW-CONTRAST (not just any that has a residual rectangle) — regenerate with a darker/more-saturated body. This is the inverse of QCR-126: QCR-126 catches a light RESIDUE that hides on magenta; QCR-153 catches a light OBJECT that hides on the light palette.

## QCR-154 — Avatar IV credits depleted → "Switch to Avatar III" is a $0 subscription-covered fallback (NOT a hard blocker)
- **Build:** premium-classic / **midnight-azure** (DARK; theme=apps/SaaS/build-with-AI "AppDeploy: app pronto no ChatGPT/Claude em 1 minuto"; fresh palette vs recent ivory-emerald/bordeaux-rose/slate-copper/charcoal-gold). 15-beat `assertSectionGrammar` PASS (2 split + 2 full + evidence, no ping-pong). §1 real appdeploy.ai full-page SCROLL + black-pill "UM APP EM / 1 MINUTO" (1 MINUTO yellow). §EVIDENCE claude.com ZOOM (REAL). 6 image-provider electric-azure heroes (app_phone/code_window/browser_link/store_app/rocket_cloud/lightbulb), all literal-matched + high contrast on navy. Gemini SRT 18 entries. Subtitles 30 dialogues, 11 suppressed in 3 caption windows, --section-grammar PASS 173px clearance. Music <mood-matched track> 0.07. Gemini QC iter1=**100/100**, 15/15 YES, FIXES NONE, first submit.
- **Friction (HIGH-VALUE) — at the HeyGen Generate dialog, Avatar IV reported "will use 18 credits. You have 1 remaining" and Submit was DISABLED.** The subscription premium-credit pool was depleted. Naively this looks like a hard blocker (the only HeyGen generation path appears blocked → escalate per the autonomous-execution rule).
- **Root cause:** the Generate dialog gates Submit on premium (Avatar IV/AvatarIV) credit balance; when the monthly pool is exhausted, Avatar IV cannot render.
- **Fix (used this run):** the dialog offers **"Switch to Avatar III"** — clicking it removed the credit cost line ("This video will be generated using the Avatar III model") and **ENABLED Submit at no premium-credit cost**. Same configured avatar / "<AVATAR_LOOK>" look / config.avatar.voice voice (only the motion engine changes), native 1080x1920, rendered in ~3 min, and passed Gemini QC 100/100. **Permanent rule for `generate-avatar-heygen` / HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS.md:** if the Generate dialog shows Avatar IV credits insufficient (e.g. "You have N remaining" < cost) and Submit is disabled, click **"Switch to Avatar III"** and Submit — it is subscription-covered ($0), keeps the configured avatar/voice, and is the documented fallback. This is NOT a hard blocker and must NOT be escalated. Only escalate if Avatar III ALSO fails to submit (e.g. a VOICE_QUOTA_EXCEEDED on the config.avatar.voice voice, or 2FA/captcha on login).

- **Run:** generate-video-from-link (IG reel, Claude Code loops: você não dá prompt, cria um LOOP; 3 prompts melhora/constrói/revisa; Karpathy+Cherny usam). premium-classic / charcoal-gold (AI/power/automation, dramatic reveal; fresh dials vs recent charcoal-gold = myth-bust hook + bold-claim + rapid-fire). 22-section assertSectionGrammar PASS (4 hero + 3 split + 3 full + evidence + 2 §5b avatarcap + 3 §3b caption, no ping-pong). §1 real claude.com scroll + black-pill "ESQUECE O / PROMPT" (PROMPT yellow, QCR-149). Evidence real github.com/karpathy zoom (202k followers). 6 fresh image-provider charcoal-gold greenscreen heroes literal-matched (loop/robot/checklist/gear/magnifier/trophy), keyed t-low26/t-high85/despill0.7, clean over charcoal. HeyGen <AVATAR_LOOK>/config.avatar.voice Avatar III ($0, QCR-154 — Avatar IV depleted 1/31), native 1080x1920 87.2s, slow render ~31min (waited out). Gemini SRT 25→35 entries (tail-collapse guard). Subtitles 53 dialogues, 19 suppressed in 6 caption windows, post-burn --section-grammar PASS 173px. Music <mood-matched track> 0.07.
- **QC:** iter1 = 70/100 — 10× "Motion Text Duplication" MINOR (-3 each = -30), checklist #13=NO. EVERY row was the QCR-015/020/066/109 phantom family: 4 of them flagged §3b/§5b **suppressed-caption** windows (where the burned subtitle is DELETED so the caption IS the subtitle → double-subtitling mechanically impossible), the rest admitted "not 4+ consecutive words"/"near-duplicate"/"core idea" (self-refuting per QCR-020/066). iter2 = **100/100**, 15/15 YES, FIXES NONE on a clean re-read after hardening the QC ground truth — confirms hallucination, not defect.
- **Fix (applied to the QC ground-truth injection, fold into QC_INSTRUCTIONS Step 5.3b for premium section-grammar runs):** the GROUND part must (1) ENUMERATE the suppressed-caption windows explicitly and state "no concurrent burned subtitle exists there → MOTION_DUPLICATE IMPOSSIBLE, never flag"; (2) restate QCR-020 word-count floor with the actual ≤3-word labels listed; (3) restate QCR-066 self-refuting-row ban. With those three, the grader stopped comparing suppressed captions to narration and #13 flipped YES → 100. Lesson: a premium video's §3b/§5b captions ALWAYS trip the MOTION_DUPLICATE grader unless the suppressed windows are named in the ground truth as duplicate-impossible.

## QCR-158 — caption_windows.json MUST be derived from the composition's ACTUAL §3b/§5b window indices, never a stale plan
**Finding (caught pre-QC, not by Gemini):** the §5b avatar-floating-caption sits at W15 (46.594–48.948s) in `<Name>.tsx`, but the hand-authored `<Name>_caption_windows.json` listed `[43.903,46.594]` (W14 — the "O EMPREGADO" robot HERO). Result before the fix: the robot hero's burned subtitle was wrongly suppressed (blank) AND the §5b caption window kept its burned subtitle → a DOUBLE subtitle under the big floating caption (the exact thing suppression prevents).
**Root cause:** the suppression windows were copied from an earlier/different section layout in my head instead of being read off the FINAL composition's `W[...]` boundary array + the actual `<PremiumKineticCaption>` / `<PremiumAvatarCaption>` Sequence indices. The two drifted by one section.
**Permanent fix (process, enforced in `generate-subtitles` Step 1b):** build `caption_windows.json` by listing the EXACT `W[i]` start/end for every §3b `<PremiumKineticCaption>` and §5b `<PremiumAvatarCaption>` Sequence in the rendered `.tsx` — read them straight from the comp, not from memory. Then VERIFY with two frame grabs BEFORE QC: (a) a §5b/§3b window shows the big caption and NO bottom subtitle; (b) the immediately adjacent §4 hero shows its bottom subtitle. This 2-frame check catches an off-by-one suppression in seconds. Done on a real run → QC 100/100 first submit.

## QCR-159 — RE-CHECK the DUP-POST GUARD right BEFORE Phase 11, not only at resume start
**Incident:** resuming an `in_progress` run, the start-of-run DUP-POST GUARD (QCR-120) grepped `pipeline-log.csv` and found NO row → proceeded to rebuild. But a PARALLEL session was concurrently finishing the SAME run and POSTED it at 21:21:59 (log row written) ~20 min into this run. This run then posted a second set at 21:28 = a near-simultaneous DOUBLE-POST on every posting surface. Caught at the log-append step (the row was suddenly there) and the duplicate set was DELETE'd (HTTP 200, verified absent); no second log row written.
**Root cause:** the dedup grep ran ONCE at resume start. With two sessions racing the same `in_progress` run, the log row can appear AFTER the start check but BEFORE this run reaches Phase 11. A start-only check cannot see a row written mid-run by a sibling.
**Permanent fix (process, `post-and-log` Step 0 + `maestro-video-pipeline` resumability):** immediately BEFORE the first Metricool POST, RE-RUN the dedup grep on `pipeline-log.csv` for this run's `run_name`/`source_url`/`topic`. If a row now exists with `*_status` = `scheduled(...) draft:false autoPublish:true`, ABORT the post (the sibling already published) — do NOT post, do NOT add a row. Only post when the pre-post grep is still clean. A 1-line re-check at the top of Phase 11 closes the race that the start-only QCR-120 guard leaves open. (Also: a single user `run full pipeline` should not spawn two workers on the same `in_progress` state — but the pre-post grep is the reliable backstop regardless.)

### QCR-160 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-161: Greenscreen heroes must NOT have a GREEN body (chroma-key conflict) — esp. on ivory-emerald (QC 100/100)
- **Finding:** For the ivory-emerald (LIGHT) palette I prompted heroes "deep emerald" for contrast on cream (QCR-153). A deep-EMERALD scroll body then keyed catastrophically: chroma_key removed the emerald body (foreground ~3.7%, only the gold pen survived) OR kept the green bg (foreground ~71%). Emerald-green object on a green screen is unkeyable.
- **Root cause:** QCR-153 ("saturated/dark body so it shows on a LIGHT palette") + a GREEN palette name pushed the prompt toward GREEN object bodies, which collide with the chroma key. The two constraints (contrast-on-cream AND keyable) are both satisfied by NON-green dark colors.
- **Permanent fix (documented here + generate-image-assets practice):** for ANY greenscreen hero, the object BODY must be a NON-green saturated/dark color — CHARCOAL, NAVY, BORDEAUX, antique GOLD/BRASS, deep TEAL — never emerald/green/lime, even when the palette is ivory-EMERALD. Emerald belongs only in tiny accents, never the dominant body. Verified: re-prompting the pen as "dark charcoal-black page + gold pen" keyed clean (foreground 61.6%) and contrasts on cream.
- **Verification:** a real run 6 heroes (charcoal-page/gold-frame/magnifier/mic/clapper/robot) all keyed clean, composited over cream with good contrast, QC 100/100 first submit.

## QCR-162 — macOS has no `timeout`; premium QC ground-truth for caption-suppressed sections
- **Finding (friction):** `timeout 1200 npx remotion render ...` failed with `command not found: timeout` (macOS ships no GNU `timeout`); wasted one render attempt. **Fix:** never wrap remotion/ffmpeg in `timeout` on macOS — just run the command (renders self-terminate); if a hard cap is ever needed use `gtimeout` (coreutils) and check it exists first. Noted in generate-motion-remotion usage.
- **Finding (QC quality):** premium-classic a real run (Gemini 3 in Google Search, midnight-azure, 23 beats) scored **100/100 first submit** (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE). The MOTION_LIST ground truth explicitly listed the §3b/§5b caption windows as "burned subtitle SUPPRESSED → exactly ONE text layer, NOT double-subtitling" + hero labels with per-label word counts + concurrent subtitle → zero phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE. Keep building the premium MOTION_LIST this way (label + word-count + concurrent sub + explicit suppression note).
- **Verification:** Gemini QC 100/100, all checklist YES, no re-render.

### QCR-163: A hand-drawn fallback (image provider unreachable) passes QC 97/100 first submit
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 97/100, all 14 checklist YES, 1 MINOR -3)
- **Finding:** (a) premium asset-gen could not run — the image provider's API failed on EVERY request (host-level block/outage) → no image produced. Directive-sanctioned auto-fallback to hand-drawn-annotation (PIPELINE_DIRECTIVES §1 / QCR-133). The real Claude Code screenshot (capture-references, claude.com/product/claude-code, scroll evidence) still satisfied the named-tool reference chain. (b) Gemini SRT again emitted a degenerate MISORDERED entry (the final clause stamped at 1.0-3.3s, QCR-109 family) — removed manually before subtitles. (c) The §1 `HeadlineHook` in-panel block read as slightly off-center-left to Gemini (-3 MINOR, COMPLEMENT) — cosmetic, passed.
- **Root cause:** (a) the provider's API was unreachable (host-level block/outage), not a key problem. Retrying won't clear a host-level block. (b) Gemini sentence-segmentation occasionally reorders the trailing clause. (c) HeadlineHook uses `transform: scale(breathe)` + flex-end which can shift the optical center a few px.
- **Permanent fix:** (a) When the provider fails on EVERY request (not a single blip), treat it as genuinely down for THIS session and fall back to hand-drawn immediately rather than burning retries — log the host-block signature. (b) Keep the Phase-3 tail-drop + degenerate-misorder cleanup (drop any entry whose text duplicates a later clause but is stamped <5s). (c) Headline centering is within tolerance; no source edit (a -3 minor at 97 does not warrant a re-render).
- **Affected files:** `generate-image-assets/SKILL.md` (host-block fast-fallback note), `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (misorder guard already present), `qc-pipeline/active-rules.md`.
- **Verification:** a real run passed 97/100 first submit with the hand-drawn fallback + real screenshot; all 14 checklist items YES.

### QCR-164: Gemini ASR mis-transcribes "Claude Corps" as "Cloud Corps"
- **First found:** a real run (QC 100/100 — caught pre-QC at Phase 3)
- **Finding:** The Gemini SRT rendered the Anthropic program name "Claude Corps" as "Cloud Corps" (one entry). Same ASR failure family as QCR-069 ("Cloud Code"→"Claude Code"). Caught + hand-fixed in the SRT before subtitles, but it could have shipped the wrong brand on screen.
- **Root cause:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES had "Cloud Code"→"Claude Code" but no rule for "Cloud Corps".
- **Permanent fix:** Added `(re.compile(r"\bcloud\s+corps\b", re.I), "Claude Corps")` to BRAND_FIXES in `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` (right after the QCR-069 cloud-code rule). Now auto-corrected on transcription.
- **Affected files:** `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py`
- **Verification:** Future Claude Corps videos transcribe the brand correctly without a manual SRT edit.

### QCR-165 — retired in this edition (concerned a removed integration)

### QCR-166: Instagram browser post CROPPED a 9:16 video to SQUARE (Crop step defaults to 1:1) — user-reported (a real run)
- **Finding:** The IG browser Reel was posted CROPPED TO SQUARE (720x720) instead of full 9:16 vertical, even though the source is 1080x1920. The user caught it: "cropped as if it were a square when it is indeed a reels format video."
- **Root cause:** IG's web "Create new post" **Crop** step DEFAULTS to a **1:1 square** crop. The skill clicked **Next** without setting the aspect, so IG center-cropped the vertical video to a square Reel. Secondary bug: a repost attempt vanished because I navigated away during the "Sharing" spinner (cancelled the upload).
- **Permanent fix (post-browser-manual SKILL.md Instagram step):** on the Crop screen, BEFORE Next, click **"Select crop"** → choose **"9:16"** (portrait), confirm the preview fills the tall frame, THEN Next. After Share, **WAIT for "Your reel has been shared"** before navigating. Then **verify post-publish**: read the live reel's `video.videoWidth/videoHeight` — must be portrait (vh>vw, ≈9:16); if square (vw===vh), DELETE + repost with 9:16 until it verifies portrait.
- **Affected files:** `.claude/skills/post-browser-manual/SKILL.md` (Instagram step), `.claude/skills/post-and-log/SKILL.md` (references it).
- **Verification:** Deleted the square reel, reposted with 9:16 selected → the new reel verified 720x1280 (9:16 portrait), full vertical frame confirmed by screenshot.

## QCR-167 — CONDENSED QC prompt re-opens the QCR-013 SUBTITLE_WRAP hallucination
- **Finding:** iter1 = 64/100 with 7× phantom SUBTITLE_WRAP (-35) — Gemini concatenated consecutive 2-line viral-subtitle SCREENS into "broken into multiple small segments" wraps, plus 1 invalid SUBTITLE_STYLE comparing a MOTION label's white casing against the subtitle's yellow highlight (cross-layer).
- **Root cause:** the inline QC prompt was condensed and dropped the full QCR-013/QCR-014 SUBTITLE_WRAP ground-truth wording (single-screen-only test, no cross-frame reconstruction) + the motion-label-≠-subtitle-layer rule.
- **Fix:** restored the FULL hardened rule into the prompt (single screen with L3 OR a single line >18 chars is the ONLY wrap; forbidden to concatenate across screens; quote-the-offending-screen-or-drop; motion labels are a separate layer from subtitle highlights). Clean re-read → 100/100, all 15 checklist YES, zero fixes (QCR-007: ground-truth hardened, subtitles untouched — verified 0 real >2-line/>18-char screens).
- **Permanent takeaway:** ALWAYS include the verbatim QCR-013 SUBTITLE_WRAP block + the motion-label-layer note in the QC prompt; never ship a condensed rubric for section-grammar viral-subtitle videos.
