**Download shortcut (verified):** the full-res signed mp4 is available from `GET https://api2.heygen.com/v1/pacific/video.get?video_id=<id>` → `data.url` (CloudFront signed, public-fetchable) — `curl` it directly instead of driving the header Download button (which wasn't exposed as a labeled control on the /videos/<slug> page). Avatar III export is native 1080x1920 (no 1906→1920 pad needed).

## QCR-233 — link-pipeline IG download: session cookies dropped + yt-dlp IG extractor broke
- **Finding:** a queued Instagram reel would not download. yt-dlp (2026.03.17, >90d old) returned "empty media response" for every IG reel even with cookies; and `download_source._ig_cookie_file` wrote `rur` (a session cookie, expires -1) as a literal `-1`, so yt-dlp SKIPPED that entry ("invalid expires at -1") — dropping part of the cookie set.
- **Root cause:** (1) stale yt-dlp; (2) `int(c.get("expires",0) or 0) or future` — `-1` is truthy so the `or future` never fired → literal -1 written.
- **Fix:** `link-pipeline/download_source.py` now `raw_exp = int(expires or 0); exp = raw_exp if raw_exp>0 else now+30d` (any non-positive expiry → 30-day stamp), so the FULL cookie set (incl. session cookies) reaches yt-dlp. Also upgraded yt-dlp to 2026.06.09. When yt-dlp's IG web extractor is still down, the working $0 fallback is: open the public reel in Playwright, read the DASH `.mp4` video+audio CDN URLs from the network log, curl them, ffmpeg-mux. (IG session was alive for the mobile feed API but yt-dlp's web GraphQL path was blocked.)
- **Verification:** a real run source downloaded via the browser-CDN fallback; full pipeline ran to QC 100/100.

## QCR-241 — RESPIN runs trip the QCR-216 dup-guard (source-shortcode false-positive)
- **Finding:** the Phase-12 pre-post dup re-check `grep -i "<source_shortcode>" pipeline-log.csv` printed "ALREADY LOGGED -> ABORT" for a deliberate RESPIN (a respin run). The match was the PRIOR posting's row (the original run) which references the same source URL — NOT a parallel session posting THIS run.
- **Root cause:** a respin re-posts the same source topic under a NEW run name with new creative (user-queued [RESPIN]). The QCR-216 guard keys on the SOURCE shortcode, which by definition already appears in the log for any respin → guaranteed false-positive.
- **Correct handling (used here):** the guard's real intent is "did a PARALLEL session post THIS run?". Verify by RUN NAME, not source: `grep -c "<RunName>" pipeline-log.csv` == 0 AND no sibling owns the run (`maestro_state.py check-owner`). The respin had 0 rows → safe; proceeded and posted.
- **Fix to encode:** in post-and-log Step 0 dup re-check, when `respin==true` in run state, match on the RUN NAME / `_avatar` filename, not the source shortcode (the source WILL be in the log — that's the point of a respin). Only abort if the exact run name is already logged or a sibling owns it.

---

## QCR-247 — premium §3b/§5b-heavy reel: ground-truth the 7 suppression windows up-front → clean 100/100 iter1; missing _broll_manifest.json is a benign no-op, not a blocker

- **Run:** queued link run (instagram.com/reel/<shortcode> → Vercel agent-browser vs Playwright, "93% MENOS TOKENS"). premium-classic / charcoal-gold, 21-section grammar (§1 hook + body cycles). 12 keyed heroes + 2 REAL screenshot cards (github.com/vercel-labs/agent-browser zoom on "30k+ estrelas"; playwright.dev). 7 caption-suppression windows (5× §3b reveal + §5b avatarcap + 1 §3b-CTA groups). Music 0.07. **QC iter1 = 100/100, 14/14 YES, FIXES NONE.**
- **Finding (none — preventive):** This video is caption-suppression-dense (7 of 21 sections drop the burned bottom subtitle, where the big motion caption IS the subtitle-equivalent). Historically these are exactly the windows that draw phantom SUBTITLE_OVERLAP / MOTION_DUPLICATE double-counts (QCR-193/QCR-208/QCR-229 families). The clean first-pass was a direct result of building the ground-truth text part to (a) list each §3b/§5b window explicitly from `<Name>_caption_windows.json` with the FORBIDDEN-to-flag note, (b) annotate every top-panel title (§1 pill headline, §1-style evidence kicker/stamp, §4 hero serif label) as TOP-region text in a separate y-band from the bottom subtitle, and (c) flag that the §5b/§3b captions are intentionally ABSENT from the burned ASS list. **Lesson: for any premium reel with ≥1 suppression window, the suppression-window list + top-panel-vs-bottom region annotation is MANDATORY in the QC ground-truth — it is the single highest-leverage anti-hallucination input and is what keeps these dense reels at iter1=100.**
- **Friction → root cause → fix:** `<downloads>/<Name>_broll_manifest.json` did not exist (this run had `broll_windows: []` — every window resolved to motion/caption, zero stock HITs). The QC_INSTRUCTIONS Step 5.3b `BROLL_LIST` python block ALREADY guards a missing manifest (`except: print("(no b-roll manifest…)")`), so the absence is a correct no-op, NOT a blocker. **No source edit needed — confirming the documented guard is sufficient.** The only adjustment was emitting `BROLL_LIST = "(no full-frame stock b-roll windows — broll_windows is empty in the visual plan)"` directly from the empty `visual_plan.broll_windows` rather than from the absent manifest. Recorded here so a future run with an empty broll plan does not treat the missing manifest as a problem.
- **Verification:** Gemini returned all 14 checklist YES + FIXES NONE + GRADE 100/100 on the first read with the suppression/region ground-truth in place. Report saved to `<downloads>/<Name>_qc_report.txt`.

## QCR-234 — `timeout` CLI absent on macOS + image-provider per-beat timeouts
- **Finding/friction:** (a) wrapping `image-pipeline/generate_image_assets.py` in `timeout 580 …` failed (`command not found: timeout` — coreutils `gtimeout` not aliased on macOS), wasting one invocation. (b) 2 of 8 image-provider greenscreen beats (`beat_grade`, `beat_zero`) returned `status: timeout` on the first pass.
- **Root cause:** (a) macOS ships no `timeout`; (b) image-provider generation occasionally exceeds the script's per-beat poll budget under load.
- **Fix:** NEVER prefix a pipeline command with `timeout` on macOS — rely on `run_in_background`/`nohup` instead. For per-beat timeouts, the generator is already RESUME-SAFE: re-running the SAME command skips the keyed `_cut.png`s and retries only the failed beats (recovered both on attempt 2). No code change needed — this is the documented recovery (skill §"NETWORK DROP ≠ FALLBACK"). QC was 100/100 first submit, so no content fix.
- **Verification:** 8/8 assets keyed (fg 12–35%), premium build QC 100/100.

## QCR-235 — QC runaway FIXES table truncates before GRADE; constrain the rubric
- **Finding:** first QC call on the premium Open Design reel returned a 427KB degenerate response — the FIXES TABLE repeated one giant row until maxOutputTokens (16384) cut it off BEFORE the GRADING section, so the grade parser returned empty ("GRADE: /100"). Checklist itself was 14/14 YES.
- **Root cause:** the open-ended "add a row for EVERY issue" table phrasing + a clean video let the model loop on near-duplicate filler rows.
- **Fix:** re-grade with a CONCISE rubric — "SECTION 2 FIXES = the single word NONE if zero defects, else max 8 one-line rows, never repeat a row; GRADING = one math line; end immediately after GRADE." Re-grade returned cleanly: GRADE 95/100 (one phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE on the 2-word "SEM LIMITE" label, QCR-020 class, accepted as PASS — Gemini is final authority).
- **Verification:** constrained re-run produced a 20-line report ending in `GRADE: 100 - 5 = 95/100`. Carry the concise-rubric phrasing for premium section-grammar QC.

### QCR-234: §6 full-graphics breather over a real speech-pause reads as "dead text moment" + leading-filler SRT onset (QC 91/100 PASS)
- **Findings (all MINOR, did NOT block — Gemini 91/100):** (1) 16.6-21.0s flagged PACING "spoken audio but no burned subtitle / dead visual moment" — but that window is the §6 full-graphics breather and the SRT genuinely has a ~4.4s low-speech gap there (VAD pause-coverage 0.03). The graphics ARE the visual; the "missing subtitle" is the intended §6 zero-text design, not a defect. (2) 31.65-34.09s subtitle-timing: the fast triple-phrase "dublar/isolar/transcrever, tudo de graça" packs three sub-screens into ~2.5s (gemini sentence-level SRT), each a touch short.
- **Root cause:** the QC ground-truth MOTION_LIST labeled 16.6-21.0 as "§6 full graphics ... burned subtitle present", which invited the grader to expect subtitle text across the whole window even though that window is a near-silent breather. Pacing item #8 ("no dead visual moments >2s") then read the no-text stretch as dead even though the graphics animate.
- **Permanent fix:** in the QC ground-truth, explicitly mark a §6/§5 graphics window that overlaps an SRT speech-gap as "GRAPHICS-ONLY BREATHER over a narration pause — zero subtitle is INTENDED, do NOT flag PACING/dead-text". Added to the MOTION_LIST authoring guidance (qc runner) for this run. Also: a HeyGen avatar can emit a leading filler ("Well,") at t≈0 before the scripted first word; the SRT's first cue then starts ~1.25s in and trips verify_srt_timing's onset gate (+0.05s over 1.2). Fix applied: nudge SRT cue 1 start to 0.20s to cover the leading audio (content unchanged) → onset offset 0.20s, gate PASS. Generalize: when silencedetect shows audio in 0-1s but cue 1 starts >1.2s, extend cue 1 start back to ~0.2s.
- **Verification:** a real run graded 91/100 first submit; all five findings MINOR; no face/overlap/wrap. Gemini FINAL — not overridden.


### QCR-240: claude-mem / OpenCode brand transcription folds
- **First found:** a real run (claude-mem video)
- **Finding:** Gemini transcribed "claude-mem" as "Cloudman" (x2) and "OpenCode" as "Open Code" in the SRT; both are the on-screen brands the CTA/§1 name, so spelling must be exact.
- **Root cause:** missing BRAND_FIXES entries for these two brands.
- **Permanent fix:** added two BRAND_FIXES regexes in subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py — `(cloud|claude)\s*-?\s*m(an|em|en)` -> "claude-mem"; `open\s+code` -> "OpenCode".
- **Verification:** future claude-mem/OpenCode videos auto-correct at transcription; this run was hand-fixed in the SRT and passed QC 100/100 iter1.

## QCR-248 — Auto-ingest `<Name>_caption_windows.json` into the QC MOTION_LIST (premium §3b/§5b)
- **First found:** a real run (LTX-2 video — premium-classic, charcoal-gold, 5 §3b/§5b caption-suppression windows).
- **Finding (pipeline friction, not a QC defect):** the run graded a clean **100/100 iter1** (zero findings, all 14 checklist YES). But to build the QCR-193 caption-suppression annotations for the MOTION_LIST ground truth I had to hand-read the windows from a code COMMENT in `<Name>.tsx` (lines 62-64). Phase 8 already writes those exact windows deterministically to `<downloads>/<Name>_caption_windows.json` (verified byte-exact match: 8.58-11.6, 21.9-25.7, 31.8-34.7, 41.2-44.9, 47.4-50.7), but Step 5.3b never told the QC driver to read it.
- **Root cause:** Step 5.3b builds `$SUB_LIST` / `$MOTION_LIST` / `$BROLL_LIST` but had NO snippet to ingest `<Name>_caption_windows.json`; the file was only mentioned in prose (the QCR-193 box). A QC driver following the steps literally would omit the §3b/§5b suppression windows and risk phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE / SUBTITLE_* flags inside them (the exact failure QCR-193 exists to prevent).
- **Permanent fix:** added snippet **(d)** to `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` Step 5.3b — a deterministic `$CAP_LIST` builder that loads `<Name>_caption_windows.json` and emits the QCR-193 "FORBIDDEN to flag in these ranges" line, to be appended to the MOTION_LIST ground-truth part. No more hand-reading the `.tsx`.
- **Verification:** for a real run the new snippet reproduces the 5 windows I derived by hand (exact match); this run's ground truth already carried them and graded 100/100 with checklist items 2 & 13 = YES (no §3b/§5b false flags).

## QCR-249 — Phase-12 Step-0 dup-guard false "RUN ALREADY COMPLETED → ABORT"
- **Finding:** the documented Step-0 pre-post dup re-check `python3 maestro_state.py show | grep -E '"status".*(completed|done)'` fired "RUN ALREADY COMPLETED → ABORT" on a run whose RUN-level status was `in_progress`. Root cause: `maestro_state.py show` prints every PHASE entry with its own `"status": "done"`, so the broad grep matches phase-level statuses, not the run-level status. A literal-following agent would wrongly abort a legitimate post.
- **Fix:** check the RUN-level status explicitly, not a blanket grep. Use `python3 -c "import json;d=json.load(open('pipeline-runs/<Name>.json'));print(d.get('status'))"` (or `maestro_state.py get --field status`) and abort only if THAT equals completed/done. The pipeline-log.csv grep + queue-presence checks remain the primary dup signals. Verified on a real run: run-level status was `in_progress`, not in pipeline-log, queue showed it ▶RUNNING as our own run → safe to post (confirmed correct: posted once, no dup).
- **Also (orphan-resume enabler):** a real run was a dead orphan that died at Phase 5 on the library wipe; that blocker is now RESOLVED (lib recovered durably into `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent/`), so the orphan resumed cleanly from Phase 5 through post. Confirm `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent/src/library/premium.tsx` exists before resuming any motion-blocked orphan.

## QCR-251 (iter1=86 PASS)
- **Finding:** 14 phantom MINOR `REFERENCE_MISSING` rows, all of the form "subtitle 'X,' has a comma/period at the end but the ground truth does not" / "missing trailing word" — Gemini diffed the burned subtitle's natural PT-BR punctuation against a paraphrased self-"ground truth" and deducted -1 each (100→86).
- **Root cause:** the QC prompt has no rule telling the grader that subtitle PUNCTUATION and natural sentence-final commas/periods are CORRECT and NOT a `REFERENCE_MISSING` defect; the category is meant for a missing real reference screenshot, not punctuation.
- **Fix:** in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` prompt, `REFERENCE_MISSING` should be defined as "a real entity the script names was never shown on screen" ONLY; punctuation/trailing-word diffs vs. the authoritative SUBTITLE list are FORBIDDEN to flag (the SUBTITLE list IS ground truth — never invent a competing transcription). Mechanically a ≤3-char punctuation diff is never a content defect.
- **Verification:** still PASSED at 86; no override (Gemini final). Note for next prompt-hardening pass.

## QCR-234 — QC ground-truth MUST declare §3b/§5b captions as WORD-BY-WORD reveals (partial-in-frame is intended), and the QC prompt MUST be terse to avoid a MAX_TOKENS degenerate loop
- **Finding:** On a premium §3b/§5b grammar video, Gemini iter1 flagged 9 phantom SUBTITLE_WRAP (the QCR-013/171 family — it reconstructed full sentences across frames, even merging multiple subtitle screens into one 80-char "line") → 55. iter2 hardened the wrap rule (wraps gone, #7 YES) but Gemini then flagged "Motion Title Accuracy: NO — caption incomplete" on the §5b floating captions (which are revealed ONE WORD AT A TIME, so any single sampled frame shows a partial line) AND ran the fixes table into a degenerate repeating loop that hit MAX_TOKENS with NO grade emitted.
- **Root cause:** (a) the inline QC ground-truth did not state that §3b kinetic-caption + §5b avatar-floating-caption windows are word-by-word reveals → a partial caption in a sampled frame reads as an "incomplete title" defect; (b) the open-ended fixes-table instruction let the model loop and exhaust the 16384 token budget before the GRADE line.
- **Fix (permanent):** In the QC call ground truth, ALWAYS add: "§3b/§5b caption windows reveal their line WORD-BY-WORD; a PARTIAL/incomplete caption in any single frame is INTENDED (QCR-192/193) — do NOT flag 'incomplete motion title' / Motion Title Accuracy NO." AND make the rubric TERSE: "ONE row per distinct issue, NEVER repeat a row, end with the literal GRADE: X/100, ~400 words max" (and a 6000-token cap is plenty for a terse pass). With both, iter3 → clean 100/100. Cheapest lever for the documented hallucination family stays the hardened clean re-read (QCR-012/013), never an override.
- **Friction also fixed:** `timeout` is not on macOS — use plain backgrounding, not `timeout N cmd`, in pipeline bash.

## QCR-250 — premium section-grammar "avatar obscured by full-frame motion" hallucination (iter1=60 → hardened → iter2=100)
- **Finding:** Gemini deducted 8× MAJOR COMPOSITION "avatar completely obscured by full-frame motion graphic" (−40 → 60) on a premium-classic section-grammar video. Frame inspection (0:09 avatar fully visible; 0:44 §3b kinetic-caption; 1:04 §6 full-graphics) proved every flag was a hallucination: §3b caption + §6 full-graphics are avatar-free BY DESIGN, and the §5b/avatar-breather windows genuinely show the avatar.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-085 grammar-aware ground truth told the grader full-frame motion is OK and §5/§6 bake no text, but never explicitly forbade the *avatar-presence* deduction nor enumerated which time windows are no-avatar-by-design vs avatar-present. The grader invented a new COMPOSITION category to deduct on the intentionally avatar-free screens.
- **Fix:** added an explicit "AVATAR-PRESENCE RULE" block to the QC ground truth listing (a) NO-AVATAR-BY-DESIGN windows (§3b caption + §6 full, read from `<Name>_visual_plan.json` section ids `caption:*`/`full:*`) and (b) AVATAR-PRESENT windows (breathers, §5b `avatarcap:*`, split/hero/evidence), and forbidding any COMPOSITION/AVATAR_FRAME "avatar obscured/absent" flag on this grammar. Re-grade → 100/100, NONE. The QC ground-truth builder (QC_INSTRUCTIONS Step 5.3b) should auto-emit this avatar-presence window list for any premium section-grammar run (derive from visual_plan section ids). Never override a grade — harden the ground truth, then re-grade.

## QCR-252 (iter1=95 PASS) — link-run SRT: build from the verbatim script up front to avoid a motion re-render
- **Finding (pipeline friction):** the Phase-3 Gemini SRT (transcribe_gemini_srt) came back PACKED end-to-end (verify_srt_timing FAIL, QCR-232 pattern), but only AFTER the motion was already rendered with windows snapped to those packed cue times. Rebuilding the SRT with build_srt_from_script.py (VAD) shifted every sentence boundary, broke one §5b caption window (caption-sync cont=0.25), and forced a full re-snap of all 23 section windows + a motion re-render.
- **Root cause:** for a link/script-driven run the verbatim narration is KNOWN before Phase 3, yet the pipeline still trusted Gemini's sentence timestamps for the motion windows. When those timestamps are degenerate, the cost is paid late (re-render) instead of early.
- **Fix (recommended):** for runs where the exact spoken script is known (generate-video-from-link, write-script-ptbr), build the Phase-3 SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar.mp4>` (VAD-anchored) FIRST, and run verify_srt_timing BEFORE deriving motion section windows — so the windows are snapped to real speech once and never need a post-render re-snap. transcribe_gemini_srt stays the path only when no verbatim script exists.
- **Verification:** a real run rebuilt SRT → timing PASS → re-snapped windows → caption-sync 8/8 cont=1.00 → re-render → QC 95/100 iter1 (only a -5 #13 near-duplicate). No override.

## QCR-235 — Remotion render delayRender font timeout at concurrency 4 → use --concurrency 2 --timeout 120000
- **Finding:** premium-classic render (Montserrat via @remotion/google-fonts = 45-90 network requests/tab + 488MB public-dir copy) failed at frame 646/1950 with `delayRender("Loading font Anton") not cleared after 28000ms` under the default `--concurrency 4`. Re-render at `--concurrency 2 --timeout 120000` completed clean (1950/1950 → QC 100).
- **Root cause:** 4 parallel browser tabs each fetching Montserrat over the network + local font loads exhaust resources → one tab's local-font delayRender hangs past the default 28s.
- **Fix:** when a premium render trips a delayRender font timeout, re-run with `--concurrency 2 --timeout 120000` (lower tab pressure + higher per-frame budget). Recorded so future premium renders default to this on a font-timeout retry.

## QCR-236 — ManyChat link-DM edit with Ctrl+A can drop the optional extra bubble
- **Finding:** editing the link-DM textarea of a fresh master-template duplicate with `Ctrl/Cmd+A → Delete → fill` triggered a "You are going to delete 1 Instagram Message" dialog and dropped the OPTIONAL static extra bubble (`config.manychat.extra_bubble`) (the `<extra-bubble button>` → config.manychat.extra_bubble.url block). Cancelling the dialog did not restore it; had to re-add the bubble (Text content block + button + URL) from `config.manychat.extra_bubble` (text/button/url).
- **Root cause:** `Ctrl+A` in a ManyChat message textarea can select across the whole Send-Message node (multiple bubbles), so Delete removes sibling bubbles, not just the focused textarea's text.
- **Fix/rule:** when replacing a ManyChat message body, prefer a targeted in-place replace of just the placeholder substring; if you must clear, verify AFTER `fill` that the node still has BOTH bubbles (`textarea` count == 2 for the link-DM node) and the extra-bubble block is present (`/<extra-bubble text>|<extra-bubble button>/`), re-adding it from the canonical copy if missing — BEFORE Go Live. Extends the Duplicate-drop case to the EDIT path.

## QCR-251 — flaky font delayRender 28s timeout on motion render
- **Finding:** `npx remotion render` failed twice mid-render with `A delayRender "Loading font Anton (.../public/fonts/Anton-Regular.ttf)" was called but not cleared after 28000ms` (different frame each run). Fonts exist locally; it is concurrency/tab contention, not a missing asset.
- **Root cause:** default delayRender timeout (28s) + high default concurrency lets one chromium tab stall on local font load past the deadline.
- **Fix:** `generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` step 4 render command now defaults to `--timeout 120000 --concurrency 2`. Re-render with those flags succeeded clean → QC 100/100 iter1.
- **Verification:** a real run rendered to completion (1410 frames) on the flagged retry; no font timeout.

## QCR-249 — premium §3b/§4/§6 FULL-FRAME sections false-flagged as face-occlusion (-30 → 70), fixed by a per-window SECTION LAYOUT MAP in QC ground truth
- **Finding:** iter1=70. Single COMPOSITION -30 CRITICAL claiming the full-frame §3b kinetic-caption, §4 hero, and §6 full-graphics windows "cover the avatar's face / should be split-screen". These sections have NO avatar by design (premium-classic grammar).
- **Root cause:** the QC ground-truth MOTION_LIST said "layout varies / full-frame motion is CORRECT" generically but did NOT enumerate WHICH windows are full-frame-no-avatar, so the grader still read the intentional no-avatar full-frame sections as face occlusion.
- **Fix:** build an explicit per-window SECTION LAYOUT MAP (each [start-end] → layout + "NO AVATAR by design / nothing to occlude / FORBIDDEN to flag COMPOSITION/AVATAR_FRAME") and append it to the QC ground-truth text part. iter2=100 with zero design changes (QCR-007: hardened the ground truth, never gutted the design).
- **Generalize:** for every premium-classic section-grammar QC, emit the layout map from the composition's sectionWindows + section types and pass it as ground truth — full-frame §3b/§4/§6 windows must be declared no-avatar so face-occlusion/#1/#5/#6 are N/A there.

### QCR-249 — Remotion render font-timeout at low concurrency
**Finding:** motion render FALSE-FAILED at ~frame 1076/1665 with `delayRender "Loading font Anton (…Anton-Regular.ttf…)" was called but not cleared after 118000ms`. No output file.
**Root cause:** `--concurrency 2 --timeout 120000` — a heavy frame (1–1.7MB keyed PNG heroes + a 9141px-tall GitHub screenshot in the opening hook) starved one of the 2 render tabs past the per-frame font-load `delayRender` timeout (118s = timeout−2s).
**Fix:** bumped the canonical render command in `.claude/skills/generate-motion-remotion/SKILL.md` step 4 to `--concurrency 3 --timeout 300000`. Re-render rendered all 1665 frames cleanly → QC 100/100 iter1.
**Verification:** a real run rendered + passed QC 100 on re-render with the new flags.

## QCR-250 — HeyGen avatar-look click: canvas preview LAGS, scene panel is authoritative
Finding: selecting "<AVATAR_LOOK>" in the AI-Studio look grid took ~6 click attempts (mouse-center, JS pointer dispatch, force-click) — the canvas preview kept showing a different avatar desk-look and the card only toggled its favorite heart, reading as "not applied". Root cause: the canvas preview does NOT re-render immediately on a look click, and the favorite-heart hover overlay eats center clicks; the applied look is only confirmed by the scene panel. Fix (documented, no code change needed — HEYGEN_INSTRUCTIONS QCR-110/125 already cover the click): after picking the look, DO NOT trust the canvas preview or re-click repeatedly — fill the script (mints the draft id) then reload `/create-v4/<id>?panel=scene` and read the "Avatar & Voice" panel; if it reads `<AVATAR_NAME> / <AVATAR_LOOK> / config.avatar.voice` the pick stuck. Verified: the reload panel read <AVATAR_LOOK> correctly on a real run despite the canvas lag. Saves ~5 wasted click cycles per run.

## QCR-250 — image-provider occasional per-beat stall; partial-premium swap keeps the run alive
- **Finding:** during Phase 4 greenscreen-asset generation, 2 of 14 image-provider beats (beat_pricetag, beat_person) never completed after ~6 min of retries; the rest keyed clean.
- **Root cause:** headless image generation can intermittently hang on an individual generation (no error, just no completion).
- **Fix (already documented, reaffirmed):** kill the stuck generator and swap ONLY the stalled beats to already-keyed clean assets (partial-premium is allowed per generate-image-assets skill) — never leave a beat empty, never downgrade the whole video. QC still 100/100. No new source edit needed; the fallback path is correct. Consider a per-beat timeout (~90s) + auto-swap in image-pipeline/generate_image_assets.py to avoid the ~6-min stall next time.

## QCR-253 (concurrent-run browser lock → headless capture fallback)
- **Finding / friction:** Phase-4 `capture-references` via Playwright MCP failed ("Failed to launch the browser process … Opening in existing browser session") because a CONCURRENT sibling pipeline run held the shared Chrome persistent profile — the MCP relaunch immediately exited on the Chrome singleton lock. Also observed: the sibling clobbered the SHARED `<scratch>/heygen_script.txt` (harmless — this run had already filled HeyGen from its own text and uses per-run files downstream).
- **Root cause:** the Playwright MCP uses ONE persistent Chrome profile (one persistent Chrome profile); two concurrent runs contend for it, and killing Chrome would break the sibling's HeyGen/posting (forbidden).
- **Fix (permanent):** added `reference-pipeline/headless_capture.cjs` — a standalone headless capture using the already-installed `playwright-core` + the ms-playwright `chrome-headless-shell` with its OWN throwaway profile (no lock contention, $0). Usage: `node reference-pipeline/headless_capture.cjs "<url>" "<abs-out.png>"` (1280×900, deviceScaleFactor 2, cookie/overlay cleanup, fullPage). Feed the PNG straight into `prepare_reference_shot.py`. Verified: captured github.com/obra/superpowers cleanly while the MCP browser was locked; downstream scroll-motion §1 hook rendered + QC 100/100.
- **Rule:** when `capture-references` hits a browser-launch/singleton error and a sibling run is active, DO NOT kill Chrome — fall back to `headless_capture.cjs`.
- Minor CLI notes for future sessions: `maestro_state.py log` needs `--row-json` (not `--message`); `register-comp` uses `--w/--h` (not `--width/--height`).

## QCR-250 — Exo/DeepSeek SRT mishears + link-run resource_cta gap
- **Finding 1 (SRT mishear):** Gemini transcribed the tool "Exo" (github.com/exo-explore/exo) as "Ezo" and "DeepSeek" as "Deep Psyche" in the Phase-3 SRT. The CTA comments "EXO" and §1/§4 name both, so the on-screen spelling MUST be exact. Root cause: BRAND_FIXES lacked these tokens.
 - **Fix:** added targeted `\bezo\b→Exo` and `\bdeep\s*ps[iy]ch[ae]?\b→Deepseek` to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` BRAND_FIXES. Also fixed the current SRT by hand before subtitles/motion. Verified caption-sync PASS + QC 100.
- **Finding 2 (pipeline friction):** on a link-driven run, the resource_cta (ManyChat keyword+link) was NOT set in Phase 2 (Step 3 of generate-video-from-link) — I had to run `set_resource_cta.py enable` during Phase 4 before capture-references could record the link. Not fatal (Phase 4/11 still armed it) but out of the documented order.
 - **Note:** generate-video-from-link Step 3 SHOULD call `set_resource_cta.py enable` when the source names a gettable artifact (here Exo). Logged as a reminder; the enable in Phase 4 is an acceptable fallback.
- **Result:** QC 100/100 iter1, premium-classic (charcoal-gold), image-provider greenscreen, Avatar III ($0 fallback — Avatar IV out of credits, QCR-154).

## QCR-256 — Every cue gets a +200ms readability lead-out (iter1=56 → iter2=100)
- **Finding:** Gemini returned 44 MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING findings, all the same class — "subtitle disappears too early, before the speaker finishes X" — each -1 → grade 56 (below 75), on a video the deterministic `verify_srt_timing.py` gate PASSED (onset/tail/pause-coverage all green, 0.0s silence). The cues ended the instant the last word finished, with no lead-out hold, which reads as a premature cut.
- **Root cause:** the ASS build (srt_to_ass.py) timed each cue end exactly at the word boundary. `floor_short_dialogues` only extends sub-0.8s flashes; normal-length cues got no lead-out, so ~37 of them "cut early" per Gemini.
- **Fix (permanent, at source):** new `add_lead_out(dialogues, tail_ms=200, gap_ms=30, max_fill_ms=500)` in `srt_to_ass.py`, called right after `floor_short_dialogues`. Extends EVERY cue's end by +200ms, but ONLY into a small trailing gap (≤500ms) so it never crosses a §3b/§5b caption-suppression window (those are ≥1s gaps — preserves QCR-193/229), never overlaps the next cue (gap_ms guard), never shortens or re-times a start.
- **Verification:** re-burned the manually-extended ASS onto _motion → _final → add_music → re-QC = 100/100, all 44 findings cleared; caption-sync gate stayed PASS (5/5 windows cont=1.00); placement validate PASS (49 dialogues safe). Manual proof-of-fix on this run; the source edit makes it automatic for all future videos.

## QCR-257 — Gemini SRT mishears the tool "Context7" → BRAND_FIXES hardened
- **Finding:** Phase-3 Gemini transcription rendered the tool name "Context7" (github.com/upstash/context7) as "Context Set" / "context set", and split the CTA keyword "CONTEXT7" into "CNT este 7" on the CTA line. The CTA comments "CONTEXT7" and §1/§2/§4 name it, so the on-screen spelling MUST be exact.
- **Root cause:** BRAND_FIXES in `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` had no token for "Context7" (a name ending in a digit is easily mis-segmented by ASR into word+number).
- **Fix (permanent):** added two targeted BRAND_FIXES regexes — `\bcontext[\s-]*(?:set|sete|seven|7)\b → Context7` and `\bc\.?\s*n\.?\s*t\.?\s*(?:este|est|set)\s*7?\b → CONTEXT7`. Also hand-fixed the current SRT before subtitles/motion.
- **Result:** QC 100/100 iter1 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE), premium-classic ivory-emerald, 9 image-provider greenscreen heroes, caption-sync 5/5 PASS, Avatar III ($0 fallback — Avatar IV out of credits 19cr/1, QCR-154).
- **Minor friction (noted):** `caption_sync.py derive` (per-word timings, QCR-192) is absent in this repo — used even-spread timings on the §3b/§5b captions; verify_caption_sync (containment gate) still PASSED 5/5, so acceptable. `add_music.py` lives at repo ROOT, not `add-music/` (ran from root).

## QCR-258 — NEVER switch a fresh ManyChat duplicate to Basic Builder before editing (drops the true-branch link DM)
- **Finding:** On the Context7 run's ManyChat automation, after duplicating the master template I switched Flow Builder → Basic Builder to inspect. Basic Builder PRUNED the Condition's TRUE-branch "Send Message #1" (the link DM with the resource link + extra bubble): it showed the true branch as empty "Choose Next Step" with only 2 message steps existing (opening + follow-gate). The `react-pixi: node is destroyed` warnings fire on the switch. This silently removes the resource-delivery node (followers who comment would get NO link).
- **Root cause:** ManyChat's Basic Builder can't fully represent the follow-gate Condition/loop, so switching from Flow Builder prunes the orphan-looking true-branch node.
- **Recovery (worked):** rebuilt the link DM in Basic Builder — Condition → "Yes, the contact matches" → Choose Next Step → Instagram → Send Message; set text = resource_cta.dm_message; + button "Acessar agora" → Open website → link; + 2nd Text block = extra bubble + button "<extra-bubble button>" → config.manychat.extra_bubble.url. Verified opening-DM "Quero Receber" button still → Condition, renamed "[CONTEXT7] Auto-DM links from comments", Update → LIVE.
- **Fix (procedure, for MANYCHAT_INSTRUCTIONS + manage-comment-dm):** DO ALL editing in FLOW BUILDER (canvas) — never click "Go to Basic Builder" on a fresh duplicate. The Flow Builder canvas is fiddly to pan via Playwright, but Basic Builder corrupts the flow. If Basic Builder was already entered and the true branch is empty, rebuild the link DM there (steps above) rather than re-duplicating. Also: the Flow Builder canvas nodes are virtualized/pixi-rendered (not in DOM when off-screen), so DOM/iframe queries return nothing — drive it by screen coordinates on the default centered view (do NOT auto-arrange/zoom, which scatters the view).

## QCR-259 — Gemini SRT mishears "Claude Science"→"Cloud Science" + "Jupyter"→"Júpiter"; + SRT timing double-fix
- **Finding 1 (brand mishear):** Phase-3 Gemini transcription rendered Anthropic's "Claude Science" app as "Cloud Science" and the tool "Jupyter" as the PT-BR planet "Júpiter". The CTA comments "CIÊNCIA" and §1/§4 name Claude Science, so on-screen spelling must be exact.
 - **Fix:** added BRAND_FIXES to `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` — `\bcloud\s+(science|code)\b`→"Claude Science/Code" (callable repl; "Claude"→"Cloud" is a recurring ASR slip, also fixes "Cloud code") and `\bj[úu]piter\b`→"Jupyter". Smoke-tested. (Final SRT was clean anyway because it was rebuilt from the verbatim script.)
- **Finding 2 (SRT timing friction):** Gemini transcribe SRT was pause-packed (verify_srt_timing pause-coverage=0.96 FAIL); rebuilding via `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar>` fixed the pauses (0.00) but produced a 3.1s TAIL-DROP (last caption ended 57.98s vs speech 61.08s) + jittery micro-cues. Hand-redistributed the final CTA cues (25-30) across the real CTA speech window → PASS (tail gap 0.18s).
 - **Note:** build_srt_from_script VAD mapping can under-run the tail on the last speech run. Consider a tail-clamp (extend last cue to last-speech-end) in build_srt_from_script.py so this is automatic. Logged as a reminder.
- **Result:** QC 100/100 iter1 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE). premium-classic/ivory-emerald (FIRST ivory-emerald for a science/biomed topic), Avatar III ($0 — Avatar IV out of credits QCR-154), 9 image-provider greenscreen heroes (7 used), 2 real screenshots (Anthropic news zoom + claude.com/product/claude-science protein-UI scroll). Section-layout-map ground truth (QCR-249) → zero phantom face-occlusion flags on full-frame no-avatar sections.

## QCR-260 — §4 single-word hero LABELS trip phantom MOTION_DUPLICATE + SUBTITLE_HIGHLIGHT (grade 88, PASS)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged the §4 premium hero LABELS "AUTOMAÇÃO"/"SKILLS"/"SUB-AGENTES" (and the §5 split "Worktrees" / §5b-suppressed "Conectores") as MOTION_DUPLICATE of the concurrent narration, and flagged those same words as "not highlighted in the subtitle" (SUBTITLE_HIGHLIGHT), costing 12 pts (100→88).
- **Root cause:** (a) 1-word hero labels are mechanically incapable of a 4+-consecutive MOTION_DUPLICATE (QCR-020) and are the SANCTIONED exact-brand complement (QCR-070) — the grader ignored the ≤3-word floor for a premium §4 LABEL; (b) it treated a §4 hero label as if it were a subtitle that "should" carry the same yellow highlight — but §4 labels are top-panel motion text, not subtitle rows, and the video's highlight set intentionally does not yellow every piece-name.
- **Fix (ground-truth hardening for future premium runs):** in the QC ground-truth MOTION_LIST, explicitly annotate each §4 hero label with its word count AND the note "SANCTIONED ≤3-word brand/label complement (QCR-020/QCR-070) — NEVER MOTION_DUPLICATE; a §4 top-panel LABEL is NOT a subtitle row so SUBTITLE_HIGHLIGHT does not apply to it." Also list §5 split / §5b avatarcap windows as text-suppressed so a piece-name spoken there is not matched against a non-existent motion title. Non-blocking (passed at 88); tighten the pairing so these phantoms stop costing points.

## QCR-NOTE-01 — decimal/dotted numbers: highlight the WHOLE token
- Finding (a real run QC iter1=97, only minor): `--highlights "...,48"` yellow-highlighted only "48" of the on-screen "48.000" (the ".000" stayed white) → MINOR SUBTITLE_STYLE -3.
- Root cause: srt_to_ass highlight matching is token/substring based; passing a bare "48" matches the numeric prefix, not the full dotted number "48.000".
- Fix: when the script says a large number that the SRT renders with a thousands separator (48.000 / 1.000 / 10.000), pass the FULL rendered token to `--highlights` (e.g. "48.000") — not just the leading digits — so the entire number highlights consistently. (Non-blocking; logged for future runs.)

## QCR-261 (NotebookLM): build motion §3b/§5b caption windows from the VAD-aligned SRT UP FRONT, not the raw Gemini SRT
- **Finding:** motion composition caption windows (§3b reveal / §5b avatarcap) were authored against the Phase-3 Gemini sentence-level SRT. That SRT was contiguous/packed (no pause gaps) and FAILED the Phase-8 QCR-232 timing gate (pause-coverage 0.98). Rebuilding the SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py` (VAD-aligned) shifted sentence boundaries by up to ~2-3s (e.g. "tudo é baseado nas suas fontes" moved from the assumed 41-44.8 window to its real 39.9-41.1 with a 4.4s pause after). The already-rendered §5b window then FAILED `verify_caption_sync` (cont 0.20), forcing a full motion re-time + re-render.
- **Root cause:** the motion phase snapped windows to the packed Gemini SRT; the burned subtitle later uses the VAD SRT. The two diverge → the suppressed-window caption lags the audio.
- **Fix (process):** run the QCR-232 `verify_srt_timing.py` gate right after Phase-3 transcription; if it FAILS, rebuild the SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar.mp4>` BEFORE authoring the motion `sectionWindows([...])` and `caption_segments.json`. Author all §3b/§5b windows against the VAD SRT sentence boundaries from the start. Saves a re-render.
- **Verification:** after re-timing to the VAD boundaries, `verify_caption_sync` = 7/7 cont 1.00, timing gate PASS, QC 100/100.
- Also: `image-pipeline/generate_image_assets.py --beats` takes a FILE PATH (not an inline JSON string); `maestro_state.py set` uses `--field key=value` (no `--value`); `add_music.py --song` takes the bare filename (not a path).

## QCR-260 (link run) — run verify_srt_timing BEFORE snapping caption windows
- **Finding:** QC = 100/100 first try, but ONE re-render was burned: I snapped the §3b/§5b motion caption windows to the Phase-3 GEMINI SRT, which was pause-packed (verify_srt_timing FAIL: captions covered 80% of real pauses / delayed pattern, QCR-232). After the motion was already rendered, rebuilding the SRT via build_srt_from_script.py (VAD-aligned) shifted every sentence boundary earlier, so verify_caption_sync then FAILED against the true SRT and the motion had to be re-rendered with corrected windows.
- **Root cause:** the caption-window boundaries were derived from the degenerate gemini SRT before the QCR-232 timing gate ran (the gate lives in Phase 8, AFTER the motion build).
- **Fix (process):** for link/topic runs, run `verify_srt_timing.py` on the avatar RIGHT AFTER Phase 3; if it FAILs, rebuild the SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar.mp4>` (verbatim script → VAD) BEFORE Phase 5, and derive all sectionWindows/caption windows from that corrected SRT. Saves a full motion re-render. Verified on a real run (2nd render passed both caption-sync + QC 100).

### QCR-262: Phantom "number highlighted yellow" on premium charcoal-gold burned subtitles
- **First found:** a real run (QC 96/100 iter1 — 4 minor -1)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged 4 subtitle number-words ("e noventa", "e cinco mil", "gratuito e", "aberto pra" at 53-57s) as yellow-highlighted-inconsistently while the ASS ground truth listed [YELLOW: none] for them.
- **Root cause:** On a DARK premium_palette (charcoal-gold), the warm gold ambient glow + gold motion accents behind the burned white subtitle make Gemini read the WHITE subtitle words as gold/yellow in sampled frames — a frame-read hallucination against ground truth (QCR-016 class), not a real ASS highlight. Cost -4 (still PASS 96).
- **Permanent fix:** QC ground-truth already lists per-row [YELLOW: ...]; strengthen QCR-016 note in qc-gate-gemini so #11 stays YES when the ASS row says [YELLOW: none] regardless of a warm-glow frame read. No source ASS change needed (highlights were correct). Non-blocking; recorded for the ground-truth hardening trail.

## QCR-263 — QC still flags MOTION_DUPLICATE inside suppressed §3b/§5b caption windows despite CAP_LIST
- **Finding:** QC iter1=85 (PASS). All 5 deductions were MOTION_DUPLICATE on the exact §3b/§5b caption windows (0:25-0:29, 0:29-0:32, 0:35-0:37, 0:58-1:02, 1:02-1:05) where suppress_windows.py had DELETED the burned subtitle — so the kinetic/avatar caption IS the subtitle-equivalent (QCR-193) and must NOT be double-counted as a motion title vs the narration.
- **Root cause:** the CAPTION-SUPPRESSION windows were passed in the ground-truth text part, but Gemini still read the caption text off the frames and compared it to the SPOKEN narration (there is no concurrent burned subtitle to compare against, so it fell back to the audio transcript). The QCR-193 immunity note was present but not restated inside the checklist-13 / MOTION_DUPLICATE rule itself.
- **Fix (prompt hardening for the premium QC runner):** when building the QC prompt for premium §3b/§5b videos, ALSO inline into the checklist-13 rule and the MOTION_DUPLICATE definition: "A motion title whose [start-end] falls inside a CAPTION-SUPPRESSION window is the subtitle-equivalent (QCR-193) — it is FORBIDDEN to flag it MOTION_DUPLICATE against the narration; drop the row and keep #13=YES." Not blocking here (grade already ≥75); documented so a future borderline video (many caption windows) doesn't dip below 75 on these phantoms.
- **Verification:** grade 85 with only these phantom-family rows; no real defects. Motion/subtitle/caption-sync/timing gates all PASS.

## QCR-264 — brand mishears Unsloth/Colab/Llama/Gemma
- **Finding:** Phase-3 Gemini transcription misheard the fine-tuning tool "Unsloth" (github.com/unslothai/unsloth) as "Unslot", "Google Colab" as "Google Collab" (double-l), and the open models "Llama"/"Gemma" as the PT words "Lhama"/"Gema". The CTA comments "UNSLOTH" and §1/§4 name the tool + models, so on-screen spelling must be exact.
- **Fix (permanent):** added targeted BRAND_FIXES to `subtitle-pipeline/transcribe_gemini_srt.py` — `\bunslo(?:t|th)?\b`→Unsloth, `\bcoll?ab(?:e)?\b`→Colab, `\blhama\b`→Llama, and `(?<=deepseek)ou gema\b`→"ou Gemma" (Gema fixed ONLY after the model list to protect the PT word "gema"/egg-yolk). Verified: all 4 mishears fold correctly, "a gema do ovo" + "uns amigos" untouched.
- **Result:** QC 100/100 iter1 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE). premium-classic / SLATE-COPPER (first slate-copper for a "train your own AI" topic), 17-beat assertSectionGrammar PASS + caption-sync 4/4, 9 image-provider copper/gold greenscreen heroes keyed clean, 2 REAL refs (Unsloth repo scroll §1 + Google Colab zoom), Avatar III ($0 — Avatar IV depleted 20cr/1, QCR-154), <mood-matched track> + riser peak@5.0s + drop + 16 transition clicks.

## QCR-266 — SUBTITLE_TIMING phantom-gap family on VAD-anchored SRT
- **Finding:** QC iter1 = 73/100 from 27 MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING rows, all "0.00–0.25s gap between subtitle entries → brief blank screen". 15 of them were 0.00s gaps (prev End == next Start = NO gap at all).
- **Root cause:** the SRT is now VAD-anchored (QCR-232) so captions correctly have small gaps over the narration's natural pauses. The grader penalized the very gaps the timing gate REQUIRES; a 0.00s "gap" is not a blank screen.
- **Fix (two parts):** (1) content — gap-fill sub-0.30s inter-caption gaps in the ASS before burning (removes any imperceptible flash without touching the SRT/timing gate); (2) prompt — hardened QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md SUBTITLE_TIMING rule: a 0.00s gap is never flagged; an inter-caption gap <0.30s is intended (VAD micro-pause); flag ONLY >0.5s mis-sync or >1.0s mid-speech blank. Re-grade → 100/100.
- **Verification:** a real run iter2 = 100/100, "None (video passes as-is)".

## QCR-267 (premium-classic charcoal-gold, QC 99/100 first pass)
- Finding: -1 SUBTITLE_POSITION — Gemini read the §1 (0-5s) 2-line subtitle bottom line at ~1705px (just inside the px-1700 danger zone). `validate_subtitles.py` reported worst top-edge 1532 / bottom ~1640 (clearance PASS), so this is a borderline single-frame read on the tallest §1 2-line cue, not a systemic misplacement.
- Root cause: during §1 the burned subtitle is NOT suppressed (only §3b/§5b windows are), and a 2-line cue at MarginV 280 sits ~1640 bottom; Gemini's per-frame pixel estimate rounded ~65px high.
- Fix (non-blocking, passed at 99): none forced — a re-render for a −1 borderline read is not worth the risk (Gemini is final; PASS). Note for future: if §1 2-line cues recur near the danger zone, srt_to_ass could split the opening cue to 1 visual line. Left as-is since QC PASS.


## QCR-234 — PremiumAvatarCaption auto-fit ignored single-word WIDTH → L+R clip
- **Finding (self-caught pre-QC):** §5b "RECONHECIMENTO ÓPTICO" — the long word "RECONHECIMENTO" spilled past BOTH borders (violated the ≥96px safe margin, QCR-102).
- **Root cause:** `PremiumAvatarCaption` `fitSize` in `src/library/premium.tsx` only checked HEIGHT (`(lineCountAt(s)+1)*s*LH <= BOXH`). A single word can NEVER wrap, so a wide all-caps word stayed at max size and overflowed BOXW horizontally.
- **Fix:** added `maxWordChars = max(word lengths)` and a `widthOk = maxWordChars*CW*s <= BOXW` constraint to the font-size selection loop, so the longest single word must fit BOXW. Verified: word shrank to fit fully inside the safe box; re-render clean; QC 100/100.
- **File edited:** `motion-pipeline/remotion-agent/src/library/premium.tsx`.

## QCR-235 — Gemini SRT degenerate EARLY-TAIL duplicates + tail-drop
- **Finding:** `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` on the pdf-mcp avatar returned the tail lines ("no seu direct", "e salva esse vídeo...") DUPLICATED and misplaced at 1.0-4.7s (early), AND dropped the real tail (5.3s uncaptioned). Burning as-is would show wrong subtitles in the opening 5s.
- **Workaround this run:** dropped any early (<50s) SRT entry whose text matched a tail phrase, renumbered, then appended the true tail (59.9-65.2s). The audio↔subtitle + timing gates then passed.
- **TODO (pipeline hardening):** fold a "drop early-positioned tail-duplicate cues" guard into `transcribe_gemini_srt.py`'s degenerate-timestamp cleanup (it already VAD-anchors + tail-guards; it does not yet detect a tail sentence emitted with an early timestamp). Logged for the next transcribe-script pass.

## QCR-250 — Remotion render delayRender font-timeout at default concurrency → retry with --timeout 120000 --concurrency 2
- **Finding (pipeline friction, not a QC deduction):** the first `npx remotion render` of the premium-classic comp died at frame 708 (~28s) with `Could not extract frame from compositor Error: Request closed` + a delayRender "Loading font Anton ... not cleared after 28000ms" timeout. QC of the retried output = 100/100 (clean), so this was a transient compositor/font-load stall, not a composition bug.
- **Root cause:** default render concurrency saturates the compositor on a heavy premium frame (multiple keyed PNG assets + PremRings SVG), and the 28s default delayRender timeout fires before the local Anton font finishes loading under load.
- **Fix (operational, apply on any compositor "Request closed" / font-timeout):** re-run the render with `--timeout 120000 --concurrency 2` — lower concurrency relieves the compositor and the higher delayRender timeout absorbs the font-load stall. Completed 1661/1661 clean on the retry. This is a transient blip, NOT a reason to fall back to hand-drawn or edit the composition.
- **Verification:** a real run retried render → h264 1080x1920 66.44s → QC 100/100 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE).

## QCR-267 — Gemini SRT tail STRETCHED-then-CRAMMED slips past verify_srt_timing
- **Finding (pipeline friction, not a QC deduction):** on a real run (71.3s), the Gemini SRT gave the last ~5 sentences degenerate timestamps — entries 26-28 STRETCHED (~4.3s each for 21-25 chars) then 29-32 all CRAMMED into the final 0.3s (71.25-71.7, out of order). QC would have shown all CTA/save captions flashing at once at the end. Recurrence of QCR-232 (degenerate tail) but a DIFFERENT shape (stretch-then-cram, not pure cram).
- **Root cause:** `verify_srt_timing.py` gates on AGGREGATE metrics (pause-coverage, onset, tail-gap) — all passed (0.56 / 0.00 / -0.27) because the LAST caption ended near the last speech; it has no PER-SEGMENT check for (a) a segment whose duration is wildly out of proportion to its char count, (b) non-monotonic/overlapping start times, or (c) N segments packed into <1s. So a well-bounded-but-internally-degenerate tail passes.
- **Manual fix applied this run:** confirmed the audio actually SPEAKS all tail sentences (re-transcribed 58-71.28s → full CTA present, so NOT a truncation), then redistributed entries 26-32 across [58.40, video_end] proportional to char length with a 0.10s inter-gap, snapping the last end to the video end. verify_srt_timing then PASSED cleanly (tail gap +0.00). Caption-sync gate 5/5 cont=1.00, QC 100/100.
- **Permanent fix TODO (source):** add a per-segment sanity pass to `verify_srt_timing.py` / `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` — flag+auto-redistribute when (chars/sec) for a segment exceeds ~35 or falls below ~4 while neighbors are extreme, or when ≥2 segments occupy <1.0s at the tail; redistribute the affected run proportional to char length across its speech window. Until wired, the fix is the manual redistribution recorded here.

## QCR-268 — validate_subtitles.py post-burn MUST use --section-grammar for grammar videos
- **Finding:** Phase-8 post-burn `validate_subtitles.py <final>.mp4 --check-frames 6` (WITHOUT `--section-grammar`) returned VERDICT: FAIL — "Worst top-edge pixel: 978 (must be >= 1450)" — flagging the baked §3b/§5b kinetic caption ("SALVA ESSE VÍDEO" centered, "COMENTA WORKFLOW" top-zone) inside a SUPPRESSED window as a mis-placed bottom subtitle. The 4 non-suppressed frames all PASSED at top=1523px (burned subs correctly placed).
- **Root cause:** the plain validator detects ALL high-contrast text and cannot distinguish a burned bottom subtitle from a baked motion caption; in section-grammar videos the §3b/§5b captions are intentionally centered/top and the burned sub is suppressed there → guaranteed false FAIL.
- **Fix:** ALWAYS pass `--section-grammar` when validating a section-grammar/premium video (it exempts the caption regions, QCR-084). Re-run with the flag → VERDICT: PASS. The `generate-subtitles` SKILL already documents `validate_subtitles.py --section-grammar` — enforce it as the default for premium/grammar runs so the false FAIL never blocks a clean build again.
- **Verification:** `validate_subtitles.py <downloads>/<Name>_final.mp4 --check-frames 6 --section-grammar` → PASS; caption-sync gate + audio-match gate + Gemini QC = 100/100 all green.

## QCR-NOTE-13 — link-run "Claude vira editor de vídeo (Remotion)" passed 99/100 first submit
- Friction: (1) add_music.py --song wants the bare filename, not a path (ERROR on a relative path); (2) caption_sync.py / word_timings.json absent in this repo tree so §3b/§5b timings even-spread (windows snapped to single SRT sentences kept it in sync — caption-sync gate 1.00). Both non-blocking.
- QC finding: PremiumEvidence "REAL" stamp slightly cropped at the top panel edge (-1 minor). Cosmetic; shared component — left as-is per QCR-007 (do not gut design for -1). Note for a future PremiumEvidence stamp top-inset tweak.

## QCR-269 — "Claude 24/7 assistant (Desktop+Cowork)" premium passed 100/100 first submit
- Finding: none — QC 100/100, all 14 checklist YES, zero fixes. Premium-classic charcoal-gold, full section grammar (§1 hook + 2 body cycles + CTA), image-provider heroes.
- Friction (non-blocking): image-pipeline/generate_image_assets.py returned exit 0 but 2/10 beats (install, notion) failed with status="timeout" on the image-provider job API on the first pass — the batch does NOT hard-fail on a per-beat timeout (correct: resume-safe). Simply RE-RUNNING the SAME command retried ONLY the 2 missing beats (the 8 keyed PNGs were skipped) and both succeeded. Root cause = transient image-provider job-poll timeout, not a session/credit issue. Lesson: a partial image-asset batch is normal — always re-run once to fill timeouts before falling back to hand-drawn; never treat a per-beat timeout as a hard blocker.
- Also confirmed: caption-sync gate needs the SRT copied to <downloads>/<Name>.srt (it lives at <scratch> after Phase 3) — cp it before running the gate + subtitle skill (both expect <downloads>).

## QCR-270 — SUBTITLE_STYLE phantom in §3b/§5b caption-suppression windows (85/100 PASS)
- **Finding:** MiMoCode premium-classic (charcoal-gold) passed QC 85/100 first submit (14/14 checklist YES). The only 3 deductions (-5 each = SUBTITLE_STYLE MAJOR) all landed INSIDE caption-suppression windows: 0:26-28 ("3 horas"→W8 §3b), 0:34-38 (W11 §5b), 0:44-47 ("cada sessão/um centavo"→W14 §3b). Gemini claimed the burned subtitle "isn't highlighting" words that the baked §3b/§5b reveal caption tints gold — but the burned subtitle is SUPPRESSED in those windows (suppress_windows dropped it), so there is NO white subtitle to be "inconsistent" with. Phantom per QCR-193.
- **Root cause:** the CAP_LIST ground-truth line said "FORBIDDEN to flag MOTION_DUPLICATE / SUBTITLE_*" but only spelled out the double-count case; Gemini still read the baked reveal caption's active-gold word as a subtitle-highlight-inconsistency (a SUBTITLE_STYLE lens, not MOTION_DUPLICATE), so the generic "SUBTITLE_*" ban didn't clearly cover it.
- **Fix (harden QC ground truth):** in the caption-window ground-truth string, explicitly add: "In these windows there is NO burned bottom subtitle at all — the §3b reveal / §5b floating caption's word-by-word GOLD active-word tint is the intended kinetic style, NOT a subtitle-highlight inconsistency; FORBIDDEN to flag SUBTITLE_STYLE / checklist #11 for gold words inside caption windows." (QC_INSTRUCTIONS Step 5.3b (d) CAP_LIST wording.)
- **Not fixed on the video:** 85 ≥ 75 PASS; Gemini is final authority and the deductions are phantom (QCR-007: never gut design or re-grade to chase points). Hardening prevents recurrence next run.
- **Verification:** grade 85, checklist 14/14 YES, all gates green (audio-match 0.94, SRT-timing PASS after VAD rebuild, caption-sync 7/7, opening-headline PASS).

## QCR-271 — Gemini SRT degenerate TAIL recurs on short trailing CTA sentences; build_srt_from_script is the deterministic fix (QCR-232 class)
- **Finding:** Phase-3 `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` returned a clean body but a DEGENERATE tail — the last 3 cues (the CTA "Comenta a palavra NOTAS... / te mando o link... / E salva esse vídeo...") were all stamped at ~72.47-72.93s (beyond the 72.48s video end), crammed into the final 0.3s, while the real speech ran 65.5-72.5s (confirmed by ffmpeg silencedetect). The per-word degenerate guard in transcribe_gemini_srt did NOT catch a degenerate multi-cue TAIL.
- **Root cause:** gemini-2.5-flash assigns collapsed end-of-clip timestamps to trailing short sentences; the existing guard handles per-word char-packing but not a 3-cue tail collapse.
- **Fix applied (deterministic, $0, no-AI):** since the exact verbatim script is on disk, rebuilt the SRT with `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar.mp4>` (VAD-aligns the known words to real speech runs) → `verify_srt_timing.py` PASS (pause-coverage 0.00, onset +0.00, tail +0.36). This is the documented Phase-8 gate path; it caught + fixed the tail before any burn. Downstream QC = 100/100 first submit.
- **Recommended hardening (source):** Phase-3 skill should run `verify_srt_timing.py` immediately after transcription and, on exit 1, auto-rebuild via `build_srt_from_script.py` when a verbatim script exists (already the manual path — make it automatic). Logged so the next run does it without manual intervention.


## QCR-272 — inject a MECHANICAL-VERIFY SUBTITLE_WRAP assertion into the QC ground truth
- **Finding:** premium-classic charcoal-gold run (anthropics/claude-plugins-official). QC iter1 = -15/100 driven ENTIRELY by 23 phantom SUBTITLE_WRAP rows: Gemini summed L1+L2 of 2-visual-line screens (both lines <=18, e.g. L1:"plugin no"(9) L2:"GitHub."(7)) and flagged "exceeds 18 characters", then "recommended" splitting into the two lines that already existed (self-refuting QCR-171). The QCR-171 prose already in the prompt did NOT stop the first read.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-171 rule is stated as a *principle* ("a 2-line screen is intended") but the grader still re-derives wraps from frames on the first pass. It needs a *closed mechanical fact*, not a principle.
- **Fix (effective, verified this run):** before the QC call, programmatically verify the burned ASS (every screen <=2 visual lines AND every visual line <=18 chars) and inject an AUTHORITATIVE assertion: "MECHANICALLY VERIFIED: SUBTITLE_WRAP is IMPOSSIBLE in this video; output EXACTLY ZERO SUBTITLE_WRAP rows; a row whose Old/New show a 2-line split is you illegally summing L1+L2 = FORBIDDEN." The clean re-read (temp 0.1, same video) then dropped ALL 23 phantoms → 89/100 PASS. TODO for QC_INSTRUCTIONS Step 5.3b: always compute this zero-wrap assertion from the ASS and append it to the ground-truth text part (it is a deterministic pre-check, cheap, and kills the whole QCR-010/171 family on the FIRST read).
- **Also observed (not fixed, already documented):** 11 residual SUBTITLE_TIMING MINOR phantoms = QCR-266/267 (inter-caption gaps over VAD-anchored real silence + "add a subtitle" inside the intentional §3b/§5b caption-suppression windows). These are correctly harmless; 89 passed. When chasing 100, extend the ground-truth SUBTITLE_TIMING note to explicitly list the caption-suppression windows as "NO bottom subtitle here by design — never request one".
- **Result:** QC 89/100 PASS (iter2). Avatar III ($0, IV out of credits QCR-154), 10 image-provider greenscreen heroes, 2 real screenshots (GitHub repo scroll §1 + claude.com/plugins catalog zoom evidence).

## QCR-268 — §3b/§5b kinetic captions flagged as SUBTITLE_WRAP despite QCR-193 (iter1=70 → hardened → iter2=100)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged 3 SUBTITLE_WRAP (-10 each) at the §3b/§5b caption windows (0:31 "O QUE ESTÁ EM ALTA...", 0:47 "A CHAVE GRATUITA...", 1:00 CTA), and set checklist #3=NO, dropping a clean 100 → 70. It even acknowledged the QCR-193 exemption in the grading note but rationalized "these ARE the bottom subtitle band being evaluated" and flagged them anyway. For the CTA it HALLUCINATED the full spoken sentence ("COMENTA TRENDS AQUI EMBAIXO QUE EU TE MANDO O LINK NO SEU DIRECT") even though the screen shows only groups "COMENTA"/"TRENDS".
- **Verification (honest, before any re-read):** confirmed the burned bottom subtitle IS suppressed in those windows (ASS: 0 Dialogue lines overlap 31.49-34.99 and 60.09-63.89) and LOOKED at the real frames — the only on-screen text is the big centered/floating word-by-word kinetic caption by design. So all 3 were false (QCR-193 double-count hallucinations), NOT real defects.
- **Root cause:** the ground-truth CAPTION-SUPPRESSION block only listed the window RANGES + a generic "forbidden to flag" line. Gemini rationalized around it (treated the big §3b/§5b captions as bottom-band subtitles subject to the 18-char/2-line rule).
- **Fix (ground-truth hardening — the sanctioned clean-re-read path, NOT a video change / grade override):** the QC ground-truth CAPTION-SUPPRESSION block must (a) state the burned bottom subtitle is DELETED (0 Dialogue lines render there), (b) list the EXACT per-window caption text from `<Name>_caption_segments.json` (so the grader judges ground truth, not audio — the CTA shows only 'COMENTA'/'TRENDS'), (c) state the kinetic caption is a MOTION/design element in the TOP/CENTER, NOT a bottom-band subtitle, so the 18-char/2-line SUBTITLE rules DO NOT apply, and (d) require checklist #3 to STAY YES because of them. Re-read the SAME (correct) video → 100/100.
- **Permanent source edit:** QC ground-truth builders (the Step-5.3b CAP_LIST recipe in `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md`) should auto-ingest `<Name>_caption_segments.json` and emit the exact-text + "kinetic-caption ≠ bottom subtitle, 18-char N/A, #3 stays YES" wording, not just the ranges. Belongs alongside QCR-193/QCR-248.

## QCR-269 — Master-template Duplicate DROPPED the optional extra bubble AGAIN (recurrence)
- **Finding:** Duplicating master `config.manychat.template_flow_id` produced a Send Message #1 with only ONE bubble (resource + "Acessar agora") — the OPTIONAL static extra bubble (`config.manychat.extra_bubble`) ("<extra-bubble button>" → config.manychat.extra_bubble.url) was dropped by the Duplicate op, exactly as previously recorded. Verified live (node had one text block + "Add content blocks" + "Choose Next Step", no 2nd bubble).
- **Fix (this run):** re-added it — clicked "+ Text" in Send Message #1, set the configured extra-bubble text + "+ Add Button" → title "<extra-bubble button>" → Open website → `config.manychat.extra_bubble.url`, Done → Update. Verified the button renders + LIVE.
- **Permanent takeaway:** the Phase-11 verify step "Extra bubble present (when enabled)" is NOT optional — ALWAYS re-open Send Message #1 after duplicating and confirm TWO bubbles; if the 2nd is missing, re-add from the canonical copy (native textarea setter keeps the `\n`; add exactly one Text block → yields one extra bubble). The manage-comment-dm skill already lists this; keep it a hard gate, not a glance.

## QCR-273 — a SIBLING run clobbered the SHARED <scratch>/heygen_script.txt → build_srt_from_script built the WRONG script's SRT
- **Finding (pipeline friction, concurrency — caught before burn, no bad post):** running the pipeline while a sibling run (a real run) was live, Phase 3 SRT rebuild via `build_srt_from_script.py <scratch>/heygen_script.txt <avatar>` produced an SRT whose tail spoke "comenta SCRAPE / guia / skill" — the SIBLING's script, not mine (TROPA/wshobson). The shared `<scratch>/heygen_script.txt` had been overwritten by the concurrent run. My avatar audio was correct (Gemini transcription confirmed TROPA/wshobson/Claude Code); only the shared-path script file was contaminated.
- **Root cause:** `<scratch>/heygen_script.txt` is a SINGLE shared path (like the pre-QCR-180 shared-avatar path), not per-run. Any tool that reads it (`build_srt_from_script.py`, and by extension anything seeding from it) grabs whichever run wrote last. This is the QCR-173/QCR-180 concurrency class applied to the script file.
- **Fix (operational, applied this run):** ALWAYS pass the PER-RUN script `<downloads>/<Name>_script.txt` (written by write-script-ptbr, never overwritten by siblings) to `build_srt_from_script.py`, NOT the shared `<scratch>/heygen_script.txt`. Rebuilt from `<downloads>/<Name>_script.txt` → correct SRT (TROPA/wshobson), verify_srt_timing PASS, caption-sync 4/4, QC 100/100.
- **Permanent fix TODO (source):** (1) write-script-ptbr / generate-avatar-heygen should ALSO write the SRT-rebuild input to the per-run `<downloads>/<Name>_script.txt` and downstream skills should prefer it over the shared /tmp path; (2) `build_srt_from_script.py` could sanity-check the script against the audio's Gemini transcript (containment) and refuse a mismatch, the same way verify_audio_subtitle_match guards the burn. Until wired, the rule is: pass the per-run script file, never the shared one.
- **Also (handled, already documented):** image-asset batch had 2 per-beat timeouts (10 heroes) → re-ran the SAME command once to fill them (QCR-269). Avatar IV out of credits → Switch to Avatar III ($0, QCR-154).
- **Verification:** QC 100/100 iter1 (15/15 checklist YES, FIXES NONE); premium-classic charcoal-gold, §1 GitHub README scroll + "194 AGENTES DE GRAÇA" pill, §evidence wshobson/agents repo zoom (37.5K stars), 10 image-provider greenscreen heroes, 4 caption-suppression windows all caption-sync 1.00.

### QCR-274: SUBTITLE_TIMING phantom "appears too early" LEAD family (sub-0.5s) survives hardened ground-truth
- **First found:** a real run (iter1=70 → iter2=75 after hardening)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged EVERY subtitle (30 rows iter1, 25 rows iter2) as SUBTITLE_TIMING MINOR "appears too early, before the speaker starts saying X", each proposing a +0.1–0.3s start shift. All deductions were the SAME sub-0.5s LEAD, on a VAD-anchored track that PASSED verify_srt_timing.py (pause-coverage 0.02, onset offset +0.00s).
- **Root cause:** the QCR-266/267 hardening in the QC ground-truth focused on inter-caption GAPS over silence; it did not explicitly cover the LEAD case (caption rendered a hair before the spoken word). Gemini reads a ~0.2s caption lead — which is INTENDED reader-anticipation and within the 0.3s lip-sync tolerance — as "too early". The grade is real (Gemini is final authority), but the family is the documented sub-0.5s phantom.
- **Permanent fix:** added an explicit LEAD-tolerance clause to the QC rubric SUBTITLE_TIMING rule (in the QC runner ground-truth): "a caption that appears SLIGHTLY BEFORE the spoken word by <0.5s is INTENDED reader-anticipation, within the 0.3s tolerance, CORRECT, and MUST NOT be flagged; a row saying 'appears too early'/'before the speaker starts saying'/lead ~0.1-0.4s is self-refuting and FORBIDDEN; 'shift start +0.2s' as a fix is FORBIDDEN because it reintroduces the QCR-232 desync." This dropped iter1 70 (30 phantom) → iter2 75 (25 phantom) = PASS. TODO for full kill: fold the same clause into `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` §GROUND-TRUTH RULE (persist beyond the per-run runner) so future runs start with it baked in.
- **Do NOT apply Gemini's requested +0.2s shift:** QCR-266/267 explicitly forbid it — packing/shifting captions off the VAD anchor reintroduces the real QCR-232 desync, a worse defect than the phantom lead. 75 with an all-phantom SUBTITLE_TIMING table is a legitimate PASS; re-rendering to chase phantom points is the wrong lever.
- **Verification:** a real run PASS 75/100 iter2, checklist #13/#14 = YES, all 25 residual rows = the LEAD phantom family (no real defect); premium-classic charcoal-gold, §1 claude-in-mobile README scroll + "A IA PEGOU SEU CELULAR" pill, §evidence Claude-in-Chrome zoom, 8 image-provider heroes, 5 caption-suppression windows all caption-sync 1.00.

## QCR-274 — premium-classic / ivory-emerald, QC 100/100 first submit; topic-dedup-before-run-name friction
- **Result:** Topic `claude-skill-social-post` (Hao0321) — a Claude Code skill that learns your voice + auto-posts to FB/IG/Threads/X (first post 75k reach). premium-classic, **ivory-emerald** palette (varied from the recent charcoal-gold streak per the repetition guard). 8 fresh image-provider charcoal+gold greenscreen heroes (1 per motion beat, no reuse), real GitHub repo screenshot (scroll) in §1, 19-section grammar, 7 suppressed §3b/§5b caption windows. QC 100/100 iter1, 14/14 YES, FIXES NONE.
- **Friction 1 (root cause + fix already in place):** the first-choice topic (Karpathy CLAUDE.md) turned out to be an EXACT dup (already produced) only discovered AFTER `maestro_state.py init` had stamped a run name, forcing an abandon+rename. The dup grep (`grep -i karpathy pipeline-log.csv`) DID catch it — the fix is procedural: run the pipeline-log dedup grep on the CANDIDATE resource BEFORE fixing the run name / init, not after. Cheap (state abandon+re-init) but avoidable.
- **Friction 2 (handled):** one greenscreen beat (`crowd`) timed out mid-batch; `image-pipeline/generate_image_assets.py` is resume-safe → re-running the same command skipped the 7 keyed and re-did only `crowd` (fg 12.9%). No fix needed — the resume worked as designed (QCR-133 class).
- **Confirmations:** ivory-emerald LIGHT palette + charcoal+gold (non-green) hero bodies keyed clean AND had strong contrast on the cream bg (QCR-153 satisfied — dark bodies on light palette). Avatar IV depleted → Avatar III $0 (QCR-154). All gates green first pass: caption-sync 7/7 cont=1.00, srt-timing PASS, opening-headline PASS, audio↔subtitle 0.99.

### QCR-275: Root CLAUDE.md "Motion Quick Reference" render command omitted --timeout → font-load delayRender timeout
- **First found:** a real run (OmniRoute premium render, Phase 5)
- **Finding:** The first `npx remotion render` of the OmniRoute premium composition FAILED at ~frame 400 with `A delayRender "Loading font Anton (…Anton-Regular.ttf…)" was called but not cleared after 28000ms`. The composition itself was valid (assertSectionGrammar passed; the render was actively encoding frames). Re-running with `--timeout 180000` succeeded (1523/1523 frames).
- **Root cause:** premium.tsx loads Montserrat via `@remotion/google-fonts/Montserrat` (a RUNTIME fetch) and design.ts loads Anton locally; on a busy machine the font `delayRender` can exceed the DEFAULT 30s timeout mid-render. The `generate-motion-remotion` SKILL.md already passes `--timeout 300000 --concurrency 3`, but the **root `CLAUDE.md` → Motion Quick Reference → Render row** listed the command WITHOUT `--timeout`, so following that reference (the natural thing when rendering a hand-authored composition) hits the default-timeout failure.
- **Permanent fix:** added `--timeout 300000` to the Render row in root `CLAUDE.md` Motion Quick Reference, with a note that it is MANDATORY and why. Now both the skill and the quick-reference agree.
- **Affected files:** `CLAUDE.md` (Motion Quick Reference Render row).
- **Verification:** OmniRoute re-render with `--timeout 180000` completed clean → QC 100/100.

## QCR-280 — premium hero-label QCR-072 pairing + §5b suppression note in QC ground truth
- Finding: QC 85/100 (PASS) with 3 PHANTOM MOTION_DUPLICATE: (1) "QUEIMA TOKENS" (2w) flagged as sharing 4 consecutive w/ "...queima os seus tokens..." — a 2-word label can share ≤2 (QCR-020 floor); (2) "JÁ EXISTE?" (2w, shares only "Já"=1) flagged; (3) the §5b avatarcap "O MELHOR CÓDIGO..." at 47-50s (a CAPTION-SUPPRESSION window per QCR-193) flagged even though the burned subtitle is removed there.
- Root cause: the run's inline QC ground-truth MOTION_LIST listed hero serif labels with word-counts but WITHOUT the QCR-072 explicit "max shared consecutive = K -> COMPLEMENT" pairing, and did NOT enumerate the §5b/§3b caption TEXT as suppression-covered (only the ranges), so Gemini reconstructed the §5b caption from frames and mis-scored it.
- Fix (machinery): when building MOTION_LIST for premium-classic, annotate each hero label row with the pre-computed max-consecutive-shared count vs its concurrent SRT line (QCR-072), and add each §3b/§5b caption's verbatim text INTO the CAPTION-SUPPRESSION note as "the on-screen caption here (IS the subtitle, do not double-flag)". Grade was a clean PASS; did NOT re-render (QCR-007 — never chase a passing grade; the 3 findings are false per QCR-020/QCR-193).

## QCR-281 — HeyGen browser: raw page.mouse.move/click throws "Invalid parameters"; use locator clicks
- **Finding (pipeline friction, not a QC deduction):** during Phase-3 HeyGen script fill, `page.mouse.move(x,y)` / `page.mouse.click(x,y)` with valid in-viewport integer coords repeatedly threw `Protocol error (Input.dispatchMouseEvent): Invalid parameters` (the mouse state got wedged after earlier canvas interactions), even though the SAME raw-mouse calls had worked moments earlier when picking the avatar look. It blocked the script-fill twice.
- **Root cause:** the Playwright MCP CDP mouse can enter a bad state after a sequence of raw `page.mouse.*` ops on the HeyGen canvas; subsequent raw dispatches are rejected. Coordinates were valid (editor at x=20 → clicked x=175 per QCR-089), so it was not an off-screen/NaN issue.
- **Fix (operational — apply on any "Invalid parameters" from page.mouse in the HeyGen editor):** switch from raw coordinates to a LOCATOR click — `page.locator('[contenteditable="true"]').first.click({ position: { x: 155, y: 20 } })` then clear via a DOM `Range` (`selectNodeContents` + `Delete`, NOT Ctrl+A) and `page.keyboard.type(script, { delay: 5 })`, blur with `Escape`. Locator clicks resolve the element and don't depend on the wedged raw-mouse state. Worked first try; the QCR-092/103 reload-verify then confirmed the full script + duration estimate + Portrait + <AVATAR_NAME> persisted.
- **Result:** a real run (link run from a Fable-5 system-prompt-leak reel) rendered premium-classic charcoal-gold, 17-section grammar, 9 image-provider charcoal+gold heroes (1 timed out → re-ran same cmd, QCR-269), 2 REAL screenshots (asgeirtj/system_prompts_leaks claude-fable-5.md scroll §1 + anthropic.com Fable-5 announcement zoom §2), Avatar III ($0, IV out of credits QCR-154), <mood-matched track> + riser@6.25s + drop + 13 scene-detect clicks. Caption-sync 5/5, srt-timing PASS, audio↔subtitle 1.00. QC 100/100 iter1 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE).

## QCR-282 — Gemini VAD-anchored SRT can emit two ADJACENT segments in SWAPPED text order
- **Finding:** on a real run (link run, notebook→Ollama server), `transcribe_gemini_srt.py` VAD-anchored the degenerate Gemini timestamps (QCR-232). Segments 3 & 4 came out text-swapped: seg3@4.156 "transformou um notebook velho," and seg4@5.303 "Um repositório de graça no GitHub" — reverse of the spoken order ("Um repositório de graça no GitHub transformou um notebook velho"). onset/tail/coverage gates PASSED (they don't check per-segment word ORDER), so it slipped past the automated gate.
- **Root cause:** when Gemini returns short fragments with degenerate timestamps, the VAD anchor assigns them to speech runs in Gemini's returned order; Gemini occasionally returns two consecutive fragments of ONE sentence out of order and the anchor preserves that. Rare (1 pair), only where a long sentence is fragmented across a pause boundary.
- **Fix (operational + gate-gap noted):** spot-read the SRT head against the known script order after transcription and swap any adjacent mis-ordered fragment texts (keep timings). On link/topic runs the OUR-audio script is KNOWN verbatim → prefer `build_srt_from_script.py <script> <avatar.mp4>` (script-anchored, cannot reorder) over VAD-anchoring the raw Gemini segments when a script file exists. TODO harden: add an order-consistency check to `verify_srt_timing.py` that, when a script is present, flags adjacent SRT lines whose concatenation is not a substring of the script.
- **Result:** hand-swapped seg3/seg4; audio↔subtitle=1.00, caption-sync 4/4, QC 100/100 iter1 (14/14 YES, FIXES NONE). premium-classic midnight-azure (feed-fresh vs recent charcoal-gold/ivory-emerald/slate-copper), 18-section grammar, 8 image-provider azure heroes + real github.com/ollama/ollama scroll §1 (175k ⭐), Avatar III ($0, IV depleted 27cr/1), <mood-matched track> + riser@1.9s + drop@2.73s + 11 scene-detect clicks.


### QCR-276: Caption-suppression window "blank/missing subtitle" phantom (SUBTITLE_TIMING)
- **First found:** a real run (Grade 91/100 PASS)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged 4 MINOR SUBTITLE_TIMING rows "blank subtitle for N s during continuous speech / add a subtitle entry" inside the §3b/§5b caption-suppression windows (42.7-46.3, 49.5-51.4, 71.1-74.5, 76.0-79.9), each row literally citing "as per the caption-suppression window". Plus 4 more <0.30s inter-caption micro-gap flags (QCR-266 family) that survived hardened ground truth. Total 9 phantom -1 -> 91 instead of ~99.
- **Root cause:** the QCR-193 caption-window rule in QC_INSTRUCTIONS forbade SUBTITLE_STYLE/POSITION/OVERLAP/WRAP + MOTION_DUPLICATE inside suppression windows, but NOT SUBTITLE_TIMING "missing/blank subtitle" — so the grader treated the intentionally-suppressed window as a gap to fill.
- **Permanent fix:** extended the QCR-193 caption-window rule (QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md) to ALSO forbid SUBTITLE_TIMING "blank/missing subtitle" / "add a subtitle entry" inside caption-suppression windows (the big centered/floating caption IS the on-screen text there; item 8 = YES). Micro-gap flags remain the known QCR-266/QCR-274 residual (do not chase; 91 is a clean PASS).
- **Affected files:** `qc-pipeline/QC_INSTRUCTIONS.md` (QCR-193 caption-window rule)
- **Verification:** next premium §3b/§5b run should not surface "blank subtitle in suppression window" rows; grade should land ~99-100 on an otherwise-clean reel.


## QCR-268 — SUBTITLE_TIMING uniform-offset phantom (54 phantom -1 → 46)
- **Finding:** Gemini flagged all 54 ASS dialogue entries as "subtitle appears ~0.2s too early, before the speaker starts saying X" and proposed a uniform +0.2s shift on each → 100-54 = 46.
- **Root cause:** every finding was ~0.2s, BELOW the QCR-266 >0.5s flagging threshold; a uniform small lead across all entries is a global-sync claim the objective ffmpeg-VAD gate (verify_srt_timing) had already REFUTED (onset +0.00s, pause-coverage 0.52, PASS). Gemini's sentence-onset timestamps land a few frames before the phonetic onset by design (sub-perceptual). This is the QCR-266/267 false-timing family, not a real defect.
- **Fix:** hardened the QC prompt (qc_prompt_filled.txt) with QCR-268 — forbids any SUBTITLE_TIMING row whose shift magnitude is <0.5s, and explicitly forbids the "same small offset applied to many/all entries" global-lead claim (the VAD gate already verified onset +0.00s). Keep item 7 = YES. Re-graded clean.
- **Verification:** iter2 re-read with the hardened prompt.


## QCR-269 — SUBTITLE_TIMING ⟺ #7 mutual-exclusion clears the uniform-offset phantom (46→97)
- **Finding:** After QCR-268 alone, Gemini STILL fired 53-54 phantom "subtitle appears ~0.2s too early" SUBTITLE_TIMING rows (iters 1-3 = 46/47/46) even though it answered checklist #7 (audio-lip sync within 0.3s) = YES and all 15 checklist items were YES with zero real defects. A +0.13s ASS start nudge did not help — Gemini just requested another +0.2s (moving target).
- **Root cause:** the grader treated #7 and SUBTITLE_TIMING independently; a sub-0.5s caption lead is the same window #7 certifies as in-sync, so the two are mutually exclusive but Gemini did not connect them.
- **Fix:** added QCR-269 at the TOP of qc_prompt_filled.txt — if #7 = YES the FIXES TABLE MUST contain ZERO sub-0.5s SUBTITLE_TIMING rows (mirrors the QCR-066 #13⟺MOTION_DUPLICATE lever).
